# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 31

# 📖 Past Experiences — অতীতের অভিজ্ঞতা

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Goal:** অতীতের ঘটনা সহজে বলা + প্রশ্ন করা + follow-up question করা + experience explain করা

Lesson 30 পর্যন্ত তুমি basic daily topics নিয়ে কথা বলেছ।
এখন থেকে আমরা **real experience** নিয়ে কথা বলা শুরু করব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 31-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি বলতে পারবে:

> **গতকাল কী করেছিলে**
> **গত সপ্তাহে কোথায় গিয়েছিলে**
> **জীবনের memorable day**
> **কোনো experience হয়েছে কি না**
> **কঠিন decision**
> **mistake**
> **কী শিখেছ**
> **কাউকে সাহায্য করা**
> **কী ঘটেছিল**
> **কেমন অনুভব করেছিলে**

সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ:

> **Past event → What happened → Details → Feeling → Lesson**

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — প্রথমে বুঝি: Past Experience কী?

**Past Experience = অতীতে ঘটে যাওয়া কোনো ঘটনা বা অভিজ্ঞতা।**

যেমন:

> I visited Cox's Bazar last year.

আমি গত বছর কক্সবাজার গিয়েছিলাম।

> I helped my friend yesterday.

আমি গতকাল আমার বন্ধুকে সাহায্য করেছিলাম।

> I made a difficult decision last month.

আমি গত মাসে একটি কঠিন সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছিলাম।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Past নিয়ে কথা বলার 2টি Important Structure

এখানেই Lesson 31-এর সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ grammar।

## ① Past Simple

কোনো **নির্দিষ্ট অতীতের ঘটনা** বলার জন্য।

### Structure:

> **Subject + V2**

Examples:

> I went to Dhaka.

> I watched a movie.

> I met my friend.

> I studied English.

> I played cricket.

### Common verbs:

| Present | Past    |
| ------- | ------- |
| go      | went    |
| come    | came    |
| eat     | ate     |
| see     | saw     |
| meet    | met     |
| buy     | bought  |
| take    | took    |
| make    | made    |
| get     | got     |
| give    | gave    |
| have    | had     |
| do      | did     |
| feel    | felt    |
| learn   | learned |
| help    | helped  |
| visit   | visited |
| watch   | watched |
| play    | played  |
| study   | studied |

---

# ② Present Perfect

কোনো **life experience** হয়েছে কি না—এটা বলার জন্য খুব useful।

### Structure:

> **Subject + have/has + V3**

Examples:

> I have traveled alone.

> I have visited Cox's Bazar.

> I have helped someone.

> I have made difficult decisions.

### Question:

> **Have you ever + V3...?**

Examples:

> Have you ever traveled alone?

> Have you ever visited another country?

> Have you ever helped someone?

> Have you ever made a difficult decision?

---

# ⚠️ Past Simple vs Present Perfect

এটা খুব সহজভাবে মনে রাখো।

### নির্দিষ্ট সময় আছে → Past Simple

> I visited Cox's Bazar **last year**.

### নির্দিষ্ট সময় নেই → Present Perfect

> I have visited Cox's Bazar.

---

### Compare:

> **What did you do yesterday?**

➡️ নির্দিষ্ট সময় → Past Simple

> I went to university yesterday.

---

> **Have you ever traveled alone?**

➡️ জীবনের experience → Present Perfect

> Yes, I have traveled alone.

---

# 🟢 Question 1

# What did you do yesterday?

### Meaning:

**তুমি গতকাল কী করেছিলে?**

### Structure:

> What did + subject + verb?

⚠️ এখানে **did** থাকলে main verb-এর **base form** হবে।

### ❌ Wrong:

> What did you went yesterday?

### ✅ Correct:

> What did you do yesterday?

---

### Short Answer

> I studied English yesterday.

### Better Answer

> I studied English yesterday. I also watched a movie in the evening.

### Natural Answer

> Yesterday, I studied English for a few hours. In the evening, I watched a movie and spent some time relaxing. It was a normal but productive day.

---

# 🔥 Follow-up Questions

> What did you study?

> Where did you study?

> Who were you with?

> What did you do in the evening?

> How did you feel?

---

# 🟢 Question 2

# Where did you go last week?

### Meaning:

**গত সপ্তাহে তুমি কোথায় গিয়েছিলে?**

### Answer:

> I went to my university last week.

### Better:

> I went to my university several times last week. I attended classes and met some of my friends.

### Natural:

> Last week, I went to my university several times. I attended my classes, met some friends, and worked on a few tasks. I was quite busy, but I enjoyed spending time with my friends.

---

# 🟢 Question 3

# What was the best day of your life?

### Meaning:

**তোমার জীবনের সবচেয়ে ভালো দিন কোনটি ছিল?**

এখানে তোমাকে একটি **specific memory** নিয়ে কথা বলতে হবে।

### Easy Structure:

> **When → What happened → Who was there → Why it was special → Feeling**

### Example:

> One of the best days of my life was my graduation day. My family and friends were there, and everyone was very happy. I felt proud because I had worked hard for several years to reach that point. It was a very special and memorable day for me.

---

# ⭐ Important Expression

> **One of the best days of my life was...**

এটা খুব useful expression।

Examples:

> One of the best days of my life was my graduation day.

> One of the best days of my life was when I got my first job.

> One of the best days of my life was when I achieved an important goal.

---

# 🟡 Question 4

# Have you ever traveled alone?

### Meaning:

**তুমি কি কখনো একা ভ্রমণ করেছ?**

এটা **Present Perfect Experience Question**।

### Structure:

> Have you ever + V3?

---

### If YES:

> Yes, I have traveled alone.

### Better:

> Yes, I have traveled alone. It was a little difficult at first, but I enjoyed the experience.

### Natural:

> Yes, I have traveled alone. At first, I was a little nervous because I had to manage everything myself. However, the experience made me more confident and independent.

---

### If NO:

> No, I haven't traveled alone yet.

Then add:

> But I'd like to try it in the future.

🔥 Natural:

> No, I haven't traveled alone yet, but I'd like to try it someday because I think it would help me become more independent.

---

# 🟡 Question 5

# Have you ever made a difficult decision?

### Meaning:

**তুমি কি কখনো কঠিন কোনো সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছ?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have made a difficult decision before.

Then explain:

> It was difficult because I had several options.

> I thought carefully before making the decision.

> Finally, I chose what I thought was best.

> It taught me to think carefully before making important decisions.

---

# 🧠 Useful Structure

> **Situation → Problem → Decision → Result → Lesson**

### Example:

> Yes, I have made a difficult decision before. I had to choose between two important options. I wasn't sure which one was better, so I thought carefully about the advantages and disadvantages. Finally, I made my decision and accepted the result. It taught me that difficult decisions require patience and careful thinking.

---

# 🔴 Question 6

# What was your biggest mistake?

### Meaning:

**তোমার সবচেয়ে বড় ভুল কী ছিল?**

এখানে খুব personal detail বলার দরকার নেই।

একটি সাধারণ mistake ব্যবহার করতে পারো।

### Example:

> One of my biggest mistakes was not managing my time properly.

### Expand:

> I sometimes spent too much time on unimportant things. As a result, I couldn't finish some important tasks on time. Later, I realized that I needed better time management.

---

# ⭐ Useful Expressions

> **One of my biggest mistakes was...**

> **I realized that...**

> **Looking back, I think...**

> **If I could go back, I would...**

---

# 🟡 Question 7

# What did you learn from it?

### Meaning:

**সেই ভুল থেকে তুমি কী শিখেছ?**

এখানে **Lesson** বলতে হবে।

### Example:

> I learned that time management is very important.

### Better:

> I learned that I should manage my time more carefully and focus on important tasks.

### Advanced:

> The experience taught me that good time management is essential. Now I try to prioritize important tasks and avoid wasting too much time on things that are not important.

---

# 🔥 Question 6 + 7 একসাথে

### Q:

> What was your biggest mistake?

### A:

> One of my biggest mistakes was not managing my time properly.

### Q:

> What did you learn from it?

### A:

> I learned that time management is extremely important. Now I try to plan my day and prioritize my important tasks.

🔥 **এভাবেই একটি topic থেকে conversation তৈরি হয়।**

---

# 🟢 Question 8

# Have you ever helped someone?

### Meaning:

**তুমি কি কখনো কাউকে সাহায্য করেছ?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have helped someone before.

কিন্তু এখানেই থামবে না।

বলবে:

> Who?

> What happened?

> How did you help?

> How did they feel?

> How did you feel?

---

# 🟢 Question 9

# What happened?

এই question সাধারণত **story continue করার জন্য** ব্যবহার হবে।

### Example:

> My friend was having difficulty with an assignment.

> He asked me for help.

> I explained the difficult parts to him.

> After that, he understood the topic better.

---

# 🟢 Question 10

# How did you feel?

এখানে **emotion** বলতে হবে।

### Useful vocabulary:

| English      | বাংলা        |
| ------------ | ------------ |
| Happy        | আনন্দিত      |
| Sad          | দুঃখিত       |
| Excited      | উত্তেজিত     |
| Nervous      | নার্ভাস      |
| Scared       | ভীত          |
| Surprised    | অবাক         |
| Proud        | গর্বিত       |
| Confident    | আত্মবিশ্বাসী |
| Relaxed      | স্বস্তিতে    |
| Worried      | চিন্তিত      |
| Embarrassed  | বিব্রত       |
| Satisfied    | সন্তুষ্ট     |
| Grateful     | কৃতজ্ঞ       |
| Disappointed | হতাশ         |

---

# 🎯 Part 3 — Emotion Formula

শুধু:

> I felt happy.

না।

এভাবে বলবে:

> **I felt + emotion + because + reason.**

### Examples:

> I felt happy because I was able to help my friend.

> I felt nervous because it was my first experience.

> I felt proud because I had achieved my goal.

> I felt disappointed because things didn't go as planned.

> I felt relieved because the problem was finally solved.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Complete Story

এখন Question 8, 9, 10 একসাথে করি।

### Q8:

> Have you ever helped someone?

### Answer:

> Yes, I have helped a friend with an assignment.

### Q9:

> What happened?

### Answer:

> My friend was having difficulty understanding a topic, so he asked me for help. I explained the topic and gave him some examples. After that, he understood it better.

### Q10:

> How did you feel?

### Answer:

> I felt happy and satisfied because I was able to help someone. It also reminded me that sharing knowledge can be useful.

🔥 **এটাই Past Experience speaking-এর basic story pattern।**

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Past Story Formula

যেকোনো past experience বলার সময় এই formula ব্যবহার করবে:

## **1. When**

কখন?

> Last year...

> Yesterday...

> A few months ago...

> When I was a student...

## **2. Situation**

কী situation ছিল?

> I was...

> I had to...

> I wanted to...

## **3. What happened**

কী ঘটেছিল?

> Then...

> After that...

> Suddenly...

## **4. Action**

তুমি কী করেছিলে?

> I decided to...

> I tried to...

> I helped...

## **5. Result**

শেষে কী হলো?

> Finally...

> As a result...

## **6. Feeling**

কেমন লেগেছিল?

> I felt...

## **7. Lesson**

কী শিখলে?

> I learned that...

---

# 🧩 Part 6 — Story Connectors

Past story বলতে এগুলো খুব important:

### Beginning

> One day...

> Last year...

> A few months ago...

> When I was younger...

> I remember one time...

### Middle

> Then...

> After that...

> A little later...

> Suddenly...

> At that moment...

### Result

> Finally...

> In the end...

> As a result...

### Lesson

> I learned that...

> It taught me that...

> Since then...

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “Have You Ever...?” Mastery

এই pattern **খুব ভালোভাবে শিখবে**।

### Structure:

> **Have you ever + V3?**

Examples:

> Have you ever traveled alone?

> Have you ever visited another city?

> Have you ever lost something important?

> Have you ever helped a stranger?

> Have you ever made a difficult decision?

> Have you ever spoken English with a foreigner?

> Have you ever won a competition?

> Have you ever failed at something?

> Have you ever tried something new?

---

# 🟢 How to Answer

## YES

> Yes, I have.

Then:

> When?

> Where?

> What happened?

> How did you feel?

---

## NO

> No, I haven't.

Then:

> But I'd like to...

Example:

> No, I haven't traveled alone yet, but I'd like to try it in the future.

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

### ❌ Don't say:

> Have you ever went there?

### ✅ Say:

> Have you ever gone there?

Because:

> **Have + V3**

---

### ❌

> Have you ever saw a tiger?

### ✅

> Have you ever seen a tiger?

---

### ❌

> Have you ever ate sushi?

### ✅

> Have you ever eaten sushi?

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Past Simple vs Present Perfect Practice

### 1.

> I went to Cox's Bazar **last year**.

Specific time → Past Simple.

### 2.

> I have been to Cox's Bazar.

Experience → Present Perfect.

---

### 3.

> I watched a movie **yesterday**.

### 4.

> I have watched many English movies.

---

### 5.

> I met him **last week**.

### 6.

> I have met him before.

---

# 🧠 Easy Rule

### Past Simple

**When exactly?**

> yesterday
> last week
> last year
> in 2025
> two days ago

### Present Perfect

**Have you experienced it?**

> ever
> never
> before
> already
> yet

---

# 🎤 Part 9 — Full Past Experience Conversation

### A:

> What did you do yesterday?

### B:

> Yesterday, I studied English and worked on some personal tasks.

### A:

> What did you do in the evening?

### B:

> I watched a movie and relaxed for a while.

### A:

> Have you ever traveled alone?

### B:

> Yes, I have. It was a little difficult at first, but I became more confident.

### A:

> Have you ever made a difficult decision?

### B:

> Yes. I once had to choose between two important options.

### A:

> What did you learn from that experience?

### B:

> I learned that I should think carefully before making important decisions.

### A:

> Have you ever helped someone?

### B:

> Yes. I once helped a friend with an assignment.

### A:

> How did you feel?

### B:

> I felt happy because I was able to help someone.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — One Experience → 10 Questions

ধরো:

> **You helped your friend with a problem.**

এখন conversation:

1. **When did it happen?**
2. **Where were you?**
3. **Who did you help?**
4. **What happened?**
5. **What was the problem?**
6. **How did you help?**
7. **Why did you help?**
8. **What happened after that?**
9. **How did your friend feel?**
10. **How did you feel?**

🔥 এই skill-টাই তোমার speaking-এর জন্য সবচেয়ে important।

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Answer Expansion

### Question:

> What did you do yesterday?

### Level 1:

> I studied English.

### Level 2:

> I studied English yesterday.

### Level 3:

> I studied English yesterday for a few hours.

### Level 4:

> I studied English yesterday for a few hours. After that, I watched a movie.

### Level 5:

> Yesterday, I studied English for a few hours. I focused mainly on speaking practice because I want to improve my communication skills. After studying, I watched a movie and relaxed for a while. Overall, it was a productive day.

🎯 **Target = Level 4–5**

---

# 🔴 Part 12 — Advanced Past Experience

এখন একটু বড় answer:

### Question:

> **Tell me about a difficult experience that taught you something important.**

### Model Answer:

> One difficult experience I had was dealing with an important decision about my future. At first, I wasn't sure which option would be best for me. I spent some time thinking about the advantages and disadvantages of each choice and also discussed the situation with people I trusted. Eventually, I made my decision and accepted the result. Although the situation was stressful, it taught me that difficult decisions require patience and careful thinking. Since then, I have tried to avoid making important decisions too quickly.

---

# 🎯 Part 13 — Important Phrases

Past experience বলার সময় এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

### শুরু করার জন্য

> I remember one time...

> One experience I remember clearly was...

> A few years ago...

> When I was younger...

> Last year...

### ঘটনা বলার জন্য

> At first...

> Then...

> After that...

> Suddenly...

> Eventually...

> Finally...

### Feeling

> I felt...

> I was really...

> I was a little...

### Learning

> I learned that...

> It taught me that...

> I realized that...

> Since then...

---

# 🗣️ Part 14 — Your First Story

এই structure দিয়ে **নিজের একটি real experience** বলবে।

## Topic:

> **A Memorable Experience in My Life**

### Structure:

**1. When?**

> A few years ago...

**2. Where?**

> I was in...

**3. What happened?**

> One day...

**4. What did you do?**

> I decided to...

**5. Result**

> Finally...

**6. Feeling**

> I felt...

**7. Lesson**

> I learned that...

---

# 🏆 Part 15 — 10 Questions Practice

এখন তোমার original 10 questions-এর **answer pattern** একসাথে দেখো।

### 1. What did you do yesterday?

> Yesterday, I ________. After that, I ________. Overall, it was ________.

### 2. Where did you go last week?

> Last week, I went to ________. I went there because ________. I ________ there.

### 3. What was the best day of your life?

> One of the best days of my life was ________. It was special because ________. I felt ________.

### 4. Have you ever traveled alone?

> Yes, I have. / No, I haven't.
> It was ________ because ________.

### 5. Have you ever made a difficult decision?

> Yes, I have. I had to ________. It was difficult because ________. Finally, I ________.

### 6. What was your biggest mistake?

> One of my biggest mistakes was ________. At that time, I ________.

### 7. What did you learn from it?

> I learned that ________. Since then, I have tried to ________.

### 8. Have you ever helped someone?

> Yes, I have. I helped ________ when ________.

### 9. What happened?

> First, ________. Then, ________. Finally, ________.

### 10. How did you feel?

> I felt ________ because ________.

---

# **“An Experience I Will Never Forget”**

এই order follow করবে:

↓

↓

↓

↓

↓

↓

↓

↓

# 📝 Part 17 — Homework

### Task 1 — Answer these 10 questions

1. What did you do yesterday?
2. Where did you go last week?
3. What was the best day of your life?
4. Have you ever traveled alone?
5. Have you ever made a difficult decision?
6. What was your biggest mistake?
7. What did you learn from it?
8. Have you ever helped someone?
9. What happened?
10. How did you feel?

**প্রতিটি answer কমপক্ষে 3–5 sentences।**

---

### Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে **10টি Past Experience questions** বানাবে।

Must use:

> **What + Where + When + Who + Why + How + Have you ever + Did you + Was/Were + What happened**

---

### Task 3 — Storytelling

এই topic-এ **3 মিনিট** বলবে:

> **“A difficult experience that taught me an important lesson.”**

Use:

> **Situation → Problem → Action → Result → Feeling → Lesson**

---

# 🏆 Lesson 31 Golden Formula

Past Experience-এর জন্য এই formula মুখস্থ রাখো:

> **WHEN → WHAT HAPPENED → WHAT I DID → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

### Example:

> **A few months ago**, I had an important problem. **I wasn't sure what to do**, so I thought carefully about the situation. **I decided to take action** and solve the problem step by step. **Eventually, everything worked out well.** I **felt relieved and happy**. **I learned that staying calm and thinking carefully can help solve difficult problems.**

---

# ⭐ Final Target

Lesson 31 শেষে notes না দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** এই topic-এ কথা বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about an important past experience, what happened, what you did, how you felt, and what you learned from it.”**

**Lesson 32 — Future Plans & Goals 🎯**

Lesson 32-এ Past থেকে naturally **Future**-এ যাব: **What are you going to do? → What will you do? → What are your goals? → Why? → How will you achieve them? → 5-minute future-plan conversation.**

---
# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 32

# 🎯 Future Plans & Goals

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** Future নিয়ে question করা → নিজের plan বলা → goal explain করা → reason বলা → action plan বলা → follow-up question করা

Lesson 31-এ আমরা শিখেছি:

> **Past Experience → What happened → Feeling → Lesson**

এখন naturally আমরা যাব:

> **Past → Present → Future**

যেমন:

> I learned from my past experience.
> **Now I have some goals for my future.**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 32-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি বলতে পারবে:

* আগামীকাল কী করবে
* আগামী সপ্তাহে কী করবে
* future plan কী
* short-term goal কী
* long-term goal কী
* career goal কী
* English শেখার goal কী
* কীভাবে goal achieve করবে
* কেন goal গুরুত্বপূর্ণ
* কোনো plan পরিবর্তন হলে কী করবে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Future সম্পর্কে 4টি Important Structure

Future নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় সবসময় শুধু **will** ব্যবহার করবে না।

আজ 4টা structure শিখবে:

### 1. **be going to**

আগে থেকে করা plan

### 2. **will**

future decision / prediction / promise

### 3. **Present Continuous**

নিশ্চিত arrangement

### 4. **Present Simple**

schedule/timetable

---

# 1️⃣ Be Going To

### Structure:

> **Subject + am/is/are + going to + V1**

Examples:

> I am going to study English tonight.

> I am going to visit my friend tomorrow.

> She is going to start a new course.

> They are going to travel next month.

### কখন ব্যবহার করবে?

যখন **আগে থেকেই plan আছে**।

---

### Question:

> What are you going to do tomorrow?

### Answer:

> I am going to study English tomorrow.

### Better:

> Tomorrow, I am going to study English for a few hours. After that, I'm going to work on my other tasks.

---

# 2️⃣ Will

### Structure:

> **Subject + will + V1**

Examples:

> I will call you later.

> I will help you.

> I will study harder.

> I will try again.

### ব্যবহার:

* instant decision
* promise
* prediction
* future statement

---

### Example:

বন্ধু বলল:

> I need some help.

তুমি:

> Don't worry. **I will help you.**

এখানে আগে থেকে plan ছিল না। মুহূর্তে decision নিয়েছ।

---

# 3️⃣ Present Continuous for Future

নিশ্চিত arrangement থাকলে:

> **Subject + am/is/are + V-ing**

Examples:

> I am meeting my friend tomorrow.

> I am visiting my family this weekend.

> I am attending a meeting tomorrow.

এখানে plan/arrangement already fixed.

---

# 4️⃣ Present Simple for Schedule

Timetable বা fixed schedule:

> The train leaves at 8 a.m.

> The class starts at 10 a.m.

> The exam begins at 9 a.m.

এগুলো future হলেও **Present Simple** ব্যবহার হয়।

---

# 🧠 Easy Difference

| Situation         | Structure              |
| ----------------- | ---------------------- |
| আগে থেকে plan     | **going to**           |
| এখন decision      | **will**               |
| fixed arrangement | **present continuous** |
| timetable         | **present simple**     |

---

# 🟢 Part 2 — Basic Future Questions

## Question 1

# What are you going to do tomorrow?

### Easy:

> I'm going to study tomorrow.

### Better:

> I'm going to study English tomorrow and finish some important tasks.

### Natural:

> Tomorrow, I'm going to spend some time studying English. I'm also going to work on some personal tasks. If I have enough time, I'll watch a movie in the evening.

Notice:

**Plan → going to**

**Possible decision → will**

---

# Question 2

# What are you going to do next weekend?

### Answer:

> Next weekend, I'm going to spend some time with my family. I may also meet some friends and relax at home.

Useful:

> **I'm going to...**

> **I may...**

> **I might...**

---

# Question 3

# What are your plans for next month?

### Answer:

> Next month, I'm going to focus more on my English speaking practice. I'm also going to improve some of my professional skills.

### Expand:

> My main goal is to become more confident when speaking English.

---

# 🎯 Part 3 — Goals

## Plan vs Goal

এগুলো এক জিনিস নয়।

### Plan:

> I am going to study English tonight.

**কখন কী করবে।**

### Goal:

> I want to become fluent in English.

**তুমি কী achieve করতে চাও।**

---

# 🟢 Short-Term Goal

**Short-term = অল্প সময়ের goal**

Examples:

> My short-term goal is to improve my English speaking skills.

> My short-term goal is to complete this course.

> My short-term goal is to improve my programming skills.

---

# 🔵 Long-Term Goal

**Long-term = দীর্ঘ সময়ের goal**

Examples:

> My long-term goal is to build a successful career.

> My long-term goal is to become a skilled software developer.

> My long-term goal is to work for a good company.

---

# 🔥 Goal Answer Formula

যেকোনো goal question:

> **Goal → Reason → Action → Timeline → Expected Result**

Example:

> My main goal is to improve my English speaking skills. I want to become more confident when communicating with other people. To achieve this, I'm going to practice speaking every day, learn new vocabulary, and have regular conversations in English. I hope to become much more fluent over the next few months.

---

# 🟡 Question 4

# What is your biggest goal?

### Easy:

> My biggest goal is to build a successful career.

### Better:

> My biggest goal is to build a successful career in technology.

### Advanced:

> My biggest goal is to build a successful career in the technology sector. I want to develop strong technical and communication skills and eventually work on meaningful projects that can create real value.

---

# 🟡 Question 5

# What is your career goal?

### Answer:

> My career goal is to become a skilled software developer.

### Expand:

> I want to become a skilled software developer because I enjoy solving problems and building useful applications. To achieve this goal, I'm going to improve my programming, database, backend, and frontend skills.

---

# 🟡 Question 6

# What are you going to do to achieve your goal?

এটা **খুব important question**।

শুধু:

> I will work hard.

বললে answer weak হবে।

### Better:

> I'm going to practice every day and improve my skills step by step.

### Strong:

> To achieve my goal, I'm going to practice regularly, work on real projects, improve my communication skills, and learn from my mistakes. I also want to build a strong portfolio and gain practical experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Goal Achievement Formula

মনে রাখো:

> **Goal**

↓

> **Why**

↓

> **What**

↓

> **How**

↓

> **When**

↓

> **Result**

### Example:

> I want to improve my English.

**Why?**

> Because communication is important for my career.

**What?**

> I want to improve my speaking and listening.

**How?**

> I'm going to practice every day.

**When?**

> I'll practice for at least 30 minutes a day.

**Result?**

> I hope to become more confident and fluent.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — Will vs Going To

এটা ভালোভাবে বুঝো।

### Situation:

তুমি আগে থেকেই plan করেছ:

> I am going to study English tonight.

### Situation:

কেউ বলল:

> You need to practice more.

তুমি immediately বললে:

> Okay, I will practice more.

---

### Another Example

আগে থেকে:

> I'm going to visit my friend tomorrow.

Phone এ friend বলল:

> Can you help me tomorrow?

তুমি:

> Sure, I will help you.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — May / Might

Future সবসময় 100% certain না।

তখন:

> **may**

> **might**

ব্যবহার করবে।

### Examples:

> I may travel next month.

> I might watch a movie tonight.

> I may join the course.

> I might visit my friend this weekend.

### Difference:

**may = possibility**

**might = possibility, often a little less certain**

Speaking-এ দুটোই useful।

---

# 🟢 Question 7

# What are you going to do this evening?

### Answer:

> This evening, I'm going to study English. After that, I'm going to have dinner and relax.

### Natural:

> This evening, I'm planning to study English for a couple of hours. After that, I'll probably have dinner and spend some time relaxing.

---

# 🟢 Question 8

# What are your plans for the next five years?

এখানে long answer দরকার।

### Easy structure:

> Career → Skills → Experience → Personal growth

### Model:

> Over the next five years, I want to develop my career, improve my technical skills, and gain practical experience. I also want to improve my English communication skills. My goal is to become a confident professional and take on more challenging responsibilities.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — Future Questions

এগুলো speaking practice-এর জন্য খুব important।

### Basic

> What are you going to do tomorrow?

> What are you doing this weekend?

> What will you do tonight?

> What are your plans for next month?

### Goals

> What is your biggest goal?

> What is your short-term goal?

> What is your long-term goal?

> What is your career goal?

### Reason

> Why is this goal important to you?

### Action

> How are you going to achieve it?

### Time

> When do you want to achieve it?

### Difficulty

> What challenges might you face?

### Backup

> What will you do if your plan doesn't work?

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Why?” Follow-up

### Q:

> What is your biggest goal?

### A:

> My biggest goal is to become a successful software developer.

Then immediately:

> **Why?**

### A:

> Because I enjoy technology and problem-solving.

Then:

> **How are you going to achieve it?**

### A:

> I'm going to improve my programming skills and build real projects.

Then:

> **What challenges might you face?**

### A:

> I may face difficulties with time management and learning complex topics.

Then:

> **What will you do if you fail?**

### A:

> I'll analyze my mistakes, learn from them, and try again.

🔥 This is **Intermediate Speaking**.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Future Problem & Solution

এখন future-এর একটি important pattern:

> **If + Present Simple → will + V1**

### Example:

> If I don't understand something, I will ask for help.

> If I fail, I will try again.

> If I have enough time, I will practice more.

> If my plan changes, I will make a new plan.

---

# ⚠️ Important

### ❌ Don't say:

> If I will fail, I will try again.

### ✅ Say:

> If I fail, I will try again.

---

# 🔴 Part 10 — What If Your Plan Fails?

### Question:

> What will you do if you don't achieve your goal?

### Strong Answer:

> If I don't achieve my goal on time, I won't give up. I'll try to understand what went wrong, improve my strategy, and continue working toward it. I believe failure can be a useful learning experience.

🔥 Excellent intermediate answer.

---

# 🎯 Part 11 — Future Prediction

### Question:

> What do you think your life will be like in five years?

### Answer:

> I think my life will be quite different in five years. I hope to have a stable career, stronger skills, and more confidence. I also want to continue learning and take on new challenges.

---

# 🟡 Useful Prediction Expressions

> I think...

> I believe...

> I hope...

> I expect...

> I'm sure...

> Probably...

> Maybe...

> I don't know, but I think...

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Future Dreams

### Question:

> What is your dream?

### Answer:

> My dream is to build a successful career and become financially independent.

### Expand:

> My dream is to build a successful career in technology and become financially independent. I also want to keep learning new skills and eventually work on projects that are meaningful and useful to people.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Goal vs Dream vs Plan

এগুলো আলাদা:

### Dream

> I want to become very successful.

**বড় আশা**

### Goal

> I want to become a skilled software developer.

**Specific target**

### Plan

> I'm going to study programming every day.

**Action**

### Timeline

> I want to achieve this within the next three years.

**কখন**

---

# 🔥 Complete Formula

> **Dream → Goal → Why → Plan → Action → Deadline → Result**

Example:

> My dream is to build a successful career in technology. My main goal is to become a skilled software developer. I want this because I enjoy solving problems and creating useful things. To achieve it, I'm going to improve my technical skills, build real projects, and gain practical experience. I'll practice consistently and track my progress. I hope to achieve my main career goals within the next few years.

---

# 🗣️ Part 14 — Full Conversation

### A:

> What are your plans for tomorrow?

### B:

> I'm going to study English and work on some important tasks.

### A:

> What is your biggest goal?

### B:

> My biggest goal is to build a successful career.

### A:

> What kind of career do you want?

### B:

> I'd like to build a career in technology.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> Because I enjoy technology and problem-solving.

### A:

> How are you going to achieve your goal?

### B:

> I'm going to improve my technical skills, build real projects, and gain practical experience.

### A:

> What challenges might you face?

### B:

> I may face challenges with time management and learning difficult topics.

### A:

> What will you do if you fail?

### B:

> I'll learn from my mistakes and try again.

### A:

> Where do you see yourself in five years?

### B:

> I hope to be a skilled professional with strong technical and communication skills.

---

# 🧩 Part 15 — Past → Present → Future

এটা Lesson 31 + 32-এর সবচেয়ে important connection।

### Past:

> I made some mistakes in the past.

### Lesson:

> I learned from those mistakes.

### Present:

> Now I am trying to improve myself.

### Future:

> In the future, I will use what I have learned to achieve my goals.

🔥 এই structure দিয়ে অনেক powerful speaking answer তৈরি করা যায়।

---

# 🎤 Part 16 — 3-Minute Speaking Practice

## Topic:

# **My Future Plans and Goals**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1. Tomorrow

> What am I going to do tomorrow?

### 2. Next Weekend

> What am I going to do next weekend?

### 3. Next Month

> What are my plans for next month?

### 4. Short-Term Goal

> What do I want to achieve soon?

### 5. Long-Term Goal

> What do I want to achieve in the future?

### 6. Career

> What career do I want?

### 7. Why

> Why do I want it?

### 8. Action

> How will I achieve it?

### 9. Challenge

> What problems might I face?

### 10. Backup

> What will I do if my plan fails?

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — 10 Important Questions

আজকের lesson-এর core questions:

1. **What are you going to do tomorrow?**
2. **What are your plans for next weekend?**
3. **What are your plans for next month?**
4. **What is your biggest goal?**
5. **What is your short-term goal?**
6. **What is your long-term goal?**
7. **What is your career goal?**
8. **Why is your goal important to you?**
9. **How are you going to achieve your goal?**
10. **What will you do if your plan doesn't work?**

---

# 📝 Part 18 — Question Making Practice

নিজে 10টি future questions তৈরি করবে।

Must use:

> **What**

> **When**

> **Where**

> **Why**

> **How**

> **Which**

> **Will**

> **Going to**

> **Might**

> **What if**

### Example:

> What are you going to do tomorrow?

> When will you start your new plan?

> How are you going to achieve your goal?

> What might stop you?

> What will you do if you fail?

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Useful Future Phrases

### Plan

> I'm planning to...

> I'm going to...

> I intend to...

> I hope to...

### Goal

> My goal is to...

> I want to...

> I'd like to...

> I aim to...

### Prediction

> I think I will...

> I believe I will...

> I expect to...

### Possibility

> I may...

> I might...

> There's a chance that...

### Commitment

> I will...

> I'll make sure to...

> I'll keep working on it.

# 🏆 Lesson 32 Final Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **“My Future Plans, Goals, and Dreams”**

**3–5 minutes** কথা বলবে।

এই structure অবশ্যই ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Present situation**

↓

> **Tomorrow's plan**

↓

> **Next week's plan**

↓

> **Short-term goal**

↓

> **Long-term goal**

↓

> **Career goal**

↓

> **Why**

↓

> **How**

↓

> **Challenges**

↓

> **What if I fail**

↓

> **Where I see myself in 5 years**

---

# ⭐ Model Answer

> At the moment, I'm focusing on improving myself and developing my skills. In the short term, I'm going to improve my English speaking skills and become more confident in communication.
>
> In the next few weeks, I plan to practice English regularly and work on my other important skills. My short-term goal is to become more comfortable speaking English without translating everything from Bengali.
>
> My long-term goal is to build a successful career in technology. I want to become a skilled professional who can solve problems and work on meaningful projects.
>
> To achieve this goal, I'm going to practice consistently, learn from experienced people, build practical projects, and improve my communication skills. I know I may face challenges, especially when learning difficult things or managing my time.
>
> If I fail, I won't give up. I'll try to understand my mistakes, change my strategy, and continue working toward my goal.
>
> In five years, I hope to be a confident professional with strong technical and communication skills. I know it will take time and effort, but I believe consistent progress is more important than trying to become perfect immediately.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 32 Golden Formula

সব future answer-এর জন্য মনে রাখো:

> **PLAN → GOAL → WHY → ACTION → TIME → CHALLENGE → SOLUTION → RESULT**

আর future speaking-এর সবচেয়ে useful 4 structures:

> **I'm going to + V1** → আগে থেকে plan

> **I will + V1** → decision/promise/prediction

> **I'm + V-ing** → fixed arrangement

> **I may/might + V1** → possibility

---

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 33

# 💭 Opinions & Giving Reasons

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** নিজের মতামত বলা → কারণ দেওয়া → Example দেওয়া → অন্যের মতামত জিজ্ঞেস করা → Agree/Disagree করা

Lesson 31–32-এর connection:

> **Lesson 31 — Past Experiences**
> What happened? → What did you learn?

> **Lesson 32 — Future Plans & Goals**
> What do you want? → How will you achieve it?

> **Lesson 33 — Opinions & Reasons**
> **What do you think? → Why? → Can you give an example?**

আজ থেকে শুধু **“I like / I don't like”** বললে হবে না। তোমাকে **নিজের opinion explain** করতে হবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 33-এর Main Goal

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* নিজের opinion কীভাবে বলবে
* “I think” ছাড়াও কীভাবে opinion বলবে
* কেন এমন মনে করো সেটা বলা
* example দেওয়া
* অন্যের opinion জিজ্ঞেস করা
* Agree করা
* Disagree করা
* Polite disagreement
* দুইটা opinion compare করা
* “What if?” দিয়ে opinion develop করা

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Opinion কী?

**Opinion = কোনো বিষয় সম্পর্কে তোমার নিজের মতামত।**

Example:

> I think exercise is important.

এটা তোমার opinion।

কিন্তু শুধু opinion বললে answer ছোট হয়।

### ❌ Basic

> I think exercise is important.

### ✅ Better

> I think exercise is important because it helps us stay healthy.

### 🔥 Strong

> I think exercise is important because it helps us stay healthy and reduces stress. For example, even a short daily walk can help people stay active.

### Golden Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example**

---

# 1️⃣ “I Think” — সবচেয়ে সহজ

### Structure:

> **I think + sentence.**

Examples:

> I think English is important.

> I think social media is useful.

> I think exercise is necessary.

> I think technology has changed our lives.

---

# 2️⃣ “I Believe”

একটু stronger opinion:

> I believe education is very important.

> I believe everyone should learn basic computer skills.

---

# 3️⃣ “In My Opinion”

Formal এবং useful:

> In my opinion, social media has both advantages and disadvantages.

> In my opinion, regular exercise is important for everyone.

---

# 4️⃣ “Personally, I Think”

Natural conversation-এ খুব useful:

> Personally, I think learning English takes time and practice.

> Personally, I think people should spend less time on social media.

---

# 5️⃣ “From My Point of View”

আরও formal:

> From my point of view, technology makes communication easier.

---

# 🧠 Opinion Expressions

| Expression                 | Use              |
| -------------------------- | ---------------- |
| I think...                 | সাধারণ opinion   |
| I believe...               | Stronger belief  |
| In my opinion...           | Formal           |
| Personally, I think...     | Personal opinion |
| From my point of view...   | Formal           |
| As far as I'm concerned... | Advanced         |
| I would say...             | Natural          |
| It seems to me that...     | Advanced         |

---

# 🟢 Part 2 — Giving Reasons

Opinion বলার পরে সবচেয়ে important word:

# **Because**

> I think exercise is important **because** it keeps us healthy.

---

## আরও Reason Expressions

### The main reason is...

> The main reason is that it saves time.

### One reason is...

> One reason is that it is convenient.

### Another reason is...

> Another reason is that it is affordable.

### This is because...

> This is because people can access information easily.

### That's why...

> It's convenient, and that's why I use it regularly.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Opinion + Reason + Example

এটাই আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula।

## Formula:

> **I think + Opinion + because + Reason + For example + Example**

### Example:

> I think learning English is important because it helps people communicate with others. For example, English can be useful when studying, traveling, or applying for international jobs.

---

# 🟢 Question 1

# Do you think English is important?

### Level 1:

> Yes, I think English is important.

### Level 2:

> I think English is important because it is widely used around the world.

### Level 3:

> I think English is very important because it helps people communicate internationally. For example, it is useful for education, technology, travel, and many careers.

### 🔥 Level 4:

> In my opinion, English is an extremely useful skill because it allows people to communicate with others from different countries. It also gives people access to a huge amount of information online. For example, many books, courses, technical resources, and professional opportunities are available in English.

---

# 🟢 Question 2

# Do you think social media is useful?

### Answer:

> Yes, I think social media is useful because it helps people communicate and share information. For example, people can stay connected with friends and learn about current events.

### But...

> However, excessive use of social media can waste time and affect people's concentration.

🔥 এখানে তুমি **balanced opinion** দিয়েছ।

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Balanced Opinion

সব বিষয়ই 100% good বা 100% bad নয়।

Natural English:

> **It has both advantages and disadvantages.**

### Example:

> I think social media has both advantages and disadvantages.

Then:

> One advantage is that it helps people communicate.

> Another advantage is that people can learn new things.

> However, excessive use can waste time.

> Overall, I think it is useful if people use it responsibly.

🔥 এটা Intermediate speaking-এর জন্য খুব important structure।

---

# 🟡 Part 5 — Agreeing

কেউ opinion দিলে তুমি agree করতে পারো।

### Simple:

> I agree.

### Natural:

> I agree with you.

### Strong:

> I completely agree with you.

### Partial:

> I agree to some extent.

### Advanced:

> I agree with your point.

> That's a good point.

> I think you're right.

> I feel the same way.

---

# 🟡 Part 6 — Disagreeing

Disagree করার সময় সরাসরি:

> ❌ You're wrong.

বলবে না।

### Polite:

> I don't completely agree.

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

> I see your point, but I think differently.

> I understand what you mean, but I have a different opinion.

> That's an interesting point, but I see it differently.

🔥 এগুলো real conversation-এর জন্য অত্যন্ত useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Strong Disagreement → Polite

### ❌ Too direct:

> You're wrong.

### ✅ Better:

> I don't think that's completely true.

### ❌

> That's a bad idea.

### ✅

> I'm not sure that's the best approach.

### ❌

> Social media is useless.

### ✅

> I don't think social media is completely useless. It can be useful if people use it responsibly.

---

# 🟢 Part 8 — Asking Someone's Opinion

Opinion conversation দুই দিকের হতে হবে।

তুমি শুধু নিজের opinion বলবে না।

### Questions:

> What do you think?

> What's your opinion?

> What do you think about this?

> How do you feel about it?

> Do you agree?

> What's your point of view?

### Natural:

> Personally, I think online learning is useful. **What do you think?**

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Opinion Conversation

### A:

> I think social media is useful.

### B:

> I agree. It helps people stay connected.

### A:

> But I think people spend too much time on it.

### B:

> That's true. Excessive use can be a problem.

### A:

> Do you think people should stop using social media?

### B:

> Not necessarily. I think they should use it responsibly instead.

🔥 Notice:

**Opinion → Agree → Reason → Question → Balanced opinion**

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Opinion + Example

Opinion stronger করার জন্য example দেবে।

### Weak:

> Technology is useful.

### Strong:

> Technology is useful because it saves time.

### Stronger:

> Technology is useful because it saves time and makes communication easier. For example, people can now attend meetings online without traveling.

---

# 🟢 Useful Example Expressions

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> A good example is...

> Take ______ as an example.

> For example, in my daily life...

---

# 🟡 Part 11 — Comparison Opinion

### Question:

> Which is better, studying alone or studying with friends?

### Answer:

> Personally, I prefer studying alone because I can concentrate better. However, studying with friends can be useful when I need to discuss difficult topics.

🔥 Structure:

> **Preference → Reason → Opposite side**

---

# 🟡 Another Example

### Q:

> Is online learning better than classroom learning?

### Answer:

> I think both have advantages. Online learning is flexible and convenient, while classroom learning provides more direct interaction with teachers and students. Personally, I prefer a combination of both.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “It Depends”

সব question-এর fixed answer নেই।

তখন:

> **It depends.**

### Example:

> Is studying alone better?

> It depends. If I need to concentrate deeply, I prefer studying alone. But if I need to discuss a difficult topic, studying with others can be better.

Very natural English।

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Important Opinion Words

| Word         | Meaning      |
| ------------ | ------------ |
| Advantage    | সুবিধা       |
| Disadvantage | অসুবিধা      |
| Benefit      | উপকার        |
| Problem      | সমস্যা       |
| Reason       | কারণ         |
| Opinion      | মতামত        |
| View         | দৃষ্টিভঙ্গি  |
| Point        | বক্তব্য/দিক  |
| Effect       | প্রভাব       |
| Impact       | প্রভাব       |
| Useful       | উপকারী       |
| Harmful      | ক্ষতিকর      |
| Important    | গুরুত্বপূর্ণ |
| Necessary    | প্রয়োজনীয়  |
| Effective    | কার্যকর      |
| Convenient   | সুবিধাজনক    |
| Flexible     | নমনীয়       |
| Expensive    | ব্যয়বহুল    |
| Affordable   | সাশ্রয়ী     |
| Responsible  | দায়িত্বশীল  |

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — 10 Core Questions

আজকের lesson-এর main questions:

### 1.

> Do you think English is important?

### 2.

> Do you think social media is useful?

### 3.

> Do you think technology makes life easier?

### 4.

> Is exercise important for everyone?

### 5.

> Is online learning better than classroom learning?

### 6.

> Do you think students should use smartphones?

### 7.

> Is it better to live in a city or a village?

### 8.

> Should people spend less time on social media?

### 9.

> Is money more important than happiness?

### 10.

> Do you think success depends more on talent or hard work?

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Question 4

## Is exercise important for everyone?

### Answer:

> Yes, I think exercise is important for most people because it helps maintain physical and mental health. For example, regular walking can improve fitness and reduce stress. However, people should choose exercises that are suitable for their age and physical condition.

Notice:

> Opinion
> ↓
> Reason
> ↓
> Example
> ↓
> Balanced point

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Question 5

## Is online learning better than classroom learning?

### Answer:

> I don't think one is always better than the other. Online learning is flexible and allows people to learn from anywhere. Classroom learning, on the other hand, provides direct interaction with teachers and classmates. Personally, I think a combination of both can be very effective.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Question 6

## Should students use smartphones?

### Answer:

> I think students can use smartphones, but they should use them responsibly. Smartphones can help students access educational resources and communicate with teachers. However, excessive use can distract them from studying. So, I think students should set limits on their screen time.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Question 7

## Is it better to live in a city or a village?

### Answer:

> I think it depends on a person's lifestyle and priorities. Cities usually provide better access to jobs, education, healthcare, and transportation. Villages, on the other hand, can offer a quieter environment and closer communities. Personally, I prefer city life because of the opportunities and convenience.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Question 8

## Should people spend less time on social media?

### Answer:

> Yes, I think many people should reduce their social media use. Social media can be useful, but spending too much time on it can affect productivity and concentration. For example, people may spend hours scrolling without realizing how much time has passed. I think using social media with a specific purpose is better than using it continuously.

---

# 🔴 Part 20 — Advanced Opinion

## Is money more important than happiness?

এখানে **balanced answer** দিতে হবে।

### Model:

> In my opinion, money is important, but it isn't the same as happiness. Having enough money can provide security and access to basic needs, education, healthcare, and other opportunities. However, money alone cannot guarantee meaningful relationships or personal satisfaction. I believe people need financial stability, but they should also focus on relationships, health, personal growth, and doing things they genuinely value.

🔥 এটা Intermediate → Advanced bridge।

---

# 🔴 Part 21 — Talent vs Hard Work

### Question:

> Do you think success depends more on talent or hard work?

### Model Answer:

> I think both talent and hard work can contribute to success, but hard work is often more important in the long run. Talent may give someone a good starting point, but without consistent effort, it may not lead to significant results. For example, a naturally talented person can still fail if they don't practice. On the other hand, someone with average ability can improve greatly through regular practice and persistence. So, I believe talent can help, but hard work and consistency are essential.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — The 5-Step Opinion Formula

এটা আজকের **সবচেয়ে important formula**।

# **O → R → E → C → O**

### O = Opinion

> I think...

### R = Reason

> because...

### E = Example

> For example...

### C = Contrast

> However...

### O = Overall

> Overall, I believe...

---

## Example:

> **I think** social media is useful **because** it helps people communicate. **For example**, we can easily stay connected with friends and family. **However**, excessive use can waste time and reduce concentration. **Overall**, I think social media is beneficial if we use it responsibly.

🔥 এই formula দিয়ে 70–80% সাধারণ opinion questions-এর answer তৈরি করতে পারবে।

---

# 🗣️ Part 23 — 3-Level Speaking Practice

### Question:

> What do you think about technology?

### 🟢 Beginner

> I think technology is useful.

### 🟡 Intermediate

> I think technology is useful because it makes communication and work easier.

### 🔴 Advanced

> In my opinion, technology has significantly improved our daily lives by making communication, education, and work more convenient. For example, people can work remotely and access educational resources from almost anywhere. However, excessive dependence on technology can create problems such as reduced face-to-face interaction. Overall, I believe technology is beneficial when it is used responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Follow-up Question Mastery

### Main:

> Do you think social media is useful?

### Follow-ups:

> Why do you think so?

> What are its advantages?

> What are its disadvantages?

> Can it be harmful?

> How can people use it responsibly?

> Do you use social media every day?

> Has your opinion changed over time?

> What do you think social media will be like in the future?

🔥 একটি question থেকে **8+ questions**।

---

# 🧩 Part 25 — Agree / Disagree Practice

### Statement:

> “Everyone should learn English.”

### You:

> I completely agree because English is useful for communication, education, and career development.

---

### Statement:

> “Social media is harmful.”

### You:

> I partly agree, but I don't think it's always harmful. It depends on how people use it.

---

### Statement:

> “Money is the most important thing in life.”

### You:

> I don't completely agree. Money is important for financial security, but I think health, relationships, and personal satisfaction are also important.

---

# 🚨 Part 26 — Common Mistakes

### ❌ Mistake 1

> I think **that** social media **are** useful.

### ✅

> I think social media **is** useful.

---

### ❌ Mistake 2

> In my opinion I think...

### ✅ Choose one:

> In my opinion,...

OR

> I think...

Don't normally combine them.

---

### ❌ Mistake 3

> I agree your opinion.

### ✅

> I agree **with** your opinion.

---

### ❌ Mistake 4

> I disagree with you because you are wrong.

### Better:

> I see your point, but I have a different opinion.

---

# 🎤 Part 27 — Full Conversation

### A:

> What do you think about online education?

### B:

> I think online education is very useful because it gives people flexibility.

### A:

> Why do you think it's better?

### B:

> One major advantage is that people can learn from anywhere. For example, someone can take a course from home.

### A:

> But don't you think classroom learning is better?

### B:

> I understand your point. Classroom learning has some advantages, especially direct interaction. However, I think both methods can be useful.

### A:

> So which one do you prefer?

### B:

> Personally, I prefer a combination of both.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> Because online learning provides flexibility, while classroom learning provides direct communication and interaction.

🔥 এটাই natural opinion conversation।

---

# 🎯 Part 28 — 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **“My Opinions About Modern Life”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1. Technology

> Do you think technology makes life easier?

### 2. Internet

> Is the internet important?

### 3. Social Media

> Is social media useful?

### 4. Education

> Is online learning effective?

### 5. English

> Why is English important?

### 6. Health

> Should everyone exercise?

### 7. Money

> Is money important for happiness?

### 8. Career

> Is hard work more important than talent?

### 9. City

> Is city life better than village life?

### 10. Your overall opinion

> What do you think makes a good life?

---

# 📝 Part 29 — Homework

## Task 1 — Answer these 10 questions

প্রতিটি answer **4–6 sentences**:

1. Do you think English is important?
2. Do you think social media is useful?
3. Does technology make life easier?
4. Should students use smartphones?
5. Is online learning better than classroom learning?
6. Is exercise important?
7. Is city life better than village life?
8. Is money more important than happiness?
9. Is hard work more important than talent?
10. What do you think makes a person successful?

---

## Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে **10টি opinion questions** তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **Do you think...?**

> **Should...?**

> **Is...?**

> **Why do you think...?**

> **What do you think about...?**

> **Which is better...?**

> **Do you agree that...?**

> **How can...?**

> **What would happen if...?**

> **Has your opinion changed...?**

---

# 🔥 Task 3 — Opinion Challenge

প্রতিটি statement-এর উপর **1 minute** কথা বলবে।

### A

> Technology makes life better.

### B

> Social media wastes people's time.

### C

> English is necessary for a successful career.

### D

> Hard work is more important than talent.

### E

> Money can buy happiness.

### F

> Online education is better than traditional education.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 33 Final Target

Notes না দেখে এই structure ব্যবহার করে **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলতে পারবে:

> **“What do you think about technology, education, social media, health, money, career, and success?”**

প্রতিটি topic-এ:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → However → Overall**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 33 Golden Formula

## **O → R → E → C → O**

> **Opinion**
> I think...

↓

> **Reason**
> because...

↓

> **Example**
> For example...

↓

> **Contrast**
> However,...

↓

> **Overall**
> Overall, I believe...

এবং অন্যের সাথে conversation চালাতে:

> **Give Opinion → Give Reason → Ask Their Opinion → Agree/Disagree → Give Another Reason**

---

## 🔗 Course Connection

**Lesson 31 — Past Experiences**
→ *What happened? What did you learn?*

↓

**Lesson 32 — Future Plans & Goals**
→ *What do you want to do? How will you achieve it?*

↓

# **Lesson 33 — Opinions & Giving Reasons**

→ *What do you think? Why? Give an example.*

↓

**Lesson 34 — Agreeing & Disagreeing**
→ *I agree / I partly agree / I see your point, but...*

এভাবে Lesson 31–34 একসাথে করলে তুমি **Past experience + Future plan + Opinion + Discussion**—এই চারটি core speaking skill ব্যবহার করতে পারবে।

---
# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 34

# 🤝 Agreeing & Disagreeing

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কারও কথায় agree করা, politely disagree করা, নিজের reason দেওয়া এবং conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া।

Lesson 33-এ তুমি শিখেছ:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example**

আজ সেই opinion নিয়ে **অন্য মানুষের সাথে discussion** করতে শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 34-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কারও কথায় agree করতে
* strongly agree করতে
* partly agree করতে
* politely disagree করতে
* নিজের reason দিতে
* অন্যের point acknowledge করতে
* alternative opinion দিতে
* discussion চালিয়ে যেতে
* disagreement-এর পরও polite থাকতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Agree মানে কী?

**Agree = একমত হওয়া**

কেউ বলল:

> I think exercise is important.

তুমি যদি একই opinion রাখো:

> I agree.

---

# 🟢 Level 1 — Basic Agreement

### 1.

> I agree.

### 2.

> I agree with you.

### 3.

> You're right.

### 4.

> That's true.

### 5.

> Exactly.

### 6.

> I think so too.

### 7.

> I feel the same way.

---

# 🟢 Examples

### A:

> English is important for communication.

### B:

> I agree with you.

---

### A:

> Exercise helps people stay healthy.

### B:

> That's true.

---

### A:

> Social media can be useful.

### B:

> Exactly.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Strong Agreement

কেউ এমন কিছু বলেছে যেটার সাথে তুমি **পুরোপুরি একমত**।

Use:

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

> I strongly agree.

> I couldn't agree more.

> Absolutely.

> Definitely.

---

### Example:

### A:

> Consistent practice is more important than studying once in a while.

### B:

> I completely agree. Regular practice helps us build long-term skills.

---

# ⭐ “I Couldn't Agree More”

এর অর্থ:

> **আমি এর সাথে পুরোপুরি একমত।**

এটা literal meaning দেখে confuse হবে না।

### Example:

> A: Hard work is essential for success.

> B: I couldn't agree more.

---

# 🟡 Part 3 — Partly Agree

সব সময় পুরোপুরি agree করার দরকার নেই।

তুমি বলতে পারো:

> I agree to some extent.

> I partly agree.

> I agree with you to some extent.

> You have a point.

> That's partly true.

---

### Example:

### A:

> Online learning is better than classroom learning.

### B:

> I partly agree. Online learning is more flexible, but classroom learning provides more direct interaction.

🔥 এখানে তুমি **agree + different point** দিয়েছ।

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — “You Have a Point”

এটা খুব natural expression।

> **You have a point.**

Meaning:

> তোমার কথায় যুক্তি আছে।

Example:

### A:

> People spend too much time on social media.

### B:

> You have a point. Social media can become distracting if we don't control our usage.

---

# 🔴 Part 5 — Disagree

**Disagree = একমত না হওয়া।**

কিন্তু English speaking-এ সরাসরি:

> ❌ You are wrong.

বললে rude শোনাতে পারে।

তাই polite disagreement শিখতে হবে।

---

# 🟡 Polite Disagreement

### Easy:

> I don't agree.

### Better:

> I don't completely agree.

### Natural:

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

### More polite:

> I see your point, but I think differently.

### Advanced:

> I understand what you mean, but I have a slightly different opinion.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Disagreement Formula

সবচেয়ে useful formula:

# **Acknowledge → Contrast → Reason**

### Step 1 — Acknowledge

> I understand your point.

### Step 2 — Contrast

> But I think differently.

### Step 3 — Reason

> I think this because...

---

### Example:

> I understand your point, but I think differently. I believe classroom learning can be more effective for some students because they can interact directly with teachers.

🔥 এটা polite এবং natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Direct vs Polite

### ❌ Too Direct

> You're wrong.

### ✅ Polite

> I don't think that's completely true.

---

### ❌

> That's a bad idea.

### ✅

> I'm not sure that's the best approach.

---

### ❌

> You don't understand.

### ✅

> Maybe we're looking at it from different perspectives.

---

### ❌

> That's false.

### ✅

> I see it a little differently.

---

# 🟢 Part 8 — Agree + Reason

শুধু:

> I agree.

বললে conversation ছোট হয়ে যায়।

তাই reason দাও।

### Formula:

> **I agree + because + reason**

### Example:

> I agree because regular exercise improves both physical and mental health.

---

### Stronger:

> I completely agree because regular exercise improves physical health, reduces stress, and helps people maintain a healthy lifestyle.

---

# 🟢 Part 9 — Disagree + Reason

### Formula:

> **I don't completely agree + because + reason**

Example:

> I don't completely agree because technology can be useful when people use it responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Agree + Add Another Point

এটা খুব powerful।

### Person A:

> English is important for getting a good job.

### Person B:

> I completely agree. **I'd also add that** English can help people access international resources and communicate with people from different countries.

Useful expressions:

> I'd also add that...

> Another point is...

> In addition,...

> Also,...

> Besides that,...

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Disagree + Alternative

শুধু disagreement করলে discussion negative হয়।

Better:

> I don't completely agree. **Instead, I think...**

Example:

> I don't completely agree. Instead, I think people should learn both technical and communication skills.

---

# 🟡 Part 12 — “On the Other Hand”

দুই দিক দেখাতে:

> **On the one hand...**

> **On the other hand...**

### Example:

> On the one hand, social media helps us stay connected. On the other hand, it can waste a lot of time.

🔥 খুব useful for discussion।

---

# 🟢 Part 13 — “However”

একটি opinion-এর বিপরীত side দেখাতে:

> However,...

Example:

> Online learning is convenient. However, some students may find it difficult to stay motivated.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Discussion Example 1

## Topic: Social Media

### A:

> I think social media is very useful.

### B:

> I agree with you. It helps people communicate easily.

### A:

> I think everyone should use it.

### B:

> I partly agree. Social media can be useful, but people should control how much time they spend on it.

### A:

> That's true.

### B:

> Exactly. I think using social media for a specific purpose is better than using it continuously.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Discussion Example 2

## Topic: Online Learning

### A:

> I think online learning is better than classroom learning.

### B:

> I see your point, but I don't completely agree.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> Online learning is flexible, but classroom learning allows students to interact directly with teachers and classmates.

### A:

> That's a good point.

### B:

> I think the best option depends on the student's needs and learning style.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Discussion Example 3

## Topic: Hard Work vs Talent

### A:

> I think talent is more important than hard work.

### B:

> I understand your point, but I think hard work is more important in the long run.

### A:

> Why do you think so?

### B:

> Because talent can give someone a good start, but consistent practice is necessary to improve and achieve long-term success.

### A:

> That's true, but talented people can learn faster.

### B:

> I agree with that. However, talent without effort may not lead to great results.

🔥 Notice:

**Disagree → Reason → Agree with one point → Give another point**

এটাই mature conversation।

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Agreeing & Disagreeing Vocabulary

| Expression                  | Meaning                  |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| I agree.                    | আমি একমত।                |
| I completely agree.         | আমি পুরোপুরি একমত।       |
| Exactly.                    | ঠিক তাই।                 |
| Absolutely.                 | অবশ্যই/সম্পূর্ণ একমত।    |
| That's true.                | এটা সত্যি।               |
| You have a point.           | তোমার যুক্তি আছে।        |
| I feel the same way.        | আমিও একইভাবে মনে করি।    |
| I partly agree.             | আমি আংশিক একমত।          |
| To some extent              | কিছুটা                   |
| I see your point.           | তোমার কথাটা বুঝতে পারছি। |
| I don't completely agree.   | আমি পুরোপুরি একমত নই।    |
| I'm not sure I agree.       | আমি নিশ্চিত নই যে একমত।  |
| I think differently.        | আমি ভিন্নভাবে মনে করি।   |
| I have a different opinion. | আমার ভিন্ন মত আছে।       |
| On the other hand           | অন্যদিকে                 |
| However                     | তবে                      |
| Instead                     | পরিবর্তে                 |
| From my perspective         | আমার দৃষ্টিকোণ থেকে      |

---

# 🎯 Part 18 — 5 Levels of Response

একই statement-এর response কীভাবে improve হয় দেখো।

### Statement:

> Social media wastes time.

### Level 1

> I agree.

### Level 2

> I agree. Social media can waste time.

### Level 3

> I agree because people can spend hours scrolling.

### Level 4

> I agree because people can spend hours scrolling without realizing how much time has passed.

### Level 5

> I agree to some extent because people can easily spend hours scrolling without realizing how much time has passed. However, I don't think social media is always harmful. It can also be useful for communication, learning, and networking if people use it responsibly.

🎯 **Your target: Level 4–5**

---

# 🟡 Part 19 — “Do You Agree?”

Conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Do you agree?

> Would you agree with that?

> Don't you think so?

> What do you think?

> Do you see it the same way?

> What's your opinion?

---

# 🔥 Example

> I think people should exercise every day. **Do you agree?**

Response:

> Yes, I agree. Regular exercise is important for maintaining good health.

OR:

> I partly agree. I think people should exercise regularly, but it doesn't necessarily have to be every day.

🔥 দ্বিতীয় answer অনেক বেশি natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “I Agree, But...”

এটা খুব useful pattern।

### Structure:

> **I agree + but + different point**

Example:

> I agree that technology is useful, but people shouldn't become completely dependent on it.

আরও:

> I agree that money is important, but I don't think it is the most important thing in life.

> I agree that English is important, but communication skills involve more than just English.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “I See Your Point, But...”

এই expression মুখস্থ করে ফেলতে পারো।

### Structure:

> **I see your point, but + my opinion**

Example:

> I see your point, but I think practical experience is more important.

---

# 🟢 Part 22 — Agreeing with One Part

সব opinion পুরোপুরি right/wrong না।

### Example:

> A: Working from home is better for everyone.

### B:

> I agree that working from home can be more comfortable, but I don't think it's better for everyone. Some people work more effectively in an office.

🔥 Useful structure:

> **I agree that X, but I don't think Y.**

---

# 🔴 Part 23 — Strong Discussion

## Topic: “University education is necessary for success.”

### A:

> I think university education is necessary for success.

### B:

> I agree to some extent, but I don't think it's necessary for everyone.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> A university degree can provide knowledge, qualifications, and networking opportunities. However, some careers can be built through practical skills, experience, and self-learning.

### A:

> That's a good point.

### B:

> I think it depends on the career a person chooses.

🔥 **This is exactly how Intermediate → Advanced discussion develops.**

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “It Depends”

When you don't want a black-and-white answer:

> It depends.

Then explain **what it depends on**.

### Example:

> Is online learning better?

> It depends on the learner, the subject, and the learning environment.

### Full answer:

> It depends on the learner and the subject. Some people learn better independently, while others need direct guidance from a teacher.

---

# 🏆 Part 25 — Golden Discussion Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **A → R → C → A**

### A = Agree/Disagree

> I agree.

### R = Reason

> Because...

### C = Contrast

> However...

### A = Add another point

> I'd also add that...

---

### Example:

> I agree that technology is useful **because** it makes many tasks easier. **However**, people can become too dependent on it. **I'd also add that** we need to learn how to use technology responsibly.

---

# 🎤 Part 26 — 10 Practice Statements

প্রতিটির সাথে **Agree / Partly Agree / Disagree** করবে এবং reason দেবে।

### 1.

> English is necessary for everyone.

### 2.

> Social media is mostly harmful.

### 3.

> Technology makes people lazy.

### 4.

> Online learning is better than classroom learning.

### 5.

> Hard work is more important than talent.

### 6.

> Money can buy happiness.

### 7.

> Students should use smartphones in class.

### 8.

> Everyone should exercise every day.

### 9.

> City life is better than village life.

### 10.

> University education is necessary for a successful career.

---

# 🗣️ Part 27 — Speaking Challenge

প্রতিটি statement-এর জন্য **1 minute** কথা বলবে।

Use:

> **I agree / I disagree / I partly agree**

↓

> **because...**

↓

> **For example...**

↓

> **However...**

↓

> **Overall...**

---

# 📝 Part 28 — Homework

## Task 1

এই 10 statements-এর উপর নিজের opinion লিখবে।

প্রতিটি answer:

**4–6 sentences**

---

## Task 2 — Conversation Practice

নিজে A এবং B দুটো role play করবে।

### Topic:

> **Online Learning**

### A:

> I think online learning is better.

### B:

> I partly agree...

তারপর অন্তত **10 turns** conversation চালাবে।

---

## Task 3 — Question Making

নিজে 10টি discussion questions তৈরি করবে:

> Do you agree that...?

> Why do you think...?

> Don't you think...?

> What's your opinion about...?

> Which is better...?

> What are the advantages...?

> What are the disadvantages...?

> What would you say about...?

> How do you feel about...?

> Do you see it differently?

---

# **“Do You Agree With Modern Lifestyle?”**

এই topics cover করবে:

1. Technology
2. Social media
3. Online learning
4. City life
5. Exercise
6. Money
7. Career
8. English
9. University education
10. Success

প্রতিটি topic-এ:

> **Opinion → Agree/Disagree → Reason → Example → Other Side → Conclusion**

# 🏆 Lesson 34 Final Target

আজকের lesson-এর পর তোমার goal হবে:

### Basic:

> I agree.

### Intermediate:

> I agree because...

### Strong Intermediate:

> I partly agree because..., but...

### Advanced:

> I see your point, and I agree with you to some extent. However, I think there is another side to the issue. For example,...

---

# ⭐ Lesson 34 Golden Expressions

এই **10টি expression** আজ মুখস্থ করো:

1. **I agree with you.**
2. **I completely agree.**
3. **That's a good point.**
4. **You have a point.**
5. **I partly agree.**
6. **I see your point, but...**
7. **I don't completely agree.**
8. **I'm not sure I agree with that.**
9. **On the other hand...**
10. **It depends on...**
