# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 35

# 👤 Describing People & Personality

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** একজন মানুষকে describe করা → দেখতে কেমন → personality কেমন → কী করে → কেন তাকে পছন্দ/অপছন্দ করো → example দেওয়া

Lesson 34-এ তুমি শিখেছ:

> **Opinion → Agree/Disagree → Reason → Example**

আজ সেই skill ব্যবহার করে **একজন মানুষ সম্পর্কে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা** শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 35-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি বলতে পারবে:

* একজন মানুষ দেখতে কেমন
* তার height/body/hair/face describe করা
* তার personality describe করা
* তার profession বলা
* তার habits বলা
* তার strengths/weaknesses বলা
* কেন তাকে পছন্দ করো
* তার সাথে তোমার relationship কী
* একজন famous person সম্পর্কে বলা
* একজন friend/family member সম্পর্কে বলা

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Person Describe করার 6-Step Formula

কাউকে describe করার সময় random ভাবে কথা বলবে না।

এই order follow করবে:

> **1. Who is the person?**
> ↓
> **2. Appearance**
> ↓
> **3. Personality**
> ↓
> **4. Habits / Behavior**
> ↓
> **5. Relationship**
> ↓
> **6. Why you like/respect them**

### Golden Formula:

# **WHO → LOOKS → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → RELATIONSHIP → OPINION**

---

# 🟢 Part 2 — “Who is this person?”

শুরুতেই বলবে:

> This is my friend, Rahim.

> He is one of my classmates.

> She is my older sister.

> He is one of my closest friends.

> She is someone I really admire.

---

# ⭐ Useful Opening Expressions

### Friend:

> I'd like to talk about one of my close friends.

### Family:

> I'd like to describe one of my family members.

### Famous person:

> The person I'd like to talk about is...

### Someone you admire:

> There is someone I really admire, and I'd like to talk about him/her.

---

# 👤 Part 3 — Physical Appearance

**Appearance = দেখতে কেমন**

প্রথমে basic information।

---

## Height

> tall

> short

> average height

> quite tall

> relatively short

### Examples:

> He is quite tall.

> She is of average height.

> My brother is taller than me.

---

# 🧑 Part 4 — Body Build

> slim

> thin

> fit

> athletic

> muscular

> well-built

> average build

### Examples:

> He is slim and fit.

> She has an athletic build.

> He is quite muscular.

⚠️ মানুষকে describe করার সময় respectful language ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 💇 Part 5 — Hair

### Hair length:

> short hair

> long hair

> medium-length hair

### Hair type:

> straight hair

> curly hair

> wavy hair

### Hair color:

> black hair

> brown hair

> dark hair

> gray hair

### Examples:

> He has short black hair.

> She has long, wavy hair.

> My friend has short, straight hair.

---

# 👀 Part 6 — Face & Features

Useful vocabulary:

> big eyes

> small eyes

> round face

> oval face

> sharp features

> broad forehead

> small nose

> pointed nose

> beard

> mustache

> glasses

---

### Examples:

> He has a round face.

> She has big brown eyes.

> He wears glasses.

> He has a short beard.

---

# 😊 Part 7 — Smile

Very useful in speaking:

> He has a nice smile.

> She has a friendly smile.

> He always smiles when he talks to people.

---

# 🎨 Part 8 — Clothing

Person describe করার সময় clothing বলতে পারো।

> He usually wears casual clothes.

> She often wears traditional clothes.

> He likes wearing T-shirts and jeans.

> She usually dresses neatly.

Useful:

> casual

> formal

> traditional

> simple

> stylish

> neat

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Appearance vs Personality

এটা খুব important।

### Appearance:

**মানুষটি দেখতে কেমন?**

> He is tall.

> She has long hair.

> He wears glasses.

### Personality:

**মানুষটি স্বভাবে কেমন?**

> He is friendly.

> She is honest.

> He is patient.

> She is hardworking.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Personality Vocabulary

## Positive Personality

| Word        | Meaning           |
| ----------- | ----------------- |
| Friendly    | বন্ধুসুলভ         |
| Kind        | দয়ালু            |
| Honest      | সৎ                |
| Helpful     | সাহায্যপ্রবণ      |
| Polite      | ভদ্র              |
| Patient     | ধৈর্যশীল          |
| Confident   | আত্মবিশ্বাসী      |
| Hardworking | পরিশ্রমী          |
| Responsible | দায়িত্বশীল       |
| Reliable    | নির্ভরযোগ্য       |
| Generous    | উদার              |
| Calm        | শান্ত             |
| Positive    | ইতিবাচক           |
| Creative    | সৃজনশীল           |
| Smart       | বুদ্ধিমান         |
| Intelligent | মেধাবী            |
| Ambitious   | উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী     |
| Motivated   | উৎসাহী            |
| Flexible    | মানিয়ে নিতে পারে |
| Sociable    | সামাজিক/মিশুক     |

---

# 🔴 Negative Personality

| Word      | Meaning           |
| --------- | ----------------- |
| Lazy      | অলস               |
| Rude      | অভদ্র             |
| Impatient | অধৈর্য            |
| Selfish   | স্বার্থপর         |
| Arrogant  | অহংকারী           |
| Careless  | অসাবধান           |
| Shy       | লাজুক             |
| Nervous   | অস্থির/নার্ভাস    |
| Stubborn  | একগুঁয়ে          |
| Serious   | গম্ভীর            |
| Quiet     | চুপচাপ            |
| Moody     | মেজাজ পরিবর্তনশীল |

⚠️ এগুলো ব্যবহার করার সময় context অনুযায়ী বলবে।

---

# 🟢 Part 11 — “What is he/she like?”

এটা খুব important question।

### Question:

> What is your friend like?

এখানে **appearance** জিজ্ঞেস করছে না।

এটা personality সম্পর্কে।

### Answer:

> He is friendly, helpful, and hardworking.

---

### Compare:

> **What does he look like?**

→ দেখতে কেমন?

> He is tall and has short black hair.

---

> **What is he like?**

→ স্বভাবে কেমন?

> He is friendly, honest, and helpful.

🔥 এই difference অবশ্যই শিখবে।

---

# 🟡 Part 12 — “What does he/she look like?”

### Question:

> What does your friend look like?

### Answer:

> He is quite tall and slim. He has short black hair and wears glasses. He usually dresses casually.

---

# 🟢 Part 13 — Personality দিয়ে Example দেওয়া

শুধু:

> He is helpful.

বললে answer ছোট।

Better:

> He is very helpful. Whenever someone has a problem, he tries to help them.

🔥 Personality + behavior = stronger description.

---

### More examples:

> She is very hardworking. She always finishes her work on time.

> He is patient. He doesn't get angry easily.

> She is honest. She always tells the truth.

> He is responsible. He takes his responsibilities seriously.

> She is friendly. She can easily start conversations with new people.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Because” দিয়ে Explain

### Basic:

> I like him because he is kind.

### Better:

> I like him because he is kind and always helps others.

### Strong:

> I really respect him because he is hardworking, honest, and always willing to help people when they need support.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Relationship

বলবে তোমার সাথে তার relationship কী।

### Examples:

> He is my best friend.

> She is my older sister.

> He is one of my classmates.

> She is my cousin.

> He is my teacher.

> She is my colleague.

> He is someone I have known for many years.

---

# 🟢 Useful Relationship Expressions

> We have known each other for years.

> We became friends at university.

> We often spend time together.

> We usually help each other.

> We have a good relationship.

> We get along very well.

---

# ⭐ “Get Along”

> **get along with someone**

= কারও সাথে ভালো সম্পর্ক থাকা

### Example:

> I get along very well with my brother.

> I get along well with my classmates.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Habits & Behavior

Personality describe করার সময় habits বললে answer অনেক natural হয়।

### Useful:

> He usually...

> She often...

> He always...

> She rarely...

> He tends to...

---

### Examples:

> He usually wakes up early.

> She often helps her friends.

> He always keeps his promises.

> She rarely gets angry.

> He tends to think carefully before making decisions.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Strengths & Weaknesses

### Strength = শক্তি/ভালো দিক

> His biggest strength is his patience.

> Her biggest strength is her determination.

> One of his strengths is communication.

### Weakness = দুর্বলতা

> His main weakness is that he sometimes worries too much.

> She can be a little impatient sometimes.

🔥 Balanced description বেশি natural:

> He is very hardworking, although he sometimes puts too much pressure on himself.

---

# 🟡 Part 18 — Question 1

# Who is your best friend?

### Easy:

> My best friend is Rahim.

### Better:

> My best friend is Rahim. We studied together at university.

### Strong:

> I'd like to talk about my close friend Rahim. We met at university and gradually became good friends. We often help each other with studies and discuss our career plans.

---

# 🟡 Question 2

# What does your best friend look like?

### Answer:

> He is quite tall and slim. He has short black hair and a round face. He usually wears casual clothes and sometimes wears glasses.

---

# 🟡 Question 3

# What is your best friend like?

### Answer:

> He is friendly, honest, and hardworking. He is also very helpful. Whenever I have a problem, he listens to me and tries to help.

---

# 🟡 Question 4

# Why do you like your best friend?

### Answer:

> I like him because he is honest and supportive. I can talk to him openly, and I know I can trust him.

---

# 🟡 Question 5

# How long have you known him?

এখানে **Present Perfect** আবার আসছে।

### Structure:

> **have/has + V3 + for/since**

### Examples:

> I have known him for five years.

> I have known her since university.

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> I know him since five years.

### ✅

> I have known him for five years.

---

# 🟢 Part 19 — Describing a Family Member

### Question:

> Describe someone in your family.

### Model Answer:

> I'd like to describe my older brother. He is quite tall and has short black hair. He is friendly, responsible, and hardworking. He usually stays calm even when there is a problem. He often gives me useful advice, especially about my career and personal decisions. We have a good relationship, and I really respect him because he is supportive and responsible.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Describing Someone You Admire

### Question:

> Who do you admire?

### Structure:

> Who → What they do → Personality → Achievement → Why you admire them

### Example:

> I really admire people who are hardworking and disciplined. One person I admire is someone who has achieved success through consistent effort. He is hardworking, patient, and responsible. I admire him because he doesn't give up easily and always tries to improve himself. His attitude motivates me to work harder.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Famous Person

কোনো famous person describe করতে পারো।

### Formula:

> **Name → Profession → Appearance → Personality → Achievement → Why you admire them**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about a famous person I admire. He is a successful professional in his field. He is known for his hard work and determination. What I admire most about him is his ability to keep improving despite difficulties. His success motivates me to work harder and stay focused on my goals.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Comparing Two People

এটা Intermediate speaking-এর জন্য খুব useful।

### Question:

> How are your two friends different?

### Answer:

> Both of my friends are helpful, but their personalities are quite different. Rahim is very outgoing and enjoys talking to people, while Karim is quieter and prefers spending time alone. Rahim makes decisions quickly, whereas Karim usually thinks carefully before making a decision. Despite these differences, I get along well with both of them.

---

# 🧠 Comparison Words

> both

> but

> while

> whereas

> however

> unlike

> similar to

> different from

---

# 🟢 Part 23 — Important Questions

আজকের speaking-এর core questions:

1. Who is your best friend?
2. What does he/she look like?
3. What is he/she like?
4. How long have you known him/her?
5. Why do you like him/her?
6. What does he/she usually do?
7. What is his/her biggest strength?
8. What is his/her weakness?
9. Who do you admire?
10. What do you like about that person?

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Follow-up Questions

একজনকে describe করার পর conversation continue করতে:

> Where did you meet?

> How long have you known him?

> What do you usually do together?

> What does he do?

> What are his hobbies?

> Why do you like him?

> What is his best quality?

> What can you learn from him?

> How are you different?

> Would you like to be like him?

---

# 🧩 Part 25 — Complete Conversation

### A:

> Who is your best friend?

### B:

> My best friend is Rahim. We met at university several years ago.

### A:

> What does he look like?

### B:

> He is quite tall and slim. He has short black hair and usually wears glasses.

### A:

> What is he like?

### B:

> He is friendly, honest, and hardworking. He is also very supportive.

### A:

> Why do you like him?

### B:

> I like him because I can trust him and talk to him openly.

### A:

> What do you usually do together?

### B:

> We usually discuss our studies, careers, and future plans. Sometimes we also watch movies or go out together.

### A:

> What can you learn from him?

### B:

> I can learn the importance of hard work and patience from him.

🔥 Notice: **Description → Personality → Behavior → Relationship → Opinion → Lesson**

---

# 🎤 Part 26 — 3-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **“Describe a Person You Really Like or Admire”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1. Who?

> Who is this person?

### 2. Relationship

> How do you know this person?

### 3. Appearance

> What does he/she look like?

### 4. Personality

> What is he/she like?

### 5. Behavior

> What does he/she usually do?

### 6. Strength

> What is his/her best quality?

### 7. Weakness

> Does he/she have any weakness?

### 8. Relationship

> What do you usually do together?

### 9. Reason

> Why do you like/respect this person?

### 10. Lesson

> What have you learned from this person?

---

# 📝 Part 27 — Homework

## Task 1 — Answer these 10 questions

প্রতিটি **3–5 sentences**:

1. Who is your best friend?
2. What does your best friend look like?
3. What is your best friend like?
4. How long have you known your best friend?
5. Why do you like your best friend?
6. Who is the most helpful person you know?
7. Who do you admire?
8. What is that person's best quality?
9. How are you different from your best friend?
10. What have you learned from someone you admire?

---

# 📝 Task 2 — Vocabulary Practice

এই 15 words দিয়ে sentence বানাও:

> friendly
> honest
> hardworking
> helpful
> patient
> confident
> responsible
> reliable
> creative
> ambitious
> shy
> quiet
> stubborn
> generous
> polite

---

# 🎯 Task 3 — Question Making

নিজে **10টি person-description questions** বানাও।

Use:

> Who...?

> What does...look like?

> What is...like?

> Why...?

> How long...?

> What does...usually do?

> What is...best quality?

> What is...weakness?

> How are...different?

> What can you learn from...?

---

# 🏆 Lesson 35 Golden Formula

কাউকে describe করার সময়:

# **WHO → LOOKS → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → RELATIONSHIP → REASON → LESSON**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about my best friend. **He is** one of my closest friends, and we met at university. **He is quite tall and has short black hair.** **He is friendly, honest, and hardworking.** **He usually helps people whenever they need support.** We have known each other for several years and **get along very well**. I really like and respect him **because he is reliable and supportive**. **I've learned the importance of patience and consistency from him.**

---

# ⭐ Most Important Difference

### Appearance:

> **What does he/she look like?**

➡️ He is tall and has short black hair.

### Personality:

> **What is he/she like?**

➡️ He is friendly, honest, and hardworking.

### Opinion:

> **What do you like about him/her?**

➡️ I like his honesty and supportive nature.

### Relationship:

> **How long have you known him/her?**

➡️ I've known him for five years.

এই চারটা question ভালোভাবে পারলে **যেকোনো person description conversation** অনেক সহজ হয়ে যাবে।

---

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 36

# 📍 Places & Locations

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কোনো জায়গা কোথায় → জায়গাটা দেখতে কেমন → সেখানে কী আছে → সেখানে কী করা যায় → কেন জায়গাটা পছন্দ → personal experience

Lesson 35-এ তুমি শিখেছ **কোনো person describe** করতে।

আজ একইভাবে **কোনো place describe** করা শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 36-এর Main Goal

আজ তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো জায়গা কোথায় বলতে
* location describe করতে
* জায়গাটা দেখতে কেমন বলতে
* সেখানে কী কী আছে বলতে
* সেখানে কী করা যায় বলতে
* জায়গাটা কেমন পরিবেশের বলতে
* কেন জায়গাটা পছন্দ বলতে
* সেখানে যাওয়ার experience বলতে
* দুইটা জায়গা compare করতে
* কোনো জায়গা সম্পর্কে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Place Describe করার Formula

কোনো জায়গা describe করার সময় এই order follow করবে:

# **WHERE → LOOK → THINGS → ACTIVITIES → ATMOSPHERE → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### অর্থ:

1. **Where?** → কোথায়?
2. **Look?** → দেখতে কেমন?
3. **Things?** → কী কী আছে?
4. **Activities?** → কী করা যায়?
5. **Atmosphere?** → পরিবেশ কেমন?
6. **Experience?** → তোমার experience কী?
7. **Opinion?** → কেন পছন্দ?

---

# 📍 Part 2 — Location বলতে শেখো

## Basic Expressions

> It is in...

> It is located in...

> It is near...

> It is close to...

> It is far from...

> It is next to...

> It is opposite...

> It is between...

> It is behind...

> It is in front of...

---

# 🟢 Examples

> My university is in Dhaka.

> The park is near my house.

> The restaurant is next to the bank.

> The hospital is opposite the school.

> The library is between the cafeteria and the main building.

> The bus station is close to the market.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “Where is it?”

### Question:

> Where is your university?

### Easy:

> My university is in Dhaka.

### Better:

> My university is located in Dhaka.

### Stronger:

> My university is located in Dhaka, and it is easy to reach by bus or rickshaw.

---

# 🗺️ Part 4 — Important Location Words

| English     | বাংলা        |
| ----------- | ------------ |
| Near        | কাছে         |
| Far         | দূরে         |
| Next to     | পাশে         |
| Opposite    | বিপরীতে      |
| Behind      | পেছনে        |
| In front of | সামনে        |
| Between     | মাঝখানে      |
| Beside      | পাশে         |
| Across from | বিপরীতে      |
| Around      | আশেপাশে      |
| Inside      | ভিতরে        |
| Outside     | বাইরে        |
| Corner      | কোণা         |
| Center      | কেন্দ্র      |
| Entrance    | প্রবেশপথ     |
| Exit        | বের হওয়ার পথ |

---

# 🏙️ Part 5 — What Does It Look Like?

কোনো place describe করতে appearance vocabulary দরকার।

### Useful adjectives:

> beautiful

> large

> small

> modern

> old

> clean

> crowded

> quiet

> peaceful

> busy

> spacious

> colorful

> attractive

> well-organized

---

### Examples:

> It is a large and modern building.

> The park is beautiful and peaceful.

> The market is usually crowded.

> The area is clean and well-organized.

---

# 🌳 Part 6 — Nature Describe করা

যদি park, village, beach, tourist place হয়:

> green trees

> fresh air

> beautiful scenery

> open space

> river

> lake

> mountains

> beach

> garden

> flowers

> natural beauty

### Example:

> The place has lots of green trees and open spaces.

> The area is surrounded by beautiful natural scenery.

---

# 🏢 Part 7 — Building Describe করা

Building-এর ক্ষেত্রে:

> tall building

> modern building

> old building

> large rooms

> several floors

> wide entrance

> beautiful design

> clean environment

### Example:

> It is a large modern building with several floors and spacious rooms.

---

# 🛍️ Part 8 — What Is There?

এখন বলবে জায়গাটায় কী কী আছে।

### Useful structure:

> There is...

> There are...

---

### Singular:

> There is a library.

> There is a large playground.

### Plural:

> There are several shops.

> There are many trees.

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> There have many shops.

### ✅

> There are many shops.

---

### ❌

> There is many people.

### ✅

> There are many people.

---

# 🟢 Part 9 — “There is / There are”

### Example:

> There is a large park near my house.

> There are several restaurants in the area.

> There is a bus stop in front of the building.

> There are many trees around the park.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Activities

কোনো জায়গায় কী করা যায়?

Use:

> You can...

> People can...

> Visitors can...

> You can enjoy...

> You can spend time...

---

### Examples:

> You can walk around the park.

> People can play sports there.

> Visitors can enjoy the beautiful scenery.

> You can sit and relax there.

> People can take photos.

> You can spend time with your friends and family.

---

# 🏖️ Part 11 — Example: A Park

### Question:

> Describe a park you like.

### Answer:

> There is a beautiful park near my home. It is quite large and surrounded by many green trees. There are walking paths, benches, and a small playground. People can walk, exercise, relax, and spend time with their families there. The park is usually peaceful in the morning, although it becomes busier in the evening. I like this park because it gives me a chance to relax and enjoy some fresh air.

🔥 Notice:

**Where → Look → Things → Activities → Atmosphere → Opinion**

---

# 🌆 Part 12 — City Describe করা

### Question:

> Describe your city.

### Structure:

> Location → Size → People → Transportation → Places → Lifestyle → Opinion

### Model:

> I live in Dhaka, which is the capital of Bangladesh. It is a large and busy city with a huge population. There are many universities, offices, shopping centers, restaurants, and hospitals. The city has many transportation options, although traffic congestion can be a major problem. Dhaka is usually crowded and busy, but it also offers many educational and career opportunities. I like living here because there are many opportunities, although I sometimes prefer quieter places.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Atmosphere

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশ / আবহ**

Place describe করার সময় খুব useful।

### Positive:

> peaceful

> relaxing

> lively

> friendly

> pleasant

> welcoming

> comfortable

### Negative:

> noisy

> crowded

> stressful

> dirty

> busy

---

### Examples:

> The atmosphere is peaceful and relaxing.

> The market has a lively atmosphere.

> The area can be noisy during the day.

> The restaurant has a warm and welcoming atmosphere.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Personal Experience

এখানে Lesson 31-এর Past Experience ব্যবহার করবে।

### Question:

> Have you ever visited this place?

### Answer:

> Yes, I have visited it several times.

Then:

> I first went there with my friends a few years ago.

> We spent the afternoon there.

> We walked around and took some photos.

> I really enjoyed the experience.

🔥 **Present Perfect + Past Simple** দুটোই ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Important Tense Connection

### Experience:

> I have visited Cox's Bazar several times.

**Present Perfect**

→ Experience

### Specific event:

> I went there last year.

**Past Simple**

→ Specific past time

### Details:

> We stayed there for three days.

> We visited the beach every morning.

---

# 🏖️ Part 16 — Describe a Tourist Place

### Question:

> Describe a tourist place you have visited.

### Model Answer:

> I'd like to talk about Cox's Bazar, a famous tourist destination in Bangladesh. It is located in the southeastern part of the country and is well known for its long natural sea beach. The area has many hotels, restaurants, shops, and tourist attractions. Visitors can walk along the beach, enjoy the scenery, take photos, and spend time with friends or family. I visited Cox's Bazar with my family, and we had a great time there. I especially enjoyed the peaceful atmosphere of the beach in the early morning. I would definitely like to visit the place again.

---

# 🟢 Part 17 — Why Do You Like This Place?

### Basic:

> I like this place because it is beautiful.

### Better:

> I like this place because it is peaceful and relaxing.

### Strong:

> I like this place because it gives me a chance to escape from my daily routine and spend some relaxing time with my family.

### Advanced:

> What I like most about this place is its peaceful atmosphere. Whenever I visit, I feel relaxed and refreshed.

🔥 **What I like most about...** খুব useful expression।

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — “There is something special about...”

আরও natural:

> There is something special about this place.

Then explain:

> I always feel comfortable there.

### Example:

> There is something special about my village because I spent most of my childhood there.

---

# 🏡 Part 19 — Describe Your Home

### Question:

> Describe your home.

### Model:

> I live in a comfortable home with my family. It is located in a relatively quiet area. Our home isn't very large, but it is well organized and comfortable. There are several rooms, including bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and a bathroom. My favorite place is my room because I can study and relax there. I like my home because it feels safe, peaceful, and familiar.

---

# 🏫 Part 20 — Describe Your University

### Question:

> Describe your university.

### Answer:

> My university is located in Dhaka. It is a large and modern campus with several academic buildings, classrooms, laboratories, and other facilities. There are also places where students can relax and spend time together. The campus can be quite busy during class hours, but it becomes quieter later in the day. I have spent several important years there, so it has a special place in my life. I especially like the learning environment and the people I have met there.

---

# 🟡 Part 21 — Comparing Two Places

Lesson 35-এ যেমন দুইজনকে compare করেছিলে, আজ দুইটা place compare করবে।

### Question:

> Which is better, city life or village life?

### Answer:

> Both city and village life have advantages and disadvantages. Cities usually offer better access to jobs, education, healthcare, and transportation. Villages, on the other hand, are generally quieter and closer to nature. Personally, I prefer city life because it provides more opportunities, although I enjoy visiting villages when I want a peaceful environment.

---

# 🧠 Comparison Expressions

> Both...and...

> On the other hand...

> While...

> Whereas...

> Compared with...

> Unlike...

> In contrast...

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Location Questions

এই questions practice করবে:

1. Where do you live?
2. Where is your home located?
3. What is your neighborhood like?
4. What places are near your home?
5. Is your area quiet or crowded?
6. How do you usually travel around your city?
7. What is your favorite place in your city?
8. Why do you like that place?
9. Have you ever visited a famous tourist place?
10. Which place would you like to visit in the future?

---

# 🗣️ Part 23 — Full Conversation

### A:

> Where do you live?

### B:

> I live in Dhaka.

### A:

> What is your area like?

### B:

> It's a fairly busy area, but there are some quiet streets nearby.

### A:

> What is your favorite place there?

### B:

> There's a small park near my home that I really like.

### A:

> What does it look like?

### B:

> It's quite green and has lots of trees and walking paths.

### A:

> What do you usually do there?

### B:

> I usually walk around or sit on a bench and relax.

### A:

> Why do you like it?

### B:

> I like it because it's peaceful and gives me a break from my daily routine.

🔥 এই conversation pattern অনেক real-life situation-এ ব্যবহার করতে পারবে।

---

# 🧩 Part 24 — Place Description Formula

যেকোনো place-এর জন্য এই template:

> **I'd like to talk about [place].**

> **It is located in [location].**

> **It is [size/appearance].**

> **There is/are [things].**

> **People can [activities].**

> **The atmosphere is [adjective].**

> **I have visited/spent time there [experience].**

> **What I like most about this place is [reason].**

> **Overall, I think it is [opinion].**

---

# 🔥 Example — Favorite Place

> I'd like to talk about my favorite place, which is a park near my home. It is located in a relatively quiet area. The park is quite large and has many trees, walking paths, and benches. People can walk, exercise, relax, and spend time with their families there. The atmosphere is peaceful, especially in the morning. I have visited this park many times, and I usually go there when I need some fresh air or want to clear my mind. What I like most about this place is its peaceful environment. Overall, it is one of my favorite places because it helps me relax.

---

# 🚨 Part 25 — Common Mistakes

### ❌ 1.

> My home is near to the market.

### ✅

> My home is near the market.

---

### ❌ 2.

> There have many shops.

### ✅

> There are many shops.

---

### ❌ 3.

> The place is very peace.

### ✅

> The place is very peaceful.

---

### ❌ 4.

> I went there many times.

যদি specific past time না থাকে এবং experience বোঝাতে চাও:

### Better:

> I have been there many times.

---

### ❌ 5.

> People can to walk there.

### ✅

> People can walk there.

**can + base verb**

---

# 🎤 Part 26 — 3-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **Describe Your Favorite Place**

এই 10-step structure follow করবে:

1. What is the place?
2. Where is it?
3. How big is it?
4. What does it look like?
5. What is there?
6. What can people do there?
7. What is the atmosphere like?
8. When did you first visit it?
9. Why do you like it?
10. Would you like to visit it again?

---

# 📝 Part 27 — Homework

## Task 1

এই questions-এর answer দাও:

1. Where do you live?
2. What is your neighborhood like?
3. What is your favorite place in your city?
4. Where is it located?
5. What does it look like?
6. What can people do there?
7. What is the atmosphere like?
8. Have you visited any tourist places?
9. Which place would you like to visit in the future?
10. Which do you prefer: city or village?

প্রতিটি answer **3–5 sentences**।

---

# 📝 Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে 10টি place-related question বানাবে।

Use:

> Where...?

> What is ... like?

> What does ... look like?

> What is there...?

> What can people do...?

> How can I get...?

> How far...?

> Why do you like...?

> Have you ever visited...?

> Would you like to visit...?

---

# 🎯 Task 3 — 5-Minute Speaking

একটানা 5 মিনিট এই 5টি topic নিয়ে কথা বলবে:

### 1. Your Home

### 2. Your Neighborhood

### 3. Your University

### 4. Your City

### 5. Your Favorite Place

প্রতিটি topic-এ:

> **Where → Look → Things → Activities → Atmosphere → Experience → Opinion**

---

# 🏆 Lesson 36 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHERE → LOOK → THINGS → ACTIVITIES → ATMOSPHERE → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### Example:

> **My favorite place is a park near my home.**
> **It is located in a quiet area.**
> **It is quite large and has many trees and walking paths.**
> **People can walk, exercise, and relax there.**
> **The atmosphere is peaceful.**
> **I have visited it many times with my friends.**
> **What I like most about this place is that it helps me relax.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 37

# ⏰ Daily Activities & Routines

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** প্রতিদিন কী করো → কখন করো → কতবার করো → কীভাবে করো → কেন করো → দিনের শেষে কী করো।

Lesson 36-এ তুমি **Places & Locations** শিখেছ।
আজ আমরা সেই knowledge ব্যবহার করে **নিজের daily routine নিয়ে natural conversation** করব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 37-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের daily routine বলতে
* সকাল থেকে রাত পর্যন্ত activities describe করতে
* time বলতে
* frequency বলতে
* **Present Simple** ব্যবহার করতে
* “usually / often / sometimes / rarely / never” ব্যবহার করতে
* routine সম্পর্কে follow-up questions-এর answer দিতে
* নিজের routine compare করতে
* 2–5 মিনিট নিজের daily life নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Daily Routine কী?

**Daily routine = প্রতিদিনের নিয়মিত কাজ।**

যেমন:

> Wake up
> Brush my teeth
> Take a shower
> Have breakfast
> Go to university/work
> Study/work
> Have lunch
> Exercise
> Use the computer
> Have dinner
> Go to bed

---

# ⭐ Daily Routine-এর Golden Formula

# **TIME → ACTIVITY → FREQUENCY → REASON**

### Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. because I like to start my day early.

---

# 🌅 Part 2 — Morning Routine

সকালের কাজগুলো:

> wake up — ঘুম থেকে ওঠা

> get up — বিছানা থেকে ওঠা

> brush my teeth — দাঁত ব্রাশ করা

> wash my face — মুখ ধোয়া

> take a shower — গোসল করা

> get dressed — পোশাক পরা

> have breakfast — নাশতা করা

> leave home — বাসা থেকে বের হওয়া

---

# 🟢 Example

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. First, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I take a shower and get dressed. After that, I have breakfast. Finally, I leave home for university/work.

---

# ⚠️ Important Difference

### Wake up

ঘুম ভাঙা:

> I wake up at 7 a.m.

### Get up

বিছানা থেকে ওঠা:

> I get up a few minutes after I wake up.

দুটো একই না, তবে conversation-এ দুটোই খুব common।

---

# ☀️ Part 3 — Afternoon Routine

Useful expressions:

> go to work

> go to university

> attend classes

> study

> work

> have lunch

> take a break

> finish work

> return home

### Example:

> I usually attend classes in the morning and study in the afternoon.

> I have lunch around 1 p.m.

> After lunch, I usually take a short break.

---

# 🌆 Part 4 — Evening Routine

> return home

> relax

> have tea

> exercise

> go for a walk

> meet friends

> study

> work on a project

> use my phone

> watch TV

### Example:

> In the evening, I usually return home and take a short rest. After that, I sometimes go for a walk or exercise. Then I spend some time studying or working on my projects.

---

# 🌙 Part 5 — Night Routine

> have dinner

> talk to family

> study

> use the computer

> browse the internet

> prepare for the next day

> go to bed

> fall asleep

### Example:

> At night, I usually have dinner with my family. After dinner, I study or work on my computer. Before going to bed, I prepare for the next day. I usually go to bed around 11 p.m.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Present Simple

Daily routine-এর জন্য প্রধান tense:

# **Present Simple**

### I / You / We / They

> I wake up at 7.

> I study English.

> I go to university.

### He / She / It

**verb + s/es**

> He wakes up at 7.

> She studies English.

> He goes to work.

---

# 🚨 Common Mistakes

### ❌

> I wakes up at 7.

### ✅

> I wake up at 7.

---

### ❌

> He wake up at 7.

### ✅

> He wakes up at 7.

---

### ❌

> She go to university.

### ✅

> She goes to university.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Frequency Words

এগুলো daily routine speaking-এর জন্য অত্যন্ত important।

| Word         | Meaning   |
| ------------ | --------- |
| Always       | সবসময়    |
| Usually      | সাধারণত   |
| Often        | প্রায়ই   |
| Sometimes    | মাঝে মাঝে |
| Occasionally | কখনো কখনো |
| Rarely       | খুব কম    |
| Never        | কখনো না   |

---

# 📊 Frequency Scale

মনে রাখো:

> **Always → Usually → Often → Sometimes → Rarely → Never**

### Examples:

> I always brush my teeth in the morning.

> I usually have breakfast at home.

> I often exercise in the evening.

> I sometimes watch movies.

> I rarely eat fast food.

> I never smoke.

---

# 🟢 Part 8 — Frequency + Activity

### Question:

> How often do you exercise?

### Answer:

> I usually exercise three or four times a week.

---

### Question:

> How often do you watch movies?

> I sometimes watch movies on weekends.

---

### Question:

> How often do you use social media?

> I use social media every day, but I try not to spend too much time on it.

---

# 🕐 Part 9 — Time Expressions

Daily routine describe করতে time খুব important।

### Exact Time

> at 7 a.m.

> at 8:30

> at noon

> at midnight

### Parts of Day

> in the morning

> in the afternoon

> in the evening

> at night

---

# ⚠️ Important

### ❌

> at morning

### ✅

> in the morning

---

### ❌

> in night

### ✅

> at night

---

### Examples:

> I exercise in the morning.

> I have lunch in the afternoon.

> I study in the evening.

> I go to bed at night.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Sequence Words

Routine বলার সময় এগুলো ব্যবহার করলে speech organized হবে।

> First

> Then

> After that

> Next

> Later

> Finally

---

## Example:

> First, I wake up. Then I brush my teeth. After that, I take a shower. Next, I have breakfast. Later, I go to university. Finally, I return home in the evening.

🔥 এই wordsগুলো তোমার speaking-এর flow অনেক improve করবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Complete Daily Routine

## Question:

> What is your daily routine?

### Beginner:

> I wake up in the morning. I have breakfast and go to university. I study during the day. In the evening, I come home. At night, I have dinner and go to bed.

### Intermediate:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. First, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I take a shower and have breakfast. After that, I start my studies or work. I usually have lunch around 1 p.m. In the evening, I return home and relax for a while. At night, I have dinner, study for a few hours, and usually go to bed around 11 p.m.

---

# 🔥 Advanced Version

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. and start my day by brushing my teeth and washing my face. After taking a shower, I have breakfast and prepare for the day. Most of my daytime is spent studying or working on different tasks. I usually take a short break after lunch before continuing my work. In the evening, I return home and spend some time relaxing or exercising. After dinner, I often study English or work on personal projects. Before going to bed, I usually review what I have accomplished during the day and make a plan for the next day.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — “Start My Day By...”

এটা খুব useful natural expression।

### Structure:

> **start my day by + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I start my day by exercising.

> I start my day by reading the news.

> I start my day by drinking a glass of water.

> I start my day by planning my tasks.

---

# 🟢 Part 13 — “Spend Time + ing”

খুব useful structure:

> **spend time + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I spend time studying English.

> I spend a lot of time working on my projects.

> I spend time with my family.

> I spend some time reading books.

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> I spend time to study English.

### ✅

> I spend time studying English.

---

# 🟡 Part 14 — “Before” & “After”

### Before:

> I brush my teeth before breakfast.

> I prepare my clothes before going to bed.

### After:

> I take a shower after exercising.

> I relax after finishing my work.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — “Before + ing”

### Example:

> Before going to bed, I check my schedule for the next day.

### “After + ing”

> After finishing my work, I usually relax.

এগুলো speaking-কে অনেক natural করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Routine vs Habit

### Routine:

প্রতিদিনের সাধারণ sequence:

> I wake up at 7, have breakfast, and go to work.

### Habit:

কোনো কাজ নিয়মিত করার অভ্যাস:

> I usually drink tea in the morning.

> I often exercise in the evening.

দুটো related, কিন্তু এক জিনিস না।

---

# 🟢 Part 17 — Asking About Someone's Routine

এখন অন্যকে প্রশ্ন করা শিখি।

### Basic:

> What do you usually do in the morning?

> What time do you wake up?

> What do you do after breakfast?

> What do you usually do in the evening?

> What time do you go to bed?

---

# 🔥 More Natural Questions

> What does a typical day look like for you?

> How do you usually spend your day?

> What's your usual morning routine?

> What do you normally do after work?

> How do you spend your evenings?

> What do you usually do before going to bed?

---

# ⭐ Very Important

## “What does a typical day look like for you?”

এর অর্থ:

> তোমার সাধারণ একটি দিন কেমন কাটে?

এটা real conversation-এ খুব useful।

---

# 🗣️ Part 18 — Full Conversation

### A:

> What time do you usually wake up?

### B:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m.

### A:

> What do you do after that?

### B:

> First, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I take a shower and have breakfast.

### A:

> What do you usually do during the day?

### B:

> I usually study or work on my projects during the day.

### A:

> What do you do in the evening?

### B:

> I usually return home and relax for a while. Sometimes I go for a walk or exercise.

### A:

> What time do you go to bed?

### B:

> I usually go to bed around 11 p.m.

### A:

> Do you think you have a healthy routine?

### B:

> I think my routine is fairly good, but I want to exercise more regularly and sleep a little earlier.

🔥 Notice: **Routine + Frequency + Opinion + Future improvement**

---

# 🟡 Part 19 — Good Routine vs Bad Routine

### Question:

> Is your daily routine healthy?

### Answer:

> I think my routine is fairly healthy, but there are some things I need to improve. I usually eat regular meals and try to stay active. However, I sometimes spend too much time on my computer at night. I also want to sleep earlier and exercise more regularly.

এখানে Lesson 33 + 34-এর skill এসেছে:

> **Opinion → Reason → However → Improvement**

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Changing Your Routine

### Question:

> Is there anything you want to change about your routine?

### Answer:

> Yes. I want to wake up earlier and exercise regularly. I also want to reduce the amount of time I spend on social media. I think these changes will help me become more productive.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “Used to” for Old Routine

Past routine describe করার জন্য:

# **used to + base verb**

### Example:

> I used to wake up late.

> I used to spend a lot of time watching TV.

> I used to play football every afternoon.

### Compare:

> I used to wake up late, but now I wake up early.

> I used to watch TV every evening, but now I spend more time studying.

🔥 এটা Lesson 31 Past Experiences-এর সাথে connection তৈরি করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — “Now vs Before”

### Formula:

> **I used to..., but now...**

Examples:

> I used to sleep late, but now I go to bed earlier.

> I used to eat fast food often, but now I try to eat healthier food.

> I used to spend a lot of time on social media, but now I use it less.

---

# 🟢 Part 23 — Daily Routine Vocabulary

| English        | বাংলা                |
| -------------- | -------------------- |
| Wake up        | ঘুম থেকে ওঠা         |
| Get up         | বিছানা থেকে ওঠা      |
| Brush my teeth | দাঁত ব্রাশ করা       |
| Wash my face   | মুখ ধোয়া            |
| Take a shower  | গোসল করা             |
| Get dressed    | পোশাক পরা            |
| Have breakfast | নাশতা করা            |
| Leave home     | বাসা থেকে বের হওয়া  |
| Attend class   | ক্লাস করা            |
| Study          | পড়াশোনা করা         |
| Work           | কাজ করা              |
| Have lunch     | দুপুরের খাবার খাওয়া |
| Take a break   | বিরতি নেওয়া         |
| Return home    | বাসায় ফেরা          |
| Relax          | বিশ্রাম নেওয়া       |
| Exercise       | ব্যায়াম করা         |
| Have dinner    | রাতের খাবার খাওয়া   |
| Prepare        | প্রস্তুতি নেওয়া     |
| Go to bed      | বিছানায় যাওয়া      |
| Fall asleep    | ঘুমিয়ে পড়া         |

---

# 🎯 Part 24 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

### 1.

> What time do you usually wake up?

### 2.

> What do you do first in the morning?

### 3.

> What do you usually do during the day?

### 4.

> Where do you usually study or work?

### 5.

> What do you do after lunch?

### 6.

> What do you usually do in the evening?

### 7.

> How often do you exercise?

### 8.

> What do you usually do at night?

### 9.

> What time do you usually go to bed?

### 10.

> Is there anything you want to change about your routine?

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why do you wake up at that time?

> What do you do after that?

> How long does it take?

> How often do you do it?

> Do you enjoy it?

> What do you usually do on busy days?

> What do you do when you have free time?

> Has your routine changed recently?

> What did your routine use to be like?

> What would you like to change?

---

# 🗣️ Part 26 — 3-Level Practice

## Question:

> What do you usually do in the evening?

### 🟢 Beginner

> I usually relax in the evening.

### 🟡 Intermediate

> I usually return home in the evening. Then I relax for a while and have dinner.

### 🔴 Advanced

> I usually return home in the early evening and take a short break. After that, I either exercise or work on my personal projects. Later, I have dinner with my family and spend some time studying English. Before going to bed, I usually plan my tasks for the following day.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **“My Typical Day”**

এই order follow করবে:

### Morning

> What time do you wake up?

### Breakfast

> What do you eat?

### Daytime

> Where do you go?

### Study/Work

> What do you do?

### Lunch

> When do you have lunch?

### Afternoon

> What do you do?

### Evening

> How do you spend your evening?

### Night

> What do you do before bed?

### Sleep

> What time do you go to bed?

### Improvement

> What would you like to change?

---

# 📝 Part 28 — Homework

## Task 1 — Answer

প্রতিটি question-এর **3–5 sentence** answer দাও:

1. What time do you usually wake up?
2. What is the first thing you do in the morning?
3. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
4. Where do you usually go during the day?
5. What do you usually do in the afternoon?
6. How often do you exercise?
7. How do you spend your evening?
8. What do you usually do at night?
9. What time do you go to bed?
10. What would you like to change about your routine?

---

# 📝 Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে 10টি daily-routine question তৈরি করো।

Use:

> What time...?

> What do you usually...?

> How often...?

> Where...?

> When...?

> How long...?

> What do you do after...?

> What do you do before...?

> Do you usually...?

> Would you like to change...?

---

# 🎤 Task 3 — Speaking Without Notes

**5 minutes** কোনো notes না দেখে বলবে:

> **My Typical Day**

এই 7টি word শুধু মনে রাখবে:

> **Morning → Breakfast → Work/Study → Lunch → Afternoon → Evening → Night**

এরপর নিজের মতো sentence তৈরি করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 37 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **TIME → ACTIVITY → FREQUENCY → REASON**

Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. I start my day by exercising because it helps me feel energetic. After that, I take a shower and have breakfast.

আর পুরো routine:

# **Morning → Daytime → Afternoon → Evening → Night → Improvement**

---

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 38

# 🎨 Hobbies & Free Time

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** অবসর সময়ে কী করো → কতবার করো → কেন করো → কার সাথে করো → কখন শুরু করেছ → কীভাবে তোমার উপকার হয়।

Lesson 37-এ তুমি শিখেছ **Daily Activities & Routines**।
আজ সেই routine-এর **free time** অংশকে আরও বিস্তারিতভাবে কথা বলা শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 38-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের hobbies বলতে
* free time কীভাবে কাটাও বলতে
* hobby কেন পছন্দ করো explain করতে
* কতবার করো বলতে
* কার সাথে করো বলতে
* কখন শুরু করেছ বলতে
* hobby থেকে কী শিখেছ বলতে
* নতুন hobby শুরু করতে চাও কিনা বলতে
* দুইটি hobby compare করতে
* 2–5 মিনিট hobby নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Hobby কী?

**Hobby = অবসর সময়ে আনন্দের জন্য নিয়মিত যে কাজ করি।**

Examples:

> Reading
> Watching movies
> Listening to music
> Playing football
> Traveling
> Photography
> Cooking
> Gardening
> Drawing
> Gaming
> Writing
> Learning languages
> Exercising

---

# ⭐ Hobby Speaking Formula

যেকোনো hobby নিয়ে কথা বলার সময়:

# **WHAT → HOW OFTEN → WHEN → WITH WHOM → WHY → BENEFIT → FUTURE**

### Example:

> My favorite hobby is reading.
> I usually read three or four times a week.
> I mostly read in the evening.
> I usually read alone.
> I enjoy reading because it helps me relax.
> It also improves my vocabulary and knowledge.
> In the future, I'd like to read more books.

এই formula follow করলে answer সহজে **1–3 মিনিট** করা যায়।

---

# 1️⃣ Part 2 — “What do you do in your free time?”

এটা খুব common question।

### Short:

> I usually watch movies and listen to music.

### Better:

> In my free time, I usually watch movies, listen to music, and work on personal projects.

### Natural:

> When I have some free time, I usually relax by watching movies, listening to music, or spending time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Hobby vs Free Time

দুটো related কিন্তু একই নয়।

### Hobby:

যে কাজটি তুমি নিয়মিত interest নিয়ে করো।

> Reading is one of my hobbies.

### Free time:

যে সময়ে তোমার কাজ/পড়াশোনা থাকে না।

> I usually watch videos in my free time.

সব free-time activity necessarily hobby নয়।

---

# 🎨 Part 4 — Common Hobbies

## 📚 Learning

> reading books

> learning English

> learning programming

> learning new skills

> watching educational videos

---

## 🎵 Entertainment

> listening to music

> watching movies

> watching TV shows

> playing video games

> browsing YouTube

---

## ⚽ Sports

> playing football

> playing cricket

> playing badminton

> going to the gym

> cycling

> jogging

---

## 🌳 Outdoor

> traveling

> hiking

> walking

> photography

> visiting new places

---

## 🎨 Creative

> drawing

> painting

> writing

> cooking

> photography

> making videos

---

# 🟢 Part 5 — “How Often?”

Hobby describe করার সময় frequency বলবে।

> every day

> every morning

> every evening

> once a week

> twice a week

> three times a week

> on weekends

> occasionally

> whenever I have free time

---

### Examples:

> I read every day.

> I play football twice a week.

> I watch movies on weekends.

> I travel whenever I have enough time.

> I occasionally go cycling.

---

# 🧠 Important Structure

### Once

> once a week

### Twice

> twice a week

### Three or more

> three times a week

> four times a month

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> I play football two time a week.

### ✅

> I play football twice a week.

---

# 🕐 Part 6 — When Do You Do It?

### Useful:

> in the morning

> in the afternoon

> in the evening

> at night

> on weekends

> after work

> after university

> before going to bed

> whenever I have free time

---

### Examples:

> I usually read before going to bed.

> I play football on weekends.

> I listen to music while working.

> I watch movies in the evening.

---

# 🧑‍🤝‍🧑 Part 7 — Who Do You Do It With?

### Examples:

> I usually do it alone.

> I usually do it with my friends.

> I enjoy it with my family.

> I sometimes play football with my classmates.

> I often travel with my friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Why Do You Like It?

এখানে **because** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Basic:

> I like reading because it is interesting.

### Better:

> I like reading because it helps me relax.

### Strong:

> I enjoy reading because it allows me to learn new things while also helping me relax.

---

# ⭐ Useful Reasons

> because it is fun.

> because it helps me relax.

> because it reduces stress.

> because it keeps me active.

> because I can learn new things.

> because it improves my skills.

> because I can spend time with my friends.

> because it gives me a break from my routine.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “It Helps Me...”

এই structure অনেক useful।

> It helps me relax.

> It helps me stay healthy.

> It helps me improve my English.

> It helps me develop new skills.

> It helps me reduce stress.

> It helps me stay focused.

### Example:

> Reading helps me improve my vocabulary.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Benefits

Hobby-এর benefit বলতে পারলে answer stronger হবে।

### Reading:

> It improves my vocabulary.

> It increases my knowledge.

### Exercise:

> It keeps me fit.

> It reduces stress.

### Traveling:

> It helps me learn about new cultures.

### Music:

> It helps me relax.

### Photography:

> It improves my creativity.

### Programming:

> It improves my problem-solving skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “How did you start?”

Past Experience-এর সাথে connection।

### Question:

> When did you start this hobby?

### Answer:

> I started reading when I was a teenager.

> I started playing football when I was in school.

> I became interested in photography a few years ago.

---

# ⭐ Useful Structure

> **I started + verb-ing + when...**

Examples:

> I started learning English when I was in school.

> I started playing cricket when I was a child.

---

# 🟢 Part 12 — “How long have you...?”

যদি এখনও hobby চালিয়ে যাচ্ছ:

> **How long have you been + verb-ing?**

### Example:

> How long have you been learning English?

### Answer:

> I've been learning English for three years.

---

### Another:

> I've been playing football since I was a child.

---

# ⚠️ Important

### ❌

> I am learning English since three years.

### ✅

> I've been learning English for three years.

---

# 🎵 Part 13 — Example: Music

### Question:

> What kind of music do you like?

### Answer:

> I enjoy listening to different kinds of music, but I especially like relaxing and emotional songs. I usually listen to music in the evening or while working. Music helps me relax and improves my mood. Sometimes I also listen to English songs because they help me practice my listening skills.

---

# 🎬 Part 14 — Example: Movies

### Question:

> Do you like watching movies?

### Answer:

> Yes, I really enjoy watching movies. I usually watch movies on weekends when I have more free time. I especially like action, comedy, and science-fiction movies. I usually watch them with my friends, although sometimes I watch movies alone. Movies help me relax after a busy week.

---

# 📚 Part 15 — Example: Reading

### Question:

> Is reading your hobby?

### Answer:

> Yes, reading is one of my favorite hobbies. I usually read in the evening or before going to bed. I mostly read books about technology, personal development, and fiction. I enjoy reading because it helps me relax and learn new things. I've been developing this habit for several years, and I'd like to read more books in the future.

---

# 💻 Part 16 — Example: Programming

যেহেতু hobby হিসেবে কোনো skill শেখা যায়:

> One of my favorite activities is learning programming. I usually spend a few hours each week learning new technologies and working on small projects. I enjoy it because I like solving problems and creating useful things. It can sometimes be challenging, but I find it very rewarding. I believe this hobby is also useful for my future career.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “Although / But”

একই answer-এ positive + negative বললে speech natural হয়।

### Example:

> I enjoy reading, although I don't always have enough time.

> I like exercising, but sometimes I feel too tired after work.

> I enjoy traveling, although it can be expensive.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Used to” Connection

Past hobby:

> I used to play football every afternoon.

> I used to watch cartoons a lot.

> I used to draw when I was a child.

### Compare:

> I used to play football regularly, but now I don't have much time.

> I used to watch movies every weekend, but now I usually spend my weekends studying.

---

# 🟡 Part 19 — Hobby You Want to Start

### Question:

> Is there any new hobby you'd like to try?

### Answer:

> Yes, I'd like to learn photography.

### Better:

> I'd like to learn photography because I enjoy taking pictures of places and nature.

### Strong:

> I'd like to take up photography in the future because I enjoy capturing interesting places and moments. I think it would also help me become more creative.

---

# ⭐ “Take Up”

**take up a hobby**

= নতুন কোনো hobby শুরু করা

### Example:

> I'd like to take up photography.

> I'm thinking about taking up swimming.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “Give Up”

**give up a hobby**

= hobby ছেড়ে দেওয়া

> I gave up playing football because I became too busy.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Compare Two Hobbies

### Question:

> Which do you prefer, reading or watching movies?

### Answer:

> I enjoy both, but I prefer reading. Watching movies is more entertaining, while reading requires more concentration. However, reading helps me improve my vocabulary and knowledge, so I find it more useful.

### Formula:

> **Both → Preference → Difference → Reason**

---

# 🗣️ Part 22 — Full Conversation

### A:

> What do you usually do in your free time?

### B:

> I usually read books, listen to music, and watch movies.

### A:

> Which one is your favorite?

### B:

> Reading is probably my favorite hobby.

### A:

> How often do you read?

### B:

> I usually read three or four times a week.

### A:

> When do you normally read?

### B:

> I usually read in the evening or before going to bed.

### A:

> Why do you enjoy reading?

### B:

> I enjoy it because it helps me relax and learn new things.

### A:

> How long have you been interested in reading?

### B:

> I've been interested in reading for several years.

### A:

> Is there any new hobby you'd like to try?

### B:

> Yes. I'd like to take up photography because I think it would be fun and creative.

---

# 🎯 Part 23 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

1. What do you do in your free time?
2. What is your favorite hobby?
3. How often do you do it?
4. When do you usually do it?
5. Who do you usually do it with?
6. Why do you enjoy it?
7. When did you start this hobby?
8. What have you learned from it?
9. Has your hobby changed over time?
10. Is there any new hobby you'd like to try?

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation continue করতে:

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you do it?

> When did you start?

> Who introduced you to it?

> Do you do it alone?

> How much time do you spend on it?

> What do you like most about it?

> Has it helped you in any way?

> Would you like to turn it into a career?

> Would you recommend it to other people?

---

# 🧩 Part 25 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **My Favorite Hobby**

> My favorite hobby is reading. I usually read three or four times a week, mostly in the evening or before going to bed. I prefer reading books about technology, personal development, and sometimes fiction. I usually read alone because I find it easier to concentrate that way.
>
> I enjoy reading because it helps me relax and learn new things at the same time. It has also helped me improve my vocabulary and general knowledge. I've been interested in reading for several years, although I sometimes don't have enough time because of my studies and other responsibilities.
>
> In the future, I'd like to read more regularly and explore different types of books. I think reading is a useful hobby because it is both enjoyable and educational.

🔥 এই answer-এর structure follow করলে তুমি যেকোনো hobby নিয়ে কথা বলতে পারবে।

---

# 📝 Part 26 — Homework

## Task 1 — Answer these 10 questions

প্রতিটি **3–5 sentences**:

1. What do you usually do in your free time?
2. What is your favorite hobby?
3. How often do you do it?
4. When do you usually do it?
5. Who do you usually do it with?
6. Why do you enjoy it?
7. When did you start it?
8. What benefits do you get from it?
9. Has your hobby changed over time?
10. What new hobby would you like to try?

---

# 📝 Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে **10টি hobby-related question** বানাও।

Use:

> What...?

> Why...?

> How often...?

> When...?

> Who...?

> How long...?

> What kind...?

> Have you ever...?

> Would you like...?

> What have you learned...?

---

# 🎤 Task 3 — 5-Minute Speaking

Topic:

# **“My Hobbies and Free Time”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **What → How often → When → With whom → Why → Benefits → Past → Future**

নিজের notes না দেখে বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 38 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → HOW OFTEN → WHEN → WITH WHOM → WHY → BENEFIT → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **My favorite hobby is reading.**
> **I usually read three times a week.**
> **I mostly read in the evening.**
> **I usually read alone.**
> **I enjoy it because it helps me relax.**
> **It also improves my vocabulary and knowledge.**
> **In the future, I'd like to read more books.**

---

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 39

# 🍛 Food & Eating Habits

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কী খাও → কী পছন্দ/অপছন্দ → কখন খাও → কতবার খাও → কীভাবে খাও → healthy/unhealthy কেন → নিজের eating habits explain করা।

Lesson 38-এ তুমি শিখেছ **Hobbies & Free Time**। আজ একই speaking pattern ব্যবহার করে **Food & Eating Habits** নিয়ে natural conversation শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 39-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের favorite food বলতে
* কী কী খাবার পছন্দ/অপছন্দ বলতে
* breakfast/lunch/dinner describe করতে
* eating habits বলতে
* healthy vs unhealthy food নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* restaurant-এ খাবার order করার basic conversation করতে
* traditional Bangladeshi food নিয়ে বলতে
* নিজের খাবারের preference explain করতে
* খাবার নিয়ে opinion + reason দিতে
* 2–5 মিনিট food নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Food Speaking Formula

যেকোনো food topic-এর জন্য:

# **WHAT → WHEN → HOW OFTEN → WHY → HOW → HEALTH → OPINION**

### Example:

> My favorite food is biryani.
> I usually eat it on special occasions.
> I don't eat it very often.
> I like it because it is delicious and flavorful.
> I usually have it with salad or a soft drink.
> However, I try not to eat too much oily food.
> Overall, I really enjoy biryani.

---

# 🍽️ Part 2 — Meals of the Day

### Breakfast

> সকালের নাশতা

### Lunch

> দুপুরের খাবার

### Dinner

> রাতের খাবার

### Snack

> হালকা খাবার

---

### Examples:

> I usually have breakfast at 8 a.m.

> I have lunch around 1 p.m.

> I usually have dinner with my family.

> I sometimes have a snack in the afternoon.

---

# 🥚 Part 3 — Common Food Vocabulary

## Breakfast

> bread

> eggs

> paratha

> vegetables

> fruits

> cereal

> milk

> tea

> coffee

---

## Lunch/Dinner

> rice

> fish

> chicken

> beef

> vegetables

> lentils

> curry

> salad

> soup

---

# 🇧🇩 Part 4 — Bangladeshi Food

বাংলাদেশের খাবার নিয়ে speaking-এর জন্য useful vocabulary:

> rice

> hilsa fish

> fish curry

> chicken curry

> beef curry

> biryani

> khichuri

> dal

> bhorta

> vegetables

> paratha

> pitha

---

### Example:

> Rice and fish are common foods in Bangladesh.

> Biryani is very popular in Bangladesh.

> I really enjoy traditional Bangladeshi food.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Taste

খাবারের taste describe করার জন্য:

> sweet — মিষ্টি

> salty — লবণাক্ত

> spicy — ঝাল

> sour — টক

> bitter — তিতা

> delicious — সুস্বাদু

> tasty — সুস্বাদু

> fresh — তাজা

> oily — তেলযুক্ত

> crispy — মচমচে

> soft — নরম

> juicy — রসালো

> flavorful — স্বাদযুক্ত

---

### Examples:

> The food is very spicy.

> This curry is delicious.

> I don't like overly salty food.

> The chicken is crispy and tasty.

> The fruit is fresh and juicy.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “What is your favorite food?”

### Beginner:

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite food is biryani. I really enjoy its taste and smell.

### Advanced:

> Biryani is probably my favorite food. I love it because it is flavorful, aromatic, and filling. I especially enjoy having it on special occasions with my family or friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Why Do You Like It?

শুধু:

> Because it is tasty.

না বলে একটু expand করবে।

### Formula:

> **I like it because + reason + additional detail.**

### Examples:

> I like it because it is delicious and easy to eat.

> I enjoy it because it is flavorful and reminds me of my childhood.

> I like it because it is healthy and gives me energy.

> I enjoy it because I can share it with my family.

---

# 🍛 Part 8 — “What do you usually eat?”

### Question:

> What do you usually eat for breakfast?

### Answer:

> I usually have bread, eggs, and tea for breakfast.

### Better:

> I usually have eggs and bread for breakfast, and I drink tea. Sometimes I also have fruit.

---

### Lunch:

> I usually have rice, vegetables, fish, or chicken for lunch.

### Dinner:

> I usually have rice with vegetables and fish or chicken for dinner.

---

# 🕐 Part 9 — Eating Frequency

Useful expressions:

> every day

> once a week

> twice a week

> occasionally

> sometimes

> rarely

> regularly

> almost every day

---

### Examples:

> I eat rice almost every day.

> I eat fast food occasionally.

> I rarely drink soft drinks.

> I usually eat fruit every day.

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> I eat biryani two times in week.

### Better:

> I eat biryani twice a week.

---

# 🥗 Part 10 — Healthy Food

Useful vocabulary:

> healthy

> nutritious

> balanced diet

> fresh vegetables

> fresh fruits

> protein

> vitamins

> minerals

> fiber

> low-fat

> homemade food

---

### Examples:

> Vegetables are an important part of a healthy diet.

> I try to eat more fresh fruits and vegetables.

> I prefer homemade food because I know what ingredients are being used.

---

# 🍔 Part 11 — Unhealthy Food

> fast food

> junk food

> fried food

> oily food

> sugary drinks

> soft drinks

> processed food

> too much salt

> too much sugar

---

### Example:

> I enjoy fast food, but I try not to eat it too often because it can be unhealthy.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Healthy vs Unhealthy

### Question:

> Do you have a healthy diet?

### Answer:

> I think my diet is fairly healthy, but there is room for improvement. I usually eat homemade food and include vegetables and protein in my meals. However, I sometimes eat fast food when I'm busy. I'd like to reduce my intake of oily and sugary foods.

🔥 এখানে:

**Opinion → Positive → Negative → Future improvement**

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “I Try to...”

খাবারের habits describe করতে খুব useful।

> I try to eat healthy food.

> I try to avoid too much sugar.

> I try to drink enough water.

> I try to eat more vegetables.

> I try to avoid eating late at night.

---

# 🚫 Part 14 — “Avoid”

**avoid + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I try to avoid fast food.

> I avoid eating too much sugar.

> I try to avoid drinking soft drinks.

### ⚠️

❌ I avoid to eat fast food.

✅ I avoid eating fast food.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “Prefer”

### Structure:

> **prefer A to B**

### Examples:

> I prefer rice to bread.

> I prefer tea to coffee.

> I prefer homemade food to fast food.

---

### Question:

> Do you prefer tea or coffee?

### Answer:

> I prefer tea to coffee because I like its taste more.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Would Rather”

আরও advanced:

> **would rather A than B**

### Example:

> I'd rather eat homemade food than fast food.

> I'd rather drink water than a soft drink.

---

# 🥤 Part 17 — Drinks

> water

> tea

> coffee

> milk

> juice

> soft drink

> lemonade

> smoothie

---

### Question:

> What do you usually drink?

### Answer:

> I mostly drink water and tea. I sometimes have coffee, but I rarely drink soft drinks.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Restaurant Conversation

Food speaking-এর practical part।

### Customer:

> Excuse me. Can I see the menu, please?

### Waiter:

> Sure. Here you are.

### Customer:

> What do you recommend?

### Waiter:

> I'd recommend the chicken biryani.

### Customer:

> Is it spicy?

### Waiter:

> Yes, it's moderately spicy.

### Customer:

> I'll have the chicken biryani, please.

### Waiter:

> Would you like anything to drink?

### Customer:

> Yes, I'd like a bottle of water, please.

---

# 🍽️ Part 19 — Ordering Food

Useful expressions:

> Can I have...?

> I'd like...

> I'll have...

> Could I get...?

> Can I order...?

---

### Examples:

> I'd like a chicken burger, please.

> Can I have a glass of water?

> Could I get a bowl of soup?

> I'll have the chicken biryani.

---

# ⚠️ Important

Restaurant-এ:

> **I want chicken.**

বোঝা যাবে, কিন্তু polite নয়।

Better:

> **I'd like some chicken, please.**

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Asking About Food

Useful questions:

> Is it spicy?

> Is it vegetarian?

> Is it fresh?

> What ingredients does it contain?

> Does it contain nuts?

> Is it sweet?

> How much does it cost?

> What do you recommend?

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Food You Dislike

### Question:

> Is there any food you don't like?

### Answer:

> Yes, I don't really like very spicy food. I can eat mildly spicy food, but extremely spicy food is difficult for me to enjoy.

### Useful:

> I don't really like...

> I'm not a big fan of...

> I don't particularly enjoy...

---

# ⭐ Natural Expression

## “I'm not a big fan of...”

= আমি খুব একটা পছন্দ করি না।

### Examples:

> I'm not a big fan of fast food.

> I'm not a big fan of very spicy food.

> I'm not a big fan of sugary drinks.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Childhood Food

Past experience connection:

### Question:

> What food did you like when you were a child?

### Answer:

> When I was a child, I really liked sweets and homemade snacks. I also loved eating biryani on special occasions. My eating habits have changed quite a lot since then.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “My Eating Habits Have Changed”

Useful structure:

> **My eating habits have changed over time.**

Then:

> I used to eat more fast food, but now I try to eat healthier food.

> I used to drink soft drinks regularly, but now I mostly drink water.

> I used to skip breakfast, but now I try to have breakfast every morning.

🔥 এটি Lesson 37-এর **used to** এবং Lesson 31-এর past experience-এর সাথে connection তৈরি করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Food & Family

### Question:

> Do you usually eat with your family?

### Answer:

> Yes, I usually have dinner with my family. We talk about our day while eating, so dinner is also a good time for us to spend time together.

---

# 🟢 Useful Expressions

> have a meal together

> eat together

> spend time together

> talk over dinner

> cook at home

> prepare a meal

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Cooking

### Question:

> Can you cook?

### Beginner:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes, such as rice, eggs, and some basic curries.

### Advanced:

> I can prepare a few simple dishes, although I'm not an expert cook. I enjoy cooking occasionally because it allows me to experiment with different ingredients.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Favorite Restaurant

### Question:

> Do you have a favorite restaurant?

### Answer:

> Yes, I have a restaurant that I really like. I enjoy going there because the food is tasty, the service is good, and the atmosphere is comfortable. I usually go there with my friends or family.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Describe a Meal You Enjoyed

### Question:

> Describe a meal you really enjoyed.

### Model Answer:

> One meal I really enjoyed was a family dinner we had recently. We had rice, chicken, vegetables, and several traditional dishes. The food was delicious, but what made the meal special was that everyone in my family was together. We talked, laughed, and spent a lot of time together. I really enjoyed the experience because it reminded me how important family time is.

Notice:

> **What → Food → Details → Experience → Feeling → Reason**

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Food Opinion

### Question:

> Do you think people should eat fast food?

### Answer:

> I think fast food is okay occasionally, but people shouldn't eat it too often. It's convenient and tasty, but eating too much of it can be unhealthy. I believe a balanced diet with fresh food is a better choice.

🔥 এটি Lesson 33-এর **Opinion + Reason** এবং Lesson 34-এর **Agree/Disagree** skill ব্যবহার করছে।

---

# 🗣️ Part 29 — Full Food Conversation

### A:

> What's your favorite food?

### B:

> Biryani is probably my favorite food.

### A:

> How often do you eat it?

### B:

> I usually eat it once or twice a month.

### A:

> Why do you like it?

### B:

> I love it because it's flavorful and delicious.

### A:

> Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?

### B:

> I prefer homemade food because it's usually healthier and I know what ingredients are being used.

### A:

> Do you eat fast food?

### B:

> Occasionally, but I try not to eat it too often.

### A:

> Are your eating habits different now?

### B:

> Yes. I used to eat more junk food, but now I try to eat a more balanced diet.

---

# 🎯 Part 30 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের সবচেয়ে important questions:

1. What is your favorite food?
2. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
3. What do you usually eat for lunch?
4. What do you usually eat for dinner?
5. How often do you eat fast food?
6. What food don't you like?
7. Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?
8. Do you think you have a healthy diet?
9. Have your eating habits changed over time?
10. What food would you like to try?

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Follow-up Questions

Conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you eat it?

> Who usually cooks it?

> Can you cook it yourself?

> Where do you usually eat it?

> Is it healthy?

> Is it expensive?

> When did you first try it?

> Would you recommend it?

> Would you like to learn how to cook it?

---

# 🧩 Part 32 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **My Eating Habits**

> My eating habits are fairly simple. I usually have breakfast in the morning, lunch around midday, and dinner in the evening. I mostly eat rice, vegetables, fish, chicken, and other homemade food. Biryani is probably my favorite food, although I don't eat it very often because it's quite heavy.
>
> I prefer homemade food to fast food because I think it is healthier and usually more nutritious. I sometimes eat fast food when I'm busy or when I'm out with my friends, but I try not to eat it too often. I also try to drink plenty of water and eat fresh fruits and vegetables.
>
> My eating habits have changed over time. I used to eat more junk food and drink more soft drinks, but now I try to follow a more balanced diet. In the future, I'd like to become more consistent about eating healthy food.

---

# **“My Food & Eating Habits”**

Notes না দেখে বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

# 🏆 Lesson 39 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → WHEN → HOW OFTEN → WHY → HOW → HEALTH → OPINION**

### Example:

> **My favorite food is biryani.**
> **I usually eat it on special occasions.**
> **I don't eat it very often.**
> **I like it because it is delicious and flavorful.**
> **I usually have it with salad.**
> **Although it is tasty, I try not to eat too much oily food.**
> **Overall, I really enjoy it.**
