# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 40

# 🛍️ Shopping & Buying Things

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কী কিনতে চাও → কেন কিনতে চাও → দাম কত → কোনটা পছন্দ → compare করা → দোকানে প্রশ্ন করা → negotiate করা → purchase decision explain করা।

Lesson 39-এ তুমি **Food & Eating Habits** শিখেছ। আজ আমরা সেই একই speaking pattern ব্যবহার করে **Shopping & Buying Things** নিয়ে কথা বলব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 40-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কী কিনতে চাও বলতে
* কেন কিনতে চাও বলতে
* দোকানে product সম্পর্কে প্রশ্ন করতে
* দাম জিজ্ঞেস করতে
* দুইটি product compare করতে
* কোনটি prefer করো বলতে
* quality, size, color, price সম্পর্কে বলতে
* দোকানে conversation করতে
* online shopping নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* bargain/discount নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* buying decision explain করতে
* 2–5 মিনিট shopping নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Shopping Speaking Formula

যেকোনো shopping topic-এর জন্য:

# **WHAT → WHY → PRICE → FEATURES → COMPARE → PREFERENCE → DECISION**

### Example:

> I'm looking for a new pair of shoes.
> I need them for everyday use.
> My budget is around 3,000 taka.
> I want something comfortable and durable.
> I've found two good options, but I prefer the second one because it has better quality.
> So, I think I'll buy that one.

---

# 🛍️ Part 2 — Common Shopping Vocabulary

### Places

> shop

> store

> shopping mall

> supermarket

> market

> online store

> department store

---

### Shopping Actions

> buy

> purchase

> choose

> compare

> check

> try on

> pay

> return

> exchange

> order

> cancel

> deliver

---

# 👕 Part 3 — Clothes Shopping

Useful vocabulary:

> shirt

> T-shirt

> pants

> jeans

> jacket

> shoes

> dress

> sweater

> hoodie

> size

> color

> design

> style

---

### Examples:

> I'm looking for a black T-shirt.

> I need a pair of jeans.

> Do you have this shirt in a larger size?

> Do you have this in blue?

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Asking About Size

Useful questions:

> What sizes do you have?

> Do you have a medium?

> Do you have a larger size?

> Do you have a smaller size?

> Can I try this on?

---

### Customer:

> Do you have this shirt in large?

### Seller:

> Yes, we do.

### Customer:

> Can I try it on?

### Seller:

> Sure. The fitting room is over there.

---

# 👟 Part 5 — Shoes

### Useful vocabulary:

> sneakers

> sandals

> boots

> formal shoes

> sports shoes

> running shoes

---

### Questions:

> What size is this?

> Do you have size 42?

> Are these comfortable?

> Are these suitable for running?

> Do you have another color?

---

# 💰 Part 6 — Asking the Price

Basic:

> How much is this?

> How much does this cost?

> What's the price?

More polite:

> How much does this one cost?

> Could you tell me how much this is?

---

### Examples:

> How much is this shirt?

> How much does this laptop cost?

> What's the price of this bag?

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

### Singular:

> How much **is** this?

### Plural:

> How much **are** these?

### Examples:

> How much is this phone?

> How much are these shoes?

---

# 💵 Part 7 — Price Expressions

> expensive

> cheap

> affordable

> reasonable

> overpriced

> good value

> worth the money

---

### Examples:

> It's quite expensive.

> It's reasonably priced.

> I think it's affordable.

> It's a little overpriced.

> It's worth the money.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Worth the Money”

Very useful expression:

> **It's worth the money.**

= দাম অনুযায়ী product ভালো।

### Example:

> It's a little expensive, but I think it's worth the money because the quality is excellent.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Discount

Useful expressions:

> discount

> sale

> special offer

> reduced price

> coupon

> promotional offer

---

### Questions:

> Is there any discount?

> Is this product on sale?

> Do you have any special offers?

> Can I get a discount?

---

# 🗣️ Part 10 — Bargaining

বিশেষ করে market/shop-এ:

### Customer:

> How much is this?

### Seller:

> It's 1,500 taka.

### Customer:

> That's a little expensive. Can you give me a discount?

### Seller:

> I can give you 100 taka off.

### Customer:

> Can you make it 1,300?

### Seller:

> Okay, 1,300 is fine.

### Customer:

> Great. I'll take it.

---

# ⭐ Useful Bargaining Expressions

> Can you give me a discount?

> Can you lower the price?

> What's your best price?

> That's a little expensive.

> Can you make it cheaper?

> I'll take it if you can give me a discount.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “I'll Take It”

দোকানে purchase করার সময়:

> **I'll take it.**

= আমি এটা নেব।

### Examples:

> This looks good. I'll take it.

> I like this one. I'll take it.

---

# ❌ যদি না কিনো:

> I'll think about it.

> I don't think I'll buy it.

> It's a little too expensive for me.

> I'll come back later.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Comparing Products

দুইটি product compare করার জন্য:

> cheaper

> more expensive

> better

> worse

> bigger

> smaller

> lighter

> heavier

> faster

> slower

> more comfortable

> more durable

> higher quality

---

### Example:

> This phone is cheaper than that one.

> This laptop is more powerful.

> This shirt is more comfortable.

> The second one has better quality.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — “Prefer A to B”

Lesson 39-এর structure এখানে আবার ব্যবহার করো।

> I prefer this one to that one.

> I prefer the black one to the blue one.

> I prefer online shopping to shopping in stores.

---

### Example:

> I prefer the second laptop to the first one because it has better performance.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “Compared to”

Advanced comparison:

> Compared to the first one, this model is lighter.

> Compared to the older model, this phone is faster.

> Compared to other stores, this shop has better prices.

---

# 📱 Part 15 — Online Shopping

Useful vocabulary:

> website

> product

> review

> rating

> customer review

> delivery

> shipping

> payment

> cash on delivery

> return policy

> seller

> order

---

### Question:

> Do you prefer online shopping or shopping in stores?

### Answer:

> I prefer online shopping because it is convenient and saves time. I can compare prices, read customer reviews, and order things from home. However, I prefer physical stores when I need to check the quality or size of a product before buying it.

🔥 Excellent answer structure:

**Preference → Reasons → Advantage → Contrast**

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Online Shopping Questions

Useful questions:

> Is delivery available?

> How long does delivery take?

> What is the delivery charge?

> Can I return the product?

> What is your return policy?

> Is cash on delivery available?

> Are there any customer reviews?

> Is this product original?

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Product Quality

Useful words:

> high quality

> low quality

> durable

> reliable

> strong

> comfortable

> lightweight

> waterproof

> genuine

> original

> fake

---

### Examples:

> I'm looking for a high-quality product.

> I want something durable.

> Is this product original?

> This phone seems reliable.

> These shoes are comfortable and lightweight.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Buying Decision

### Question:

> How do you decide what to buy?

### Answer:

> Before buying something, I usually compare the price, quality, and features. I also read customer reviews if I'm shopping online. If the product is expensive, I take more time to make a decision.

🔥 Useful structure:

# **PRICE → QUALITY → FEATURES → REVIEWS → DECISION**

---

# 💡 Part 19 — “I'm Looking For...”

Shopping-এর সবচেয়ে useful structure:

> **I'm looking for + noun**

### Examples:

> I'm looking for a new phone.

> I'm looking for a pair of shoes.

> I'm looking for a laptop.

> I'm looking for a gift for my friend.

---

### More natural:

> I'm looking for something comfortable and affordable.

> I'm looking for a laptop with good performance.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “I Need Something...”

### Structure:

> **I need something + adjective**

Examples:

> I need something comfortable.

> I need something affordable.

> I need something durable.

> I need something lightweight.

> I need something suitable for everyday use.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Shopping for Someone Else

### Question:

> What do you buy as gifts?

### Answer:

> I usually buy clothes, books, watches, or other useful things as gifts. Before buying a gift, I try to think about the person's interests and preferences.

---

### Useful:

> I'm looking for a gift for my brother.

> It's his birthday.

> He likes technology.

> Do you have anything suitable?

---

# 🗣️ Part 22 — Full Shop Conversation

### Customer:

> Excuse me. I'm looking for a pair of running shoes.

### Seller:

> Sure. What size do you need?

### Customer:

> I need size 42.

### Seller:

> We have these two models.

### Customer:

> What's the difference between them?

### Seller:

> This one is cheaper, while the other one is lighter and more durable.

### Customer:

> How much are they?

### Seller:

> The first pair is 2,500 taka, and the second pair is 3,500 taka.

### Customer:

> Can I try the second pair?

### Seller:

> Of course.

### Customer:

> They feel comfortable. Is there any discount?

### Seller:

> I can give you 10% off.

### Customer:

> Great. I'll take them.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Shopping Problems

কখনো product ভালো না হলে:

> It's damaged.

> It's the wrong size.

> The color is different.

> The product doesn't work.

> I received the wrong product.

> I'd like to return it.

> I'd like to exchange it.

---

### Example:

> I received the wrong size. I'd like to exchange it for a larger one.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Return & Exchange Conversation

### Customer:

> Excuse me. I'd like to return this shirt.

### Seller:

> What's the problem?

### Customer:

> It's the wrong size.

### Seller:

> Would you like a refund or an exchange?

### Customer:

> I'd like to exchange it for a larger size.

### Seller:

> Sure. Do you have the receipt?

### Customer:

> Yes, here it is.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Too” & “Enough”

Shopping-এর জন্য খুব useful।

### Too expensive

> This phone is too expensive.

### Cheap enough

> This phone is affordable enough for me.

### Big enough

> Is this bag big enough?

### Small enough

> Is this table small enough for my room?

---

# ⚠️ Difference

### Too expensive

= প্রয়োজনের চেয়ে বেশি দামি

> It's too expensive for me.

### Expensive but worth it

= দাম বেশি কিন্তু quality ভালো

> It's expensive, but it's worth the money.

---

# 🟢 Part 26 — Your Shopping Habits

### Question:

> How often do you go shopping?

### Answer:

> I don't go shopping very often. I usually shop when I need something. I prefer planning my purchases rather than buying things impulsively.

---

# 🔥 “Impulse Buying”

= না ভেবে হঠাৎ কিছু কিনে ফেলা।

### Example:

> I try to avoid impulse buying.

> I sometimes buy things I don't really need.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Saving Money

### Question:

> How do you save money when shopping?

### Answer:

> I usually compare prices before buying something. I also look for discounts and avoid buying things I don't really need. For expensive products, I wait a few days before making a final decision.

Excellent natural speaking answer.

---

# 🎯 Part 28 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

1. How often do you go shopping?
2. What do you usually buy?
3. What is the most expensive thing you've bought?
4. Do you prefer online shopping or shopping in stores?
5. What do you consider before buying something?
6. Do you usually compare prices?
7. Do you ask for discounts?
8. Have you ever bought something you didn't need?
9. What was the last thing you bought?
10. What would you like to buy in the future?

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Follow-up Questions

Conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়ার জন্য:

> Why do you need it?

> How much does it cost?

> Where did you buy it?

> Why did you choose that one?

> Did you compare it with other products?

> Was it worth the money?

> Are you satisfied with it?

> Would you recommend it?

> Would you buy it again?

> What would you do if it stopped working?

---

# 🧩 Part 30 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **My Shopping Habits**

> I don't go shopping very often. I usually go shopping when I need something rather than just for fun. I often buy clothes, shoes, books, and electronic products.
>
> Before buying something, I usually consider its price, quality, and usefulness. If I'm buying an expensive product, I compare different brands and read customer reviews. I also look for discounts because I don't want to spend more money than necessary.
>
> I prefer online shopping for some products because it's convenient and allows me to compare prices easily. However, when I'm buying clothes or shoes, I sometimes prefer physical stores because I can check the size and quality myself.
>
> I try to avoid impulse buying. If I really want something expensive, I usually wait a few days before deciding. This helps me make better decisions and save money.

---

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → WHY → PRICE → QUALITY → COMPARE → PREFERENCE → DECISION**

### Example:

> **I'm looking for a new phone.**
> **I need it because my current phone is quite old.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 41

# ✈️ Travel & Transportation

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কোথায় যাবে → কেন যাবে → কীভাবে যাবে → কার সাথে যাবে → কতদিন থাকবে → কী করবে → অভিজ্ঞতা কেমন ছিল → future travel plan কী।

Lesson 40-এ তুমি শিখেছ **Shopping & Buying Things**। আজ আমরা সেই একই speaking pattern ব্যবহার করে **Travel & Transportation** নিয়ে কথা বলব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 41-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের travel experience বলতে
* কোথাও যাওয়ার plan বলতে
* destination describe করতে
* transportation নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* bus/train/plane নিয়ে প্রশ্ন করতে
* travel-এর reason বলতে
* hotel ও journey নিয়ে basic conversation করতে
* past trip describe করতে
* future travel plan বলতে
* 2–5 মিনিট travel নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Travel Speaking Formula

যেকোনো travel topic-এর জন্য:

# **WHERE → WHY → WHEN → HOW → WITH WHOM → WHAT → EXPERIENCE → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I'd like to visit Cox's Bazar.
> I'd like to go there because I love beaches.
> I'm hoping to go during my next vacation.
> I'll probably travel by bus.
> I may go with some friends.
> I'd like to spend a few days there and explore the area.
> I think it would be a relaxing experience.
> I'd definitely like to visit again in the future.

---

# 🌍 Part 2 — Travel Vocabulary

### Common words

> travel — ভ্রমণ করা

> trip — ভ্রমণ/সফর

> journey — যাত্রা

> vacation — ছুটি/অবকাশ

> holiday — ছুটি

> destination — গন্তব্য

> tourist — পর্যটক

> tourist attraction — দর্শনীয় স্থান

> sightseeing — দর্শনীয় স্থান ঘোরা

> luggage — লাগেজ

> ticket — টিকিট

> passport — পাসপোর্ট

> hotel — হোটেল

> reservation — বুকিং

---

# 🧠 Trip vs Journey vs Travel

এগুলো খুব important।

### Travel

সাধারণভাবে ভ্রমণ:

> I love to travel.

### Trip

একটি নির্দিষ্ট ভ্রমণ:

> I went on a trip to Cox's Bazar.

### Journey

এক জায়গা থেকে অন্য জায়গায় যাওয়ার যাত্রা:

> The journey took five hours.

### সহজে মনে রাখো:

> **Travel = general activity**
> **Trip = specific visit**
> **Journey = the process of going**

---

# 🏖️ Part 3 — Destination

Destination সম্পর্কে বলার জন্য:

> beach

> mountain

> village

> city

> island

> forest

> historical place

> tourist attraction

> natural beauty

---

### Examples:

> Cox's Bazar is a popular tourist destination.

> Sylhet is famous for its natural beauty.

> I enjoy visiting historical places.

> I would love to visit the mountains.

---

# 🇧🇩 Part 4 — Bangladesh Travel

Speaking practice-এর জন্য Bangladesh-এর কিছু destination:

> Cox's Bazar

> Sylhet

> Sajek

> Bandarban

> Rangamati

> Sundarbans

> Saint Martin's Island

---

### Example:

> One place I'd really like to visit is Bandarban because of its mountains and natural beauty.

---

# 🚗 Part 5 — Transportation

### Road

> bus

> car

> taxi

> CNG

> rickshaw

> motorcycle

> train

---

### Air

> airplane

> flight

> airport

---

### Water

> boat

> launch

> ferry

> ship

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — “How Do You Travel?”

### Question:

> How do you usually travel?

### Answer:

> I usually travel by bus.

> I usually travel by train when I have enough time.

> I prefer traveling by train because it is comfortable.

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

Transportation-এর আগে সাধারণত:

> **by + transport**

### Correct:

> by bus

> by train

> by car

> by plane

> by boat

### ❌

> by the bus

যদি general transportation বোঝাও।

---

# 🧠 But...

যদি নির্দিষ্ট vehicle বোঝাও:

> I went **on the bus**.

> I left my bag **in the car**.

> I was **on the train**.

---

# ✈️ Part 7 — Plane Travel

Useful vocabulary:

> airport

> flight

> boarding pass

> gate

> luggage

> passport

> security check

> departure

> arrival

> take off

> land

---

### Examples:

> I arrived at the airport two hours before my flight.

> I checked in and went through security.

> The plane took off on time.

> We landed safely.

---

# 🚆 Part 8 — Train Travel

Useful expressions:

> train station

> platform

> ticket

> seat

> carriage

> departure

> arrival

> railway station

---

### Examples:

> I bought my ticket online.

> The train left at 8 a.m.

> I waited on the platform.

> The journey was comfortable.

---

# 🚌 Part 9 — Bus Travel

Useful:

> bus station

> bus stop

> ticket counter

> seat

> driver

> traffic

> highway

---

### Example:

> I usually take a bus for short trips because it's affordable and convenient.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Why Do You Travel?

এই question খুব important।

### Question:

> Why do you like traveling?

### Short:

> I like traveling because it is fun.

### Intermediate:

> I like traveling because it helps me relax and experience new places.

### Advanced:

> I enjoy traveling because it gives me a break from my daily routine. It also allows me to experience different cultures, meet new people, and see places I've never visited before.

---

# 🔥 Useful Reasons

> to relax

> to explore new places

> to experience different cultures

> to meet new people

> to learn new things

> to spend time with family

> to spend time with friends

> to escape from daily routine

> to take beautiful photos

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Who Do You Travel With?

### Examples:

> I usually travel with my family.

> I prefer traveling with friends.

> Sometimes I travel alone.

> I'd like to travel with my friends next time.

---

### Question:

> Who would you like to travel with?

### Answer:

> I'd like to travel with a few close friends because we enjoy exploring new places together.

---

# 🗓️ Part 12 — When Do You Travel?

Useful expressions:

> during my vacation

> during holidays

> on weekends

> during the winter

> during the summer

> during the Eid holidays

> whenever I get the chance

---

### Examples:

> I usually travel during holidays.

> I prefer traveling during the winter because the weather is more comfortable.

---

# ⏳ Part 13 — How Long?

### Question:

> How long are you going to stay?

### Answers:

> I'll stay for three days.

> I'm planning to stay for a week.

> I stayed there for five days.

> We spent three nights there.

---

# 🧠 Important:

### for + duration

> for three days

> for two weeks

> for a month

### since + starting point

> since Monday

> since last year

Travel-এর ক্ষেত্রে **for** বেশি common:

> I stayed there **for five days.**

---

# 🏨 Part 14 — Hotel Vocabulary

> hotel

> room

> reservation

> booking

> reception

> check-in

> check-out

> single room

> double room

> room service

> facilities

---

# 🗣️ Part 15 — Hotel Conversation

### Guest:

> I'd like to check in.

### Receptionist:

> Certainly. Do you have a reservation?

### Guest:

> Yes. I booked a double room.

### Receptionist:

> May I have your name, please?

### Guest:

> It's Atiar Rahman.

### Receptionist:

> How many nights will you be staying?

### Guest:

> I'll be staying for three nights.

### Receptionist:

> Here's your room key.

### Guest:

> Thank you.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Asking for Directions

Travel-এ directions জানা খুব useful।

> Excuse me, how can I get to the museum?

> Where is the nearest bus station?

> Is it far from here?

> How long does it take to get there?

> Can I walk there?

> Which bus should I take?

---

### Example:

> Excuse me, how can I get to the railway station?

> You can take a bus or a taxi. It's about fifteen minutes from here.

---

# 🗺️ Part 17 — Giving Directions

Useful words:

> go straight

> turn left

> turn right

> go past

> next to

> opposite

> near

> between

> behind

> in front of

---

### Example:

> Go straight for about five minutes, then turn left. The hotel is next to the bank.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Describe a Past Trip

এখানে Lesson 31-এর **Past Experiences** আবার ব্যবহার করব।

### Question:

> Tell me about a trip you took.

### Formula:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → TRANSPORT → ACTIVITIES → FEELING**

### Example:

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with a few friends. We traveled there by bus and stayed for three days. We visited the beach, tried different foods, and took lots of photos. The journey was a little tiring, but the trip was really enjoyable. I especially loved watching the sunset at the beach.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Past Tense for Travel

Travel experience describe করতে:

> I went...

> I visited...

> I stayed...

> I traveled...

> I saw...

> I tried...

> I enjoyed...

> I took...

> We spent...

---

### Example:

> We visited several tourist attractions.

> We stayed in a small hotel.

> I took many photos.

> We tried some local food.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Have You Ever...?”

Travel speaking-এর খুব common question।

### Questions:

> Have you ever traveled alone?

> Have you ever traveled abroad?

> Have you ever missed a flight?

> Have you ever lost your luggage?

> Have you ever stayed in a hotel?

---

### Answer:

> Yes, I have.

> No, I haven't.

### Expand:

> Yes, I have traveled alone once. It was a little challenging at first, but I really enjoyed the experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Past Simple vs Present Perfect

### Specific past time:

> I went to Cox's Bazar **last year**.

→ Past Simple

### Life experience:

> I have been to Cox's Bazar.

→ Present Perfect

---

### Compare:

> I went there last December.

> I've been there several times.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Travel Problems

Travel সবসময় perfect হয় না।

Useful problems:

> missed the bus

> missed the train

> flight delay

> traffic jam

> lost luggage

> lost passport

> bad weather

> canceled flight

> wrong direction

> hotel problem

---

### Example:

> Our flight was delayed because of bad weather.

> I once missed my bus because I arrived late.

> We got stuck in a traffic jam.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Got Stuck”

Very useful natural expression:

> **get stuck in traffic**

### Example:

> We got stuck in traffic for two hours.

= দুই ঘণ্টা traffic-এ আটকে ছিলাম।

---

# 🌧️ Part 24 — Weather & Travel

Travel-এর সাথে weather connect করতে পারো:

> The weather was perfect.

> It was sunny.

> It was raining heavily.

> It was quite cold.

> It was hot and humid.

> The weather was pleasant.

---

### Example:

> The weather was pleasant, so we spent most of the day outside.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Future Travel Plan

### Question:

> Where would you like to travel in the future?

### Beginner:

> I'd like to visit Sylhet.

### Intermediate:

> I'd like to visit Sylhet because I've heard that it has beautiful natural scenery.

### Advanced:

> I'd love to visit Sylhet in the future. I've heard that the area is famous for its natural beauty, tea gardens, and peaceful environment. I'd probably go with a few friends and spend several days exploring the area.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Future Travel Structures

### Plan:

> I'm planning to visit...

### Intention:

> I'd like to visit...

### Possibility:

> I might visit...

### Strong desire:

> I'd love to visit...

### Future possibility:

> Hopefully, I'll be able to visit...

---

### Examples:

> I'm planning to visit Cox's Bazar next year.

> I'd love to visit Japan someday.

> I might travel to Sylhet during my next vacation.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — “Someday”

**Someday = ভবিষ্যতে কোনো একদিন**

> I'd like to visit Europe someday.

> I hope to travel abroad someday.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Dream Destination

### Question:

> What is your dream travel destination?

### Answer:

> My dream destination is Japan. I'd love to visit Japan because I'm interested in its culture, technology, food, and natural beauty. I'd like to visit Tokyo, Kyoto, and Mount Fuji. Hopefully, I'll be able to go there someday.

---

# 🗣️ Part 29 — Full Travel Conversation

### A:

> Do you enjoy traveling?

### B:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling.

### A:

> Why do you like it?

### B:

> It helps me relax and explore new places.

### A:

> What's the best place you've visited?

### B:

> One of the best places I've visited is Cox's Bazar.

### A:

> Who did you go with?

### B:

> I went with a few friends.

### A:

> How did you get there?

### B:

> We traveled by bus.

### A:

> How long did you stay?

### B:

> We stayed for three days.

### A:

> What did you do there?

### B:

> We visited the beach, tried local food, and took lots of photos.

### A:

> Where would you like to go next?

### B:

> I'd love to visit Sylhet next because I'd like to explore its natural beauty.

---

# 🎯 Part 30 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

1. Do you enjoy traveling?
2. What is the best place you've visited?
3. Where did you go on your last trip?
4. Who did you go with?
5. How did you travel there?
6. How long did you stay?
7. What did you do there?
8. What was the best part of the trip?
9. Have you ever traveled alone?
10. Where would you like to travel in the future?

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why did you go there?

> Who did you go with?

> How did you get there?

> How long did it take?

> Where did you stay?

> What did you do there?

> What did you eat?

> What was the weather like?

> What did you enjoy most?

> Would you go there again?

---

# 🧩 Part 32 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **A Memorable Trip**

> One of the most memorable trips I've had was a trip to Cox's Bazar with some of my friends. We traveled there by bus and stayed for three days. Although the journey was quite long, we had a lot of fun during the trip.
>
> After arriving, we checked into our hotel and went to the beach. We spent a lot of time walking along the beach, taking photos, and enjoying the scenery. We also tried some local food and visited a few nearby places.
>
> The best part of the trip was watching the sunset together. The weather was pleasant, and the atmosphere was very peaceful. I really enjoyed the trip because it gave me a break from my usual routine and allowed me to spend quality time with my friends.
>
> I'd definitely like to visit Cox's Bazar again, although next time I'd also like to explore some other parts of Bangladesh.

---

2. Where did you go on your last trip?

5. How long did you stay?
6. Where did you stay?
7. What did you do there?
8. What was the best part of the trip?
9. Have you ever traveled alone?
10. Where would you like to travel in the future?

নিজে **10টি Travel-related questions** তৈরি করো।

এই words ব্যবহার করো:

> Where...?

> Why...?

> When...?

> How...?

> Who...?

> How long...?

> What did...?

> Have you ever...?

> Would you like...?

> What would...?

---

# 🎤 Task 3 — 5-Minute Speaking

Topic:

# **“A Memorable Trip”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **When → Where → Who → Transportation → Hotel → Activities → Food → Best Moment → Feeling → Future**

Notes না দেখে বলবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 41 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHERE → WHY → WHEN → HOW → WITH WHOM → WHAT → EXPERIENCE → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I'd like to visit Sylhet.**
> **I'd like to go there because I love natural scenery.**
> **I'm hoping to go during my next vacation.**
> **I'll probably travel by train.**
> **I may go with some friends.**
> **I'd like to visit the tea gardens and explore the local area.**
> **I think it would be a relaxing experience.**
> **I'd definitely like to visit again in the future.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 42

# 🎓 Work, Study & Career

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** তুমি কী পড়ো/কাজ করো → কেন করো → কী শিখছ → কী ভালো লাগে → কী সমস্যা → future career goal কী।

Lesson 41-এর **Travel**-এর মতো এখানেও আমরা একইভাবে **What → Why → Details → Experience → Future** pattern ব্যবহার করব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 42-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের study সম্পর্কে বলতে
* নিজের subject/major explain করতে
* কেন এই subject বেছে নিয়েছ বলতে
* daily study routine বলতে
* নিজের skills সম্পর্কে বলতে
* কাজ/চাকরি সম্পর্কে বলতে
* নিজের strengths বলতে
* career goal বলতে
* dream job explain করতে
* future plan নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* interview-style questions-এর উত্তর দিতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Work & Study Speaking Formula

যেকোনো study/career topic-এর জন্য:

# **WHAT → WHERE → WHY → WHAT YOU DO → SKILLS → CHALLENGES → GOAL → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I study Computer Science and Engineering.
> I chose this subject because I'm interested in technology and software development.
> I spend a lot of time learning programming and building projects.
> I'm currently improving my Python, Django, and React skills.
> Sometimes programming can be challenging, but I enjoy solving problems.
> My goal is to become a skilled full-stack developer.

---

# 🎓 Part 2 — Study Vocabulary

> subject — বিষয়

> course — কোর্স

> degree — ডিগ্রি

> university — বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়

> college — কলেজ

> semester — সেমিস্টার

> class — ক্লাস

> lecture — লেকচার

> assignment — অ্যাসাইনমেন্ট

> exam — পরীক্ষা

> project — প্রজেক্ট

> presentation — উপস্থাপনা

> research — গবেষণা

> skill — দক্ষতা

> knowledge — জ্ঞান

> experience — অভিজ্ঞতা

---

# 💻 Part 3 — Talking About Your Subject

### Question:

> What do you study?

### Short:

> I study Computer Science and Engineering.

### Better:

> I study Computer Science and Engineering. I'm particularly interested in software development and web technologies.

### Advanced:

> I'm studying Computer Science and Engineering, with a particular interest in software development and backend technologies. I enjoy learning how applications are designed, developed, and deployed.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — “What are you studying?”

এই question-এর উত্তর:

> I'm studying...

### Examples:

> I'm studying Computer Science.

> I'm studying business administration.

> I'm studying English.

> I'm studying programming.

---

# ⚠️ Common Mistake

### ❌

> I am study Computer Science.

### ✅

> I am studying Computer Science.

অথবা:

> I study Computer Science.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Why Did You Choose Your Subject?

### Question:

> Why did you choose your major?

### Answer:

> I chose Computer Science because I'm interested in technology.

আরও natural:

> I chose Computer Science because I enjoy technology, programming, and problem-solving. I also believe it offers many career opportunities.

### Advanced:

> I chose Computer Science because I've always been interested in technology and problem-solving. I enjoy creating things with code, and I believe this field offers a lot of opportunities for continuous learning and professional growth.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “I'm Interested in...”

Very useful structure:

> **I'm interested in + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm interested in web development.

> I'm interested in artificial intelligence.

> I'm interested in learning Python.

> I'm interested in building software.

### ❌

> I'm interested to learn Python.

### ✅

> I'm interested in learning Python.

---

# 🧑‍💻 Part 7 — Talking About Work

### Question:

> What do you do?

এটা খুব common question।

### Student:

> I'm a student.

### Employee:

> I work as a software developer.

### Job seeker:

> I'm currently looking for a job in software development.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — “What Do You Do?”

এই question-এর অর্থ সবসময় “এখন কী করছ?” নয়।

Conversation-এ:

> **What do you do?**

অনেক সময় অর্থ:

> তুমি কী কাজ/পেশা করো?

### Example:

**A:** What do you do?

**B:** I'm a software developer.

---

# 💻 Part 9 — Talking About Your Job

### Question:

> What do you do at work?

### Answer:

> I work on web applications and APIs.

আরও detailed:

> I mainly work on backend development. I build APIs, work with databases, fix bugs, and improve application performance.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Job Responsibilities

Useful vocabulary:

> develop

> design

> build

> test

> maintain

> manage

> analyze

> solve problems

> fix bugs

> communicate

> collaborate

> deploy

---

### Examples:

> I develop web applications.

> I build REST APIs.

> I work with databases.

> I fix bugs.

> I test new features.

> I collaborate with other developers.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “I’m Responsible For...”

খুব useful professional expression:

> **I'm responsible for + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm responsible for developing APIs.

> I'm responsible for maintaining the database.

> I'm responsible for testing new features.

### ❌

> I'm responsible to develop APIs.

### ✅

> I'm responsible for developing APIs.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Daily Study Routine

### Question:

> What is your study routine like?

### Answer:

> I usually study in the morning and evening. I spend some time learning theory and then practice what I've learned by solving problems or building small projects.

---

### Useful expressions:

> I usually...

> I normally...

> I spend time...

> I try to...

> I focus on...

> I practice...

---

# 💡 Part 13 — “I Spend Time...”

### Structure:

> **spend + time + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I spend time practicing English.

> I spend two hours learning programming.

> I spend a lot of time reading technical articles.

### ❌

> I spend time to learn English.

### ✅

> I spend time learning English.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Skills

Useful skill categories:

### Technical Skills

> programming

> web development

> database management

> problem-solving

> debugging

> software development

---

### Soft Skills

> communication

> teamwork

> leadership

> time management

> adaptability

> creativity

> critical thinking

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — “I'm Good At...”

### Structure:

> **I'm good at + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm good at problem-solving.

> I'm good at programming.

> I'm good at explaining technical concepts.

---

### “I'm Improving...”

> I'm improving my communication skills.

> I'm improving my English speaking skills.

> I'm improving my programming skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Strengths

### Question:

> What are your strengths?

### Answer:

> I think my main strengths are problem-solving, consistency, and willingness to learn.

### Expanded:

> One of my main strengths is that I'm willing to learn new things. I also enjoy solving problems and don't easily give up when I face a difficult task.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Weaknesses

Interview-এ common question:

> What is your weakness?

Don't say:

> I have no weaknesses.

Better:

> Sometimes I spend too much time trying to make my work perfect.

Then improvement:

> However, I'm learning to manage my time better and focus on priorities.

### Formula:

# **Weakness → Impact → Improvement**

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Challenges

### Question:

> What is the most difficult part of your studies?

### Answer:

> Sometimes difficult technical topics can be challenging. However, I try to understand them by practicing regularly and working on practical projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — “I'm Struggling With...”

Natural expression:

> **I'm struggling with + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm struggling with time management.

> I'm struggling with speaking English fluently.

> I'm struggling with understanding advanced programming concepts.

---

### But:

> I'm working on it.

> I'm trying to improve.

> I'm getting better gradually.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Career Goal

### Question:

> What is your career goal?

### Short:

> My goal is to become a software developer.

### Better:

> My goal is to become a skilled full-stack developer and work on real-world software projects.

### Advanced:

> My long-term goal is to become a highly skilled full-stack developer. I want to build scalable applications, work with experienced developers, and continuously improve my technical and communication skills.

---

# 🎯 Part 21 — Short-Term vs Long-Term Goals

### Short-term goal

> My short-term goal is to improve my technical skills and gain practical experience.

### Long-term goal

> My long-term goal is to become an experienced software engineer and work on large-scale projects.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — “I Want to...”

### Examples:

> I want to improve my English.

> I want to gain practical experience.

> I want to work for a good company.

> I want to become a better programmer.

---

# 🔥 More Advanced

Instead of always saying **I want to**:

> I'd like to...

> I hope to...

> I'm planning to...

> I'm aiming to...

> My goal is to...

---

### Example:

> I'm aiming to become a professional backend developer.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Future Career Plan

### Question:

> What are your plans for the future?

### Answer:

> I'm planning to improve my technical skills, gain practical experience, and find a good job. In the long term, I'd like to become an experienced software engineer.

---

# 💼 Part 24 — Job Interview Questions

আজকের lesson-এর সবচেয়ে important part।

### 1.

> Tell me about yourself.

### 2.

> What do you do?

### 3.

> What did you study?

### 4.

> Why did you choose this field?

### 5.

> What are your strengths?

### 6.

> What is your weakness?

### 7.

> What skills do you have?

### 8.

> What are your career goals?

### 9.

> Why should we hire you?

### 10.

> Where do you see yourself in five years?

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — “Tell Me About Yourself”

একটি simple structure:

# **PRESENT → PAST → SKILLS → GOAL**

### Example:

> I'm a Computer Science and Engineering graduate with a strong interest in software development. I've been learning and practicing Python, Django, REST APIs, and React. I've also worked on several practical projects, which helped me improve my problem-solving and development skills. Currently, I'm looking for opportunities where I can gain professional experience and grow as a software developer.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “Why Should We Hire You?”

Simple formula:

# **SKILLS → EXPERIENCE → ATTITUDE → VALUE**

### Answer:

> I believe you should hire me because I have a strong foundation in software development and I'm willing to learn quickly. I've worked on practical projects and have experience with technologies such as Python, Django, REST APIs, and React. I'm responsible, hardworking, and interested in continuously improving my skills.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — “Where Do You See Yourself in 5 Years?”

### Answer:

> In five years, I hope to be an experienced software engineer with strong technical and communication skills. I'd like to work on challenging projects, take more responsibility, and contribute to my team and organization.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Talking About Experience

Useful phrases:

> I have experience in...

> I've worked on...

> I've worked with...

> I've built...

> I've developed...

> I've learned...

---

### Examples:

> I have experience in backend development.

> I've worked with Django and REST APIs.

> I've built several web applications.

> I've developed APIs for different projects.

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

### Experience in + noun

> I have experience **in web development.**

### Experience with + technology

> I have experience **with Django.**

### Experience + verb-ing

> I have experience **building APIs.**

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Talking About Projects

### Question:

> Tell me about a project you've worked on.

### Formula:

# **PROJECT → PURPOSE → TECHNOLOGY → YOUR ROLE → CHALLENGE → RESULT**

### Example:

> I worked on a hotel management system. The goal was to make it easier to manage rooms, bookings, and customers. I used Django and Django REST Framework for the backend and React for the frontend. I worked mainly on the backend and API development. One challenge was designing the database and handling different types of relationships. The project helped me improve my backend development and problem-solving skills.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **My Study & Career**

> I'm a Computer Science and Engineering graduate, and I'm interested in software development. I chose Computer Science because I've always been interested in technology, programming, and problem-solving.
>
> During my studies, I learned different areas of computer science, but I became particularly interested in web development. I've been working with Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and databases. I've also built several practical projects, which helped me understand how real-world applications work.
>
> One of my strengths is that I'm willing to learn new technologies. I also enjoy solving problems and working on practical projects. However, I'm still working on improving my communication and English speaking skills.
>
> My short-term goal is to gain professional experience and become a better developer. My long-term goal is to become a skilled full-stack software engineer who can build reliable and scalable applications. I'm also planning to continue learning new technologies and improving my problem-solving skills.

---

# 🎯 Part 31 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের সবচেয়ে important questions:

1. What do you study or what do you do?
2. Why did you choose your field?
3. What do you enjoy most about your studies or work?
4. What skills are you currently learning?
5. What are your strengths?
6. What is one weakness you're trying to improve?
7. What is the most difficult part of your studies or job?
8. Tell me about a project you've worked on.
9. What is your short-term career goal?
10. Where do you see yourself in five years?

---

# 🔄 Part 32 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why did you choose it?

> When did you start learning it?

> What do you enjoy about it?

> What is difficult about it?

> How are you improving?

> What projects have you worked on?

> What did you learn from that project?

> What skills do you want to develop?

> What kind of job do you want?

> Why is that your goal?

---

প্রতিটি question-এর **3–5 sentences** answer দাও:

1. What do you study or what do you do?
2. Why did you choose your field?
3. What skills are you learning now?
4. What are you good at?
5. What is one weakness you're trying to improve?
6. What is your biggest study/work challenge?
7. Tell me about one project you've worked on.
8. What is your short-term goal?
9. What is your long-term goal?
10. Where do you see yourself in five years?

এই question words ব্যবহার করো:

> What...?

> Why...?

> Where...?

> When...?

> How...?

> Which...?

> What kind of...?

> Have you ever...?

> What are you planning to...?

> Where do you see...?

Topic:

# **“My Study, Skills & Career Goal”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **What I Study → Why I Chose It → What I Learn → My Skills → My Strengths → My Weakness → My Project → My Short-Term Goal → My Long-Term Goal**

প্রথমে **notes দেখে** বলবে → তারপর **notes ছাড়া** বলবে।

# 🏆 Lesson 42 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **PRESENT → PAST → SKILLS → EXPERIENCE → CHALLENGE → GOAL → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I'm a Computer Science graduate.**
> **I chose this field because I enjoy technology and programming.**
> **I'm currently improving my Python, Django, and React skills.**
> **I've worked on several practical projects.**
> **Sometimes I struggle with difficult technical problems, but I keep practicing.**
> **My short-term goal is to gain professional experience.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 43

# 👥 Friends & Social Life

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** বন্ধু কে → কীভাবে পরিচয় → কী করো একসাথে → friendship কেন important → ভালো বন্ধুর qualities → social life → disagreement → friendship-এর future।

Lesson 42-এ তুমি **Work, Study & Career** শিখেছ। আজ আমরা সেই একই speaking pattern ব্যবহার করে **Friends & Social Life** নিয়ে natural conversation শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 43-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের friends সম্পর্কে বলতে
* best friend describe করতে
* কীভাবে বন্ধুত্ব হয়েছে বলতে
* friends-এর সাথে কী করো বলতে
* friendship কেন important বলতে
* good friend-এর qualities বলতে
* social life describe করতে
* disagreement নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* friend-কে advice দিতে
* friendship-এর past experience বলতে
* future social plans বলতে
* 2–5 মিনিট friends & social life নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Friends Speaking Formula

যেকোনো friendship topic-এর জন্য:

# **WHO → HOW → WHAT → WHY → EXPERIENCE → FEELING → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I have a few close friends.
> I met most of them at university.
> We usually spend time together, talk about our studies, and sometimes go out.
> I value them because they are supportive and honest.
> We've been through some difficult situations together.
> I feel comfortable talking to them about personal problems.
> I hope we'll stay close even when our lives become busier.

---

# 👥 Part 2 — Friends Vocabulary

> friend — বন্ধু

> best friend — সবচেয়ে কাছের বন্ধু

> close friend — ঘনিষ্ঠ বন্ধু

> classmate — সহপাঠী

> roommate — একই room-এ থাকা ব্যক্তি

> colleague — সহকর্মী

> childhood friend — শৈশবের বন্ধু

> old friend — পুরোনো বন্ধু

> acquaintance — পরিচিত ব্যক্তি

> friendship — বন্ধুত্ব

> social life — সামাজিক জীবন

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Types of Friends

### Close friend

> He is one of my closest friends.

### Best friend

> She is my best friend.

### Childhood friend

> We've been friends since childhood.

### University friend

> I met him at university.

### Online friend

> We first met through an online community.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “How Did You Meet?”

### Question:

> How did you meet your best friend?

### Answer:

> I met my best friend at university.

আরও natural:

> I met my best friend when we were studying at university. We were in the same class, and we gradually became close friends.

---

# 🧠 Useful Structures

> We first met...

> I met him/her when...

> We became friends because...

> We've known each other for...

---

### Examples:

> We first met in university.

> We became friends because we had similar interests.

> We've known each other for several years.

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

### ❌

> I know him since 2022.

### ✅

> I've known him since 2022.

কারণ **since + starting point** এবং relationship এখনো continue করছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “How Long Have You Known?”

### Question:

> How long have you known your best friend?

### Answer:

> I've known him for five years.

### Since:

> I've known him since university.

### Remember:

> **for + period**

> for five years

> for two months

### **since + starting point**

> since 2022

> since university

---

# 🗣️ Part 6 — What Do You Do Together?

### Question:

> What do you usually do with your friends?

### Answer:

> We usually talk, eat together, and hang out.

আরও detailed:

> We usually hang out in our free time. Sometimes we go to restaurants, watch movies, play games, or simply sit together and talk.

---

# 🎮 Useful Activities

> hang out

> watch movies

> play games

> play sports

> go shopping

> go to restaurants

> travel

> study together

> discuss ideas

> have conversations

> take photos

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “Hang Out”

**Hang out = একসাথে casualভাবে সময় কাটানো**

### Examples:

> I like hanging out with my friends.

> We usually hang out on weekends.

> We sometimes hang out at a café.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Why Is Friendship Important?

### Question:

> Why are friends important to you?

### Simple:

> Friends are important because they support me.

### Intermediate:

> Friends are important because they provide emotional support and make life more enjoyable.

### Advanced:

> I think good friends are important because they give us emotional support, help us during difficult times, and make our lives more enjoyable. They also give us different perspectives when we're facing a problem.

---

# ❤️ Part 9 — Good Friend Qualities

Useful vocabulary:

> honest — সৎ

> loyal — বিশ্বস্ত

> supportive — সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ

> kind — দয়ালু

> caring — যত্নশীল

> trustworthy — বিশ্বাসযোগ্য

> understanding — বোঝার ক্ষমতাসম্পন্ন

> respectful — সম্মানজনক

> reliable — নির্ভরযোগ্য

> helpful — সহায়ক

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “A Good Friend Should...”

### Examples:

> A good friend should be honest.

> A good friend should be supportive.

> A good friend should respect your opinions.

> A good friend should be there when you need help.

---

# 🔥 Better Answer

### Question:

> What makes someone a good friend?

### Answer:

> I think a good friend should be honest, trustworthy, and supportive. A good friend should listen without judging and be there during difficult times. I also think mutual respect is very important in a friendship.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Describe Your Best Friend

Formula:

# **WHO → PERSONALITY → INTERESTS → RELATIONSHIP → WHY**

### Example:

> My best friend is a very friendly and supportive person. He is honest, responsible, and easy to talk to. We have similar interests, especially technology and movies. We've known each other for several years, and I can always talk to him when I have a problem. That's why I really value our friendship.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Personality Vocabulary

Friend describe করার জন্য:

> friendly

> outgoing

> quiet

> funny

> serious

> responsible

> hardworking

> intelligent

> creative

> patient

> confident

> helpful

> talkative

> shy

---

### Examples:

> He is very outgoing.

> She is quiet but friendly.

> My friend has a great sense of humor.

> He's very reliable.

---

# 😂 Part 13 — Sense of Humor

**sense of humor = হাস্যরসের বোধ**

> My best friend has a great sense of humor.

> I like people who have a good sense of humor.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Social Life

### Question:

> How would you describe your social life?

### Beginner:

> My social life is quite simple.

### Intermediate:

> My social life is fairly active. I usually spend time with my friends on weekends.

### Advanced:

> I'd describe my social life as fairly balanced. I enjoy spending time with close friends, but I also need some time alone to focus on my studies, work, and personal goals.

🔥 এখানে **balanced** ব্যবহার করা হয়েছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Social vs Alone

### Question:

> Do you prefer spending time alone or with friends?

### Answer:

> It depends. I enjoy spending time with friends because it's fun and relaxing, but I also need some alone time to focus on my work and think about my goals.

### Useful phrase:

> **It depends.**

এটি opinion answer-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “I'm More of a...”

Natural speaking:

> I'm more of an introvert.

> I'm more of an extrovert.

> I'm more of a quiet person.

> I'm more of a social person.

---

### Example:

> I'm more of a quiet person, so I prefer spending time with a few close friends rather than being in a large group.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Group of Friends

### Question:

> Do you have a large group of friends?

### Answer:

> I know many people, but I only have a few close friends. I prefer having a small group of friends because I can build deeper relationships with them.

🔥 Useful distinction:

> **many people I know**

vs

> **a few close friends**

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Friendship & Trust

### Question:

> What is the most important thing in a friendship?

### Answer:

> I think trust is one of the most important things in a friendship. Without trust, it's difficult to build a strong and lasting relationship.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Trust Expressions

> trust someone

> trust each other

> keep a secret

> tell the truth

> be honest

> keep your promise

---

### Examples:

> I trust my best friend.

> We trust each other.

> A good friend should keep your secrets.

> You should always be honest with your friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Helping Friends

### Question:

> How do you help your friends?

### Answer:

> I try to help my friends whenever I can. If they have a problem, I listen to them and try to give useful advice. Sometimes, simply being there for someone can make a big difference.

---

# 🔥 Useful Expressions

> help someone out

> give advice

> listen to someone

> support someone

> be there for someone

> cheer someone up

---

### Examples:

> I always try to help my friends out.

> I listen when my friends need someone to talk to.

> I try to cheer my friends up when they're feeling down.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Giving Advice to a Friend

### Situation:

Your friend is stressed about exams.

### You can say:

> You should take a short break.

> You shouldn't study all night.

> Why don't you take some rest?

> I think you should make a study plan.

> If I were you, I'd try to stay calm.

---

# 🔥 “If I Were You...”

Very useful intermediate/advanced structure:

> **If I were you, I'd + verb**

### Examples:

> If I were you, I'd talk to him.

> If I were you, I'd apologize.

> If I were you, I'd take some rest.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Disagreement With Friends

Friendship-এ disagreement হতে পারে।

### Question:

> Have you ever disagreed with a friend?

### Answer:

> Yes, I've disagreed with my friends a few times. Usually, the disagreements are about small things. We try to talk about the problem calmly and understand each other's point of view.

---

# 🔥 Useful Expressions

> We had a disagreement.

> We didn't agree on something.

> We had different opinions.

> We talked about it.

> We sorted it out.

> We apologized to each other.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Sort It Out”

**sort something out = সমস্যা সমাধান করা**

### Example:

> We had an argument, but we talked about it and sorted it out.

---

# 🗣️ Part 24 — Friendship Conversation

### A:

> Do you have a best friend?

### B:

> Yes, I do.

### A:

> How did you meet?

### B:

> We met at university.

### A:

> How long have you known each other?

### B:

> We've known each other for several years.

### A:

> What do you usually do together?

### B:

> We usually hang out, watch movies, and talk about our work and studies.

### A:

> What do you like about your friend?

### B:

> He's very supportive and honest. I can always talk to him when I have a problem.

### A:

> Have you ever had an argument?

### B:

> Yes, a few times. But we usually talk about the problem and sort it out.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Social Media & Friendship

এটি Lesson 24-এর **Social Media** topic-এর সাথে connection করছে।

### Question:

> Do you keep in touch with your friends online?

### Answer:

> Yes. I usually keep in touch with my friends through messaging apps and social media. We send messages, share interesting things, and sometimes have video calls.

---

# 🔥 “Keep in Touch”

= যোগাযোগ রাখা

### Examples:

> I keep in touch with my school friends.

> We still keep in touch even though we live in different places.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Long-Distance Friendship

### Question:

> Can friendship remain strong when people live far apart?

### Answer:

> Yes, I think it can. Technology makes it much easier to stay connected. As long as both people make an effort to communicate regularly, distance doesn't necessarily destroy a friendship.

🔥 Advanced expression:

> **make an effort**

= চেষ্টা করা

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Childhood Friends

### Question:

> Do you still talk to your childhood friends?

### Answer:

> I still keep in touch with some of my childhood friends. Although we don't meet very often anymore, we sometimes message each other and talk about old memories.

---

# 🔥 Useful Expression

> **old memories**

> **childhood memories**

> **remember the good old days**

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Best Memory With Friends

### Question:

> What is your best memory with your friends?

### Formula:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → WHAT → FEELING → WHY**

### Example:

> One of my best memories with my friends was a trip we took together. We spent several days exploring a new place, trying different foods, and taking photos. We laughed a lot and had a great time. I remember it because everyone was relaxed and happy.

---

# 🎯 Part 29 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

1. Do you have a best friend?
2. How did you meet your best friend?
3. How long have you known each other?
4. What do you usually do together?
5. What do you like about your best friend?
6. What makes someone a good friend?
7. Do you prefer a large group of friends or a few close friends?
8. How do you keep in touch with your friends?
9. Have you ever disagreed with a friend?
10. What is your best memory with your friends?

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Follow-up Questions

Conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়ার জন্য:

> Why are you close?

> How often do you meet?

> Where do you usually hang out?

> What do you usually talk about?

> What do you have in common?

> How are you different?

> Do you trust each other?

> Have you ever helped each other?

> What do you do when you disagree?

> Do you think you'll remain friends in the future?

---

# 🧩 Part 31 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **My Best Friend**

> I have a few close friends, but one of them is particularly important to me. We met at university and gradually became close friends. We've known each other for several years.
>
> My friend is friendly, honest, supportive, and easy to talk to. We have several common interests, so we usually have a lot to talk about. In our free time, we sometimes hang out, watch movies, eat together, or simply talk about our studies, work, and future plans.
>
> What I value most about our friendship is trust. I know that I can talk to him when I have a problem, and he usually gives me honest advice. Of course, we've had disagreements from time to time, but we normally talk about the problem and sort it out.
>
> I think good friendships require trust, honesty, respect, and effort. I hope we'll remain close friends even as our careers and responsibilities change in the future.

---

Topic:

# **“My Friends & Social Life”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **My Friends → Best Friend → How We Met → Personality → Common Interests → What We Do → Trust → Disagreement → Best Memory → Future Friendship**

প্রথমে **notes দেখে** বলবে।

তারপর একই topic **notes ছাড়া** বলবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 43 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHO → HOW → WHAT → WHY → EXPERIENCE → FEELING → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I have a few close friends.**
> **I met most of them at university.**
> **We usually hang out, watch movies, and talk about our lives.**
> **I value them because they are honest and supportive.**
> **We've been through difficult situations together.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 44

# 💻 Technology & Modern Life

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** Technology কী → কীভাবে ব্যবহার করো → কেন ব্যবহার করো → সুবিধা → অসুবিধা → daily life-এ impact → future technology।

Lesson 43-এ আমরা **Friends & Social Life** শিখেছি। আজকের Lesson 44-এ সেই topic-এর সাথে connection রেখে শিখব কীভাবে **technology, smartphone, internet, AI, social media এবং modern life** নিয়ে natural English-এ কথা বলতে হয়।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 44-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* technology সম্পর্কে নিজের opinion বলতে
* smartphone/internet কীভাবে ব্যবহার করো বলতে
* technology-এর সুবিধা ও অসুবিধা বলতে
* technology কীভাবে daily life পরিবর্তন করেছে বলতে
* social media ও technology connect করতে
* AI নিয়ে basic opinion দিতে
* technology-related problem explain করতে
* future technology নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* **advantages vs disadvantages** compare করতে
* 2–5 মিনিট technology নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Technology Speaking Formula

Technology topic-এর যেকোনো question-এর জন্য:

# **WHAT → HOW → WHY → BENEFIT → PROBLEM → OPINION → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I use technology every day.
> I mainly use my smartphone and computer for studying, communication, and work.
> Technology saves me a lot of time and makes many tasks easier.
> However, excessive use of technology can be distracting.
> In my opinion, technology is extremely useful if we use it responsibly.
> I think technology will become even more important in the future.

---

# 💻 Part 2 — Technology Vocabulary

> technology — প্রযুক্তি

> device — ডিভাইস

> smartphone — স্মার্টফোন

> computer — কম্পিউটার

> laptop — ল্যাপটপ

> tablet — ট্যাবলেট

> internet — ইন্টারনেট

> software — সফটওয়্যার

> application / app — অ্যাপ

> website — ওয়েবসাইট

> digital — ডিজিটাল

> online — অনলাইন

> offline — অফলাইন

> artificial intelligence — কৃত্রিম বুদ্ধিমত্তা

> automation — স্বয়ংক্রিয়করণ

> information — তথ্য

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “How Often Do You Use Technology?”

### Question:

> How often do you use technology?

### Beginner:

> I use technology every day.

### Intermediate:

> I use technology every day, mainly for studying, communication, entertainment, and work.

### Advanced:

> Technology is a major part of my daily life. I use different devices and online services throughout the day for learning, communication, work, and entertainment.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “What Do You Use Your Phone For?”

### Question:

> What do you usually use your smartphone for?

### Answer:

> I mainly use my smartphone for communication, browsing the internet, watching videos, using social media, and learning new things.

আরও natural:

> I use my phone for both productive and recreational purposes. I use it to communicate with people, learn new things, manage my daily activities, and relax by watching videos or listening to music.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Productive vs Recreational

### Productive

> studying

> working

> reading

> learning

> communicating

> managing tasks

### Recreational

> watching movies

> listening to music

> playing games

> browsing social media

---

### Example:

> I try to use my phone mainly for productive activities, although I also use it for entertainment.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Technology in Daily Life

### Question:

> How has technology changed your daily life?

### Answer:

> Technology has made my daily life easier and faster. I can communicate with people instantly, find information quickly, study online, and complete many tasks without leaving home.

---

# 🧠 Useful Expressions

> makes life easier

> saves time

> saves money

> improves communication

> provides easy access to information

> increases productivity

> makes work more convenient

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “Technology Saves Time”

### Examples:

> Technology saves me a lot of time.

> Online services make many tasks faster.

> I can pay bills online instead of going to a bank.

> I can attend meetings without traveling.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Advantages of Technology

Question:

> What are the advantages of technology?

### Main advantages:

### 1. Communication

> Technology makes communication faster and easier.

### 2. Education

> Students can learn from online courses and videos.

### 3. Work

> Technology helps people work more efficiently.

### 4. Information

> We can access information within seconds.

### 5. Healthcare

> Technology has improved medical diagnosis and treatment.

### 6. Entertainment

> People can watch movies, listen to music, and play games online.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Disadvantages of Technology

### Question:

> What are the disadvantages of technology?

Useful points:

> addiction

> distraction

> privacy problems

> cybercrime

> misinformation

> lack of physical activity

> social isolation

> screen time

---

### Example:

> One major disadvantage is that people can become too dependent on technology.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “Too Much”

Very useful structure:

> **too much + uncountable noun**

> too much screen time

> too much technology

> too much information

### Example:

> Too much screen time can affect our daily routine.

---

### **too many + plural noun**

> too many notifications

> too many apps

> too many messages

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Technology & Communication

এটা Lesson 43-এর **Friends & Social Life**-এর সাথে directly connected।

### Question:

> Has technology changed the way people communicate?

### Answer:

> Yes, definitely. People can now communicate instantly through messaging apps, social media, and video calls. However, I think face-to-face communication is still important because it feels more personal.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Face-to-Face vs Online

### Question:

> Do you prefer online communication or face-to-face communication?

### Answer:

> I think both have advantages. Online communication is convenient when people are far away, but I prefer face-to-face communication for important or personal conversations.

---

# 🔥 Useful Comparison

> **Online communication is more convenient, whereas face-to-face communication is more personal.**

### Whereas

= অন্যদিকে / যেখানে

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Internet

### Question:

> What do you usually use the internet for?

### Answer:

> I mainly use the internet for studying, research, communication, entertainment, and work.

### Advanced:

> I rely on the internet for a wide range of activities, including education, research, communication, professional work, and entertainment.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “Rely On”

**rely on = নির্ভর করা**

### Examples:

> I rely on the internet for information.

> Many businesses rely on technology.

> Students rely on online resources for learning.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Social Media & Technology

### Question:

> How often do you use social media?

### Answer:

> I use social media every day, but I try not to spend too much time on it.

### Better:

> I use social media regularly to keep in touch with friends, follow interesting content, and stay updated with current events. However, I try to limit my screen time.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Social Media Problems

### Question:

> What are the disadvantages of social media?

### Answer:

> Social media can be useful, but excessive use can become a problem. It can waste time, create distractions, and sometimes expose people to misinformation or unrealistic lifestyles.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — “Excessive”

**excessive = অতিরিক্ত**

> excessive screen time

> excessive use of social media

> excessive dependence on technology

### Example:

> Excessive use of social media can negatively affect productivity.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Artificial Intelligence

### Question:

> What do you think about AI?

### Beginner:

> I think AI is very useful.

### Intermediate:

> I think AI is a powerful technology that can make many tasks easier and faster.

### Advanced:

> I think artificial intelligence has enormous potential. It can improve productivity, support education and healthcare, and automate repetitive tasks. However, it also raises concerns about privacy, misinformation, job displacement, and responsible use.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — AI Advantages

> automate repetitive tasks

> improve productivity

> analyze large amounts of data

> support decision-making

> personalize learning

> assist professionals

---

### Example:

> AI can automate repetitive tasks and help people focus on more complex work.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — AI Concerns

> job displacement

> privacy

> misinformation

> bias

> security

> overdependence

---

### Example:

> One concern is that people may become too dependent on AI.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Technology & Education

### Question:

> How has technology changed education?

### Answer:

> Technology has made education more accessible. Students can attend online classes, watch educational videos, use digital libraries, and communicate with teachers from almost anywhere.

---

### Useful expressions:

> online classes

> digital resources

> online courses

> educational platforms

> virtual classrooms

> digital textbooks

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Technology & Work

### Question:

> How does technology help people at work?

### Answer:

> Technology helps people communicate, manage information, automate repetitive tasks, and work remotely. It can also improve productivity and reduce the time needed to complete certain tasks.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Remote Work

### Question:

> Would you like to work remotely?

### Answer:

> Yes, I would. Remote work can save commuting time and give people more flexibility. However, it requires good time management and communication skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “Would You Like...?”

Future preference:

> Would you like to work remotely?

> Would you like to learn AI?

> Would you like to work in a technology company?

### Answer:

> Yes, I would.

> No, I wouldn't.

> I'd definitely like to.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Technology Problems

### Scenario:

Your internet isn't working.

### Useful expressions:

> My internet isn't working.

> The connection is unstable.

> The internet is very slow.

> The connection keeps dropping.

> I can't connect to the Wi-Fi.

> Could you check the connection?

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “Keeps + Verb-ing”

Very useful natural English:

> The internet keeps disconnecting.

> My computer keeps freezing.

> The app keeps crashing.

> My phone keeps restarting.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Problem-Solving Conversation

### A:

> What's wrong with your computer?

### B:

> It's running very slowly.

### A:

> Have you tried restarting it?

### B:

> Yes, but the problem is still there.

### A:

> Maybe you should check the storage.

### B:

> That's a good idea. I'll check it.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Technology Opinion

### Question:

> Do you think technology makes people lazy?

### Answer:

> Sometimes, yes. Technology makes many tasks easier, which can reduce physical effort. However, I don't think technology itself makes people lazy. It depends on how people use it.

🔥 Very useful opinion structure:

# **Sometimes → However → It depends**

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “It Depends”

এই expression অনেক opinion question-এ ব্যবহার করা যাবে।

### Question:

> Is social media good or bad?

### Answer:

> It depends on how people use it.

### Question:

> Is AI dangerous?

### Answer:

> It depends on how it is developed and used.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Future Technology

### Question:

> How do you think technology will change in the future?

### Answer:

> I think technology will become more intelligent, connected, and automated. AI will probably become a bigger part of education, healthcare, business, and everyday life.

### Advanced:

> I expect technology to become increasingly integrated into our everyday lives. AI, automation, robotics, and smart devices will probably play a much larger role in how we work, learn, communicate, and solve problems.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Prediction Language

Future prediction-এর জন্য:

> I think...

> I believe...

> I expect...

> I imagine...

> I predict...

> It's likely that...

> It's possible that...

---

### Examples:

> I think AI will become more common.

> It's likely that more people will work remotely.

> It's possible that robots will perform more repetitive tasks.

---

# 🎯 Part 32 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের main questions:

1. How often do you use technology?
2. What do you mainly use your smartphone for?
3. How has technology changed your daily life?
4. What are the main advantages of technology?
5. What are the disadvantages?
6. Has technology changed the way people communicate?
7. Do you prefer online or face-to-face communication?
8. What do you think about artificial intelligence?
9. How does technology help students and workers?
10. How do you think technology will change in the future?

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why do you use it?

> How often do you use it?

> When did you start using it?

> What are the benefits?

> What are the disadvantages?

> How has it changed your life?

> Do you think it's necessary?

> What problems can it cause?

> Would you like to use it more?

> What will happen in the future?

---

# 🧩 Part 34 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **Technology in My Life**

> Technology is an important part of my daily life. I use my smartphone and computer every day for studying, communication, work, and entertainment. I also use the internet to learn new things, search for information, and stay connected with other people.
>
> I think technology has made my life much easier. For example, I can communicate with someone instantly, attend online classes, work from home, and find information within seconds. It also gives me access to many useful educational resources.
>
> However, technology also has some disadvantages. Spending too much time on smartphones and social media can be distracting and can reduce productivity. Privacy and misinformation are also important concerns.
>
> In my opinion, technology itself is neither completely good nor completely bad. It depends on how we use it. If we use technology responsibly, it can make our lives more convenient and productive.
>
> In the future, I think AI and automation will become even more important. Technology will probably change the way we work, study, communicate, and solve everyday problems.

---

# 📝 Part 35 — Homework

## Task 1 — Answer These 10 Questions

প্রতিটি প্রশ্নের **3–5 sentences** answer দাও:

1. How often do you use technology?
2. What do you use your smartphone for?
3. What do you use your computer for?
4. How has technology changed your daily life?
5. What are three advantages of technology?
6. What are three disadvantages?
7. Has technology changed communication?
8. What do you think about AI?
9. Do you think people depend too much on technology?
10. How will technology change our lives in the future?

---

# 📝 Task 2 — Question Making

নিজে **10টি Technology-related questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> What...?

> Why...?

> How...?

> How often...?

> Do you think...?

> Have you ever...?

> What would happen if...?

> Would you like...?

> How has...?

> How will...?

---

# 🎤 Task 3 — 5-Minute Speaking

Topic:

# **“Technology in My Daily Life”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **Technology I Use → How I Use It → Why I Use It → Benefits → Problems → Social Media → AI → My Opinion → Future**

প্রথমে notes দেখে বলবে।

তারপর **notes ছাড়া 5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 44 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → HOW → WHY → BENEFIT → PROBLEM → OPINION → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I use technology every day.**
> **I mainly use my phone and computer for study, work, and communication.**
> **It saves me time and makes many tasks easier.**
> **However, excessive use can be distracting.**
> **I think technology is very useful if we use it responsibly.**
> **In the future, I expect AI and automation to become even more important.**
