# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 45

# 💰 Money, Saving & Spending

**Level:** Intermediate
**Main Focus:** টাকা কীভাবে ব্যবহার করো → কীসে খরচ করো → কীভাবে save করো → প্রয়োজন বনাম ইচ্ছা → budgeting → shopping-এর সাথে connection → financial goals।

Lesson 44-এ আমরা **Technology & Modern Life** শিখেছি। আজকের Lesson 45-এ সেই একই speaking structure ব্যবহার করে **Money, Saving & Spending** নিয়ে natural English conversation শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 45-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* money সম্পর্কে নিজের opinion বলতে
* income ও expenses নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* কোথায় টাকা খরচ করো বলতে
* saving সম্পর্কে বলতে
* needs vs wants explain করতে
* budget নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* unnecessary spending নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* financial goals বলতে
* টাকা ধার নেওয়া/দেওয়া নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* shopping-এর সাথে money topic connect করতে
* 2–5 মিনিট money নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Money Speaking Formula

Money-related যেকোনো topic-এর জন্য:

# **INCOME → EXPENSE → NEED → WANT → SAVING → PROBLEM → GOAL → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I try to manage my money carefully.
> I usually spend money on food, transportation, education, and other necessary things.
> I try to distinguish between what I need and what I simply want.
> I also try to save some money every month.
> Sometimes I spend more than I planned, especially when I go shopping.
> My goal is to build a good savings habit and become financially independent.

---

# 💰 Part 2 — Money Vocabulary

> money — টাকা

> income — আয়

> salary — বেতন

> expense — খরচ

> spending — ব্যয়

> saving — সঞ্চয়

> budget — বাজেট

> price — দাম

> cost — খরচ/মূল্য

> cash — নগদ টাকা

> bank account — ব্যাংক অ্যাকাউন্ট

> wallet — মানিব্যাগ

> payment — পেমেন্ট

> bill — বিল

> debt — ঋণ

> loan — ঋণ

> investment — বিনিয়োগ

> financial goal — আর্থিক লক্ষ্য

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Income vs Expense

### Income

যে টাকা তুমি **পাও**:

> salary

> business income

> freelance income

> allowance

### Expense

যে টাকা তুমি **খরচ করো**:

> food

> rent

> transportation

> education

> shopping

> entertainment

---

### Example:

> My income is limited, so I try to control my expenses.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “Spend Money On”

এটি খুব important structure।

# **spend money on + noun**

### Examples:

> I spend money on food.

> I spend money on transportation.

> I spend money on books.

> I spend money on education.

### ❌

> I spend money for food.

### ✅

> I spend money on food.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “Spend Time Doing”

এটা Lesson 42-এর structure-এর সাথে মিল আছে।

> I spend money **on books**.

কিন্তু:

> I spend time **reading books**.

### মনে রাখো:

**money → on**

**time → verb-ing**

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — What Do You Spend Money On?

### Question:

> What do you usually spend money on?

### Answer:

> I usually spend money on food, transportation, education, and personal expenses.

### Better:

> Most of my spending goes toward food, transportation, education, and other necessary expenses. I also spend some money on entertainment and shopping.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Spend” vs “Pay”

এখানে অনেক learner mistake করে।

### Spend:

> I spend money on food.

### Pay:

> I pay the bill.

> I pay for my internet.

> I paid for the meal.

---

### সহজভাবে:

> **spend money on something**

> **pay for something**

---

# 💡 Part 8 — Need vs Want

এটি today's lesson-এর সবচেয়ে important concept।

### Need = প্রয়োজন

> food

> medicine

> education

> transportation

> basic clothing

### Want = ইচ্ছা

> expensive phone

> new shoes

> gaming device

> unnecessary gadgets

---

### Question:

> What's the difference between a need and a want?

### Answer:

> A need is something necessary for our daily life, while a want is something we'd like to have but don't necessarily need.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “I Need” vs “I Want”

### Need:

> I need a new laptop for my work.

### Want:

> I want a new laptop because the latest model looks better.

---

### Advanced:

> I try to prioritize my needs over my wants.

**prioritize = অগ্রাধিকার দেওয়া**

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Saving Money

### Question:

> Do you save money?

### Simple:

> Yes, I try to save money.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I try to save a certain amount of money every month.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I try to save regularly because I want to build an emergency fund and achieve my long-term financial goals.

---

# 🔥 Useful Expressions

> save money

> save regularly

> put money aside

> build savings

> emergency fund

> financial goal

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Put Money Aside”

**put money aside = টাকা আলাদা করে রাখা**

### Example:

> I try to put some money aside every month.

> I'm putting money aside for a new laptop.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Budget

### Question:

> Do you have a monthly budget?

### Answer:

> Yes. I try to make a simple monthly budget so I know how much I can spend and save.

---

### Budget categories:

> food

> transportation

> education

> bills

> shopping

> entertainment

> savings

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Stick to a Budget”

**stick to = মেনে চলা**

> I try to stick to my monthly budget.

### Example:

> Making a budget is easy, but sticking to it can be difficult.

🔥 Very natural English.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Unnecessary Spending

### Question:

> Do you sometimes spend money unnecessarily?

### Answer:

> Yes, sometimes I do. I occasionally buy things that I don't really need, especially when I see a good discount.

---

# 🔥 Useful Expression

> **I don't really need it.**

### Example:

> I wanted to buy it, but I realized that I didn't really need it.

---

# 🛍️ Part 15 — Shopping & Money Connection

এটি Lesson 40-এর সাথে directly connected।

### Question:

> Do discounts make you spend more money?

### Answer:

> Sometimes they do. A discount can make a product look like a good deal, but I try to ask myself whether I actually need it before buying it.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — “Good Deal”

**good deal = ভালো দামে ভালো জিনিস/সুযোগ**

### Example:

> I bought this laptop at a good deal.

আরও natural:

> I got a good deal on this laptop.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Impulse Buying

**Impulse buying = হঠাৎ চিন্তা না করে কেনাকাটা করা**

### Question:

> Do you ever buy things impulsively?

### Answer:

> Yes, sometimes. If I see something interesting online, I may want to buy it immediately. But I'm trying to avoid impulse buying.

---

# 🧠 Useful Structure

> **I'm trying to avoid + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm trying to avoid unnecessary spending.

> I'm trying to avoid buying things I don't need.

---

# 💰 Part 18 — Saving for Something

### Question:

> What are you saving money for?

### Examples:

> I'm saving money for a new computer.

> I'm saving money for my education.

> I'm saving money for future emergencies.

> I'm saving money for a trip.

---

### Advanced:

> I'm currently putting money aside for a future investment.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Short-Term vs Long-Term Financial Goals

### Short-term:

> buy a laptop

> pay a course fee

> save for a trip

### Long-term:

> buy a house

> build an emergency fund

> invest money

> become financially independent

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Financial Independence

**financially independent = নিজের অর্থনৈতিক প্রয়োজন নিজে পূরণ করতে সক্ষম**

### Example:

> One of my long-term goals is to become financially independent.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Can You Afford It?”

Very useful expression:

> **Can you afford it?**

= এটা কেনার সামর্থ্য আছে?

### Examples:

> I can't afford a new car right now.

> I can afford it, but I don't think it's worth the money.

---

# 🔥 “Worth the Money”

= খরচের তুলনায় ভালো value পাওয়া

### Example:

> This laptop is expensive, but I think it's worth the money.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Cheap vs Expensive

### Cheap

> This phone is cheap.

### Affordable

> This phone is affordable.

### Expensive

> This phone is expensive.

### Overpriced

> I think this phone is overpriced.

---

# ⚠️ Important Difference

**Cheap** কখনও negative meaning দিতে পারে।

> This product is cheap.

মানে কম দাম—কিন্তু quality খারাপও বোঝাতে পারে।

**Affordable** বেশি positive:

> This product is affordable.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Price vs Cost

### Price

কোনো জিনিসের বিক্রয়মূল্য:

> What's the price of this phone?

### Cost

কত টাকা লাগছে:

> How much does it cost?

### Examples:

> The price is $500.

> It costs $500.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Borrow vs Lend

এগুলো খুব important।

### Borrow = অন্যের কাছ থেকে নেওয়া

> Can I borrow some money?

> I borrowed a book from my friend.

### Lend = অন্যকে দেওয়া

> Can you lend me some money?

> I lent my friend some money.

---

### Easy formula:

# **Borrow = take**

# **Lend = give**

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Talking About Debt

### Question:

> Do you think people should avoid unnecessary debt?

### Answer:

> Yes. I think people should be careful with debt and only borrow money when necessary. They should also make sure they can repay it.

---

# 🔥 Useful Vocabulary

> borrow money

> repay a loan

> pay back

> debt

> loan

> interest

---

### Example:

> I borrowed some money from my friend, and I'll pay it back next week.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Money & Happiness

### Question:

> Does money make people happy?

### Intermediate:

> Money can make life easier, but I don't think it is the only source of happiness.

### Advanced:

> I think money is important because it provides security and freedom, but it doesn't guarantee happiness. Relationships, health, personal satisfaction, and meaningful experiences are also important.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Opinion Structure

এই structure মনে রাখবে:

# **ACKNOWLEDGE → CONTRAST → OPINION**

### Example:

> Money is important, **but** I don't think it is everything.

আরও advanced:

> Although money is important for financial security, I don't believe that having more money automatically leads to greater happiness.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Money Management

### Question:

> How can people manage their money better?

### Answer:

> I think people should create a budget, track their expenses, avoid unnecessary purchases, and save regularly.

---

### 4-step formula:

# **Budget → Track → Control → Save**

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Track Expenses

**track expenses = কোথায় কত খরচ হচ্ছে হিসাব রাখা**

### Examples:

> I track my expenses using a mobile app.

> Tracking expenses helps me understand where my money goes.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — “Where Does Your Money Go?”

Natural question:

> Where does most of your money go?

### Answer:

> Most of my money goes toward food, transportation, education, and other daily expenses.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Money Conversation

### A:

> Do you save money?

### B:

> Yes, I try to save some money every month.

### A:

> What are you saving for?

### B:

> I'm saving for a new laptop and future emergencies.

### A:

> Do you follow a budget?

### B:

> I try to. I usually make a monthly budget and track my expenses.

### A:

> Do you ever buy things you don't need?

### B:

> Sometimes, especially when there's a big discount.

### A:

> How do you avoid unnecessary spending?

### B:

> I try to think carefully before buying something and ask myself whether I really need it.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **How I Manage Money**

> I think managing money is an important life skill. I try to be careful about how I spend my money because I don't want to waste it on unnecessary things.
>
> Most of my spending goes toward food, transportation, education, and other daily expenses. I also spend some money on entertainment and personal items. I try to distinguish between my needs and my wants before making a purchase.
>
> I also try to save some money regularly. I usually put some money aside for future needs and emergencies. Making a budget helps me understand how much I can spend and how much I should save.
>
> However, sometimes I still spend money impulsively, especially when I see an attractive discount. I'm trying to avoid this by thinking carefully before buying something.
>
> In the future, I want to become financially independent. I hope to develop better saving habits, manage my expenses wisely, and eventually invest some of my money.

---

# 🎯 Part 33 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. How do you usually manage your money?
2. What do you spend most of your money on?
3. Do you save money regularly?
4. What are you saving money for?
5. Do you follow a monthly budget?
6. What's the difference between a need and a want?
7. Do you sometimes buy things you don't need?
8. Do discounts make you spend more?
9. Do you think money makes people happy?
10. What are your long-term financial goals?

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> Why do you spend money on that?

> How much do you usually save?

> What are you saving for?

> How do you control your expenses?

> Do you use a budget?

> Do you track your expenses?

> What was the most expensive thing you bought?

> Was it worth the money?

> Have you ever borrowed money?

> What is your biggest financial goal?

---

নিজে **10টি Money-related questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> What...?

> Why...?

> How...?

> How much...?

> How often...?

> Do you...?

> Have you ever...?

> Can you...?

> Would you...?

> What would you...?

# 🎤 Task 3 — 5-Minute Speaking

Topic:

# **“How I Manage My Money”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **Income → Expenses → Needs → Wants → Saving → Budget → Unnecessary Spending → Financial Goal → Future**

প্রথমে **notes দেখে** বলবে।

তারপর notes বন্ধ করে **5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 45 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **INCOME → EXPENSE → NEED → WANT → SAVE → CONTROL → GOAL → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I try to manage my money carefully.**
> **Most of my money goes toward necessary expenses.**
> **I try to distinguish between my needs and wants.**
> **I also put some money aside every month.**
> **Sometimes I spend unnecessarily, but I'm trying to control it.**
> **My long-term goal is to become financially independent.**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 47

# 🧩 Problems, Challenges & Solutions

**Level:** Intermediate  
**Main Focus:** Problem কী → কেন হলো → কীভাবে solve করবে → কী চেষ্টা করেছ → কী শিখেছ → ভবিষ্যতে কী করবে।

Lesson 46-এ আমরা **Goals, Dreams & Future Plans** শিখেছি। সেখানে আমরা বলেছি goal achieve করতে **challenges** আসতে পারে। আজ সেই **challenge → problem → solution** বিষয়টাকে বিস্তারিতভাবে speaking practice করব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 47-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

- নিজের problem explain করতে
    
- problem-এর কারণ বলতে
    
- difficult situation describe করতে
    
- solution suggest করতে
    
- advice দিতে
    
- নিজের experience বলতে
    
- problem solve করার steps বলতে
    
- failure থেকে কী শিখেছ বলতে
    
- hypothetical problem নিয়ে কথা বলতে
    
- **What happened → Why → What did you do → Result → Lesson** structure ব্যবহার করতে
    

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Problem Speaking Formula

যেকোনো problem-এর answer:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → ACTION → RESULT → LESSON → FUTURE**

### Example:

> I had a problem with my time management.  
> I was spending too much time on social media.  
> So I created a daily schedule and limited my screen time.  
> After a few weeks, I became more productive.  
> I learned that having a clear routine is important.  
> Now I try to plan my day in advance.

---

# 🧩 Part 2 — Problem Vocabulary

> problem — সমস্যা

> difficulty — অসুবিধা

> challenge — চ্যালেঞ্জ

> situation — পরিস্থিতি

> issue — সমস্যা/বিষয়

> mistake — ভুল

> failure — ব্যর্থতা

> pressure — চাপ

> stress — মানসিক চাপ

> solution — সমাধান

> decision — সিদ্ধান্ত

> result — ফলাফল

> lesson — শিক্ষা

> experience — অভিজ্ঞতা

> obstacle — বাধা

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Problem vs Challenge

দুটো কাছাকাছি হলেও একটু difference আছে।

### Problem

একটা নির্দিষ্ট সমস্যা:

> I have a problem with my computer.

### Challenge

কোনো difficult situation যা overcome করতে হবে:

> Learning English is a challenge for me.

### Natural:

> I faced several challenges while learning English.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — “I Have a Problem With...”

Very useful structure:

> I have a problem with my internet.

> I have a problem with time management.

> I have a problem with concentration.

> I have a problem with speaking English confidently.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — “I’m Having Trouble...”

**have trouble + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I'm having trouble concentrating.

> I'm having trouble understanding this topic.

> I'm having trouble managing my time.

> I'm having trouble sleeping.

### ❌

> I'm having trouble to understand.

### ✅

> I'm having trouble **understanding**.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Explain the Cause

Problem বলার পরে **Why?** explain করবে।

Useful expressions:

> because...

> because of...

> due to...

> the main reason is...

> one reason is...

> this happened because...

---

### Examples:

> I couldn't finish the work because I was busy.

> I was late because of heavy traffic.

> The problem happened due to a technical issue.

> The main reason was poor planning.

---

# ⚠️ Important Grammar

### Because + sentence

> I was late because **the traffic was heavy**.

### Because of + noun

> I was late because of **heavy traffic**.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “The Main Reason Is...”

Very useful for speaking:

> The main reason is that I didn't manage my time properly.

> The main reason was poor communication.

> One of the main reasons is lack of experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Talking About a Difficult Experience

### Question:

> Tell me about a difficult situation you faced.

### Formula:

# **WHEN → PROBLEM → CAUSE → ACTION → RESULT → LESSON**

### Example:

> A difficult situation I faced was when I had to complete an important project within a short deadline. I had several tasks to complete, and I initially felt overwhelmed. I decided to prioritize the most important tasks and create a schedule. I worked consistently and managed to finish the project on time. From that experience, I learned the importance of planning and staying calm under pressure.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “I Felt...”

Problem experience-এ feelings describe করা খুব important।

Useful words:

> worried

> nervous

> stressed

> confused

> frustrated

> disappointed

> scared

> overwhelmed

> confident

> relieved

> proud

---

### Examples:

> I felt really nervous.

> I was worried about the result.

> I felt frustrated because I couldn't solve the problem.

> I was relieved when everything was finally completed.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “At First... But Then...”

Storytelling-এর জন্য extremely useful।

### Formula:

# **At first → but then → eventually**

### Example:

> At first, I didn't know what to do, but then I made a plan. Eventually, I found a solution.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “Eventually”

**eventually = শেষ পর্যন্ত**

> Eventually, I solved the problem.

> Eventually, everything worked out.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Giving Solutions

### Question:

> How would you solve this problem?

Useful expressions:

> I would...

> I think we should...

> We could...

> One solution would be...

> The best approach would be...

> If I were you, I'd...

---

### Examples:

> I would talk to the person directly.

> We could try another approach.

> I think we should make a plan.

> If I were you, I'd stay calm.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — “If I Were You”

Advice দেওয়ার জন্য:

# **If I were you, I would + verb**

### Examples:

> If I were you, I'd apologize.

> If I were you, I'd talk to my teacher.

> If I were you, I'd take some rest.

> If I were you, I'd make a new plan.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “You Should”

Simple advice:

> You should talk to him.

> You should take a break.

> You should practice more.

> You should ask for help.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — “You Could”

**could** = একটা possible suggestion

> You could ask your friend for help.

> You could try a different method.

> You could take a short break.

Difference:

**should** → stronger advice

**could** → possible option

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — “Why Don't You...?”

Natural conversational advice:

> Why don't you take a break?

> Why don't you ask someone for help?

> Why don't you try again?

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Problem-Solving Steps

Problem solve করার সময়:

### Step 1

> First, identify the problem.

### Step 2

> Then, find the cause.

### Step 3

> Next, think of possible solutions.

### Step 4

> Choose the best solution.

### Step 5

> Take action.

### Step 6

> Check the result.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Complete Problem-Solving Example

### Problem:

You are not improving your English.

### Answer:

> First, I would identify the problem. I might realize that I'm not practicing enough. Then I would create a daily speaking routine. I'd practice speaking for at least 30 minutes every day. I'd also record myself and identify my mistakes. After a few weeks, I'd check my progress and adjust my routine if necessary.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — “What Went Wrong?”

Very useful question:

> What went wrong?

= কোথায় সমস্যা হয়েছিল?

### Example:

> We missed the deadline. What went wrong?

Answer:

> We didn't plan the project properly.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “It Didn't Work”

### Examples:

> I tried that, but it didn't work.

> The first solution didn't work.

> I tried several methods, but none of them worked.

### Then:

> So I decided to try something different.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “Try Again”

> I'll try again.

> I decided to try again.

> I'm going to give it another try.

> I'll keep trying until I succeed.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — “Give Up”

**give up = হাল ছেড়ে দেওয়া**

### Examples:

> I don't want to give up.

> I failed, but I didn't give up.

> Sometimes things are difficult, but we shouldn't give up easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “Keep + Verb-ing”

Very useful:

> Keep practicing.

> Keep trying.

> Keep learning.

> Keep working hard.

### Examples:

> I'll keep practicing until I become confident.

> Even if I fail, I'll keep trying.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Learning From Problems

### Question:

> What did you learn from a difficult experience?

### Answer:

> I learned that problems can be solved if we stay calm and think carefully. I also learned that good planning can prevent many problems.

---

# 🔥 Useful Structures

> I learned that...

> The experience taught me that...

> One thing I learned was...

> It made me realize that...

---

### Example:

> The experience taught me that preparation is more important than I had realized.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Mistake vs Failure

### Mistake

তুমি ভুল কিছু করেছ:

> I made a mistake.

### Failure

চেষ্টা সফল হয়নি:

> I failed the test.

But:

> Failure doesn't mean the end.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “Make a Mistake”

সঠিক:

> I made a mistake.

❌ I did a mistake.

### More examples:

> Everyone makes mistakes.

> I learned from my mistake.

> I don't want to make the same mistake again.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “Deal With”

**deal with = মোকাবিলা করা**

### Examples:

> I need to deal with this problem.

> How do you deal with stress?

> She knows how to deal with difficult situations.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Stress & Problems

### Question:

> How do you deal with stress?

### Answer:

> When I'm stressed, I usually take a short break and try to organize my thoughts. Sometimes I go for a walk or talk to someone I trust. I also try to focus on one problem at a time instead of worrying about everything at once.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — One Problem at a Time

Very useful phrase:

> **I try to deal with one problem at a time.**

Meaning:

একসাথে সব সমস্যা নিয়ে চিন্তা না করে একটার পর একটা solve করা।

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Asking for Help

Problem হলে সবকিছু নিজে solve করতে হবে না।

### Useful expressions:

> Can you help me?

> Could you help me with this?

> I need some advice.

> Could you give me some advice?

> Do you have any suggestions?

> I don't know what to do.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Asking for Advice Conversation

### A:

> I'm having trouble managing my time.

### B:

> Why don't you make a daily schedule?

### A:

> I've tried that, but I can't stick to it.

### B:

> Maybe you should start with a simpler schedule.

### A:

> That's a good idea. I'll try that.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “Stick to”

Lesson 45-এ আমরা **stick to a budget** শিখেছি।

আজ:

> stick to a schedule

> stick to a plan

> stick to a routine

### Examples:

> I find it difficult to stick to my study schedule.

> You need discipline to stick to your plan.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Hypothetical Problems

এখন real problem থেকে imaginary problem।

### Question:

> What would you do if you lost your job?

### Answer:

> If I lost my job, I'd stay calm and start looking for new opportunities. I'd update my resume, improve my skills, and apply for suitable positions.

---

# 🔥 Second Conditional

# **If + past → would + verb**

### Examples:

> If I lost my job, I would look for another one.

> If I had more time, I would learn another language.

> If I were rich, I would travel around the world.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Real vs Imaginary

### Real possibility:

> If I have time, I will help you.

### Imaginary:

> If I had more time, I would help you.

এটা speaking-এ খুব important।

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Common Life Problems

Practice topics:

> lack of time

> lack of money

> stress

> traffic

> poor communication

> technical problems

> relationship problems

> study problems

> work pressure

> lack of confidence

> failure

> decision-making

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Complete 2-Minute Answer

## Topic: **A Difficult Problem I Faced**

> One difficult problem I faced was managing my time effectively. I had several responsibilities at the same time, and I often felt that I didn't have enough time to complete everything.
> 
> The main reason was poor planning. I didn't have a clear daily routine, and I sometimes spent too much time on less important activities.
> 
> At first, I tried to do everything at once, but that didn't work. Then I decided to prioritize my tasks and create a simple daily schedule. I started focusing on one task at a time and limited unnecessary distractions.
> 
> After some time, I became more organized and productive. I still have busy days, but I can manage my time much better now.
> 
> The experience taught me that having a clear plan is important. I also learned that problems don't always disappear immediately. Sometimes we need to be patient, try different solutions, and keep improving.

---

# 🎯 Part 37 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. What is a difficult problem you have faced?
    
2. What caused the problem?
    
3. How did you solve it?
    
4. How did you feel at that time?
    
5. What went wrong?
    
6. Have you ever failed at something important?
    
7. What did you learn from your failure?
    
8. How do you deal with stress?
    
9. How do you usually solve problems?
    
10. What would you do if you faced a serious problem in the future?
    

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Follow-up Questions

একটা answer থেকে conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> What happened?

> Why did it happen?

> How did you feel?

> What did you do?

> Did it work?

> What happened next?

> Who helped you?

> What did you learn?

> Would you do anything differently?

> What would you do next time?

---

# 📝 Part 39 — Question Making Practice

নিজে **10টি Problems & Solutions questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> What happened...?

> Why did...?

> How did...?

> What would you...?

> How would you...?

> Have you ever...?

> What did you learn...?

> What should...?

> What could...?

> What would happen if...?

---

# 🎤 Part 40 — 5-Minute Speaking

Topic:

# **“A Problem I Faced and How I Solved It”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **Problem → When → Cause → Feeling → First Attempt → Solution → Result → Lesson → Future**

প্রথমে notes দেখে বলবে।

তারপর notes ছাড়া **5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 47 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → ACTION → RESULT → LESSON → FUTURE**

### Example:

> **I had a problem with time management.**  
> **The main reason was poor planning.**  
> **So I created a daily schedule and prioritized my tasks.**  
> **As a result, I became more productive.**  
> **I learned that planning is very important.**  
> **Now I try to plan my day in advance.**

---

# 🔗 Course Connection

**Lesson 44 — Technology & Modern Life**  
→ Technology-এর benefits ও problems

↓

**Lesson 45 — Money, Saving & Spending**  
→ Money manage করা ও unnecessary spending control

↓

**Lesson 46 — Goals, Dreams & Future Plans**  
→ Goal achieve করার পথে challenges

↓

# **Lesson 47 — Problems, Challenges & Solutions**

→ **Problem → Cause → Solution → Result → Lesson**

↓

### **Lesson 48 — Opinions, Reasons & Discussions**

→ _What do you think? Why? Do you agree or disagree? What are the advantages and disadvantages?_

---
# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 48

# 💬 Opinions, Reasons & Discussions

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** নিজের opinion বলা → reason দেওয়া → agree/disagree করা → অন্যের opinion জিজ্ঞেস করা → advantages/disadvantages বলা → discussion চালিয়ে যাওয়া।

Lesson 47-এ আমরা **Problems, Challenges & Solutions** শিখেছি। সেখানে problem-এর কারণ ও solution explain করেছি। এখন সেই skill ব্যবহার করে **যেকোনো topic নিয়ে নিজের মতামত প্রকাশ** করা শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 48-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের opinion clearly বলতে
* opinion-এর কারণ explain করতে
* অন্যের opinion জিজ্ঞেস করতে
* agree/disagree করতে
* politely disagree করতে
* advantages/disadvantages বলতে
* দুইটি idea compare করতে
* example দিতে
* discussion চালিয়ে যেতে
* simple opinion → advanced opinion-এ যেতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Opinion Speaking Formula

যেকোনো opinion question-এর জন্য:

# **OPINION → REASON → EXAMPLE → CONTRAST → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> I think online education is very useful.
> The main reason is that it gives students more flexibility.
> For example, students can attend classes from home.
> However, face-to-face classes can provide better interaction.
> Overall, I think both methods have their advantages.

এটাই আজকের সবচেয়ে important speaking formula।

---

# 💬 Part 2 — Opinion দেওয়ার Basic Expressions

### Beginner

> I think...

> I believe...

> I feel...

> In my opinion...

### Examples:

> I think technology is useful.

> I believe education is very important.

> In my opinion, social media has both advantages and disadvantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Stronger Opinion

Intermediate-এ শুধু **I think** বারবার বলবে না।

এর পরিবর্তে:

> Personally, I think...

> From my point of view,...

> As far as I'm concerned,...

> In my view,...

> I would say that...

---

### Example:

> Personally, I think learning English is essential for career development.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Giving Reasons

Opinion বলার পরে **WHY?** answer করতে হবে।

Useful expressions:

> because...

> because of...

> the main reason is...

> one reason is...

> this is because...

> that's mainly because...

---

### Example:

> I prefer online learning because it's flexible.

আরও natural:

> I prefer online learning mainly because it gives me more flexibility.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — “The Main Reason Is...”

### Question:

> Why do you like technology?

### Answer:

> I like technology. The main reason is that it makes many tasks easier and faster.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Giving Examples

Opinion stronger করার জন্য example দেবে।

Useful expressions:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> Such as...

> A good example is...

### Example:

> Technology saves time. For example, we can pay bills online instead of going to a bank.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “For Example” vs “For Instance”

দুটোর meaning একই।

> For example, I use online resources to study.

> For instance, I watch educational videos when I don't understand a topic.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Agreeing

অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে agree করলে:

### Simple:

> I agree.

> I agree with you.

### Natural:

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

> That's true.

> Exactly.

> I think you're right.

---

### Example:

### A:

> Technology saves a lot of time.

### B:

> Absolutely. I completely agree.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Partially Agreeing

সব সময় পুরোপুরি agree করতে হবে না।

Useful:

> I agree to some extent.

> I partly agree.

> That's true to some extent.

> I can see your point.

### Example:

> I agree to some extent, but I think there are some disadvantages too.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Disagreeing Politely

❌ Too direct:

> You're wrong.

Better:

> I don't completely agree.

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

> I see it differently.

> I have a slightly different opinion.

> I understand your point, but...

---

# 🔥 Best Structure

# **I understand your point, but...**

### Example:

> I understand your point, but I think social media can also be useful if people use it responsibly.

এটি খুব natural এবং polite।

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Asking Someone's Opinion

Conversation চালানোর জন্য:

> What do you think?

> What do you think about this?

> What's your opinion?

> How do you feel about it?

> What's your view on this?

> Do you agree?

---

### Example:

> I think online education is useful. What do you think?

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “What Do You Think About...?”

একটা universal question pattern:

> What do you think about social media?

> What do you think about online education?

> What do you think about AI?

> What do you think about working from home?

> What do you think about exams?

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Advantages & Disadvantages

এটি speaking-এর খুব important structure।

### Advantages:

> One advantage is...

> One major benefit is...

> Another advantage is...

### Disadvantages:

> One disadvantage is...

> One major drawback is...

> Another problem is...

---

### Example:

> One major advantage of online learning is flexibility. Another benefit is accessibility. However, one disadvantage is that students may become less engaged.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Advantage” vs “Benefit”

দুটো প্রায় একই।

> One advantage of technology is convenience.

> One benefit of technology is convenience.

---

### Disadvantage:

> One disadvantage is distraction.

### Drawback:

> One major drawback is the lack of face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Compare Two Things

Opinion discussion-এ comparison খুব useful।

### Structure:

> **A is..., whereas B is...**

### Example:

> Online learning is more flexible, whereas traditional learning provides more face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🔥 Other Comparison Structures

> A is better than B for...

> Compared with...

> Unlike...

> On the other hand...

---

### Example:

> Unlike online classes, traditional classes require students to be physically present.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — “On the Other Hand”

এটা খুব important।

একটা side বলার পরে অন্য side:

> Online learning is convenient. On the other hand, it can sometimes feel less interactive.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Balanced Opinion

Advanced speaking-এ শুধু:

> Online learning is good.

বলবে না।

Better:

> I think online learning has both advantages and disadvantages. It's flexible and convenient, but it may not provide the same level of interaction as traditional classroom learning.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — “It Depends”

খুব powerful expression।

### Question:

> Is technology good or bad?

### Answer:

> It depends on how people use it.

### Question:

> Is working from home better?

### Answer:

> It depends on the type of job and the person's working style.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — “It Depends On...”

### Examples:

> It depends on the situation.

> It depends on the person.

> It depends on how you use it.

> It depends on the circumstances.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Agree + Contrast

একটা advanced structure:

# **I agree that X, but I also think Y.**

### Example:

> I agree that social media is useful, but I also think excessive use can be harmful.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Although

**although = যদিও**

### Example:

> Although technology is useful, excessive use can be harmful.

> Although online learning is convenient, some students prefer traditional classes.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — However

**however = তবে / কিন্তু**

> Technology is useful. However, it can also create problems.

### More natural:

> Technology is useful; however, we need to use it responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — On the One Hand / On the Other Hand

দুই দিকের argument দিতে:

> On the one hand, social media helps people stay connected.

> On the other hand, excessive use can waste time.

এটা **advanced discussion**-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Agree or Disagree

### Question:

> "Technology has made people less social." Do you agree or disagree?

### Answer:

> I partly agree with this statement. Technology has changed the way people communicate, and some people spend too much time online. However, technology can also help people stay connected with friends and family, especially when they live far away. So, I think the impact depends on how people use technology.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Statement থেকে Opinion

### Statement:

> "Money is more important than happiness."

### Answer:

> I disagree with this statement. Money is important because it provides financial security and makes life easier. However, I don't think money alone can make people happy. Good relationships, health, and personal satisfaction are also important.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “I Don't Think...”

Polite disagreement:

> I don't think money can buy happiness.

> I don't think technology is always harmful.

> I don't think exams are the best way to measure ability.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — “From My Experience”

নিজের experience দিয়ে opinion strong করতে:

> From my experience,...

> In my experience,...

### Example:

> In my experience, practicing every day is much more effective than studying English only once a week.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — “In General”

General opinion:

> In general, I think technology makes life easier.

> In general, people need a balance between work and personal life.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Strong Opinion vs Weak Opinion

### Weak:

> I think exercise is good.

### Stronger:

> I strongly believe that regular exercise is essential for a healthy lifestyle.

### Useful words:

> strongly

> definitely

> certainly

> clearly

> probably

> generally

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Certainty

### Strong certainty:

> I'm certain that...

> I strongly believe...

### Medium:

> I think...

> I believe...

### Less certain:

> I suppose...

> I guess...

> It seems to me that...

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Discussion Example

## Topic: Online Learning

### A:

> What do you think about online learning?

### B:

> I think it's very useful because it's flexible and convenient.

### A:

> Do you think it's better than traditional education?

### B:

> Not necessarily. Online learning is more flexible, whereas traditional classrooms provide more direct interaction.

### A:

> So which one do you prefer?

### B:

> Personally, I prefer a combination of both. Online resources are great for self-study, but face-to-face classes are also valuable.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Discussion Connectors

Speaking fluency বাড়ানোর জন্য:

> First of all,...

> In addition,...

> Also,...

> Besides,...

> However,...

> On the other hand,...

> For example,...

> As a result,...

> Therefore,...

> Overall,...

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — “Overall”

শেষে conclusion দিতে:

> Overall, I think technology is beneficial if we use it responsibly.

> Overall, I believe both options have advantages and disadvantages.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Full Opinion Answer

## Topic: **Is Social Media Good or Bad?**

> Personally, I think social media has both advantages and disadvantages. One major advantage is that it allows people to communicate easily and stay connected with friends and family. For example, people can communicate with someone who lives in another country within seconds.
>
> On the other hand, excessive use of social media can waste time and affect productivity. It can also expose people to misinformation and unrealistic lifestyles.
>
> I don't think social media itself is completely good or bad. It depends on how people use it. If we use it responsibly and limit our screen time, I think it can be a useful tool.
>
> Overall, I believe the key is balance.

---

# 🎯 Part 35 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. What do you think about social media?
2. Is technology good or bad?
3. Do you agree that money can buy happiness?
4. Is online education better than traditional education?
5. Should people work from home?
6. Is it better to live in a big city or a small town?
7. Do you think English is important for career development?
8. Are smartphones making people less social?
9. Is it better to save money or enjoy life?
10. What makes someone successful?

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Follow-up Questions

একটা opinion থেকে discussion চালিয়ে যাও:

> Why do you think so?

> What makes you say that?

> Can you give me an example?

> Do you agree with this?

> What are the advantages?

> What are the disadvantages?

> What about the other side?

> Has your opinion changed over time?

> What would you do?

> What do you think will happen in the future?

---

# 🧩 Part 37 — Question Making Practice

নিজে **10টি Opinion Questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> What do you think about...?

> Do you agree that...?

> Do you think...?

> Why do you think...?

> What are the advantages of...?

> What are the disadvantages of...?

> Which is better...?

> Would you rather...?

> How do you feel about...?

> Should people...?

---

# 🎤 Part 38 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

Topic:

# **“Is Technology Making Our Lives Better?”**

এই order follow করবে:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Advantage → Disadvantage → Other Side → Personal Opinion → Conclusion**

### শুরু:

> Personally, I think technology has made our lives better in many ways.

### মাঝখানে:

> One major advantage is...

> For example,...

> On the other hand,...

### শেষে:

> Overall, I believe...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 48 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **OPINION → REASON → EXAMPLE → CONTRAST → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> **I think online learning is useful.**
> **The main reason is that it is flexible.**
> **For example, students can study from home.**
> **However, traditional classes provide more direct interaction.**
> **Overall, I think both methods have their advantages.**

---

## 🔗 Course Connection

**Lesson 45 — Money, Saving & Spending**
→ What do you spend? What do you save?

↓

**Lesson 46 — Goals, Dreams & Future Plans**
→ What do you want? Why? How?

↓

**Lesson 47 — Problems, Challenges & Solutions**
→ What happened? Why? How did you solve it?

↓

# **Lesson 48 — Opinions, Reasons & Discussions**

→ **What do you think? Why? Example? Other side? Conclusion?**

↓

### **Lesson 49 — Agreeing, Disagreeing & Debate**

→ *I agree, I disagree, I partly agree, defend your opinion, challenge another opinion, and discuss both sides.*


---
# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 49

# 🗣️ Agreeing, Disagreeing & Debate

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** Opinion বলা → agree/disagree → নিজের opinion defend করা → অন্যের argument-এর উত্তর দেওয়া → politely challenge করা → দুই দিকের argument → conclusion।

**Lesson 48-এর সাথে direct connection:**
Lesson 48-এ তুমি শিখেছ **“What do you think?” + Reason + Example**।
Lesson 49-এ সেই opinion নিয়ে **real conversation/debate** করা শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 49-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কারো opinion-এর সাথে agree করতে
* politely disagree করতে
* partly agree করতে
* নিজের opinion defend করতে
* অন্যের opinion challenge করতে
* counter-argument দিতে
* evidence/example দিতে
* discussion-এ interruption ছাড়া কথা চালাতে
* debate-এ দুই side explain করতে
* conclusion দিতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Debate-এর Basic Formula

একটা debate answer এভাবে তৈরি করো:

# **POSITION → REASON → EVIDENCE → COUNTERPOINT → RESPONSE → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> I believe online education is useful.
> The main reason is that it is flexible.
> For example, students can learn from anywhere.
> However, some people argue that online classes are less interactive.
> I agree that this can be a problem, but good online platforms can provide live discussions and group activities.
> So, I believe online education can be very effective when it is used properly.

---

# 🗣️ Part 2 — Agreeing

### Simple

> I agree.

> I agree with you.

> That's true.

> Exactly.

> You're right.

### Stronger

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

> I strongly agree with that.

> I couldn't agree more.

---

## 🔥 “I Couldn't Agree More”

Meaning:

**আমি পুরোপুরি একমত।**

### Example:

> A: Exercise is important for good health.

> B: I couldn't agree more.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Agreeing With a Reason

শুধু:

> I agree.

বললে conversation ছোট হয়ে যাবে।

Better:

> I agree because...

### Example:

> I agree because regular exercise improves both physical and mental health.

আরও natural:

> I completely agree. One of the main reasons is that exercise helps people stay healthy and reduces stress.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Partial Agreement

সব argument 100% agree করতে হবে না।

Useful expressions:

> I agree to some extent.

> I partly agree.

> I agree up to a point.

> That's true to some extent.

> I can see your point.

---

### Example:

> I agree to some extent, but I think there are other factors to consider.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “I Can See Your Point”

এটি polite disagreement-এর জন্য খুব important।

### Example:

> I can see your point, but I don't completely agree.

Meaning:

> আমি তোমার point বুঝতে পারছি, কিন্তু পুরোপুরি একমত নই।

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Disagreeing

### Direct:

> I disagree.

### Polite:

> I don't completely agree.

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

> I see it differently.

> I have a different opinion.

> I don't think that's necessarily true.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Strong but Polite Disagreement

এই structure মনে রাখো:

# **I understand your point, but + YOUR OPINION**

### Example:

> I understand your point, but I think technology has made communication easier.

---

আরও:

> I understand why you think that, but I see it differently.

> I understand your argument, but I don't think that's the whole picture.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “That's Not Necessarily True”

**not necessarily = অবশ্যই এমন নয়**

### Example:

> A: Rich people are always happy.

> B: That's not necessarily true. Money can provide comfort, but happiness depends on many other things.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Defending Your Opinion

কেউ যদি তোমার opinion challenge করে, তোমার answer:

# **RESTATE → REASON → EXAMPLE**

### Example:

> I still believe that online learning is useful because it gives students flexibility. For example, people who work full-time can study online in their free time.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “I Still Believe...”

Very useful debate expression:

> I still believe that...

> I still think that...

### Example:

> I understand your argument, but I still believe that social media can be beneficial when used responsibly.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “The Reason I Say This Is...”

Advanced natural expression:

> The reason I say this is that...

### Example:

> I think practical experience is extremely important. The reason I say this is that real-world problems are often different from what we learn in textbooks.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Giving Evidence

Opinion stronger করার জন্য:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> Based on my experience,...

> From what I've seen,...

> One example is...

### Example:

> Based on my experience, regular speaking practice is more effective than simply memorizing vocabulary.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Counter-Argument

**counter-argument = বিপরীত argument**

### Example:

Your position:

> Online learning is useful.

Other side:

> Online learning is not interactive.

Your response:

> That's a valid concern, but I think technology has improved online interaction significantly.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “That's a Valid Point”

**valid point = যুক্তিসঙ্গত কথা**

### Example:

> That's a valid point. However, I think there is another side to the issue.

This is excellent for polite debate.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “However”

Argument change করার জন্য:

> I agree that technology is useful. However, excessive use can create problems.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Although”

Two opposite ideas:

> Although social media helps people communicate, excessive use can waste a lot of time.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “Even Though”

> Even though online learning is convenient, some students prefer face-to-face classes.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “On the Other Hand”

এক side থেকে অন্য side:

> Online education is flexible. On the other hand, traditional education provides direct interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “At the Same Time”

দুইটা truth একসাথে:

> Social media can be entertaining. At the same time, it can become addictive.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Challenging Someone's Opinion

Polite challenge:

> What makes you think that?

> Why do you think so?

> Could you explain what you mean?

> Can you give me an example?

> What evidence do you have?

> Do you think that's always true?

---

### Example:

> A: Working from home is always better.

> B: What makes you think that's always the case?

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Is That Always True?”

Excellent debate question:

> Is that always true?

### Example:

> A: Technology makes people lazy.

> B: Is that always true? I think technology can also increase productivity.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Asking for Clarification

কেউ কী বলেছে বুঝতে না পারলে:

> What do you mean?

> Could you explain that?

> Could you give me an example?

> Could you clarify what you mean?

> Do you mean that...?

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Interrupting Politely

Real conversation-এ মাঝে কিছু বলতে হতে পারে।

❌

> Stop. Let me talk.

Better:

> Sorry to interrupt, but...

> If I may add something...

> Can I just add something?

> Could I make a point?

---

### Example:

> Sorry to interrupt, but I'd like to add something.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Getting Back to Your Point

কথার মাঝে topic চলে গেলে:

> Coming back to my main point...

> To get back to what I was saying...

### Example:

> Coming back to my main point, I think education should focus more on practical skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Changing Your Opinion

একজন ভালো speaker নিজের opinion change করতে পারে।

Useful:

> I hadn't thought about it that way.

> That's an interesting point.

> You may be right.

> I might have to reconsider that.

> You've given me something to think about.

---

### 🔥 Very Natural:

> **You've given me something to think about.**

Meaning:

> তুমি আমাকে বিষয়টা নতুনভাবে ভাবার কারণ দিয়েছ।

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Agreeing After Discussion

> After hearing your argument, I agree.

> You've convinced me.

> I think you have a point.

> I can see why you think that.

---

# 🔥 “You've Convinced Me”

**convince = কাউকে বিশ্বাস করানো**

### Example:

> I wasn't sure at first, but you've convinced me.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Strong Argument

Argument strong করতে:

> The main reason is...

> Another important point is...

> More importantly,...

> What's even more important is...

### Example:

> Another important point is that online education can make learning accessible to people who live far from educational institutions.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Weakening an Argument

Politeভাবে argument-এর limitation দেখাতে:

> That's true, but...

> However,...

> That's not always the case.

> There are exceptions.

> It depends on the situation.

### Example:

> Working from home can increase productivity, but that's not always the case. It depends on the person's job and working environment.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “It Depends”

Debate-এর অন্যতম useful expression:

> It depends on the situation.

> It depends on the individual.

> It depends on how it's used.

### Example:

> A: Is social media good?

> B: It depends on how people use it.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Debate Topic: Money & Happiness

### Statement:

> “Money can buy happiness.”

### Side A:

> I partly agree. Money can make life more comfortable and reduce financial stress.

### Side B:

> I disagree. Money can provide comfort, but it cannot guarantee happiness or meaningful relationships.

### Balanced:

> I think money can contribute to happiness, especially when it provides financial security, but it isn't the only factor that matters.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Debate Topic: Technology

### Statement:

> “Technology makes people lazy.”

### Answer:

> I don't completely agree. Technology can make some people less active, especially if they depend on it too much. However, it can also make people more productive by automating repetitive tasks and providing quick access to information.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Debate Topic: Social Media

### Statement:

> “Social media is harmful.”

### Answer:

> I think that's only partly true. Social media can be harmful when people use it excessively or compare themselves with others. However, it can also help people communicate, learn new things, and build professional connections. So, I think responsible use is the key.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Debate Topic: Online Education

### Statement:

> “Online education is better than traditional education.”

### Answer:

> I don't think we can say that one is always better. Online education offers flexibility and convenience, while traditional education provides face-to-face interaction and a structured environment. Personally, I think a combination of both can be the most effective approach.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Debate Topic: Work From Home

### Statement:

> “Everyone should work from home.”

### Answer:

> I disagree with this idea. Working from home has several advantages, such as flexibility and less commuting. However, not every job can be done remotely, and some people work better in an office environment. So, I think the best option depends on the type of job and the individual.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Complete Debate Conversation

## Topic: **Is Social Media Good for Society?**

### Person A:

> I think social media is good for society because it helps people communicate.

### Person B:

> I agree to some extent, but I think excessive use can be harmful.

### A:

> That's true, but people can simply control how much time they spend on it.

### B:

> That's a valid point. However, many people find it difficult to control their screen time.

### A:

> I can see your point. But social media is also useful for education and business.

### B:

> I agree. In fact, I use social media to learn new things and connect with professionals.

### A:

> So perhaps the real issue isn't social media itself, but how people use it.

### B:

> Exactly. I think responsible use is the key.

---

# 🎯 Part 36 — 10 Core Debate Questions

আজকের most important:

1. Do you agree that money can buy happiness?
2. Is technology making people lazy?
3. Is social media good or bad for society?
4. Is online education better than traditional education?
5. Should people work from home?
6. Is it better to save money or enjoy life?
7. Should university education be free?
8. Is success more important than happiness?
9. Is competition good for people?
10. Should people spend less time on smartphones?

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Follow-up Questions

প্রতিটি topic-এ এই questions ব্যবহার করবে:

> Why do you think so?

> What makes you say that?

> Can you give me an example?

> Do you agree with me?

> What about the other side?

> Is that always true?

> What are the disadvantages?

> What would happen if...?

> Can you explain your point?

> Has your opinion changed?

---

# 🧩 Part 38 — Question Making Practice

নিজে **10টি debate questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> Do you agree that...?

> Do you disagree with...?

> Should people...?

> Is it better to...?

> Which is more important...?

> Is ... good or bad?

> What are the arguments for...?

> What are the arguments against...?

> Would you support...?

> What would you say to someone who believes...?

---

# 🎤 Part 39 — 5-Minute Debate Practice

আজকের topic:

# **“Is Social Media More Helpful Than Harmful?”**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Position

> Personally, I think...

### 2️⃣ Reason

> The main reason is...

### 3️⃣ Example

> For example,...

### 4️⃣ Opposite side

> However,...

### 5️⃣ Counter-argument

> I understand that point, but...

### 6️⃣ Personal response

> From my experience,...

### 7️⃣ Conclusion

> Overall, I believe...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 49 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **AGREE → REASON → DISAGREE → COUNTER → EXAMPLE → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> **I agree to some extent.**
> **The main reason is that technology makes life more convenient.**
> **However, I don't completely agree that technology always makes people lazy.**
> **That's a valid concern, but technology can also increase productivity.**
> **For example, automation can save people a lot of time.**
> **Overall, I think technology is beneficial when used responsibly.**

---

# 🚀 Speaking Upgrade

### Beginner:

> I agree.

### Intermediate:

> I agree because it's convenient.

### Advanced:

> I agree to some extent. While it certainly offers greater convenience, I think we also need to consider its potential disadvantages.

এটাই তোমার লক্ষ্য—**শুধু answer দেওয়া নয়, answer-এর মধ্যে reason + example + contrast + conclusion যোগ করা।**

---

**Lesson 46 — Goals, Dreams & Future Plans**
→ What do you want to achieve?

↓

**Lesson 47 — Problems, Challenges & Solutions**
→ What problems can stop you?

↓

**Lesson 48 — Opinions, Reasons & Discussions**
→ What do you think? Why?

→ **Can you defend your opinion and respond to another person's opinion?**

# 🟡 English Speaking Course — Lesson 50

# 📖 Storytelling & Narration

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** ঘটনা/অভিজ্ঞতা সুন্দরভাবে বলা → beginning → middle → ending → feelings → problem → action → result → lesson।

🎯 **Lesson 50 একটি গুরুত্বপূর্ণ milestone।**
Lesson 31-এ আমরা **Past Experiences** দিয়ে past ঘটনা বলা শুরু করেছিলাম। আজ সেটাকেই আরও advanced করে **complete storytelling** শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 50-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো ঘটনা শুরু থেকে শেষ পর্যন্ত বলতে
* past tense ব্যবহার করে story বলতে
* ঘটনা chronological order-এ সাজাতে
* background explain করতে
* problem/turning point বলতে
* নিজের reaction বলতে
* story interesting করতে
* conclusion দিতে
* story থেকে lesson বলতে
* 3–5 মিনিটের একটি complete story বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Storytelling Formula

যেকোনো story-এর জন্য এই formula মনে রাখো:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → WHAT → PROBLEM → ACTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

### Example:

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with some friends.
> We stayed there for two days and visited several places.
> On the second day, we suddenly lost our way while exploring a new area.
> At first, we were worried, but then we checked the map on our phones.
> Eventually, we found the right road and returned to our hotel safely.
> We were relieved and also laughed about the experience later.
> The experience taught me that it's important to stay calm when something unexpected happens.

---

# 🧩 Part 2 — Story-এর 3টি Main Part

প্রতিটি story-কে তিন ভাগে ভাগ করো:

## 1️⃣ Beginning

কখন? কোথায়? কার সাথে?

> Last year, I went to...

## 2️⃣ Middle

কী ঘটল? Problem কী ছিল?

> Suddenly,...

> Unfortunately,...

> At that point,...

## 3️⃣ Ending

শেষে কী হলো?

> Eventually,...

> In the end,...

> Finally,...

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Story শুরু করার Expressions

একটা story শুরু করার জন্য:

> Let me tell you about...

> I'd like to tell you about...

> One day,...

> A few years ago,...

> Last year,...

> When I was younger,...

> I remember one time when...

---

### Example:

> I'd like to tell you about a memorable trip I had with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — “One Day”

Simple storytelling:

> One day, I was walking home when I suddenly received a phone call.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — Background তৈরি করা

Story শুরুতেই সরাসরি problem-এ যেও না।

আগে situation তৈরি করো:

> It was a rainy evening, and I was on my way home.

> I was with a few friends, and we were planning to have dinner together.

> At that time, I was preparing for an important exam.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Time Expressions

Story chronological করতে এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

> first

> at first

> then

> after that

> later

> meanwhile

> suddenly

> a few minutes later

> eventually

> finally

> in the end

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “At First”

**at first = প্রথমে**

### Example:

> At first, I thought everything would be easy.

তারপর:

> But things didn't go as planned.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — “Suddenly”

Story-তে unexpected ঘটনা introduce করতে:

> Suddenly, the lights went out.

> Suddenly, I realized that I had lost my phone.

> Suddenly, it started raining heavily.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “Unfortunately”

খারাপ ঘটনা:

> Unfortunately, we missed the bus.

> Unfortunately, my phone battery died.

> Unfortunately, I forgot my wallet.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “Luckily”

ভালো ঘটনা:

> Luckily, I had my friend's phone number.

> Luckily, someone helped us.

> Luckily, we arrived on time.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “Eventually”

Problem-এর পরে result:

> Eventually, we found the right place.

> Eventually, everything worked out.

> Eventually, I solved the problem.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — “In the End”

Story conclude করার জন্য:

> In the end, everything was fine.

> In the end, we had a great time.

> In the end, I learned an important lesson.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — Past Simple

Storytelling-এর সবচেয়ে important tense:

# **Past Simple**

### Examples:

> I went.

> I saw.

> I met.

> I called.

> I decided.

> I realized.

> I returned.

---

### Example:

> I went to Dhaka last Friday.

> I met an old friend.

> We talked for an hour.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Past Continuous

Background action:

# **was/were + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> I was walking home.

> It was raining.

> We were talking.

> She was waiting for the bus.

---

### Story:

> I was walking home when I saw an old friend.

এখানে:

**was walking** → ongoing background

**saw** → suddenly happened

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Past Simple + Past Continuous

এই pattern খুব important:

# **I was doing X when Y happened.**

### Examples:

> I was studying when my friend called me.

> I was sleeping when the phone rang.

> We were having dinner when the lights went out.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Past Perfect

Advanced storytelling-এর জন্য:

# **had + past participle**

কোনো past event-এর আগেই আরেকটা ঘটনা ঘটেছিল।

### Example:

> When I arrived at the station, the train had already left.

আগে:

**train left**

পরে:

**I arrived**

---

### Another:

> I was tired because I hadn't slept well the night before.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — “Before” & “After”

### Before:

> Before I left home, I checked my phone.

### After:

> After I arrived, I called my friend.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Feelings

Story interesting করতে feelings বলবে।

### Negative:

> nervous

> worried

> scared

> confused

> embarrassed

> frustrated

> disappointed

### Positive:

> happy

> excited

> surprised

> relieved

> proud

> grateful

---

### Example:

> At first, I was really nervous, but eventually I felt relieved.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — “I Couldn't Believe...”

Surprise story-এর জন্য:

> I couldn't believe what happened.

> I couldn't believe my luck.

> I couldn't believe that I had won.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “I Realized...”

Important discovery:

> I suddenly realized that I had forgotten my wallet.

> That's when I realized that I was on the wrong bus.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “That's When...”

Very useful storytelling phrase:

> That's when I realized I had made a mistake.

> That's when my friend called me.

> That's when everything changed.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Turning Point

Story-এর important moment:

# **At that point...**

### Example:

> At that point, I didn't know what to do.

> At that point, we decided to go back.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Problem → Action

Storytelling-এর powerful structure:

> **Something happened → I reacted → I took action.**

### Example:

> My phone suddenly stopped working. I was worried, so I tried restarting it. When that didn't work, I borrowed my friend's phone and called the service center.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “So I Decided To...”

Action describe করার জন্য:

> So I decided to...

> I decided to...

### Example:

> I didn't know the answer, so I decided to ask my teacher for help.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — “Instead”

Alternative action:

> I planned to take a bus, but instead I walked home.

> I wanted to call him, but instead I sent him a message.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “As a Result”

Result:

> As a result, we arrived late.

> As a result, I learned an important lesson.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Story Conclusion

শেষে:

> In the end,...

> Eventually,...

> Looking back,...

> Overall,...

> The experience taught me...

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — “Looking Back”

**looking back = এখন পিছনে তাকিয়ে দেখলে**

### Example:

> Looking back, I think it was a valuable experience.

> Looking back, I realize that I learned a lot from that mistake.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Story থেকে Lesson

Story-এর শেষে lesson দিলে answer অনেক stronger হয়।

### Examples:

> The experience taught me to stay calm under pressure.

> I learned that preparation is extremely important.

> It taught me not to give up easily.

> I realized that mistakes can help us learn.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Memorable Experience

### Question:

> Tell me about a memorable experience.

### Answer structure:

> **When → Where → Who → What happened → Feeling → Result → Lesson**

---

### Example:

> One of the most memorable experiences I've had was a trip with my friends. We went to Cox's Bazar a few years ago. We spent several days exploring the area and enjoying the beach. One evening, we got caught in heavy rain while we were outside. At first, we were frustrated, but eventually we started laughing about it. In the end, it became one of the most memorable parts of the trip. Looking back, I think unexpected moments often become the best memories.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Story About a Mistake

### Question:

> Tell me about a mistake you made.

### Answer:

> A mistake I remember making was forgetting an important deadline. At the time, I had several tasks to manage, and I didn't organize them properly. When I realized my mistake, I felt really stressed. I immediately contacted the person in charge and explained the situation. Fortunately, I was given some extra time to complete the work. The experience taught me the importance of planning and keeping track of deadlines.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Story About Success

### Question:

> Tell me about something you achieved.

### Answer:

> One achievement I'm proud of was completing a difficult project. At first, I wasn't sure whether I could finish it because there were several technical problems. I spent a lot of time researching and testing different solutions. Eventually, I solved the main problems and completed the project. I felt extremely proud because I had learned a lot during the process. It taught me that patience and consistency can lead to good results.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Story About Helping Someone

### Question:

> Tell me about a time when you helped someone.

### Answer:

> A few months ago, a friend of mine was having difficulty understanding a particular topic. I had already studied that topic, so I offered to help. We sat together and went through the difficult parts step by step. After some practice, my friend understood the topic much better. I felt happy because I was able to help. It reminded me that sharing knowledge can benefit both people.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Story About an Unexpected Event

### Question:

> Tell me about something unexpected that happened to you.

### Answer:

> One evening, I was returning home when I suddenly realized that I had lost my wallet. I checked my bag several times, but I couldn't find it. At first, I was really worried because it contained some cash and important cards. I retraced my steps and eventually found it near a shop where I had stopped earlier. I felt extremely relieved. Since then, I've become much more careful about keeping my important belongings safe.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Complete 3-Minute Story

## Topic: **A Difficult Experience That Taught Me Something**

> I'd like to tell you about a difficult experience that taught me an important lesson.
>
> A while ago, I had to complete an important project within a short deadline. At first, I thought I had plenty of time, so I didn't plan my work properly. I spent too much time on small details and didn't focus enough on the most important tasks.
>
> As the deadline got closer, I realized that I had a lot of work left. At that point, I became quite stressed and worried that I wouldn't be able to finish everything on time.
>
> Instead of giving up, I decided to make a clear plan. I listed all the remaining tasks and prioritized them according to their importance. I also avoided unnecessary distractions and focused on one task at a time.
>
> Fortunately, I managed to complete the project before the deadline. I felt extremely relieved and proud of myself.
>
> Looking back, the experience taught me that good planning is just as important as hard work. I also learned that when we face a difficult situation, we shouldn't panic. We should stay calm, make a plan, and take action step by step.

---

# 🎯 Part 36 — 10 Core Storytelling Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. Tell me about a memorable experience.
2. Tell me about a difficult experience.
3. Tell me about a time you made a mistake.
4. Tell me about a time you helped someone.
5. Tell me about a time someone helped you.
6. Tell me about something you achieved.
7. Tell me about a time you failed.
8. Tell me about a time you were surprised.
9. Tell me about a time you solved a difficult problem.
10. Tell me about an experience that taught you an important lesson.

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Follow-up Questions

Story বলার পরে এই questions-এর উত্তর দিতে practice করবে:

> When did it happen?

> Where did it happen?

> Who was with you?

> What happened first?

> What happened next?

> How did you feel?

> What did you do?

> What happened in the end?

> What did you learn?

> Would you do anything differently?

---

# 🧩 Part 38 — Story Question Making

নিজে **10টি storytelling questions** তৈরি করো।

এই patterns ব্যবহার করো:

> Tell me about a time when...

> Can you describe a time when...?

> What happened when...?

> Have you ever...?

> What was the most memorable...?

> Tell me about an experience that...

> Describe a situation where...

> What did you do when...?

> How did you feel when...?

> What did you learn from...?

---

# 🎤 Part 39 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

আজকের topic:

# **“A Memorable Experience in My Life”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Background

> When?

> Where?

> Who?

### 2️⃣ Situation

> What were you doing?

### 3️⃣ Problem/Event

> Suddenly...

> Unfortunately...

### 4️⃣ Reaction

> I felt...

> At that point...

### 5️⃣ Action

> So I decided to...

### 6️⃣ Result

> Eventually...

> In the end...

### 7️⃣ Lesson

> The experience taught me...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 50 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → WHAT → PROBLEM → ACTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

### Short version:

> **At first → Then → Suddenly → So → Eventually → In the end → I learned that...**
