----
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 51

# 👤 Describing People & Personalities

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** একজন মানুষকে **appearance + personality + behavior + strengths + weaknesses + relationship + example** দিয়ে বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করা।

**Lesson 50-এর সাথে connection:**
Lesson 50-এ তুমি **storytelling** শিখেছ। এখন story বলার সময় **কোনো মানুষকে কীভাবে naturally describe করতে হয়**, সেটা শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 51-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* একজন মানুষের appearance describe করতে
* personality describe করতে
* positive/negative qualities বলতে
* behavior explain করতে
* strengths ও weaknesses বলতে
* relationship explain করতে
* real example দিতে
* একজন মানুষকে 2–3 মিনিট ধরে describe করতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — People Describe করার Formula

কাউকে describe করার সময় এই order follow করো:

# **WHO → APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → STRENGTHS → WEAKNESS → EXAMPLE → OPINION**

### Example:

> I'd like to describe one of my close friends. His name is Rahim. He's in his mid-twenties and has short black hair. He's quite friendly and easygoing. He's also hardworking and responsible. Whenever someone needs help, he's usually willing to help. One thing I really admire about him is his patience. However, he can sometimes be a little stubborn. Overall, I think he's a reliable and trustworthy person.

---

# 👤 Part 2 — WHO?

প্রথমে বলবে তুমি কাকে describe করছ।

### Useful expressions:

> I'd like to describe...

> The person I'd like to talk about is...

> I'm going to describe...

> One person I really admire is...

> One of my closest friends is...

---

### Examples:

> I'd like to describe one of my closest friends.

> The person I'd like to talk about is my older brother.

> One person I really admire is my teacher.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Relationship

মানুষটির সাথে তোমার relationship কী?

> my friend

> my best friend

> my classmate

> my colleague

> my teacher

> my neighbor

> my brother

> my sister

> my cousin

> my mentor

---

### Natural sentences:

> I've known him for several years.

> We've been friends since university.

> I met her when I was in school.

> We work together.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “I've Known Him/Her For...”

খুব useful structure:

# **I've known + person + for + period**

### Examples:

> I've known him for five years.

> I've known her for a long time.

### Since:

> I've known him since university.

> I've known her since childhood.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Appearance

Appearance describe করার সময়:

### Height

> tall

> short

> average height

### Body

> slim

> thin

> fit

> muscular

> well-built

### Hair

> short hair

> long hair

> curly hair

> straight hair

> black hair

> brown hair

### Face

> round face

> oval face

> sharp features

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Appearance Sentence

একসাথে অনেক adjective ব্যবহার করবে না।

### Natural:

> He's quite tall and has short black hair.

> She's of average height and has long, straight hair.

> He's fairly slim and has a friendly-looking face.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Age Describe করা

Exact age না জানলেও বলতে পারো:

> He's in his early twenties.

> She's in her mid-twenties.

> He's in his late thirties.

### Meaning:

**early twenties** → 20–23 approximately
**mid-twenties** → 24–26 approximately
**late twenties** → 27–29 approximately

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Personality

এটাই সবচেয়ে important part।

### Positive Personality

> friendly — বন্ধুসুলভ

> kind — দয়ালু

> helpful — সাহায্যপ্রবণ

> honest — সৎ

> reliable — নির্ভরযোগ্য

> responsible — দায়িত্বশীল

> hardworking — পরিশ্রমী

> confident — আত্মবিশ্বাসী

> patient — ধৈর্যশীল

> calm — শান্ত

> polite — ভদ্র

> generous — উদার

> thoughtful — অন্যের কথা বিবেচনা করে

> optimistic — আশাবাদী

> ambitious — উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী

> creative — সৃজনশীল

> intelligent — বুদ্ধিমান

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Negative Personality

সব মানুষ perfect নয়।

> shy — লাজুক

> quiet — চুপচাপ

> impatient — অধৈর্য

> stubborn — একগুঁয়ে

> careless — অসাবধান

> lazy — অলস

> selfish — স্বার্থপর

> moody — মেজাজ পরিবর্তনশীল

> nervous — নার্ভাস

> sensitive — সংবেদনশীল

> disorganized — অগোছালো

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “He Is” vs “He Has”

### Personality:

> He is friendly.

> She is confident.

> He is hardworking.

### Appearance:

> He has short hair.

> She has a round face.

❌

> He has friendly.

✅

> He is friendly.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Very” এর বদলে Better Words

Beginner:

> He is very good.

Intermediate:

> He is extremely helpful.

> She is incredibly patient.

> He is highly responsible.

> She's particularly creative.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Personality + Example

শুধু:

> He's helpful.

বলবে না।

Example যোগ করো:

> He's very helpful. Whenever I have a problem, he's always willing to help me.

এতে description অনেক natural হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Whenever”

**whenever = যখনই**

### Examples:

> Whenever I need help, I call him.

> Whenever someone needs advice, she tries to help.

> Whenever we have a problem, he stays calm.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Describing Behavior

Personality কীভাবে behavior-এ দেখা যায়:

> He always listens carefully.

> She treats everyone with respect.

> He never gives up easily.

> She always keeps her promises.

> He likes helping other people.

> She stays calm under pressure.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “Treat Someone”

**treat someone = কারো সাথে আচরণ করা**

### Examples:

> He treats everyone with respect.

> She treats her colleagues kindly.

> He treats people fairly.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Keep a Promise”

**keep a promise = প্রতিশ্রুতি রাখা**

> He always keeps his promises.

> She's someone who keeps her promises.

এটা **reliable** explain করার excellent way।

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Strengths

কোনো মানুষের strong points:

> One of his strengths is...

> One thing I admire about her is...

> His biggest strength is...

> She's particularly good at...

---

### Examples:

> One of his strengths is his ability to stay calm under pressure.

> One thing I admire about her is her determination.

> He's particularly good at solving problems.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Weaknesses

Politeভাবে weakness বলবে:

> One weakness is...

> One thing he could improve is...

> He sometimes tends to...

> He can be a little...

---

### Examples:

> He can be a little impatient sometimes.

> She sometimes tends to worry too much.

> One thing he could improve is his time management.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Tends To”

**tend to = সাধারণত এমন করার প্রবণতা থাকা**

### Examples:

> He tends to work late.

> She tends to worry too much.

> I tend to be quiet around new people.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “But” দিয়ে Balanced Description

একজন মানুষকে realisticভাবে describe করতে:

> He's very hardworking, but he can sometimes be a little impatient.

> She's friendly and confident, but she tends to worry too much.

এটা শুধু adjective list দেওয়ার চেয়ে অনেক better।

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “I Admire”

কাউকে respect করার জন্য:

> I admire him because...

> I really admire her for...

### Examples:

> I really admire him for his determination.

> I admire her because she never gives up.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “Look Up To”

**look up to = কাউকে শ্রদ্ধা/অনুপ্রেরণার মানুষ হিসেবে দেখা**

### Examples:

> I really look up to my father.

> Many students look up to their teachers.

> I look up to people who work hard and stay humble.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Get Along With”

**get along with = ভালো সম্পর্ক থাকা**

### Examples:

> I get along very well with my brother.

> We get along really well.

> I don't always get along with everyone.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Have Something in Common”

**have something in common = একই ধরনের কিছু পছন্দ/গুণ থাকা**

### Examples:

> We have a lot in common.

> We both enjoy technology, so we have a lot in common.

> We have similar interests.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Describing a Friend

### Question:

> Describe your best friend.

### Answer:

> I'd like to describe one of my closest friends. I've known him for several years. He's fairly tall and has short black hair. Personality-wise, he's friendly, reliable, and hardworking. He's also very easygoing, so it's easy to talk to him.
>
> One thing I really admire about him is his willingness to help others. Whenever I have a problem, he listens carefully and tries to help me find a solution. However, he can sometimes be a little stubborn.
>
> Overall, I consider him a trustworthy person, and I'm lucky to have him as a friend.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Describing a Teacher

### Question:

> Describe a teacher you admire.

### Answer:

> I'd like to talk about a teacher I really admire. He's a very knowledgeable and patient person. He's also friendly and approachable, so students don't feel nervous about asking questions.
>
> One of his greatest strengths is his ability to explain difficult topics in a simple way. He always encourages students to think for themselves instead of simply memorizing information.
>
> I really admire him because he's passionate about teaching and genuinely wants his students to succeed.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Describing a Family Member

### Question:

> Describe a family member you are close to.

### Answer:

> I'd like to describe my older brother. He's quite tall and has short black hair. He's calm, responsible, and hardworking. He's also very supportive whenever I need advice.
>
> One thing I appreciate about him is that he always listens before giving advice. However, he can sometimes be a little serious.
>
> Overall, he's someone I can trust, and I really value our relationship.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Describing Someone You Don't Like

Polite language ব্যবহার করবে।

❌

> He's a bad person.

Better:

> We don't get along very well.

> We have very different personalities.

> He can sometimes be difficult to deal with.

> I find him a little difficult to communicate with.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Difficult to Deal With”

**deal with = মোকাবিলা/ব্যবহার করা**

### Example:

> He can be difficult to deal with sometimes.

এটা direct insult না করে negative personality describe করার ভালো উপায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Personality Comparison

দুইজনকে compare:

> My brother is more outgoing than I am.

> My friend is more patient than me.

> I'm quieter, whereas my friend is more talkative.

---

### Useful:

> whereas

> while

> compared with

> unlike

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Outgoing” vs “Introverted”

### Outgoing

মানুষের সাথে সহজে কথা বলে, social।

> She's very outgoing and enjoys meeting new people.

### Introverted

চুপচাপ/নিজের মতো থাকতে পছন্দ করে।

> He's more introverted and prefers spending time alone.

**Note:** Introverted মানেই shy নয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “Easygoing”

**easygoing = relaxed, সহজে বিরক্ত হয় না**

> He's very easygoing.

> She's easygoing and doesn't worry about small things.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — “Approachable”

**approachable = যার কাছে সহজে যাওয়া/কথা বলা যায়**

> My teacher is very approachable.

> She's friendly and approachable.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Reliable”

**reliable = যার ওপর ভরসা করা যায়**

> He's very reliable.

আরও natural:

> He's someone I can always rely on.

### Example:

> Whenever I need help, I know I can rely on him.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “Supportive”

**supportive = উৎসাহ/সহায়তা করে**

> My family is very supportive.

> She's always supportive when I'm going through a difficult time.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — “Determined”

**determined = দৃঢ়প্রতিজ্ঞ**

> He's very determined.

> Once he sets a goal, he doesn't give up easily.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — “Humble”

**humble = বিনয়ী**

> Despite his success, he's very humble.

এই structure খুব useful:

# **Despite + noun**

> Despite his success, he remains humble.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Complete 3-Minute Answer

## Topic: **A Person I Really Admire**

> I'd like to talk about a person I really admire. He's someone I've known for several years, and I've always respected him because of his personality and attitude toward life.
>
> In terms of appearance, he's fairly tall and has short black hair. But his personality is much more interesting than his appearance. He's hardworking, responsible, patient, and very supportive. He's also quite easygoing, which makes him easy to talk to.
>
> One thing I really admire about him is his determination. When he sets a goal, he doesn't give up easily. Even when he faces difficulties, he stays calm and looks for a solution.
>
> He's not perfect, of course. Sometimes he can be a little stubborn, and he tends to spend too much time working. But I think his positive qualities are much stronger.
>
> I've learned a lot from him, especially the importance of staying consistent and not giving up when things become difficult. Overall, he's someone I really look up to, and I'm grateful to have him in my life.

---

# 🎯 Part 39 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. Describe your best friend.
2. Describe a family member you are close to.
3. Describe a teacher you admire.
4. Describe someone who inspires you.
5. Describe a person you respect.
6. Describe someone who has influenced you.
7. Describe a person with an interesting personality.
8. Describe someone who is very hardworking.
9. Describe someone who is difficult to deal with.
10. Describe a person you would like to meet.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Follow-up Questions

একজন মানুষ describe করার পরে:

> How long have you known him/her?

> What does he/she look like?

> What is his/her personality like?

> What do you like about him/her?

> What don't you like?

> What is his/her greatest strength?

> What is his/her weakness?

> Why do you admire him/her?

> How has this person influenced you?

> What have you learned from this person?

---

# 🧩 Part 41 — Question Making Practice

এই patterns ব্যবহার করে 10টা question বানাও:

> What does he/she look like?

> What is he/she like?

> What kind of person is he/she?

> What do you admire about...?

> How would you describe...?

> What is his/her greatest strength?

> What is his/her biggest weakness?

> How long have you known...?

> How has ... influenced you?

> What have you learned from...?

---

# 🎤 Part 42 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

আজকের topic:

# **“Describe a Person Who Has Influenced You”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Who?

> I'd like to talk about...

### 2️⃣ Relationship

> I've known him/her for...

### 3️⃣ Appearance

> In terms of appearance,...

### 4️⃣ Personality

> Personality-wise,...

### 5️⃣ Behavior

> Whenever...

### 6️⃣ Strength

> One thing I admire about...

### 7️⃣ Weakness

> However,...

### 8️⃣ Example

> For example,...

### 9️⃣ Influence

> This person has taught me...

### 🔟 Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 51 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHO → APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → STRENGTH → WEAKNESS → EXAMPLE → INFLUENCE → OPINION**

### Short version:

> **Who is the person? → What does he/she look like? → What is he/she like? → What does he/she do? → What do you admire? → Example → What did you learn?**

---

-------
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 52

# 🏙️ Describing Places & Locations

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কোনো **place/location**-কে শুধু *“It is beautiful”* না বলে **where → appearance → atmosphere → activities → people → advantages → personal experience → opinion** দিয়ে বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করা।

**Lesson 51-এর সাথে connection:**
Lesson 51-এ আমরা **মানুষকে কীভাবে describe করতে হয়** শিখেছি।
আজ একই technique ব্যবহার করে **place describe** করা শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 52-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো place-এর location বলতে
* place দেখতে কেমন বলতে
* atmosphere describe করতে
* সেখানে কী করা যায় বলতে
* মানুষ ও environment describe করতে
* positive/negative side বলতে
* নিজের experience বলতে
* familiar ও unfamiliar place describe করতে
* 2–3 মিনিট ধরে একটি place describe করতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Place Description Formula

যেকোনো place-এর জন্য এই formula follow করো:

# **WHERE → WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE → ATMOSPHERE → WHAT PEOPLE DO → SPECIAL FEATURES → ADVANTAGES → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about a place I really like. It's a quiet park near my home. It's surrounded by trees and has several walking paths. The atmosphere is peaceful, especially in the early morning. People usually go there to walk, exercise, or spend time with their families. One thing I particularly like is how clean and quiet it is. I often go there when I want to relax. Overall, it's one of my favorite places to spend some free time.

---

# 📍 Part 2 — WHERE?

প্রথমে location বলবে।

### Useful expressions:

> It's located in...

> It's near...

> It's close to...

> It's not far from...

> It's about 10 kilometers from...

> It's in the center of...

> It's on the outskirts of...

---

### Examples:

> It's located in Dhaka.

> It's near my university.

> It's close to the city center.

> It's not far from my home.

> It's about 20 minutes from my house.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — “Located”

**located = অবস্থিত**

### Examples:

> The restaurant is located near the main road.

> The park is located in the center of the city.

> The village is located beside a river.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Direction & Position

Useful words:

> north of

> south of

> east of

> west of

> beside

> next to

> opposite

> behind

> in front of

> between

> near

> close to

---

### Examples:

> The school is next to the mosque.

> The hotel is opposite the shopping mall.

> The park is between two busy roads.

> The village is beside a river.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — “Surrounded By”

Very useful place-description expression:

# **be surrounded by + noun**

### Examples:

> The village is surrounded by green fields.

> The house is surrounded by trees.

> The lake is surrounded by hills.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — What Does It Look Like?

Place-এর appearance describe করো।

Useful adjectives:

> beautiful

> attractive

> modern

> traditional

> crowded

> spacious

> small

> large

> clean

> colorful

> peaceful

> lively

> quiet

> busy

> well-designed

---

### Examples:

> It's a modern and spacious building.

> It's a small but beautiful village.

> The area is clean and well-maintained.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “It Has...”

Place-এর features বলতে:

> It has...

### Examples:

> It has a large garden.

> It has several restaurants.

> It has beautiful walking paths.

> It has a large parking area.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — “There Is / There Are”

### Singular:

> There is a large lake.

### Plural:

> There are several restaurants.

---

### Example:

> There is a large playground, and there are several small shops nearby.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Atmosphere

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশের অনুভূতি**

Very important for advanced description.

Useful words:

> peaceful

> relaxing

> lively

> energetic

> friendly

> crowded

> noisy

> calm

> welcoming

> pleasant

> stressful

---

### Examples:

> The atmosphere is very peaceful.

> It's usually lively in the evening.

> The atmosphere becomes quite crowded during holidays.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “Especially”

একটা particular time highlight করতে:

> especially in the morning

> especially at night

> especially during weekends

> especially during winter

### Example:

> The park is especially beautiful in the early morning.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — What People Do There

Place description-এ activity বলবে।

### Useful verbs:

> visit

> walk

> relax

> exercise

> shop

> eat

> take photos

> meet friends

> spend time

> play

> explore

> study

---

### Examples:

> People usually go there to relax.

> Visitors often take photos.

> Families spend time there during weekends.

> Tourists come to explore the area.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — “It's Popular For...”

### Examples:

> The place is popular for its beautiful scenery.

> It's popular for its food.

> The area is popular for shopping.

> The city is popular for its historical buildings.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — “Known For”

**be known for = কোনো কিছুর জন্য পরিচিত**

### Examples:

> Dhaka is known for its busy streets.

> The area is known for its restaurants.

> Cox's Bazar is known for its long beach.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “Famous For”

> The city is famous for its historical sites.

> The restaurant is famous for its traditional food.

> The place is famous for its natural beauty.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Natural Beauty

Nature-related vocabulary:

> scenery

> landscape

> greenery

> mountains

> hills

> river

> lake

> beach

> forest

> waterfall

> countryside

> fields

---

### Example:

> The scenery is absolutely beautiful.

> The area has a lot of greenery.

> The landscape is surrounded by hills.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — “Scenery”

**scenery = প্রাকৃতিক দৃশ্য**

### Example:

> I really enjoyed the beautiful scenery.

> The mountain scenery was breathtaking.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — “Breathtaking”

**breathtaking = অত্যন্ত সুন্দর/মুগ্ধকর**

### Examples:

> The view was breathtaking.

> The scenery was absolutely breathtaking.

এটি **beautiful**-এর advanced alternative।

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — “View”

**view = দৃশ্য / কোনো দিক থেকে দেখা দৃশ্য**

### Examples:

> The hotel has a beautiful view of the sea.

> We had a great view of the city.

> The view from the hill was amazing.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Describe a City

City describe করার formula:

# **LOCATION → SIZE → PEOPLE → TRANSPORT → BUILDINGS → FOOD → ACTIVITIES → PROBLEMS → OPINION**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about Dhaka, where I live. It's a large and busy city with millions of people. It's known for its busy roads, diverse food, and vibrant lifestyle. There are many universities, shopping centers, restaurants, and offices. The city is very lively, especially during the day. However, traffic congestion and air pollution can be major problems. Despite these challenges, I enjoy living there because there are many opportunities for education and work.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Crowded

**crowded = অনেক মানুষ/ভিড়**

### Examples:

> The market is usually crowded.

> The city center gets very crowded during the evening.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “Busy” vs “Crowded”

### Busy:

অনেক activity হচ্ছে।

> The street is busy.

### Crowded:

অনেক মানুষ/vehicle আছে।

> The street is crowded.

একসাথে:

> The city center is busy and crowded.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Quiet vs Peaceful

### Quiet

শব্দ কম:

> The neighborhood is quiet.

### Peaceful

মনকে শান্ত করে:

> The village is peaceful.

### Natural:

> It's a quiet and peaceful place.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “Off the Beaten Path”

Advanced phrase:

**off the beaten path = সাধারণ tourist route-এর বাইরে/কম পরিচিত জায়গা**

### Example:

> It's a beautiful place that's slightly off the beaten path.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “Hidden Gem”

**hidden gem = কম পরিচিত কিন্তু অসাধারণ সুন্দর place**

### Example:

> It's a hidden gem that not many tourists know about.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Advantages & Disadvantages of a Place

Lesson 48–49-এর skill ব্যবহার করো।

### Advantage:

> One major advantage is...

> One thing I like about the place is...

### Disadvantage:

> One drawback is...

> One problem is...

---

### Example:

> One major advantage of living in the city is easy access to services. However, one major drawback is traffic congestion.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Personal Experience

Place description-এ নিজের experience যোগ করলে answer strong হয়।

### Useful:

> I first visited this place when...

> I've been there several times.

> I usually go there when...

> I remember visiting it with...

> The last time I went there...

---

### Example:

> I first visited the place when I was a child, and I've been there several times since then.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — “I Usually Go There When...”

Natural speaking structure:

> I usually go there when I want to relax.

> I usually go there when I need some fresh air.

> I usually visit the place when I have free time.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Describe a Favorite Place

### Question:

> Describe your favorite place.

### Answer:

> I'd like to talk about one of my favorite places. It's a quiet park near my home. It's surrounded by trees and has several walking paths. The atmosphere is peaceful, especially in the early morning.
>
> People usually go there to walk, exercise, or spend time with their families. I usually visit the park when I want to relax or clear my mind.
>
> One thing I really like about it is that it's clean and not too crowded. The only drawback is that it can become busy on weekends.
>
> Overall, it's a simple place, but I really enjoy spending time there because it helps me feel relaxed.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Describe a Tourist Place

### Question:

> Describe a tourist place you have visited.

### Answer:

> I'd like to talk about a tourist place I visited a few years ago. It was a beautiful coastal area with a long sandy beach. The scenery was amazing, and the atmosphere was lively, especially in the evening.
>
> There were many restaurants, hotels, and small shops nearby. Tourists were walking along the beach, taking photos, and enjoying the view.
>
> What I enjoyed most was the natural scenery and the peaceful sound of the sea. I also tried some local food with my friends.
>
> Overall, it was a memorable experience, and I'd definitely like to visit the place again.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Describe Your Hometown

### Question:

> Describe your hometown.

### Structure:

> My hometown is...

> It's located...

> It's known for...

> The area is...

> People usually...

> One thing I like...

> One problem...

> Overall...

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Describe Your University

### Question:

> Describe your university.

### Answer:

> I'd like to describe my university. It's a modern campus with several academic buildings, classrooms, laboratories, and recreational areas. The campus is generally clean and well-maintained.
>
> The atmosphere is usually busy during class hours, but it becomes much quieter in the afternoon. Students spend their time attending classes, studying, working on projects, and socializing with friends.
>
> One thing I particularly like is the learning environment. However, like any large institution, there are some areas that could be improved.
>
> Overall, my university has given me many opportunities to learn and develop new skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Describe a Place You Want to Visit

এখানে **future + imagination** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Question:

> Describe a place you would like to visit.

### Answer:

> I'd love to visit Japan someday. I've always been interested in its culture, technology, food, and natural scenery. I'd like to visit Tokyo, explore some traditional areas, and try local food.
>
> I'd also love to see Mount Fuji because I've seen many beautiful pictures of it. I think the trip would give me an opportunity to experience a completely different culture.
>
> If I get the chance, I'd definitely like to spend at least two weeks there.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — If I Get the Chance

Future possibility:

> If I get the chance, I'd definitely visit Japan.

আরও:

> If I get the opportunity, I'd like to travel there.

> If I ever get the chance, I'd love to explore the area.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Comparing Places

Lesson 49-এর debate skill ব্যবহার করো।

### Example:

> Dhaka is much busier than my hometown.

> My hometown is quieter, whereas Dhaka is more crowded.

> Compared with the city, the countryside is more peaceful.

> Unlike Dhaka, the countryside has less traffic.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Complete 3-Minute Answer

## Topic: **A Place I Really Like**

> I'd like to talk about a place I really like. It's a quiet area near my home that I often visit when I need some time to relax.
>
> The place is surrounded by trees and has a small lake nearby. There are several walking paths, and the area is generally clean and well-maintained. The atmosphere is particularly peaceful in the early morning, when there aren't many people around.
>
> People usually go there to walk, exercise, take photos, or spend time with their families. I usually go there when I feel stressed or when I simply want some fresh air.
>
> One thing I really like about the place is its peaceful environment. Unlike the busy parts of the city, it doesn't have much traffic or noise. However, it can become quite crowded during weekends.
>
> I've been visiting this place for several years, so I have many good memories there. Looking back, it's not a famous tourist destination or anything extraordinary, but it's special to me because it gives me a chance to relax and clear my mind.
>
> Overall, I think a good place doesn't necessarily have to be famous or expensive. Sometimes, a simple and peaceful place can be the most valuable.

---

# 🎯 Part 36 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. Describe your hometown.
2. Describe your favorite place.
3. Describe a place you have visited.
4. Describe a tourist attraction.
5. Describe your university.
6. Describe your neighborhood.
7. Describe a place you go to relax.
8. Describe a place you would like to visit.
9. Describe a beautiful natural place.
10. Describe a busy place you have visited.

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Follow-up Questions

একটা place describe করার পরে এগুলোর উত্তর দেবে:

> Where is it located?

> What does it look like?

> What is the atmosphere like?

> What do people do there?

> What is it famous for?

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you go there?

> Who do you usually go with?

> What are the advantages?

> What are the disadvantages?

> Would you like to visit it again?

---

# 🧩 Part 38 — Question Making Practice

এই patterns ব্যবহার করে 10টা question বানাও:

> Where is... located?

> What does ... look like?

> What is ... like?

> What is ... known for?

> What can people do there?

> Why do you like...?

> How often do you visit...?

> What is special about...?

> What are the advantages of...?

> Would you like to visit... again?

---

# 🎤 Part 39 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

## Topic:

# **“Describe a Place You Really Like”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Location

> It's located...

### 2️⃣ Appearance

> It's surrounded by...

### 3️⃣ Atmosphere

> The atmosphere is...

### 4️⃣ Activities

> People usually...

### 5️⃣ Special feature

> One thing I particularly like...

### 6️⃣ Experience

> I first visited...

### 7️⃣ Advantage

> One major advantage...

### 8️⃣ Disadvantage

> However,...

### 9️⃣ Feeling

> I feel...

### 🔟 Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 52 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHERE → LOOKS → ATMOSPHERE → ACTIVITIES → SPECIAL → ADVANTAGE → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### Short version:

> **Where is it? → What does it look like? → What's the atmosphere like? → What do people do there? → Why is it special? → What's your experience? → Why do you like it?**

---

→ অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে discussion

**Lesson 51 — Describing People & Personalities**
→ মানুষকে detailedভাবে describe করা

↓

# **Lesson 52 — Describing Places & Locations**

→ **কোনো place/location-কে detailedভাবে describe করা**

↓

→ *কোনো object/product কী, দেখতে কেমন, কীভাবে কাজ করে, কী কাজে ব্যবহার হয়, features, advantages/disadvantages এবং personal opinion দিয়ে describe করা।*


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 53

# 📦 Describing Objects, Things & Products

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কোনো **object / thing / product**-কে শুধু *“It is good”* না বলে **what it is → appearance → material → features → use → advantages → disadvantages → experience → opinion** দিয়ে বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করা।

### 🔗 Previous lesson connection

**Lesson 51 — People** → মানুষ describe
**Lesson 52 — Places** → place describe
**Lesson 53 — Objects/Products** → জিনিস/product describe

এখন তুমি **person + place + thing**—তিন ধরনের description করতে পারবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 53-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো object কী তা বলতে
* দেখতে কেমন বলতে
* size/shape/color বলতে
* কী দিয়ে তৈরি বলতে
* কীভাবে কাজ করে বলতে
* কী কাজে ব্যবহার হয় বলতে
* features বলতে
* advantages/disadvantages বলতে
* নিজের experience বলতে
* product recommend করতে
* 2–3 মিনিট ধরে কোনো object describe করতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Object Description Formula

যেকোনো object/product-এর জন্য:

# **WHAT → APPEARANCE → SIZE/SHAPE → MATERIAL → FEATURES → USE → ADVANTAGES → DISADVANTAGES → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### Example:

> I'd like to describe my laptop. It's a personal computer that I use mainly for studying and programming. It's relatively thin and lightweight, with a large screen and a simple design. It's made mostly of metal and plastic. One of its main features is its fast processor and enough storage for my work. I use it for coding, browsing the internet, watching videos, and studying. One major advantage is its portability. However, the battery doesn't last very long when I run heavy programs. Overall, it's a very useful device for my daily work.

---

# 🔹 Part 2 — What Is It?

প্রথমে object-এর পরিচয় দেবে।

### Useful structures:

> It's a...

> It's a type of...

> It's basically a...

> It's a device used for...

> It's something that...

---

### Examples:

> It's a smartphone.

> It's a device used for communication.

> It's basically a portable computer.

> It's something I use every day.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “It's Used For...”

Purpose বলার সবচেয়ে useful structure:

# **It's used for + noun / verb-ing**

### Examples:

> It's used for communication.

> It's used for storing data.

> It's used for studying.

> It's used for making phone calls.

---

### ❌ Incorrect:

> It's used for study.

### ✅ Better:

> It's used for studying.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “I Use It To...”

নিজের usage বলতে:

# **I use it to + verb**

### Examples:

> I use it to study.

> I use it to browse the internet.

> I use it to communicate with my friends.

> I use it to edit videos.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Appearance

Object দেখতে কেমন?

Useful adjectives:

> small

> large

> big

> compact

> lightweight

> heavy

> thin

> thick

> modern

> simple

> stylish

> attractive

> colorful

> plain

> elegant

---

### Examples:

> It's small and lightweight.

> It has a simple and modern design.

> It's relatively thin.

> It's compact enough to carry around easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Size

Useful expressions:

> It's quite small.

> It's relatively large.

> It's medium-sized.

> It's about 20 centimeters long.

> It's small enough to fit in my pocket.

---

### Advanced:

> It's compact enough to carry around easily.

**enough to + verb**

> It's light enough to carry with one hand.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Shape

Useful words:

> round

> square

> rectangular

> oval

> flat

> curved

> cylindrical

---

### Examples:

> It's rectangular in shape.

> It has a flat surface.

> The edges are slightly curved.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Color

Simple:

> It's black.

> It's dark blue.

> It's silver.

Better:

> It's mainly black with a silver finish.

> It's available in several colors.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Material

কী দিয়ে তৈরি?

> plastic

> metal

> glass

> wood

> leather

> cotton

> steel

> aluminum

> rubber

---

### Structures:

> It's made of...

> It's made from...

> It has a...

### Examples:

> It's made of metal.

> The body is made of plastic.

> It has a glass screen.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Made of vs Made from

### Made of

Original material easily recognizable:

> The table is made of wood.

### Made from

Material significantly changed:

> Paper is made from wood.

For everyday speaking, **made of** is very common for objects.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Features

Product description-এর সবচেয়ে important part।

### Useful expressions:

> One of its main features is...

> Another useful feature is...

> It comes with...

> It has...

> It includes...

> It allows me to...

---

### Examples:

> One of its main features is its large screen.

> It comes with a fast charger.

> It has a fingerprint sensor.

> It allows me to store a lot of files.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “It Comes With”

**comes with = সাথে পাওয়া যায়**

### Examples:

> The phone comes with a charger.

> The laptop comes with a carrying case.

> The software comes with several useful tools.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “It Allows Me To...”

Very useful advanced structure:

# **It allows me to + verb**

### Examples:

> It allows me to work from anywhere.

> It allows me to store large files.

> It allows me to communicate easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “It Helps Me...”

### Examples:

> It helps me save time.

> It helps me stay organized.

> It helps me work more efficiently.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Function

Object কীভাবে কাজ করে?

Useful:

> It works by...

> It works through...

> You can use it to...

> It operates using...

### Example:

> The device works by connecting to the internet.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Simple Function Explanation

### Question:

> How does your smartphone help you?

### Answer:

> I use my smartphone for communication, studying, entertainment, and many other tasks. It allows me to access information quickly and stay connected with other people.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Advantages

Lesson 48–49-এর skill ব্যবহার করো।

> One major advantage is...

> One of the best things about it is...

> Another benefit is...

### Example:

> One major advantage is that it's lightweight and easy to carry.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Disadvantages

> One disadvantage is...

> One drawback is...

> The main problem is...

> The only downside is...

### Example:

> The main drawback is that the battery doesn't last very long.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Downside”

**downside = negative side**

> The main downside is its price.

> One downside is that it's quite heavy.

Very natural in conversation.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Expensive vs Affordable

### Expensive:

> It's quite expensive.

> It's relatively expensive.

> It's a bit pricey.

### Affordable:

> It's affordable.

> It's reasonably priced.

> It's good value for money.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Good Value for Money”

Meaning:

**দাম অনুযায়ী ভালো product**

### Example:

> It's not the cheapest option, but I think it's good value for money.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Quality

Useful words:

> high-quality

> durable

> reliable

> strong

> well-made

> long-lasting

---

### Examples:

> It's a well-made product.

> The build quality is excellent.

> It's quite durable.

> It's reliable for everyday use.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Durable

**durable = দীর্ঘদিন টেকে**

> It's made of durable materials.

> The product is lightweight but durable.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Reliable

**reliable = নির্ভরযোগ্য**

> It's very reliable.

> I've been using it for years, and it's still reliable.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — User Experience

Product describe করতে নিজের experience দাও।

### Useful expressions:

> I've been using it for...

> I've had it for...

> I bought it...

> I use it every day.

> I've found it very useful.

---

### Examples:

> I've been using this laptop for two years.

> I've had this phone for about a year.

> I've found it very useful for my studies.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “I've Been Using...”

খুব important:

# **I've been using + object + for + time**

### Examples:

> I've been using this laptop for three years.

> I've been using this app for several months.

এখানে **Present Perfect Continuous** ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “I've Had It For...”

### Examples:

> I've had this phone for two years.

> I've had this watch for a long time.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Recommend করা

কাউকে product recommend করতে:

> I'd recommend it because...

> I would definitely recommend it.

> I wouldn't recommend it if...

> It's a good choice for...

---

### Example:

> I'd recommend this laptop to students because it's lightweight and powerful enough for everyday tasks.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “It’s Suitable For...”

**suitable for = উপযুক্ত**

### Examples:

> It's suitable for students.

> It's suitable for beginners.

> It's suitable for professional use.

> It's not really suitable for heavy gaming.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “Ideal For”

**ideal for = খুব উপযুক্ত**

> It's ideal for students.

> It's ideal for everyday use.

> It's ideal for people who travel frequently.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Compare Products

Lesson 49-এর comparison ব্যবহার করো।

### Example:

> This phone is cheaper than that one.

> This model is lighter, whereas the other one is more powerful.

> Compared with my old laptop, this one is much faster.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “Compared With”

### Example:

> Compared with my old phone, this one has a much better camera.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — “Whereas”

দুই product-এর difference:

> This laptop is lightweight, whereas my old one was quite heavy.

> This phone is affordable, whereas the premium model is much more expensive.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Problem With a Product

### Question:

> What's one problem with your phone?

### Answer:

> The main problem is the battery. It drains quite quickly when I use the phone for a long time.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Solving Product Problems

Lesson 47-এর problem-solving skill:

> I had a problem with...

> At first,...

> Then I...

> Eventually,...

### Example:

> I had a problem with my laptop overheating. At first, I didn't know what was causing it. Then I cleaned the cooling system and reduced some background processes. Eventually, the problem improved.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Describe a Smartphone

### Question:

> Describe your smartphone.

### Answer:

> I'd like to describe my smartphone. It's a modern device that I use every day for communication, studying, entertainment, and work.
>
> It's relatively thin and lightweight, with a large touchscreen. It has a good camera, a powerful processor, and enough storage for my everyday needs.
>
> One of its main advantages is its portability. I can use it almost anywhere to access information, communicate with people, and manage different tasks. It also allows me to take photos, watch videos, and use various apps.
>
> However, one drawback is that the battery drains fairly quickly when I use it heavily.
>
> I've been using it for about a year, and overall, I've found it very useful. I'd recommend a similar phone to anyone who wants a reliable device for everyday use.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Describe a Laptop

### Question:

> Describe your laptop/computer.

### Answer:

> I'd like to describe my laptop. It's a portable computer that I mainly use for programming, studying, browsing the internet, and watching videos.
>
> It's relatively thin and lightweight, and it has a large screen and a comfortable keyboard. The design is simple and modern.
>
> One of its main advantages is its performance. It can handle my everyday programming tasks without too many problems. It also has enough storage for my files and projects.
>
> The main drawback is that the battery life isn't very good when I'm running heavy applications.
>
> I've been using it for several years, and it has become an important part of my daily routine. Overall, I think it's a reliable and useful device.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Describe Something You Use Every Day

### Question:

> Describe something you use every day.

Possible answers:

* smartphone
* laptop
* keyboard
* watch
* backpack
* headphones
* notebook
* water bottle

### Formula:

> I use it every day because...

> It's...

> It has...

> I use it for...

> One advantage...

> One drawback...

> Overall...

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Describe a Useful Product

### Question:

> What is the most useful product you own?

### Answer:

> One of the most useful things I own is my laptop. I use it every day for studying, programming, communication, and entertainment. It saves me a lot of time and allows me to work from almost anywhere. I've also learned many new skills using it. Although it was relatively expensive, I think it was a worthwhile investment.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “Worthwhile Investment”

**worthwhile investment = এমন কিছুতে টাকা/সময় দেওয়া যা long-term benefit দেয়**

### Example:

> Buying a good laptop was a worthwhile investment for me.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Describe a Product You Want to Buy

এখানে **future + reason + features** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Example:

> I'd like to buy a new laptop in the future. I'm looking for something lightweight but powerful enough for programming and other demanding tasks. I'd prefer a model with good battery life and enough RAM and storage. My main priority is performance, but I also want something durable. If I find a model that offers good value for money, I'll probably buy it.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Product Recommendation

### Question:

> Would you recommend your phone to others?

### Answer:

> Yes, I would. I'd recommend it mainly because it's reliable, easy to use, and has good performance. However, I wouldn't recommend it to someone who needs extremely long battery life.

Notice:

**recommend + positive + limitation**

এতে answer balanced হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Complete 3-Minute Answer

## Topic: **Describe a Product You Use Every Day**

> I'd like to describe a product that I use every day—my laptop. It's a portable computer that I mainly use for studying, programming, browsing the internet, and entertainment.
>
> In terms of appearance, it's relatively thin and lightweight. It has a large screen, a comfortable keyboard, and a simple design. The body is mostly made of metal and plastic.
>
> One of its main features is its good performance. It allows me to work on programming projects, browse the internet, attend online classes, and store a large number of files. I also use it to watch videos and communicate with other people.
>
> One major advantage is its versatility. I can use the same device for study, work, and entertainment. Another benefit is that it's portable, so I can carry it with me when necessary.
>
> However, there are some disadvantages. The battery doesn't last as long when I'm running heavy programs, and the device can become warm during intensive use.
>
> I've been using it for several years, and I've found it very useful. It has helped me learn many technical skills and complete different projects.
>
> Overall, I think it's one of the most useful things I own, and I'd definitely recommend having a good laptop to anyone who needs a device for study or work.

---

# 🎯 Part 44 — 10 Core Speaking Questions

আজকের most important questions:

1. Describe your smartphone.
2. Describe your laptop/computer.
3. Describe something you use every day.
4. Describe a useful product you own.
5. Describe something you bought recently.
6. Describe a product you would like to buy.
7. Describe a gift you received.
8. Describe something that is important to you.
9. Describe a product you would recommend.
10. Describe something that has made your life easier.

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — Follow-up Questions

Object/product নিয়ে এগুলো practice করবে:

> What is it?

> What does it look like?

> What is it made of?

> How big is it?

> What is it used for?

> How does it work?

> What are its main features?

> What do you like about it?

> What don't you like about it?

> Would you recommend it?

---

# 🧩 Part 46 — Question Making Practice

এই patterns ব্যবহার করে 10টা question তৈরি করো:

> What is...?

> What does ... look like?

> What is ... made of?

> What is it used for?

> How does it work?

> What are its main features?

> What are the advantages of...?

> What are the disadvantages of...?

> How long have you used...?

> Would you recommend...?

---

# 🎤 Part 47 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

## Topic:

# **“Describe a Useful Product You Own”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Introduction

> I'd like to describe...

### 2️⃣ What?

> It's a...

### 3️⃣ Appearance

> It's...

> It has...

### 4️⃣ Material

> It's made of...

### 5️⃣ Features

> One of its main features is...

### 6️⃣ Usage

> I use it to...

### 7️⃣ Advantages

> One major advantage is...

### 8️⃣ Disadvantages

> However,...

### 9️⃣ Experience

> I've been using it for...

### 🔟 Opinion

> Overall,...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 53 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → LOOKS → SIZE → MATERIAL → FEATURES → USE → ADVANTAGES → DISADVANTAGES → EXPERIENCE → OPINION**

### Short version:

> **What is it? → What does it look like? → What is it made of? → What does it do? → How do you use it? → What's good/bad about it? → What's your experience?**

---

# 🚀 Speaking Upgrade

### Beginner:

> This is my phone. It is black. I use it every day.

### Intermediate:

> This is my smartphone. It's lightweight and has a large screen. I use it for communication, studying, and entertainment.

### Advanced:

> I'd like to describe a smartphone that I use on a daily basis. It's relatively lightweight and has a modern design. One of its main advantages is its versatility, as it allows me to communicate, study, access information, and manage different tasks from almost anywhere. Although the battery life could be better, I've found it to be a reliable device overall.

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 54

# 🛠️ Daily Problems & Real-Life Situations

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** বাস্তব জীবনে কোনো **problem হলে কীভাবে English-এ explain, ask for help, complain, request, apologize, clarify এবং solve** করতে হয়।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর সাথে connection

**Lesson 51 — People** → মানুষ describe
**Lesson 52 — Places** → place describe
**Lesson 53 — Objects/Products** → জিনিস describe
**Lesson 54 — Real-Life Situations** → এখন সেই **people + places + objects** ব্যবহার করে বাস্তব জীবনের conversation handle করা।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 54-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো problem explain করতে
* সাহায্য চাইতে
* polite request করতে
* complaint করতে
* ভুল হলে apologize করতে
* clarification চাইতে
* দোকানে problem বলতে
* lost item নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* directions চাইতে
* appointment করতে
* service problem explain করতে
* solution suggest করতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Real-Life Problem Formula

কোনো problem হলে এই structure মনে রাখো:

# **SITUATION → PROBLEM → DETAILS → REQUEST → SOLUTION → THANKS**

### Example:

> Excuse me. I have a problem with my phone. The screen isn't working properly. It started this morning. Could you please take a look at it? If possible, I'd like to get it repaired. Thank you.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Problem শুরু করার Expressions

কোনো problem explain করার সময়:

> Excuse me, I have a problem.

> I'm having a problem with...

> There's a problem with...

> I'm having some trouble with...

> Something seems to be wrong with...

---

### Examples:

> I'm having a problem with my internet.

> There's a problem with my order.

> Something seems to be wrong with my laptop.

> I'm having some trouble with this machine.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “I'm Having Trouble With...”

খুব useful structure:

# **I'm having trouble with + noun**

### Examples:

> I'm having trouble with my internet connection.

> I'm having trouble with my account.

> I'm having trouble with this application.

---

### Verb হলে:

# **I'm having trouble + verb-ing**

> I'm having trouble connecting to the internet.

> I'm having trouble logging into my account.

> I'm having trouble understanding this question.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “Something Is Wrong With...”

কোনো জিনিস কাজ না করলে:

> Something is wrong with my phone.

> Something is wrong with the computer.

> Something seems to be wrong with the printer.

আরও natural:

> Something seems to be wrong with my internet connection.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Problem কখন শুরু হয়েছে?

Problem-এর timeline বলবে:

> It started this morning.

> It started yesterday.

> It happened a few hours ago.

> I've been having this problem for two days.

> This has been happening since yesterday.

---

### Important:

**for + duration**

> for two days

> for three hours

**since + starting point**

> since yesterday

> since Monday

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Asking for Help

সরাসরি:

> Help me.

এটা rude/sharp শোনাতে পারে।

Better:

> Could you please help me?

> Would you mind helping me?

> Could you give me a hand?

> I was wondering if you could help me.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Could You Please...?”

Polite request:

# **Could you please + verb?**

### Examples:

> Could you please help me?

> Could you please check this?

> Could you please explain it again?

> Could you please show me how to use it?

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Would You Mind...?”

# **Would you mind + verb-ing?**

### Examples:

> Would you mind helping me?

> Would you mind checking this?

> Would you mind opening the window?

### Important:

❌ Would you mind to help me?

✅ Would you mind helping me?

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “I Was Wondering If...”

এটি খুব polite।

> I was wondering if you could help me.

> I was wondering if you could check my order.

> I was wondering if you could explain this.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Asking for Clarification

কিছু বুঝতে না পারলে:

> Sorry, I didn't understand.

> Could you repeat that?

> Could you say that again?

> Could you speak a little more slowly?

> What do you mean by that?

> Could you explain what you mean?

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Could You Repeat That?”

কথোপকথনে খুব important:

> Sorry, could you repeat that?

> I'm sorry, I didn't catch that.

**I didn't catch that** = আমি কথাটা শুনতে/বুঝতে পারিনি।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Asking Someone to Speak Slowly

> Could you speak a little more slowly?

> Would you mind speaking a little slower?

### Don't say:

> Speak slowly.

এটা command-এর মতো শোনাতে পারে।

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Asking Directions

রাস্তা জিজ্ঞেস করতে:

> Excuse me, could you tell me how to get to the station?

> How can I get to the hospital?

> Is there a bank near here?

> Could you tell me where the nearest ATM is?

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Directions Vocabulary

> turn left

> turn right

> go straight

> cross the road

> take the first left

> take the second right

> next to

> opposite

> behind

> in front of

> at the corner

---

### Example:

> Go straight for about 500 meters, then turn left. The bank is on your right.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Asking for a Location

### Example:

> Excuse me, where is the nearest pharmacy?

> Could you tell me where the bus station is?

> Is this the way to the university?

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Shopping Problem

দোকানে product problem হলে:

> Excuse me, I bought this yesterday, but it's not working properly.

> I'd like to return this.

> I'd like to exchange this.

> Could I get a refund?

> Do you have a different size?

> Could I try this on?

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Return vs Exchange vs Refund

### Return

Product ফেরত দেওয়া:

> I'd like to return this.

### Exchange

অন্য product/size নেওয়া:

> I'd like to exchange this for a different size.

### Refund

টাকা ফেরত:

> Could I get a refund?

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Wrong Product

### Situation:

তুমি অন্য product পেয়েছ।

> Excuse me, I think there's been a mistake. I ordered a black shirt, but I received a blue one.

আরও:

> I ordered size L, but I received size M.

> This isn't what I ordered.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Damaged Product

> Unfortunately, the product arrived damaged.

> There's a crack on the screen.

> The product was damaged when I received it.

> Could you replace it?

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Restaurant Problem

খাবারের problem হলে:

> Excuse me, I didn't order this.

> I'm sorry, but my food is cold.

> I ordered chicken, but this is fish.

> Could you please replace it?

> Could I speak to the manager?

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Polite Complaint

Complaint মানে rude হওয়া নয়।

Formula:

# **POLITE OPENING → PROBLEM → DETAILS → REQUEST**

### Example:

> Excuse me. I'm sorry to bother you, but there's a problem with my order. I ordered this about 30 minutes ago, but I still haven't received it. Could you please check on it?

Excellent real-life English।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “I'm Sorry to Bother You”

Polite interruption:

> I'm sorry to bother you, but could you help me?

> Sorry to bother you, but I have a question.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Hotel Problem

### Example:

> Excuse me, there's a problem with my room. The air conditioner isn't working.

> The room hasn't been cleaned yet.

> There's no hot water.

> Could someone please take a look at it?

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Phone/Internet Problem

### Example:

> I'm having trouble with my internet connection.

> The Wi-Fi keeps disconnecting.

> The connection is very slow.

> I've been having this problem since yesterday.

> Could you please check the connection?

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Keeps + Verb-ing”

কোনো problem বারবার হলে:

# **keep + verb-ing**

### Examples:

> The Wi-Fi keeps disconnecting.

> My computer keeps freezing.

> The app keeps crashing.

> The phone keeps restarting.

এটি real-life English-এর খুব useful pattern।

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Computer Problem

### Example:

> I'm having a problem with my computer. It keeps freezing whenever I open a large program. I've already restarted it, but the problem is still there. Could you please take a look at it?

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Lost Item

কিছু হারিয়ে গেলে:

> I've lost my phone.

> I can't find my wallet.

> I think I left my bag somewhere.

> I may have left it on the bus.

> Has anyone found a black backpack?

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Reporting a Lost Item

### Example:

> Excuse me, I think I've lost my phone. It's a black Samsung phone with a black case. I think I may have left it in the waiting area. Could you please check if anyone has handed it in?

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Hand It In”

**hand something in = কর্তৃপক্ষের কাছে জমা দেওয়া**

### Example:

> Has anyone handed in a lost phone?

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Appointment

Appointment নিতে:

> I'd like to make an appointment.

> I'd like to book an appointment.

> Do you have any available appointments tomorrow?

> What time would be convenient?

> I'd like to reschedule my appointment.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Cancel / Reschedule

### Cancel:

> I'd like to cancel my appointment.

### Reschedule:

> I'd like to reschedule my appointment.

### Example:

> Something came up, so I'd like to reschedule my appointment.

**Something came up** = হঠাৎ জরুরি কিছু হয়েছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Asking Permission

### Informal:

> Can I...?

### Polite:

> Could I...?

### Formal:

> Would it be possible to...?

---

### Examples:

> Could I use your phone?

> Could I sit here?

> Would it be possible to change my appointment?

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Apologizing

Common expressions:

> I'm sorry.

> I'm really sorry.

> I apologize.

> I'm sorry about that.

> My apologies.

---

### Situation:

তুমি দেরি করেছ:

> I'm sorry I'm late. I got stuck in traffic.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Explaining a Reason

Problem-এর reason:

> because...

> because of...

> due to...

### Examples:

> I was late because of traffic.

> The flight was delayed due to bad weather.

> I couldn't come because I wasn't feeling well.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “Because” vs “Because of”

### Because + sentence

> I was late because the traffic was heavy.

### Because of + noun

> I was late because of heavy traffic.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Suggesting a Solution

Problem solve করার সময়:

> Maybe we could...

> We could...

> Why don't we...?

> How about...?

> I think we should...

---

### Examples:

> Maybe we could try restarting the computer.

> Why don't we contact customer support?

> How about replacing the product?

> I think we should wait a little longer.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Offering Help

> Can I help you?

> Is there anything I can do?

> Let me help you.

> Would you like me to help?

---

### Example:

> You look lost. Can I help you?

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Accepting Help

> Yes, please.

> That would be great.

> I'd really appreciate it.

> Thanks, that would help a lot.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Refusing Help Politely

> Thanks, but I think I'm okay.

> I appreciate it, but I can manage.

> That's very kind of you, but I'm fine.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “I Appreciate It”

**I appreciate it = আমি আপনার সাহায্য/ভালো কাজের জন্য কৃতজ্ঞ।**

### Example:

> Thanks for helping me. I really appreciate it.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Full Conversation: Asking for Help

### Situation: Computer Problem

**You:**

> Excuse me, could you help me with my computer?

**Technician:**

> Sure. What's the problem?

**You:**

> It keeps freezing whenever I open a large program.

**Technician:**

> When did the problem start?

**You:**

> It started a few days ago.

**Technician:**

> Have you tried restarting it?

**You:**

> Yes, I have, but the problem is still there.

**Technician:**

> Okay. Let me take a look.

**You:**

> Sure. I'd really appreciate it.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Full Conversation: Shopping

**Customer:**

> Excuse me, I bought this shirt yesterday, but I noticed there's a small problem with it.

**Staff:**

> What's wrong with it?

**Customer:**

> There's a small tear near the sleeve.

**Staff:**

> I'm sorry about that. Would you like to exchange it?

**Customer:**

> Yes, please. Do you have the same shirt in a different size?

**Staff:**

> Yes, we do.

**Customer:**

> Great. I'd like to exchange it for that one.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Full Conversation: Asking Directions

**You:**

> Excuse me, could you tell me how to get to the train station?

**Person:**

> Sure. Go straight and turn right at the traffic light.

**You:**

> Is it far from here?

**Person:**

> No, it's about a ten-minute walk.

**You:**

> Okay, thank you very much.

**Person:**

> You're welcome.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Full Conversation: Restaurant Complaint

**Customer:**

> Excuse me, I'm sorry to bother you, but there's a problem with my order.

**Waiter:**

> I'm sorry. What's the problem?

**Customer:**

> I ordered chicken, but this is fish.

**Waiter:**

> I'm really sorry about that. I'll replace it immediately.

**Customer:**

> Thank you. I'd appreciate that.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Full Conversation: Lost Phone

**You:**

> Excuse me, I think I've lost my phone.

**Staff:**

> Where did you last have it?

**You:**

> I think I had it in the waiting area.

**Staff:**

> What does it look like?

**You:**

> It's a black phone with a black case.

**Staff:**

> I'll check with the reception desk.

**You:**

> Thank you. I really appreciate your help.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — The Most Important Polite Expressions

এই 10টা **মুখস্থ না করে speaking-এ repeatedly use** করবে:

1. **Excuse me, could you please help me?**
2. **I'm having a problem with...**
3. **Something seems to be wrong with...**
4. **Could you please check it?**
5. **Would you mind helping me?**
6. **I'm sorry to bother you, but...**
7. **Could you repeat that, please?**
8. **I was wondering if you could...**
9. **I'd really appreciate it.**
10. **Thank you for your help.**

---

# 🧩 Part 47 — Problem-Solving Vocabulary

| English    | বাংলা               |
| ---------- | ------------------- |
| problem    | সমস্যা              |
| issue      | সমস্যা              |
| trouble    | ঝামেলা/সমস্যা       |
| solution   | সমাধান              |
| fix        | ঠিক করা             |
| repair     | মেরামত করা          |
| replace    | পরিবর্তন করে দেওয়া |
| return     | ফেরত দেওয়া         |
| refund     | টাকা ফেরত           |
| complain   | অভিযোগ করা          |
| request    | অনুরোধ              |
| explain    | ব্যাখ্যা করা        |
| clarify    | পরিষ্কার করা        |
| apologize  | ক্ষমা চাওয়া        |
| reschedule | সময় পরিবর্তন করা   |

---

# 🎯 Part 48 — Important Difference

### Problem

> I have a problem with my phone.

### Issue

> There's an issue with the payment.

### Trouble

> I'm having trouble logging in.

### Solution

> We need to find a solution.

### Fix

> Can you fix it?

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — Don't Translate Directly from Bangla

বাংলা:

> আমার একটু সমস্যা হচ্ছে।

❌

> My little problem is happening.

✅

> I'm having a little problem.

---

বাংলা:

> একটু দেখে দেবেন?

❌

> Will you see it?

✅

> Could you please take a look at it?

---

বাংলা:

> এটা কাজ করছে না।

✅

> It's not working.

---

বাংলা:

> বারবার বন্ধ হয়ে যাচ্ছে।

✅

> It keeps shutting down.

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — Emergency Communication

জরুরি situation হলে short and clear:

> I need help.

> Please call an ambulance.

> I need a doctor.

> I've lost my wallet.

> My phone has been stolen.

> I can't find my way.

> Please stay with me.

**Goal:** Emergency-তে complicated English নয়—**clear English**।

---

# 🎤 Part 51 — 5-Minute Speaking Practice

## Topic:

# **“Describe a Problem You Had and How You Solved It”**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1️⃣ Situation

> A few days ago, I had a problem with...

### 2️⃣ Problem

> The problem was that...

### 3️⃣ Details

> It started when...

### 4️⃣ Action

> First, I...

### 5️⃣ Next step

> Then, I...

### 6️⃣ Solution

> Eventually,...

### 7️⃣ Result

> In the end,...

### 8️⃣ Feeling

> I felt...

---

# 🏆 Part 52 — Complete Speaking Example

## Topic: **A Problem I Had With My Computer**

> A few weeks ago, I had a problem with my computer. It suddenly became very slow, and it kept freezing whenever I opened several programs.
>
> At first, I thought it was a temporary problem, so I restarted the computer. However, the problem continued. Then I checked the storage and noticed that it was almost full.
>
> I removed some unnecessary files and programs and restarted the computer again. After that, the performance improved significantly.
>
> I was relieved because I needed the computer for my work and study. The experience also taught me that I should regularly clean up unnecessary files and maintain my computer properly.
>
> Overall, it was a simple problem, but I learned how to deal with it more effectively.

---

# 🧠 Part 53 — 10 Real-Life Speaking Situations

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

### 1. 📱 Phone Problem

> Your phone isn't working properly.

### 2. 💻 Computer Problem

> Your computer keeps freezing.

### 3. 🌐 Internet Problem

> Your internet is very slow.

### 4. 🛍️ Shopping

> You received the wrong product.

### 5. 🍔 Restaurant

> Your food is cold.

### 6. 🏨 Hotel

> There's no hot water in your room.

### 7. 🚌 Transportation

> You missed your bus.

### 8. 📍 Directions

> You can't find a place.

### 9. 📅 Appointment

> You need to reschedule.

### 10. 🎒 Lost Item

> You lost your bag.

---

# 🎯 Part 54 — Role-Play Practice

নিজে নিজে **A এবং B দুই role** বলবে।

### Situation:

**You:** Customer
**Me/Staff:** Shopkeeper

Start:

> Excuse me, I have a problem with this product.

তারপর explain করবে:

> I bought it yesterday, but...

তারপর request:

> Could you please...?

তারপর solution:

> I'd like to...

শেষে:

> Thank you. I really appreciate it.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 54 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important:

# **SITUATION → PROBLEM → DETAILS → REQUEST → SOLUTION → THANKS**

### Example:

> **Excuse me, I have a problem with my phone.**
> **It keeps restarting.**
> **It started yesterday.**
> **I've already tried restarting it.**
> **Could you please take a look at it?**
> **I'd really appreciate it.**

এই 6-step pattern শিখে ফেললে বাস্তব জীবনের অনেক English conversation handle করতে পারবে।

---

↓

→ **problem explain + help ask + complaint + request + solution**
