# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 59

# 🌍 Describing Experiences in Detail

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** শুধু ঘটনা বলা নয়—**কী দেখেছিলে, কী অনুভব করেছিলে, পরিবেশ কেমন ছিল, কীভাবে ঘটেছিল, অন্যরা কী করেছিল**—এসব detail দিয়ে natural English-এ experience describe করা।

### 🔗 Previous lesson connection

**Lesson 58 — Storytelling & Narration**
→ ঘটনা **শুরু → সমস্যা → action → result → feeling** দিয়ে বলা

**Lesson 59 — Describing Experiences in Detail**
→ এখন সেই story-কে **আরও জীবন্ত, detailed এবং natural** করা শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 59-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* কোনো experience বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করতে
* **place + people + weather + atmosphere** describe করতে
* নিজের emotion বিস্তারিতভাবে বলতে
* ছোট details যোগ করতে
* past experience-এ sensory vocabulary ব্যবহার করতে
* “It was...” এর পরিবর্তে advanced expressions ব্যবহার করতে
* story-তে personal reaction যোগ করতে
* 3–5 মিনিট একটি experience নিয়ে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Experience Description কী?

ধরো তুমি বললে:

> I went to Cox's Bazar.

এটা শুধু information।

কিন্তু detailed description:

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with some friends. The weather was warm and slightly windy, and the beach was crowded with tourists. We arrived in the afternoon and spent several hours walking along the beach. The sound of the waves and the cool breeze made me feel relaxed.

এখানে আমরা জানলাম:

* কখন
* কোথায়
* কার সাথে
* weather
* মানুষ
* কী করেছিলে
* কী দেখেছিলে
* কেমন লেগেছিল

এটাই **detailed experience**।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — The 8-Part Experience Formula

একটি experience describe করতে এই structure ব্যবহার করো:

### 1️⃣ Background

**When?**

### 2️⃣ Place

**Where?**

### 3️⃣ People

**Who?**

### 4️⃣ Atmosphere

**What was it like?**

### 5️⃣ Activities

**What happened?**

### 6️⃣ Details

**What did you see/hear/notice?**

### 7️⃣ Feelings

**How did you feel?**

### 8️⃣ Reflection

**What did you learn/think afterward?**

---

# 🧩 Part 3 — Background Describe করা

শুরু করতে:

> Last year,...

> A few months ago,...

> During my university days,...

> When I was a child,...

> Last summer,...

> A few weeks ago,...

### Example:

> Last summer, I had a memorable experience when I traveled with some friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Place Describe করা

শুধু:

> The place was nice.

না।

আরও detail:

> The place was beautiful and peaceful.

> The area was crowded and noisy.

> The streets were busy with people.

> The environment was calm and relaxing.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Useful Place Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা            |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| crowded     | ভিড়পূর্ণ        |
| peaceful    | শান্তিপূর্ণ      |
| quiet       | নিরিবিলি         |
| noisy       | কোলাহলপূর্ণ      |
| busy        | ব্যস্ত           |
| lively      | প্রাণবন্ত        |
| beautiful   | সুন্দর           |
| scenic      | মনোরম দৃশ্যপূর্ণ |
| modern      | আধুনিক           |
| traditional | ঐতিহ্যবাহী       |
| clean       | পরিষ্কার         |
| messy       | অগোছালো          |
| spacious    | প্রশস্ত          |
| narrow      | সরু              |

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — People Describe করা

মানুষের appearance + behavior describe করতে পারো।

### Appearance:

> He was tall and friendly-looking.

> She had a cheerful personality.

### Behavior:

> Everyone seemed excited.

> People were talking and laughing.

> The children were running around.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Everyone Seemed...”

খুব natural expression:

> Everyone seemed excited.

> Everyone seemed relaxed.

> Everyone seemed busy.

> The people there seemed friendly.

**seemed = মনে হচ্ছিল**

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Atmosphere

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশের overall feeling**

Examples:

> The atmosphere was lively.

> The atmosphere was peaceful.

> The atmosphere was relaxing.

> The atmosphere was a little tense.

> The atmosphere was exciting.

---

### Example:

> The atmosphere was lively because there were many people enjoying themselves.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Weather Description

শুধু:

> It was hot.

এর পরিবর্তে:

> It was quite warm.

> It was extremely hot.

> It was cool and windy.

> It was cloudy but comfortable.

> It was raining heavily.

> There was a light breeze.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Useful Weather Expressions

### Sunny

> It was bright and sunny.

### Windy

> There was a strong breeze.

### Rainy

> It was raining heavily.

### Cloudy

> The sky was covered with clouds.

### Pleasant

> The weather was pleasant and comfortable.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Describing What You Saw

Use:

> I noticed...

> I saw...

> I could see...

> What caught my attention was...

### Example:

> I noticed several small shops along the street.

> What caught my attention was the beautiful sunset.

🔥 **What caught my attention was...** খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Describing What You Heard

> I could hear...

> I heard...

> There was the sound of...

### Examples:

> I could hear people talking and laughing.

> I could hear the sound of the waves.

> There was the sound of traffic everywhere.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Describing Smell

> It smelled fresh.

> The air smelled clean.

> I could smell fresh food from the nearby restaurants.

> The place smelled like freshly cooked food.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Describing Touch / Feeling

> The wind felt cool.

> The water was surprisingly cold.

> The sand felt warm under my feet.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Five Senses

Experience describe করার powerful technique:

# 👀 Sight

> I could see...

# 👂 Sound

> I could hear...

# 👃 Smell

> I could smell...

# ✋ Touch

> It felt...

# 👅 Taste

> It tasted...

---

### Example:

> I could see the sunset, hear the sound of the waves, and feel the cool breeze on my face.

🔥 এটা simple story-কে অনেক বেশি vivid করে।

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Describe Food Experience

শুধু:

> The food was good.

Better:

> The food was delicious.

আরও detailed:

> The food was delicious, especially the grilled fish. It was fresh, spicy, and full of flavor.

---

# 🧠 Useful Food Words

> delicious
> tasty
> spicy
> sweet
> salty
> sour
> fresh
> crispy
> soft
> juicy
> flavorful
> deliciously cooked

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Describe Emotions in Detail

শুধু:

> I was happy.

আরও natural:

> I was really excited.

> I felt extremely happy.

> I was genuinely surprised.

> I felt a huge sense of relief.

> I was a little nervous at first.

> I felt proud of myself.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Emotion Intensity

### Low:

> I was a little nervous.

### Medium:

> I was quite nervous.

### Strong:

> I was extremely nervous.

### Very strong:

> I was absolutely terrified.

এভাবে adjective-এর intensity পরিবর্তন করতে পারো।

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — “At First... But Later...”

Emotion change দেখাতে:

> At first, I was nervous, but later I became more comfortable.

> At first, I felt confused, but eventually everything became clear.

> At first, I didn't enjoy it, but after a while, I started having fun.

এই structure খুব useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Explain Why You Felt Something

শুধু:

> I was nervous.

Better:

> I was nervous because it was my first time speaking in front of a large audience.

---

### Formula:

# **I felt + emotion + because + reason**

> I felt excited because I was about to start something new.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “I Wasn't Expecting...”

Unexpected experience:

> I wasn't expecting the place to be so crowded.

> I wasn't expecting the weather to change so quickly.

> I wasn't expecting to meet my old friend there.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — “To My Surprise”

Meaning:

**আমার অবাক করার মতো**

> To my surprise, my old teacher was there.

> To my surprise, the restaurant was almost empty.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “What Surprised Me Most Was...”

Very useful:

> What surprised me most was how friendly everyone was.

> What surprised me most was how quickly everything changed.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “What I Liked Most Was...”

নিজের favorite part:

> What I liked most was the peaceful environment.

> What I enjoyed most was spending time with my friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — “The Best Part Was...”

> The best part was watching the sunset.

> The best part was spending time with my family.

> The best part was meeting new people.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “The Hardest Part Was...”

Problem describe:

> The hardest part was dealing with the traffic.

> The hardest part was speaking in front of everyone.

> The hardest part was finding a place to stay.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — “The Most Memorable Part...”

> The most memorable part of the trip was our visit to the beach.

> The most memorable part was meeting an old friend.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Adding Comparison

Experience explain করতে comparison:

> It was much better than I expected.

> The place was more crowded than I had imagined.

> The weather was much cooler than I expected.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — “More Than I Expected”

> The experience was more enjoyable than I expected.

> The place was more beautiful than I expected.

> The event was much bigger than I expected.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — “Less Than I Expected”

> It was less expensive than I expected.

> The journey was less difficult than I expected.

> The place was less crowded than I expected.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Personal Reaction

Experience-এ নিজের reaction:

> I was impressed by...

> I was surprised by...

> I really enjoyed...

> I was fascinated by...

> I was disappointed by...

---

### Examples:

> I was impressed by the cleanliness of the place.

> I was fascinated by the beautiful architecture.

> I was disappointed by the poor service.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — “I Was Impressed By...”

খুব useful intermediate phrase:

> I was impressed by how organized everything was.

> I was impressed by the quality of the service.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — “I Couldn't Believe...”

Strong surprise:

> I couldn't believe how crowded the place was.

> I couldn't believe how quickly everything happened.

> I couldn't believe my eyes.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — “It Felt Like...”

Experience describe করার জন্য:

> It felt like a completely different world.

> It felt like everyone was having a great time.

> It felt like time was passing very quickly.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — “For Me...”

Personal perspective:

> For me, the best part was...

> For me, it was a very meaningful experience.

> For me, the experience was worth it.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Detailed Experience Example

# 🏖️ A Trip to Cox's Bazar

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with a few of my friends. It was my first time visiting the beach with them, so I was really excited.
>
> We arrived in the afternoon. The weather was warm, but there was a cool breeze coming from the sea. The beach was quite crowded, and I could see families, tourists, and local vendors everywhere.
>
> At first, we walked along the beach and took some photos. Then we sat near the shore and talked for a while. I could hear the sound of the waves, and the atmosphere was really relaxing.
>
> What I enjoyed most was watching the sunset. The sky slowly changed color, and the whole beach looked beautiful.
>
> The only problem was that it became quite crowded later in the evening. However, that didn't ruin the experience.
>
> Overall, it was one of the most enjoyable trips I've ever had. I felt relaxed, happy, and grateful to spend that time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Why This Answer Is Good

এখানে আছে:

### Background

> Last year...

### People

> with a few of my friends

### Emotion

> I was really excited.

### Weather

> warm + cool breeze

### Place

> crowded beach

### Sight

> families, tourists, vendors

### Sound

> sound of waves

### Activity

> walked, took photos, talked

### Highlight

> watching the sunset

### Problem

> crowded

### Final feeling

> relaxed, happy, grateful

🔥 এটাই **detailed speaking**।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Detailed Experience Example

# 🎓 First Day at University

> I still remember my first day at university. I was excited but also quite nervous because everything was new to me.
>
> When I arrived, the campus seemed much bigger than I had expected. There were students everywhere, and everyone seemed busy trying to find their classrooms.
>
> At first, I wasn't sure where to go, so I asked another student for directions. Fortunately, he was very friendly and showed me the way.
>
> When I entered the classroom, I met some of my classmates. We introduced ourselves and started talking. After a while, I began to feel more comfortable.
>
> What I remember most is how quickly my nervousness disappeared. By the end of the day, I felt much more confident.
>
> Looking back, it was a very important experience because it was the beginning of a new chapter in my life.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Detailed Experience Example

# 💼 First Job/Interview

> A few months ago, I had an important job interview. I had prepared for several days, but I was still nervous that morning.
>
> I arrived at the office about twenty minutes early. The building was modern and quite busy, and there were several other candidates waiting in the reception area.
>
> At first, I was worried because some of the other candidates looked very confident. However, I reminded myself to stay calm and focus on my preparation.
>
> When I entered the interview room, the interviewer greeted me warmly. The first few questions were easy, so I gradually became more comfortable.
>
> The most challenging part was answering a technical question that I hadn't expected. I couldn't give a perfect answer, but I explained what I knew and was honest about what I didn't know.
>
> When the interview ended, I felt relieved. Regardless of the result, I was proud that I had handled the situation calmly.
>
> The experience taught me that good preparation is important, but confidence and honesty are equally valuable.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Detailed Experience Example

# 🤝 Helping Someone

> A few weeks ago, I had an experience that reminded me how important small acts of kindness can be.
>
> I was returning home when I noticed an elderly man standing near a busy road. He looked confused and seemed to be trying to find a particular address.
>
> I walked over and asked if he needed any help. He showed me a piece of paper with an address written on it. I knew the area, so I explained how he could get there.
>
> However, the place was quite far away, and he seemed uncomfortable traveling alone. So, I decided to help him find the correct bus.
>
> Eventually, we found the bus he needed. He thanked me several times, and I felt really happy that I could help.
>
> It wasn't a huge thing, but it made me realize that even a small amount of help can make someone's day easier.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Experience vs Story

### Storytelling:

> Something happened → events → result.

### Detailed experience:

> Something happened
>
> * place
> * people
> * atmosphere
> * senses
> * emotions
> * reaction
> * reflection

So:

# **Story + Details + Feelings + Reflection = Strong Speaking**

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Don't Overuse “Very”

❌

> very beautiful
> very good
> very bad
> very interesting
> very big

Try:

> beautiful
> impressive
> excellent
> terrible
> fascinating
> huge

### Example:

❌

> The place was very beautiful.

✅

> The place was breathtaking.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Better Vocabulary

| Basic          | Better       |
| -------------- | ------------ |
| very good      | excellent    |
| very bad       | terrible     |
| very beautiful | breathtaking |
| very big       | huge         |
| very small     | tiny         |
| very tired     | exhausted    |
| very happy     | delighted    |
| very sad       | devastated   |
| very surprised | amazed       |
| very scared    | terrified    |
| very hungry    | starving     |
| very busy      | hectic       |

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Making Your Answer Longer Naturally

Suppose question:

> How was your trip?

### Short:

> It was good.

### Better:

> It was really enjoyable.

### Detailed:

> It was really enjoyable. We visited several interesting places and spent a lot of time together.

### Advanced:

> It was one of the most enjoyable trips I've had. The weather was pleasant, the scenery was beautiful, and I really enjoyed spending time with my friends. What I liked most was the relaxed atmosphere.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — The 5W + 1H Method

যেকোনো experience-এর details বের করতে:

### **Who?**

কার সাথে?

### **What?**

কি ঘটেছিল?

### **When?**

কখন?

### **Where?**

কোথায়?

### **Why?**

কেন?

### **How?**

কীভাবে?

---

### Example:

> Who? — My friends
> What? — We went on a trip
> When? — Last summer
> Where? — Cox's Bazar
> Why? — To relax
> How? — By bus

তারপর এগুলোকে story বানাবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Add “Because”

Simple answer:

> I enjoyed the trip.

Detailed:

> I enjoyed the trip because I had the opportunity to spend quality time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Add “Which”

Details যোগ করতে:

> We visited a small restaurant **which was famous for seafood.**

> We stayed at a hotel **which was close to the beach.**

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Add “Where”

> We stayed at a hotel **where we could see the sea from our room.**

> We visited a village **where my grandfather grew up.**

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — Add “That”

> It was an experience **that I will never forget.**

> She gave me some advice **that really helped me.**

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — 3-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **“Describe a Memorable Experience”**

এই order follow করবে:

### 1. When?

> A few months ago...

### 2. Where?

> I was in...

### 3. Who?

> I was with...

### 4. What happened?

> We...

### 5. What did you see/hear?

> I could see/hear...

### 6. What was the atmosphere?

> The atmosphere was...

### 7. How did you feel?

> I felt...

### 8. What was the best part?

> What I enjoyed most was...

### 9. Was there a problem?

> The only problem was...

### 10. What did you learn?

> The experience taught me...

---

# 🎤 Part 51 — Practice Topic 1

## **A Memorable Trip**

Speak about:

* When?
* Where?
* Who?
* Why did you go?
* How did you travel?
* What did you see?
* What did you do?
* What was the best part?
* Any problem?
* How did you feel?

---

# 🎤 Part 52 — Practice Topic 2

## **Your First Day at University**

Use:

> I still remember...

> At first...

> I was nervous because...

> What surprised me was...

> After a while...

> By the end of the day...

> Looking back...

---

# 🎤 Part 53 — Practice Topic 3

## **A Time You Helped Someone**

Use:

> A few weeks ago...

> I noticed...

> He/She seemed...

> I decided to...

> Eventually...

> I felt...

> The experience taught me...

---

# 🎤 Part 54 — Practice Topic 4

## **A Difficult Experience**

Use:

> It happened...

> At first...

> The problem started when...

> I didn't know what to do.

> Eventually...

> Looking back...

> I learned that...

---

# 🏆 Part 55 — Golden Formula

যেকোনো experience-এর জন্য:

# **WHEN**

↓

# **WHERE**

↓

# **WHO**

↓

# **WHAT**

↓

# **DETAILS**

↓

# **SENSES**

↓

# **FEELINGS**

↓

# **PROBLEM**

↓

# **RESULT**

↓

# **REFLECTION**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 59-এর 15টি Must-Learn Expressions

1. **I still remember...**
2. **It was one of the most memorable experiences...**
3. **At first...**
4. **The atmosphere was...**
5. **Everyone seemed...**
6. **I noticed...**
7. **What caught my attention was...**
8. **What I enjoyed most was...**
9. **What surprised me most was...**
10. **I wasn't expecting...**
11. **To my surprise...**
12. **I was impressed by...**
13. **The best part was...**
14. **The hardest part was...**
15. **Looking back...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 59 Golden Rule

**শুধু ঘটনা বলবে না।**

Instead:

> **Event + Details + Senses + Emotion + Reaction + Reflection**

### Example:

❌ **Basic:**

> I went to a restaurant. The food was good. I came home.

### ✅ Strong:

> I went to a small restaurant with two of my friends last Friday. The place was quite crowded, but the atmosphere was lively. We ordered some grilled chicken, which was delicious and full of flavor. What I enjoyed most was sitting together and talking for a few hours. I felt relaxed and happy afterward. Looking back, it was a simple but memorable evening.

এটাই **Lesson 59-এর target speaking level**।


----


# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 60

# 🗣️ Describing People, Personality & Relationships

**Level:** Intermediate → Upper-Intermediate
**Main Focus:** কোনো মানুষকে শুধু **“He is good”** না বলে তার **appearance, personality, behavior, habits, strengths, weaknesses এবং relationship** বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করা।

### 🔗 Previous lesson connection

**Lesson 58 — Storytelling & Narration**
→ ঘটনা organizedভাবে বলা

**Lesson 59 — Describing Experiences in Detail**
→ experience-এ place, people, atmosphere, emotion ও details যোগ করা

# **Lesson 60 — Describing People, Personality & Relationships**

→ এখন **মানুষকে detailed ও natural English-এ describe** করা শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 60-এর Main Goal

আজকের lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* একজন মানুষের appearance describe করতে
* personality describe করতে
* positive/negative characteristics বলতে
* behavior describe করতে
* strengths ও weaknesses বলতে
* একজন close person সম্পর্কে বলতে
* friendship/relationship describe করতে
* comparison করতে
* example দিয়ে personality explain করতে
* 2–3 মিনিট একজন মানুষ সম্পর্কে কথা বলতে

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Person Describe করার 6-Part Formula

একজন মানুষ describe করার সময়:

### 1️⃣ Basic Information

Who is the person?

### 2️⃣ Appearance

What does he/she look like?

### 3️⃣ Personality

What kind of person is he/she?

### 4️⃣ Behavior

How does he/she behave?

### 5️⃣ Relationship

How do you know him/her?

### 6️⃣ Your Opinion

Why do you like/respect/appreciate this person?

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Basic Introduction

শুরু করতে:

> I'd like to talk about...

> The person I'd like to describe is...

> One person who has influenced me is...

> Someone I really admire is...

> One of my closest friends is...

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about one of my closest friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Relationship

তুমি কীভাবে মানুষটিকে চেনো:

> He's my best friend.

> She's one of my classmates.

> He's a colleague of mine.

> She's a family member.

> We met at university.

> We've known each other for several years.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “We've Known Each Other For...”

খুব useful expression:

> We've known each other for five years.

> We've known each other since university.

### Difference:

**for + duration**

> for five years

**since + starting point**

> since 2022

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Appearance

প্রথমে basic:

> He's tall.

> She's short.

> He's of average height.

> She has long hair.

> He has short black hair.

> She has a friendly smile.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Appearance Vocabulary

### Height:

> tall
> short
> average height

### Build:

> slim
> thin
> fit
> athletic
> well-built

### Hair:

> short hair
> long hair
> curly hair
> straight hair
> wavy hair

### Face:

> round face
> oval face
> sharp features

### Other:

> beard
> glasses
> smile

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Don't Overfocus on Appearance

Speaking-এ একজন মানুষকে describe করার সময় শুধু:

> He is tall. He has black hair. He wears glasses.

বললে answer basic থাকবে।

এরপর personality-তে যাও:

> More importantly, he's extremely reliable and supportive.

🔥 **Personality is more important for detailed description.**

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Personality

সবচেয়ে important vocabulary:

### Positive:

> friendly
> kind
> honest
> helpful
> polite
> patient
> confident
> hardworking
> reliable
> responsible
> generous
> supportive
> ambitious
> calm
> cheerful
> creative
> intelligent
> respectful

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Personality Vocabulary with Bangla

| Word        | বাংলা         |
| ----------- | ------------- |
| friendly    | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ |
| honest      | সৎ            |
| kind        | দয়ালু        |
| helpful     | সাহায্যপ্রবণ  |
| patient     | ধৈর্যশীল      |
| confident   | আত্মবিশ্বাসী  |
| hardworking | পরিশ্রমী      |
| reliable    | নির্ভরযোগ্য   |
| responsible | দায়িত্বশীল   |
| generous    | উদার          |
| supportive  | সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ |
| ambitious   | উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী |
| calm        | শান্ত         |
| cheerful    | প্রফুল্ল      |
| creative    | সৃজনশীল       |
| respectful  | শ্রদ্ধাশীল    |

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Negative Personality

সব মানুষ perfect নয়।

Useful words:

> impatient
> careless
> stubborn
> shy
> nervous
> lazy
> selfish
> serious
> short-tempered
> forgetful
> sensitive

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Negative Word Politely ব্যবহার

সরাসরি:

> He is lazy.

আরও natural:

> He can be a little lazy sometimes.

সরাসরি:

> She is stubborn.

Polite:

> She can be quite stubborn at times.

এতে English softer হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “Can Be”

একটি characteristic সবসময় নয়:

> He can be a little impatient sometimes.

> She can be quite serious at times.

> He can be a bit shy around new people.

এই structure খুব natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “At Times”

Meaning:

**মাঝে মাঝে / কিছু সময়ে**

> He can be quiet at times.

> She can be impatient at times.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Behavior Describe করা

Personality-এর evidence দিতে behavior বলো।

### Example:

> He's very helpful. He always offers to help when someone has a problem.

> She's very responsible. She always finishes her work on time.

এটাই strong description।

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Personality + Example Formula

# **QUALITY → BEHAVIOR → EXAMPLE**

### Example:

> He's very reliable. Whenever I have a problem, I know I can count on him.

### Another:

> She's extremely hardworking. She usually finishes her tasks before the deadline.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “I Can Count On Him/Her”

Meaning:

**আমি তার ওপর ভরসা করতে পারি।**

> I can always count on him.

> I know I can count on her when I need help.

Very natural expression।

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “There for Me”

Supportive person:

> She's always there for me when I need advice.

Meaning:

**প্রয়োজনের সময় সে আমার পাশে থাকে।**

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Get Along With”

Relationship:

> I get along really well with him.

> We get along very well.

Meaning:

**ভালো সম্পর্ক / ভালোভাবে মিশতে পারা।**

---

### Negative:

> We don't always get along.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Have a Lot in Common”

Common interests:

> We have a lot in common.

> We both enjoy technology and traveling.

Meaning:

**আমাদের অনেক মিল আছে।**

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “See Eye to Eye”

Meaning:

**কোনো বিষয়ে একই মত হওয়া।**

> We don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's opinions.

🔥 Advanced natural expression।

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Look Up To”

কাউকে admire করা:

> I really look up to my older brother.

> I look up to my teacher because of his dedication.

Meaning:

**শ্রদ্ধা/অনুপ্রেরণার চোখে দেখা।**

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “Role Model”

> He's a role model for me.

> My father has always been a role model for me.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Influenced Me”

কেউ তোমাকে influence করলে:

> She has influenced me a lot.

> He has had a positive influence on me.

### Example:

> My university teacher had a positive influence on my career.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “What I Like Most About Him”

খুব useful structure:

> What I like most about him is his honesty.

> What I like most about her is that she's always willing to help others.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “What I Admire About Her”

> What I admire most about her is her determination.

> What I admire about him is his ability to stay calm under pressure.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Describing Someone's Strengths

Strength:

> One of his greatest strengths is his patience.

> Her biggest strength is her ability to communicate with people.

> He's particularly good at solving problems.

> She's very good at motivating others.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Describing Weaknesses

Polite:

> One of his weaknesses is that he sometimes overthinks things.

> She can be a little impatient at times.

> He sometimes has difficulty making quick decisions.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “He's the Kind of Person Who...”

Very powerful structure:

> He's the kind of person who always keeps his promises.

> She's the kind of person who makes everyone feel comfortable.

> He's the kind of person you can trust.

এটি মুখস্থ করার মতো structure।

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “She's Someone Who...”

> She's someone who always tries to help others.

> He's someone who never gives up easily.

> She's someone who takes her responsibilities seriously.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “You Can Tell That...”

Behavior থেকে conclusion:

> You can tell that he's very hardworking.

> You can tell that she really cares about other people.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Describe a Friend

### Question:

> Tell me about one of your close friends.

### Answer:

> I'd like to talk about one of my closest friends. We've known each other for several years.
>
> He's of average height and has short black hair. But what stands out most about him is his personality.
>
> He's very friendly, reliable, and supportive. Whenever I have a problem, I know I can count on him. He's also quite ambitious and always tries to improve himself.
>
> We have a lot in common, especially our interest in technology and learning. We don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's opinions.
>
> What I like most about him is his honesty. He's the kind of person who tells you the truth even when it's difficult.
>
> Overall, I really value our friendship because I can trust him and be myself around him.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Why This Answer Is Strong

এখানে আছে:

**Introduction**

> one of my closest friends

**Relationship**

> We've known each other for several years.

**Appearance**

> average height + short black hair

**Personality**

> friendly + reliable + supportive

**Behavior**

> helps when I have a problem

**Common interest**

> technology + learning

**Difference**

> don't always see eye to eye

**Main quality**

> honesty

**Conclusion**

> I value our friendship

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Describe a Family Member

### Question:

> Describe someone in your family whom you admire.

### Structure:

> The person I'd like to talk about is...

> He/She is...

> I've known him/her all my life.

> What I admire most is...

> One of his/her greatest strengths is...

> He/She has taught me...

> That's why...

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Example: A Person You Admire

> The person I'd like to talk about is someone in my family whom I really admire.
>
> I've known him all my life, so I've had many opportunities to learn from him. He's a calm, responsible, and hardworking person.
>
> What I admire most about him is his ability to remain calm when things become difficult. He doesn't panic easily, and he usually thinks carefully before making a decision.
>
> He's also very supportive. Whenever someone in the family needs help, he's usually the first person to offer it.
>
> One thing I've learned from him is that being successful isn't only about working hard; it's also about being responsible and treating other people with respect.
>
> That's why I consider him an important role model in my life.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Describe Someone You Don't Like

এখানে rude হওয়ার দরকার নেই।

### Better expressions:

> We don't have much in common.

> We have very different personalities.

> We don't always get along.

> I sometimes find him difficult to deal with.

> We see things differently.

---

### Example:

> We have very different personalities. He's quite outspoken, while I'm generally more reserved. We don't always get along, but I still respect him.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Personality Comparison

দুইজনকে compare:

> He's more outgoing than his brother.

> She's more patient than me.

> I'm generally quieter than my friend.

> He's much more confident than he used to be.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — “Used to Be”

Past personality vs now:

> He used to be shy, but now he's much more confident.

> I used to be quite nervous around new people, but I've become more comfortable over time.

🔥 Very useful speaking structure।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Personality Change

### Formula:

**Used to → Change → Now**

> I used to be shy. However, after meeting more people at university, I became more confident. Now I can communicate with new people much more easily.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Describing Someone's Communication Style

> He's very talkative.

> She's a good listener.

> He's easy to talk to.

> She speaks confidently.

> He's quite outspoken.

> She's a little reserved.

---

### Important:

**Talkative** = বেশি কথা বলে

**Good listener** = মন দিয়ে শোনে

**Outspoken** = নিজের মতামত openly বলে

**Reserved** = কম কথা বলে / নিজের feelings সহজে প্রকাশ করে না

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “Easy to Talk To”

Very natural:

> She's very easy to talk to.

> He's the kind of person you can talk to about anything.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Emotional Support

> He always listens to me.

> She gives me good advice.

> He encourages me when I'm struggling.

> She's always there when I need someone to talk to.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Describe a Leader

### Vocabulary:

> confident
> responsible
> decisive
> organized
> fair
> supportive
> inspiring
> good at communication

### Example:

> A good leader should be confident and responsible. More importantly, they should be able to listen to other people and make fair decisions.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — “Decisive”

**Decisive = দ্রুত ও আত্মবিশ্বাসের সাথে সিদ্ধান্ত নিতে পারে**

> He's very decisive when there's a problem.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Describe a Teacher

> My teacher is patient and supportive.

> She's very good at explaining difficult concepts.

> What I appreciate most is that she encourages students to ask questions.

> She's the kind of teacher who makes students feel comfortable.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Describe a Colleague

> He's hardworking and reliable.

> She's very professional.

> He's easy to work with.

> She communicates clearly.

> We work well together.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — “Easy to Work With”

> He's very easy to work with.

> She's supportive and easy to work with.

Meaning:

**তার সাথে কাজ করা সহজ।**

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — “Work Well Together”

> We work really well together.

> Our team works well together because we communicate openly.

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Describe Yourself

এখন অন্য মানুষ নয়—নিজেকে describe করো।

### Formula:

> I'd describe myself as...

### Example:

> I'd describe myself as a hardworking and curious person. I'm usually calm, but I can become a little nervous in unfamiliar situations. I'm also quite ambitious, and I always try to improve my skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — “I'd Describe Myself As...”

Excellent interview phrase:

> I'd describe myself as responsible, hardworking, and adaptable.

> I'd describe myself as a calm but ambitious person.

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — Strength + Weakness

### Interview style:

> One of my strengths is that I'm very persistent. If I have a difficult task, I usually don't give up easily.
>
> One weakness I'm working on is that I sometimes spend too much time trying to make things perfect.

এটি job interview-এর জন্যও useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 51 — Personality + Evidence

কখনো শুধু adjective বলবে না।

❌

> I'm hardworking.

Better:

> I'm hardworking. When I have an important task, I usually keep working until I finish it.

---

### Formula:

# **Adjective → Behavior → Example**

---

# 🔥 Part 52 — Natural Conversation

### A:

> What is your best friend like?

### B:

> He's very friendly and easy to talk to.

### A:

> What do you like most about him?

### B:

> Probably his honesty. I can always trust him to tell me the truth.

### A:

> Do you have anything in common?

### B:

> Yes. We both enjoy technology and learning new things.

### A:

> Do you ever disagree?

### B:

> Of course. We don't always see eye to eye, but we respect each other's opinions.

🔥 This is natural conversational English।

---

# 🧠 Part 57 — 10 Must-Know Expressions

আজ অন্তত এগুলো speaking-এ ব্যবহার করার চেষ্টা করবে:

1. **I'd like to talk about...**
2. **We've known each other for...**
3. **I'd describe him/her as...**
4. **He's/She's the kind of person who...**
5. **What I like most about him/her is...**
6. **I can always count on him/her.**
7. **He's/She's always there for me.**
8. **We have a lot in common.**
9. **We don't always see eye to eye.**
10. **I really look up to him/her.**

# 🏆 Lesson 60 Golden Formula

একজন মানুষ describe করার সময়:

# **WHO**

↓

# **RELATIONSHIP**

↓

# **APPEARANCE**

↓

# **PERSONALITY**

↓

# **BEHAVIOR**

↓

# **STRENGTH**

↓

# **EXAMPLE**

↓

# **RELATIONSHIP**

↓

# **YOUR FEELING**

↓

# **WHY YOU ADMIRE/LIVE WITH THEM**

### Short formula:

> **Who → Looks → Personality → Behavior → Example → Relationship → Feeling**

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 61

# 💬 Giving Opinions

**Main Goal:** Conversation না থামিয়ে **2–5 মিনিট নিজের opinion explain করা**।

আজ থেকে Lesson 61–90-এর focus হবে:

> **Question → Think → Explain → Give Reasons → Example → Conclusion**

শুধু **“Yes, I think so.”** বললে conversation থেমে যাবে।
আমাদের লক্ষ্য হবে **একটি প্রশ্নের উত্তর থেকে 2–5 মিনিট naturally কথা বলা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions

### Main Question:

> **Is technology making our lives better?**

এই question-এর মাধ্যমে আমরা শিখব কীভাবে **একটি opinion-এর ওপর দীর্ঘ সময় কথা বলতে হয়।**

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Opinion কী?

**Opinion = কোনো বিষয় সম্পর্কে তোমার নিজের মতামত।**

### Basic:

> I think technology is making our lives better.

আরও natural:

> Personally, I think technology has made our lives much easier.

আরও strong:

> In my opinion, technology has had a mostly positive impact on our lives.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Opinion দেওয়ার 10টি Natural Expression

### 1. I think...

> I think technology is useful.

### 2. I believe...

> I believe technology has improved our lives.

### 3. In my opinion,...

> In my opinion, technology has many benefits.

### 4. Personally,...

> Personally, I think technology is extremely useful.

### 5. From my point of view,...

> From my point of view, technology has changed the way we live.

### 6. As far as I'm concerned,...

> As far as I'm concerned, technology is necessary nowadays.

### 7. I would say that...

> I would say that technology has made communication easier.

### 8. I feel that...

> I feel that technology has more advantages than disadvantages.

### 9. It seems to me that...

> It seems to me that technology has improved our daily lives.

### 10. If you ask me,...

> If you ask me, technology is extremely important today.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Don't Stop After Your Opinion

### ❌ Weak Answer

> Yes, I think technology makes our lives better.

**Problem:** 8–10 secondsেই শেষ।

### ✅ Better Answer

> Yes, I think technology makes our lives better because it saves time and makes communication easier.

এখনও short।

### 🔥 Strong Answer

> Personally, I think technology has made our lives much easier. One major reason is that it saves us a lot of time. For example, we can now pay bills, order food, or buy products online without leaving home. Another benefit is communication. We can instantly communicate with people anywhere in the world through messaging apps and video calls. However, I also think technology has some disadvantages, especially when people use it too much. Overall, I believe technology is beneficial as long as we use it responsibly.

এটাই আমাদের target।

---

# 🏆 Part 4 — The Golden Opinion Structure

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **OPINION**

↓

# **REASON 1**

↓

# **REASON 2**

↓

# **EXAMPLE**

↓

# **OTHER SIDE**

↓

# **CONCLUSION**

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — Step 1: Give Your Opinion

Question:

> Is technology making our lives better?

Start:

> Personally, I believe technology has made our lives much easier.

তারপর থামবে না।

বলবে:

> There are several reasons why I think this.

এটি তোমাকে next point-এ যাওয়ার সময় দেয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Step 2: Reason 1

Useful expressions:

> One major reason is that...

> The first reason is that...

> One of the main benefits is...

> The biggest advantage is...

### Example:

> One major reason is that technology saves us a lot of time.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — Explain Reason 1

শুধু:

> Technology saves time.

বলবে না।

Explain:

> Technology saves us a lot of time because many everyday tasks can now be done online.

তারপর example:

> For example, we can pay bills, shop for groceries, and book tickets online.

এখন answer বড় হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Reason 2

Transition:

> Another important reason is...

> Another major benefit is...

> In addition,...

> Besides that,...

### Example:

> Another important benefit is that technology has made communication much easier.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Explain Reason 2

> In the past, people had to wait a long time to communicate with someone who lived far away. Nowadays, we can send a message or make a video call almost instantly.

এখানে **past vs present comparison** answer-কে stronger করেছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Give a Personal Example

Opinion answer-এ personal example খুব powerful।

Use:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> In my own experience,...

> From my experience,...

> I personally...

### Example:

> In my own experience, technology has made learning much easier. If I don't understand something, I can search for an explanation online and learn from videos or articles.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Personal Example Formula

# **Personally → Situation → Action → Result**

Example:

> In my own experience, I often use technology for learning. When I don't understand a topic, I search for tutorials online. As a result, I can learn things much faster than I could in the past.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Add the Other Side

একটি strong opinion মানে শুধু নিজের side বলা নয়।

বলতে পারো:

> However,...

> On the other hand,...

> At the same time,...

> That being said,...

### Example:

> However, technology also has some disadvantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — Explain the Disadvantage

> For example, people can become too dependent on their phones and social media. This can reduce face-to-face communication and affect their ability to concentrate.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Balanced Opinion

খুব useful structure:

> I think technology is beneficial, but it depends on how we use it.

🔥 এই sentence অনেক opinion topic-এ ব্যবহার করা যাবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Conclusion

শেষ করার সময়:

> Overall,...

> In conclusion,...

> To sum up,...

> All things considered,...

> So, overall, I would say...

### Example:

> Overall, I believe technology has made our lives better, but we need to use it responsibly.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Full Answer

## ❓ Is technology making our lives better?

> Personally, I believe technology has made our lives much easier and more convenient. There are several reasons why I think this.
>
> One major reason is that technology saves us a lot of time. Many everyday tasks can now be completed online. For example, we can pay bills, order food, buy products, and book tickets without leaving home.
>
> Another important benefit is communication. In the past, communicating with someone who lived far away could take a long time. Nowadays, we can send messages, make voice calls, or have video calls almost instantly.
>
> In my own experience, technology has also made learning much easier. Whenever I don't understand something, I can search for information online, watch tutorials, or use educational platforms. This allows me to learn at my own pace.
>
> However, I don't think technology is completely positive. One major disadvantage is that people can become too dependent on their phones and social media. Excessive use of technology can reduce face-to-face communication and affect concentration.
>
> Overall, I would say that technology has more advantages than disadvantages. It can make our lives easier, save time, and give us access to information, as long as we use it responsibly.

⏱️ **Speaking time: approximately 2–3 minutes.**

---

# 🎯 Part 17 — How to Reach 5 Minutes

যদি 2 মিনিটের answer সহজ হয়ে যায়, আরও 3টি section যোগ করবে:

### 1. Past vs Present

> In the past,...

> Nowadays,...

### 2. Different Perspectives

> Some people believe...

> Others argue...

### 3. Future

> In the future,...

---

# 🔥 5-Minute Formula

# **Opinion**

↓

# **Reason 1**

↓

# **Example**

↓

# **Reason 2**

↓

# **Example**

↓

# **Past vs Present**

↓

# **Advantages**

↓

# **Disadvantages**

↓

# **Different Perspective**

↓

# **Future**

↓

# **Conclusion**

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Past vs Present

Technology topic:

> In the past, people depended more on physical stores and traditional communication methods. Nowadays, many things can be done online within a few minutes.

Useful:

> In the past...

> Nowadays...

> These days...

> Compared with the past...

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Different Perspectives

বলতে পারো:

> Some people believe that technology has made people less social.

তারপর:

> I can understand that point of view, but I think it depends on how people use technology.

🔥 এই structure future lessons-এও বারবার ব্যবহার হবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — “It Depends”

Opinion-এ অত্যন্ত useful:

> It depends on the situation.

> It depends on how people use it.

> I think it depends on the individual.

### Example:

> Is social media good or bad?

> I think it depends on how people use it.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Strong Reason Expressions

মুখস্থ করো:

> One major reason is that...

> Another important reason is that...

> One of the main advantages is...

> One possible benefit is...

> The biggest problem is...

> Another concern is...

> The main point is that...

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Example Expressions

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> To give you an example,...

> A good example of this is...

> In my own experience,...

> From my experience,...

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Adding More Ideas

যখন আরও কথা বলতে চাও:

> In addition,...

> Besides that,...

> What's more,...

> Furthermore,...

> Another thing worth mentioning is...

### Example:

> Another thing worth mentioning is that technology gives people access to information.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Clarifying Your Opinion

যদি মনে হয় তোমার কথা ভুল বোঝা যেতে পারে:

> What I mean is...

> What I'm trying to say is...

> To be more specific,...

> In other words,...

### Example:

> Technology is useful. What I mean is that it can make everyday tasks more efficient.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Changing Direction

একটি point থেকে অন্য point:

> Moving on to another point,...

> Another thing to consider is...

> There's another side to this.

> That brings me to another important point.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Buying Time While Speaking

Conversation থামানো যাবে না।

ভাবার সময়:

> That's an interesting question.

> Let me think about that for a moment.

> Well, I'd say...

> I think the main reason is...

> There are a couple of ways to look at it.

> If I had to choose one reason, I'd say...

এগুলো **filler নয় শুধু**—natural thinking expressions।

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — If You Don't Know

Question-এর answer না জানলেও:

> I'm not completely sure, but I would say...

> I haven't thought about that before, but...

> That's an interesting question. I'd probably say...

> I don't know much about it, but from what I understand,...

Conversation থামবে না।

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Strong Opinion vs Soft Opinion

### Strong:

> I strongly believe that...

> There's no doubt that...

### Medium:

> I believe that...

> I would say that...

### Soft:

> I think...

> I feel that...

> It seems to me that...

### Uncertain:

> I'm not entirely sure, but...

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Agreeing With Your Own Point

একটি point বলার পর reinforce:

> That's why I think...

> This is one of the main reasons why...

> That's exactly why...

### Example:

> Online learning is convenient. That's one of the main reasons why I prefer it.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Cause & Effect

Opinion speaking-এর জন্য খুব important।

### Because

> Technology is useful because it saves time.

### Therefore

> Technology saves time. Therefore, people can be more productive.

### As a result

> We can access information quickly. As a result, learning has become easier.

### This means that

> We can work remotely. This means that people don't always need to travel to an office.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Useful Cause-Effect Chain

> Technology saves time
> → people become more efficient
> → productivity increases
> → daily life becomes easier

এভাবে **একটি idea থেকে আরেকটি idea বের করবে।**

---

| communication | যোগাযোগ          |
| information   | তথ্য             |

> save time

> make life easier

মনে রাখবে:

> Overall...
