# 🧠 Lesson 61-এর 20 Must-Learn Expressions

1. **Personally, I think...**
2. **In my opinion...**
3. **I strongly believe that...**
4. **I would say that...**
5. **One major reason is that...**
6. **Another important reason is...**
7. **One of the main advantages is...**
8. **For example...**
9. **For instance...**
10. **In my own experience...**
11. **However...**
12. **On the other hand...**
13. **That being said...**
14. **It depends on...**
15. **Some people argue that...**
16. **I can understand that point of view, but...**
17. **What I mean is...**
18. **That's one of the main reasons why...**
19. **Overall, I would say...**
20. **All things considered...**

---

# 🎯 আজকের Final Speaking Task

## Question:

> **Is technology making our lives better?**

**Target:** প্রথমে **2 মিনিট**, তারপর একই topic-এ **3 মিনিট**, শেষে **5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

### মনে রাখবে:

> **Opinion → Reason → Explain → Example → Reason → Explain → Example → Other Side → Personal Experience → Conclusion**

**একটি answer মুখস্থ করবে না।**
বরং এই **structure + expressions** ব্যবহার করে নিজের ভাষায় বলবে।

---

## 🔗 Lesson 61 → Lesson 62

**Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions**
→ নিজের opinion **2–5 মিনিট ধরে explain করা**

### **Lesson 62 — Giving Reasons & Explaining Ideas**

→ শুধু **“I think...”** বলার পর **কেন think করছো, কীভাবে কাজ করে, কী effect হয়, কী example আছে**—একটি idea-কে অনেকক্ষণ ধরে explain করা।


----
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 62

# 💡 Giving Reasons & Explaining Ideas

**Main Goal:** Conversation না থামিয়ে **একটি idea-কে 2–5 মিনিট ধরে explain করা**।

### 🔗 Lesson 61-এর সাথে connection

**Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions**

> I think technology is useful.

আজ আমরা শিখব কীভাবে এটাকে expand করতে হয়:

> **I think technology is useful → Why? → How? → Example? → Result? → Other side?**

---

# 🎯 আজকের মূল Formula

# **IDEA → WHY → HOW → EXAMPLE → RESULT → CONCLUSION**

উদাহরণ:

> **I think online learning is useful.**
> **One reason is that** it gives students more flexibility.
> **This means that** they can study whenever they have free time.
> **For example,** students can watch recorded classes at night.
> **As a result,** they can learn at their own pace.
> **That's why** I think online learning is very useful.

এটাই আজকের মূল skill।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — “Why?” Question-এর Answer

যখন তুমি opinion বলবে, নিজেকে জিজ্ঞেস করবে:

> **Why do I think this?**

### Example:

> I think exercise is important.

নিজেকে জিজ্ঞেস করো:

**Why?**

> Because it keeps us healthy.

আবার:

**How?**

> It improves our physical fitness and reduces stress.

আবার:

**Example?**

> For example, walking for 30 minutes every day can improve our fitness.

এভাবেই answer বড় হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Reason দেওয়ার Expressions

### Basic

> because...

> since...

> as...

### Natural

> One reason is that...

> The main reason is that...

> One of the main reasons is...

> This is mainly because...

> The reason for this is...

### Example:

> I prefer working from home. **One reason is that** I don't have to spend time commuting.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Because

সবচেয়ে সহজ structure:

> I like online learning **because** it's flexible.

> I exercise regularly **because** I want to stay healthy.

### Formula:

**IDEA + because + REASON**

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Because of

এখানে একটা important difference:

### Because + sentence

> I stayed home **because it was raining.**

### Because of + noun

> I stayed home **because of the rain.**

❌

> because of it was raining

✅

> because it was raining

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Since / As

Reason দিতে:

> Since technology is becoming more important, we need to learn digital skills.

> As online services are becoming more common, people are spending more time online.

এগুলো একটু বেশি formal।

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “One Reason Is That...”

এটি আজকের সবচেয়ে useful structure।

> One reason is that technology saves time.

> One reason is that exercise improves our health.

> One reason is that reading improves vocabulary.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Multiple Reasons

একটি reason-এর পর:

> Another reason is that...

তারপর:

> A third reason is that...

Example:

> I think learning English is important. One reason is that it helps us communicate internationally. Another reason is that it creates more career opportunities.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Explain “How”

Reason দেওয়ার পর নিজেকে জিজ্ঞেস করবে:

> **How does this happen?**

### Example:

> Technology saves time.

How?

> It allows us to complete many tasks online.

আরও:

> We can pay bills, shop, book tickets, and communicate without traveling.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “This Means That...”

একটি idea explain করার জন্য:

> This means that...

### Example:

> Online learning is flexible. **This means that** students can study whenever they have time.

আরেকটি:

> Technology allows remote work. **This means that** people don't always need to travel to an office.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “In Other Words”

একই idea সহজভাবে explain করতে:

> In other words,...

### Example:

> Online learning is flexible. In other words, students can choose when and where they study.

Meaning:

**অন্যভাবে বললে।**

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “What I Mean Is...”

যখন idea clarify করতে চাও:

> What I mean is...

### Example:

> Technology is useful, but it can also be distracting. What I mean is that people may spend too much time on their phones.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “For Example”

একটি idea-কে বাস্তব করতে example দাও।

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> A good example of this is...

> To give you an example,...

---

### Example:

> Reading is useful because it improves vocabulary. **For example**, when I read English articles, I often learn new words from context.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Personal Example

সবচেয়ে powerful:

> In my own experience,...

> From my experience,...

> Personally,...

> In my case,...

### Example:

> In my own experience, practicing English every day has improved my confidence.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Result Explain করা

Reason → Action → Result

Useful expressions:

> As a result,...

> Therefore,...

> Because of this,...

> This leads to...

> This can help...

> This allows...

---

### Example:

> I practice English every day. **As a result,** I feel more confident when speaking.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “As a Result”

> People use smartphones for learning. As a result, information is easier to access.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “This Leads To”

> Lack of exercise can lead to health problems.

> Regular practice leads to better performance.

### Formula:

**CAUSE → leads to → RESULT**

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “This Allows”

Very useful:

> Technology allows people to work remotely.

> Online courses allow students to learn from home.

> English allows people to communicate with people from different countries.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “This Helps”

> Exercise helps people stay healthy.

> Reading helps improve vocabulary.

> Technology helps us save time.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Explain With a Simple Chain

একটি idea:

> **Exercise is important.**

Expand:

> Exercise is important **because** it keeps us healthy.
> **For example**, regular walking can improve our fitness.
> **As a result**, we may feel more energetic.
> **This also helps** reduce stress.
> **That's why** I think everyone should exercise regularly.

একটি sentence → 5–6 sentences।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — The “Why → How → Example → Result” Method

এটা আজকের সবচেয়ে important method।

### Question:

> Why is learning English important?

### Step 1 — IDEA

> Learning English is very important.

### Step 2 — WHY

> One reason is that it allows us to communicate with people from different countries.

### Step 3 — HOW

> We can use English for education, work, travel, and communication.

### Step 4 — EXAMPLE

> For example, many international websites and courses are available in English.

### Step 5 — RESULT

> As a result, people who know English have access to more information and opportunities.

### Step 6 — CONCLUSION

> That's why I think English is an important skill to develop.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Don't Repeat “Because”

Weak:

> I like English because it's useful because I can communicate because there are many jobs because...

❌ Repetitive.

Instead:

> One reason is that...

> Another reason is that...

> In addition,...

> This means that...

> As a result,...

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Connecting Ideas

Conversation দীর্ঘ করার জন্য connectors অত্যন্ত important।

### Adding:

> Also,...

> In addition,...

> Besides that,...

> Furthermore,...

> What's more,...

### Contrast:

> However,...

> On the other hand,...

> At the same time,...

> Although...

### Result:

> Therefore,...

> As a result,...

> Because of this,...

### Example:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Although”

একসাথে দুই side:

> Although technology has many benefits, it also has some disadvantages.

> Although online learning is convenient, it may reduce face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “While”

Comparison:

> While online learning is flexible, classroom learning provides more face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Even Though”

Stronger contrast:

> Even though technology saves time, people can become too dependent on it.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Explain a Problem

Problem question হলে:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → EFFECT → EXAMPLE → SOLUTION**

Example:

> One problem with social media is that people spend too much time online. This is mainly because social media platforms are designed to keep users engaged. As a result, people may have less time for work or study. For example, someone may plan to spend ten minutes on social media but end up spending an hour. One possible solution is to set daily screen-time limits.

🔥 এই formula future opinion topics-এ অনেক কাজে লাগবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Explain a Solution

# **SOLUTION → HOW → BENEFIT → EXAMPLE**

Example:

> One way to improve English speaking is to practice every day. This can be done by speaking for 15–20 minutes about a familiar topic. The benefit is that regular practice builds confidence and fluency. For example, you can describe your daily routine or talk about your future plans.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Explain a Process

যেকোনো process explain:

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Next,...

> Finally,...

### Example:

> First, I choose a topic. Then, I think about three main points. After that, I give examples. Finally, I summarize my opinion.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “The Main Point Is...”

যখন answer-এ অনেক idea হয়ে যায়:

> The main point is that...

> The key point is that...

Example:

> There are many benefits, but the main point is that technology should be used responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “What Matters Most Is...”

Advanced:

> What matters most is how we use technology.

> What matters most is consistency.

> What matters most is not how quickly you learn, but whether you keep practicing.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Explain Abstract Ideas

Question:

> Why is confidence important?

Weak:

> Confidence is important because it is important.

❌

Better:

> Confidence is important because it helps people communicate without being afraid of making mistakes.

Then expand:

> When people are confident, they're more willing to speak, ask questions, and try new things. As a result, they have more opportunities to improve.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Question: Why Is Exercise Important?

### 2-minute answer:

> I think exercise is important for several reasons. One major reason is that it helps us stay physically healthy. Regular exercise can improve our fitness and reduce the risk of certain health problems.
>
> Another reason is that exercise can improve our mental health. Physical activity can help reduce stress and make us feel more energetic.
>
> In my own experience, I feel more focused after doing some physical activity. For example, even a short walk can help me feel refreshed.
>
> Of course, people don't need to spend hours in the gym. Simple activities like walking, cycling, or stretching can also be useful.
>
> Overall, I think regular exercise is one of the simplest ways to improve both physical and mental well-being.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Question: Why Is Reading Important?

Use the same structure:

> I think reading is important for several reasons.

### Reason 1:

> It improves vocabulary.

### Reason 2:

> It increases knowledge.

### Example:

> For example, reading articles in English can help learners learn new vocabulary.

### Result:

> As a result, they become better at understanding and expressing ideas.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Question: Why Do People Learn English?

Possible answer:

> People learn English for many different reasons. One major reason is communication. English is widely used around the world, so knowing it allows people to communicate with international students, professionals, and travelers.
>
> Another reason is education. Many books, courses, websites, and academic resources are available in English.
>
> English can also create career opportunities. For example, many international companies expect employees to communicate in English.
>
> In my own experience, improving English gives me access to more learning resources and makes communication easier.
>
> Overall, I think English is not just a school subject; it's a practical skill that can be useful in many areas of life.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Make Your Answer Longer Without Repeating

When you run out of ideas, ask yourself:

### Question 1:

> **Why?**

### Question 2:

> **How?**

### Question 3:

> **Can I give an example?**

### Question 4:

> **What happens as a result?**

### Question 5:

> **What is the opposite side?**

### Question 6:

> **What happens in my own experience?**

### Question 7:

> **What might happen in the future?**

এই 7টি question তোমাকে **2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে সাহায্য করবে।**

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — The 7-Question Expansion Method

যেকোনো topic:

> **I think X is important.**

Then ask:

1. **Why?**
2. **How?**
3. **Example?**
4. **Result?**
5. **Problem?**
6. **Personal experience?**
7. **Future?**

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Example: “Is Exercise Important?”

### IDEA

> Exercise is important.

### WHY

> Why? Because it improves physical and mental health.

### HOW

> How? It keeps the body active and helps reduce stress.

### EXAMPLE

> For example, walking for 30 minutes every day can improve fitness.

### RESULT

> As a result, people may feel healthier and more energetic.

### PROBLEM

> However, many people don't exercise because they have busy schedules.

### PERSONAL

> In my own experience, even a short walk helps me feel more refreshed.

### FUTURE

> In the future, I want to make exercise a regular part of my routine.

🔥 এখন একটি ছোট idea থেকে অনেক বড় answer তৈরি হলো।

---

# 🎤 Part 38 — Practice Questions

আজ এগুলোর answer practice করবে:

### 1.

> Why is education important?

### 2.

> Why do people use social media?

### 3.

> Why is exercise important?

### 4.

> Why do people learn English?

### 5.

> Why is time management important?

### 6.

> Why do people use smartphones?

### 7.

> Why is communication important?

### 8.

> Why is teamwork important?

### 9.

> Why do people travel?

### 10.

> Why is having a goal important?

---

# 🏆 Part 39 — Your Speaking Rule

আজ থেকে answer দেওয়ার সময়:

### ❌ Don't do this:

> I think education is important because it is important.

### ❌ Don't do this:

> I think education is important. That's all.

### ✅ Do this:

> I think education is important **because...**

↓

> **One reason is...**

↓

> **This means...**

↓

> **For example...**

↓

> **As a result...**

↓

> **However...**

↓

> **In my experience...**

↓

> **Overall...**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 62-এর Golden Formula

# **I → W → H → E → R → C**

**I = Idea**

> I think...

**W = Why**

> One reason is...

**H = How**

> This means...

**E = Example**

> For example...

**R = Result**

> As a result...

**C = Conclusion**

> That's why...

---

# 🎯 Final Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **Why is learning English important?**

### Target:

**Beginner attempt:** 1 minute
**Normal attempt:** 2 minutes
**Good attempt:** 3 minutes
**Challenge:** 5 minutes

### Use this structure:

> I think learning English is important because...
>
> One major reason is...
>
> This means that...
>
> Another reason is...
>
> For example...
>
> In my own experience...
>
> As a result...
>
> However...
>
> In the future...
>
> Overall, that's why...

---

## 🔗 Lesson 61 → Lesson 62 → Lesson 63

**Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions**
→ **What do you think?**

**Lesson 62 — Giving Reasons & Explaining Ideas**
→ **Why do you think so? How? Give an example.**

### **Lesson 63 — Agreeing & Disagreeing Naturally**

→ অন্য মানুষের opinion শুনে **“I agree”, “I partly agree”, “I see your point, but…”, “I don't completely agree”**—এভাবে conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া এবং disagreement-কে polite ও natural করা।


------

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 63

# 🤝 Agreeing & Disagreeing Naturally

**Main Goal:** Conversation বন্ধ না করে অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে **agree, partly agree, disagree, explain why, give examples এবং নিজের position defend** করা।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

**Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions**
→ নিজের মতামত বলা

**Lesson 62 — Giving Reasons & Explaining Ideas**
→ মতামতের কারণ + explanation + example দেওয়া

# **Lesson 63 — Agreeing & Disagreeing Naturally**

→ এখন অন্য কেউ opinion দিলে **শুধু “Yes/No” না বলে conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া।**

---

# 🎯 আজকের Main Skill

ধরো কেউ বলল:

> **“I think social media is bad for people.”**

তুমি শুধু:

> ❌ Yes.

বা

> ❌ No.

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **I agree. One of the main problems is that people spend too much time on it.**

অথবা:

> **I partly agree, but I think it depends on how people use it.**

অথবা:

> **I see your point, but I'm not sure I completely agree.**

এভাবে conversation continue করবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Agree করা

সবচেয়ে basic:

> I agree.

> I agree with you.

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

কিন্তু এগুলোর পর **reason** যোগ করবে।

### Example:

> I completely agree. Social media can be very distracting.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Strong Agreement

### 1. Absolutely.

> Absolutely. I think that's true.

### 2. Definitely.

> Definitely. Technology has changed our lives.

### 3. Exactly.

> Exactly. That's what I was thinking.

### 4. That's true.

> That's true. People spend too much time online.

### 5. I couldn't agree more.

> I couldn't agree more. Exercise is important for everyone.

🔥 **I couldn't agree more** = আমি এর সাথে পুরোপুরি একমত।

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Natural Agreement

Conversation-এ শুধু:

> I agree.

এর চেয়ে এগুলো natural:

> That's a good point.

> That's a very good point.

> I think you're right.

> I agree with you on that.

> I feel the same way.

> I have to agree with you.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “That's a Good Point”

এটি খুব useful।

Someone:

> Online learning is more flexible.

You:

> That's a good point. Students can study whenever they have time.

এখানে:

**Agree → Explain**

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “I Feel the Same Way”

Meaning:

**আমিও একইভাবে মনে করি।**

> I feel the same way. I also think technology saves a lot of time.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “I Have to Agree”

Natural conversational phrase:

> I have to agree with you. Social media can be useful for learning.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Agree + Add Another Point

এটা খুব important।

### Person:

> Exercise is good for physical health.

### You:

> I completely agree. **I'd also add that** exercise is good for mental health.

Useful expressions:

> I'd also add that...

> Another thing I'd mention is...

> That's true, and I'd also say that...

> I agree, and there's another benefit...

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Example

> I agree that technology saves time. **I'd also add that** it gives people access to information very quickly.

এখন conversation এগিয়ে গেল।

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Partial Agreement

সব opinion-এ পুরো agree করতে হবে না।

Useful:

> I partly agree.

> I agree to some extent.

> I agree up to a point.

> I can see both sides.

> I agree with you to some extent.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “I Partly Agree”

### Example:

> I partly agree. Social media has some disadvantages, but it can also be useful for communication and learning.

🔥 Formula:

# **Partial Agreement → Other Side**

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “I Agree to Some Extent”

Meaning:

**কিছুটা একমত।**

> I agree to some extent, but I think there are other factors to consider.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “I Agree Up to a Point”

Natural advanced expression:

> I agree up to a point, but I don't think technology is always the problem.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Polite Disagreement

সরাসরি:

> ❌ You're wrong.

এটা rude শোনাতে পারে।

Better:

> I'm not sure I agree.

> I don't completely agree.

> I see your point, but...

> I understand what you mean, but...

> I'm not sure that's always the case.

> I look at it slightly differently.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “I See Your Point, But...”

এটি আজকের **most important disagreement phrase**।

### Example:

> I see your point, but I think technology has more benefits than disadvantages.

Structure:

# **Acknowledge → But → Your Opinion**

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “I Understand What You Mean, But...”

> I understand what you mean, but I think social media can also have positive effects.

এতে disagreement polite থাকে।

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “I'm Not Sure I Agree”

> I'm not sure I agree. I think the problem is not technology itself, but how people use it.

🔥 Strong disagreement হলেও polite।

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “I Look at It Differently”

Advanced:

> I look at it slightly differently.

Then explain:

> I think technology is useful when people use it responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Disagree + Reason

কখনো শুধু:

> I disagree.

বলবে না।

বলবে:

> I don't completely agree because...

### Example:

> I don't completely agree because social media can also help people learn, network, and find job opportunities.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Disagree + Example

> I see your point, but I don't completely agree. For example, many people use social media to learn new skills and connect with professionals.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Disagree Without Saying “Disagree”

Very natural:

> I'm not convinced that...

> I'm not sure that's the best way to look at it.

> I wouldn't necessarily say that.

> I'm not sure that's always true.

### Example:

> I'm not convinced that smartphones are making people less social.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “It Depends”

Many disagreements are actually conditional.

Someone:

> Online learning is better than classroom learning.

You:

> It depends. Online learning is more flexible, but classroom learning offers more face-to-face interaction.

🔥 This is an excellent answer because তুমি **দুই side** দেখাচ্ছো।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Agree on One Point, Disagree on Another

This is a very advanced speaking skill.

> I agree with you that technology saves time. However, I'm not sure I agree that it always improves people's quality of life.

Excellent structure:

# **Agree + However + Disagree**

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “While”

> I agree that social media is useful, while I also think it can become addictive.

Better:

> While I agree that social media is useful, I think excessive use can be harmful.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Although”

> Although I agree that technology is convenient, I don't think we should depend on it too much.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “That Being Said”

এটি খুব useful।

> I agree that working from home is convenient. **That being said**, it can sometimes be difficult to communicate with your team.

Meaning:

**এটা ঠিক, তবে তারপরও...**

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “At the Same Time”

> I understand the benefits of social media. At the same time, we shouldn't ignore its negative effects.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “On the Other Hand”

> Online learning is flexible. On the other hand, traditional classrooms provide more direct interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Agreeing With a Specific Point

পুরো opinion-এর সাথে agree না করেও একটা point agree করতে পারো।

> I agree with that particular point.

> I agree with you on that specific issue.

> I think you're right about that.

### Example:

> I don't completely agree with your overall argument, but I think you're right about the importance of communication.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Asking the Other Person

Conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়ার জন্য question করো।

> What do you think?

> Why do you think that?

> What makes you say that?

> Do you really think so?

> What about...?

> How do you see it?

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “What Makes You Say That?”

Very useful conversational expression।

### A:

> I think social media is harmful.

### B:

> What makes you say that?

এখন A reason দিতে বাধ্য হবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Why Do You Think So?”

> I agree with you, but why do you think so?

> That's interesting. Why do you think so?

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Challenge Someone Politely

> But don't you think...?

> Wouldn't you say that...?

> What about the fact that...?

### Example:

> I think online learning is always better.

> Wouldn't you say that classroom interaction is also important?

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Full Conversation

### Topic: Social Media

**A:**

> I think social media is bad for society.

**B:**

> I see your point, but I'm not sure I completely agree.

**A:**

> Why not?

**B:**

> Well, I agree that excessive use can be harmful. People can waste a lot of time scrolling through social media. However, I'd also say that social media has some important benefits.

**A:**

> Such as?

**B:**

> For example, people can use it to learn new skills, promote their businesses, and communicate with people around the world.

**A:**

> That's true, but don't you think people are becoming too dependent on it?

**B:**

> Yes, I agree with that. I think the real issue is not social media itself, but how people use it.

🔥 এটাই conversational English।

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Conversation Formula

এই formula মুখস্থ করো:

# **LISTEN**

↓

# **ACKNOWLEDGE**

↓

# **AGREE / PARTLY AGREE / DISAGREE**

↓

# **REASON**

↓

# **EXAMPLE**

↓

# **QUESTION BACK**

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Three Response Levels

### 🟢 Level 1 — Agree

> I agree. I think technology has made life easier.

### 🟡 Level 2 — Partial

> I partly agree. Technology has made life easier, but it also creates new problems.

### 🔴 Level 3 — Disagree

> I see your point, but I don't completely agree. I think technology has more benefits than disadvantages when used responsibly.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Opinion Conversation: Technology

### Question:

> Technology is making people lazy. Do you agree?

### Answer:

> I partly agree with that. Technology can certainly make some tasks easier, and people may become dependent on it. For example, instead of doing calculations themselves, people can use calculators or apps.
>
> However, I don't think technology automatically makes people lazy. It depends on how we use it. The same technology can also help people work more efficiently and learn new skills.
>
> So overall, I'd say technology can encourage laziness if we misuse it, but it can also increase productivity when used properly.

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Agreeing With a Friend

### Friend:

> I think learning English takes too much time.

### You:

> I understand what you mean. It does take time, especially if you want to become fluent. However, I think regular practice makes a big difference. You don't necessarily need to study for several hours every day.

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — Disagreeing With a Teacher

Polite:

> I understand your point, but I have a slightly different opinion.

> I see what you mean, although I think...

> That's an interesting perspective. However,...

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — Disagreeing in an Interview

> I understand the argument, but I would look at the issue slightly differently.

> I agree with part of that, but I think there are some other factors to consider.

Very professional।

---

# 🧠 Part 40 — Avoid These Mistakes

### ❌ Too direct:

> You're wrong.

### Better:

> I'm not sure I agree.

---

### ❌

> No, that's wrong.

### Better:

> I see your point, but I don't think that's always the case.

---

### ❌

> I disagree because you're wrong.

### Better:

> I don't completely agree because I think there are other factors to consider.

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — Useful Vocabulary

| Expression    | Meaning      |
| ------------- | ------------ |
| agree         | একমত হওয়া   |
| disagree      | দ্বিমত হওয়া |
| partly agree  | আংশিক একমত   |
| point of view | দৃষ্টিভঙ্গি  |
| perspective   | দৃষ্টিভঙ্গি  |
| argument      | যুক্তি/মত    |
| opinion       | মতামত        |
| issue         | বিষয়        |
| benefit       | সুবিধা       |
| drawback      | অসুবিধা      |
| advantage     | সুবিধা       |
| disadvantage  | অসুবিধা      |
| reasonable    | যুক্তিসঙ্গত  |
| convincing    | বিশ্বাসযোগ্য |
| perspective   | দৃষ্টিভঙ্গি  |

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — 15 Must-Learn Expressions

### Agreement

1. **I completely agree.**
2. **Absolutely.**
3. **Exactly.**
4. **That's a good point.**
5. **I couldn't agree more.**

### Partial Agreement

6. **I partly agree.**
7. **I agree to some extent.**
8. **I agree up to a point.**
9. **I can see both sides.**
10. **I agree with you on that.**

### Disagreement

11. **I see your point, but...**
12. **I understand what you mean, but...**
13. **I'm not sure I completely agree.**
14. **I look at it slightly differently.**
15. **I'm not convinced that...**

---

# 🎤 Part 43 — Practice Topic 1

## **“Social Media Is More Harmful Than Useful.”**

Answer:

> Do you agree?

Try three versions:

### Version A — Agree

> I completely agree because...

### Version B — Partly Agree

> I partly agree, but...

### Version C — Disagree

> I see your point, but...

---

# 🎤 Part 44 — Practice Topic 2

## **“Money Can Buy Happiness.”**

Start:

> I don't completely agree with that.

Then:

> One reason is...

> For example...

> However...

> At the same time...

> Overall...

---

# 🎤 Part 45 — Practice Topic 3

## **“Online Education Is Better Than Traditional Education.”**

Try:

> I can see why people think that.

Then:

> One advantage is...

> However...

> On the other hand...

> In my opinion...

---

# 🎤 Part 46 — Practice Topic 4

## **“Everyone Should Work From Home.”**

Try a balanced answer:

> I agree that working from home has many advantages, but I'm not sure it works for everyone.

Then explain:

* flexibility
* productivity
* communication
* teamwork
* job type

---

# 🎤 Part 47 — 2-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **“Technology has made people less social.”**

Use:

### 1. Your position

> I partly agree...

### 2. Reason

> One reason is...

### 3. Example

> For example...

### 4. Opposite side

> However...

### 5. Personal experience

> In my own experience...

### 6. Conclusion

> Overall...

---

# 🎤 Part 48 — 5-Minute Challenge

Topic:

# **“Social Media Is Good for Society.”**

Speak for 5 minutes.

Include:

1. Your opinion
2. Why you think so
3. First reason
4. Example
5. Second reason
6. Example
7. Opposing opinion
8. Why people disagree
9. Your response
10. Personal experience
11. Future impact
12. Conclusion

---

# 🏆 Lesson 63 — Golden Formula

## When someone gives an opinion:

# **ACKNOWLEDGE → POSITION → REASON → EXAMPLE → QUESTION**

### Example:

> **That's a good point.**
> **I partly agree, but** I think there are some exceptions.
> **One reason is that**...
> **For example**...
> **What do you think about...?**

---

# ⭐ The Most Important Rule

Conversation-এ **Yes/No answer দেবে না।**

### Instead:

> **Yes + Why**

> **No + Why**

> **Partly + Why**

> **Agree + Add**

> **Disagree + Explain**

---

# 🎯 Final Challenge

আজ একজন partner থাকলে তাকে এই question করো:

> **“Do you think social media is good or bad for society?”**

তার answer শোনার পর তোমাকে **কমপক্ষে 30–60 seconds** response দিতে হবে।

### তোমার response structure:

> **I see your point...**
>
> **I agree / partly agree / disagree...**
>
> **The main reason is...**
>
> **For example...**
>
> **Another thing I'd add is...**
>
> **What do you think about...?**

এভাবে practice করলে **conversation থামিয়ে দেওয়ার বদলে conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়ার skill** তৈরি হবে।

---

## 🔗 Next Lesson

### **Lesson 64 — Asking Follow-up Questions & Keeping a Conversation Going**

এখানে শিখবে কীভাবে অন্য ব্যক্তির answer থেকে **নতুন question বের করে conversation 5–10 মিনিট পর্যন্ত চালিয়ে যেতে হয়**—যেমন:

> **Really? Why?**
> **How did that happen?**
> **What happened next?**
> **What do you mean by that?**
> **Can you give me an example?**
> **How do you feel about it?**
> **What would you do if...?**


------

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 64

# 💬 Asking Follow-up Questions & Keeping a Conversation Going

**Main Goal:** অন্য একজনের answer শোনার পর conversation যেন থেমে না যায়।
আজ আমরা শিখব কীভাবে **একটি answer থেকে পরের question তৈরি করতে হয়** এবং conversation **5–10 মিনিট** চালিয়ে যেতে হয়।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

**Lesson 61 — Giving Opinions**
→ নিজের মতামত বলা

**Lesson 62 — Giving Reasons & Explaining Ideas**
→ কারণ + explanation + example

**Lesson 63 — Agreeing & Disagreeing**
→ অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে agree/disagree করা

**Lesson 64 — Follow-up Questions**
→ অন্যের answer থেকে নতুন question করে conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া

---

# 🎯 আজকের সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ Skill

ধরো আমি বললাম:

> **I like traveling.**

তুমি শুধু:

> Oh, nice.

বললে conversation শেষ।

কিন্তু তুমি যদি বলো:

> **Where do you usually travel?**

আমি বললাম:

> I usually travel with my friends.

তুমি:

> **Where did you go most recently?**

আমি:

> I went to Cox's Bazar.

তুমি:

> **What did you like most about the trip?**

আমি:

> I really enjoyed the beach.

তুমি:

> **How long did you stay there?**

এভাবে conversation চলতে থাকবে।

# **Answer → Follow-up Question → Answer → Follow-up Question**

এটাই আজকের মূল skill।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Follow-up Question কী?

**Follow-up question** হলো আগের কথার ওপর ভিত্তি করে করা পরের প্রশ্ন।

### Example:

> A: I watched a movie yesterday.

❌ Random question:

> What is your favorite food?

✅ Follow-up:

> What movie did you watch?

আরও:

> Did you enjoy it?

> Who did you watch it with?

> What was it about?

> Would you recommend it?

প্রতিটি প্রশ্ন **আগের answer-এর সাথে connected**।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — The Golden Rule

## **Don't change the topic too quickly.**

যদি কেউ বলে:

> I went to Cox's Bazar last month.

তাহলে immediately:

> What's your favorite food?

❌

বরং একই topic থেকে:

> Who did you go with?

> How long did you stay?

> What did you do there?

> What did you like most?

এভাবে topic-এর **depth** বাড়াবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — 7 Types of Follow-up Questions

আজ আমরা 7 ধরনের question শিখব:

1. **What?**
2. **Where?**
3. **When?**
4. **Who?**
5. **Why?**
6. **How?**
7. **What happened next?**

এগুলো দিয়ে অসংখ্য conversation তৈরি করা যায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “What?” Questions

যখন কেউ কোনো কিছু mention করে:

> What did you do?

> What happened?

> What was it like?

> What did you enjoy most?

> What kind of...?

### Example:

> I watched a movie yesterday.

> **What movie did you watch?**

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “Where?” Questions

> Where did you go?

> Where did you buy it?

> Where did you learn that?

> Where do you usually go?

### Example:

> I bought a new phone.

> **Where did you buy it?**

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “When?” Questions

> When did you start?

> When did it happen?

> When did you first learn about it?

> When do you usually practice?

### Example:

> I started learning English last year.

> **When exactly did you start?**

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Who?” Questions

> Who did you go with?

> Who told you about it?

> Who helped you?

> Who do you usually practice with?

### Example:

> I went to the museum yesterday.

> **Who did you go with?**

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Why?” Questions

এটি conversation চালানোর সবচেয়ে powerful question।

> Why did you choose it?

> Why do you like it?

> Why did you decide to do that?

> Why do you think so?

### Example:

> I want to learn Python.

> **Why do you want to learn Python?**

Answer:

> Because I want to become a backend developer.

এখন নতুন question:

> **Why did you choose backend development?**

Conversation গভীর হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “How?” Questions

> How did you learn it?

> How did you feel?

> How long did it take?

> How often do you do it?

> How was your experience?

### Example:

> I learned English from YouTube.

> **How did you practice speaking?**

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “What Happened Next?”

Storytelling-এর জন্য অসাধারণ।

> What happened next?

> And then what happened?

> What did you do after that?

### Example:

> I missed my bus.

> Oh, really? **What happened next?**

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Tell Me More”

যখন তুমি আরও information চাও:

> Tell me more about that.

> Can you tell me more about it?

> I'd like to hear more about that.

### Example:

> I had an interesting experience at university.

> Really? **Tell me more about it.**

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “What Do You Mean?”

যদি বুঝতে না পারো:

> What do you mean?

আরও polite:

> What do you mean by that?

> Could you explain what you mean?

> Could you explain that a little more?

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Can You Give Me an Example?”

কেউ general কথা বললে example চাও:

> Technology is very useful.

তুমি:

> **Can you give me an example?**

অথবা:

> What kind of benefits do you mean?

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “How Do You Feel About...?”

Opinion + emotion বের করার জন্য:

> How do you feel about it?

> How did you feel about that?

> How do you feel about working from home?

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “What Do You Think About...?”

Opinion বের করতে:

> What do you think about online learning?

> What do you think about social media?

> What do you think about this idea?

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Would You...?”

Future/preference:

> Would you do it again?

> Would you recommend it?

> Would you like to go there again?

### Example:

> I visited Cox's Bazar last year.

> **Would you like to go there again?**

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “Have You Ever...?”

Experience বের করতে:

> Have you ever traveled alone?

> Have you ever tried this?

> Have you ever experienced something similar?

### Example:

> I traveled to Sylhet last year.

> **Have you ever been to Sylhet?**

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “How Often...?”

Routine:

> How often do you exercise?

> How often do you watch movies?

> How often do you use social media?

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “How Long...?”

Duration:

> How long have you been learning English?

> How long did you stay there?

> How long did it take?

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “What Was It Like?”

Experience describe করার জন্য।

> What was your trip like?

> What was your first day at university like?

> What was the interview like?

### Example:

> I had a job interview yesterday.

> **What was it like?**

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Follow-up Question Ladder

এটা আজকের সবচেয়ে important technique।

একটি answer থেকে **পরের question বের করবে।**

### Example:

**A:**

> I went to Sylhet last week.

### Q1:

> Who did you go with?

**A:**

> I went with my friends.

### Q2:

> How long did you stay?

**A:**

> We stayed for three days.

### Q3:

> What did you do there?

**A:**

> We visited several places.

### Q4:

> What place did you like most?

**A:**

> I liked Jaflong the most.

### Q5:

> What did you like about it?

**A:**

> The natural scenery was beautiful.

### Q6:

> Would you like to go there again?

🔥 এক topic থেকেই 5–10 মিনিট conversation করা সম্ভব।

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Don't Interrogate

একটার পর একটা question করলে interview-এর মতো শোনাতে পারে।

### ❌

> Where did you go?

> Who did you go with?

> When did you go?

> How long did you stay?

> What did you eat?

এভাবে শুধু question করলে unnatural।

### ✅ Better:

> Oh, you went to Sylhet? **That sounds interesting. Who did you go with?**

তার answer-এর ওপর reaction দাও।

> Oh, with your friends? **How long did you stay?**

তারপর:

> Three days? **What did you enjoy most?**

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Reaction + Question

আজকের সবচেয়ে important conversational formula:

# **REACTION → COMMENT → QUESTION**

### Example:

> Oh, really? That sounds exciting. **Where did you go?**

> Wow, that's interesting. **How did you learn that?**

> That's nice. **Who did you go with?**

> I didn't know that. **How did that happen?**

এতে conversation natural হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Natural Reactions

মুখস্থ করো:

> Oh, really?

> Really?

> That's interesting.

> That's nice.

> That's great.

> Wow, that's amazing.

> That sounds interesting.

> That sounds difficult.

> I didn't know that.

> That's a good point.

> I can understand that.

> No way!

> Seriously?

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Reaction + Follow-up

### Example 1

> I got a new job.

> **Oh, really? Congratulations! How did you find the job?**

### Example 2

> I went to a concert.

> **Wow, that sounds fun! Who did you go with?**

### Example 3

> I failed my exam.

> **Oh, I'm sorry to hear that. What happened?**

### Example 4

> I started learning guitar.

> **That's interesting! What made you decide to learn guitar?**

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Open Questions vs Closed Questions

### Closed question:

> Did you enjoy the trip?

Answer:

> Yes.

Conversation শেষ হতে পারে।

### Open question:

> What did you enjoy most about the trip?

Answer:

> I enjoyed the beach and the food.

Conversation continue করা সহজ।

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Use More Open Questions

Instead of:

> Did you like it?

Use:

> What did you like about it?

Instead of:

> Was it difficult?

Use:

> What was difficult about it?

Instead of:

> Did you enjoy the movie?

Use:

> What did you think about the movie?

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Convert Closed → Open

### ❌

> Did you like your trip?

### ✅

> What did you like most about your trip?

---

### ❌

> Was your interview difficult?

### ✅

> What was the most difficult part of your interview?

---

### ❌

> Did you enjoy the movie?

### ✅

> What did you think about the movie?

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Follow-up Questions for Experiences

Someone:

> I went to a wedding.

Ask:

> Who got married?

> Where was the wedding?

> Who did you go with?

> What was the wedding like?

> What did you enjoy most?

> What did you eat?

> How long did it last?

> Would you go to another wedding like that?

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Follow-up Questions for Opinions

Someone:

> I think online learning is better.

Ask:

> Why do you think so?

> What makes you say that?

> What are the main advantages?

> Can you give me an example?

> Do you think it works for everyone?

> What about traditional classrooms?

> Has your opinion always been the same?

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Follow-up Questions for Plans

Someone:

> I want to travel next month.

Ask:

> Where are you planning to go?

> Who are you going with?

> Why did you choose that place?

> How long will you stay?

> What do you want to do there?

> Have you been there before?

> What are you most excited about?

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Follow-up Questions for Problems

Someone:

> I'm having trouble learning English.

Don't immediately give advice.

Ask:

> What kind of problems are you having?

> Which part is most difficult for you?

> How long have you been learning?

> What have you tried so far?

> What do you find most frustrating?

Then give advice.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Follow-up Questions for Daily Life

Someone:

> I usually wake up at 7 AM.

Ask:

> What do you usually do after waking up?

> Do you exercise in the morning?

> What time do you start work or study?

> What do you usually have for breakfast?

> Do you prefer mornings or evenings?

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Conversation Example: Morning

**A:**

> I usually wake up early.

**B:**

> Oh, really? What time do you usually wake up?

**A:**

> Around 6:30.

**B:**

> That's quite early. What do you usually do first?

**A:**

> I drink some water and go for a walk.

**B:**

> Nice. How long do you usually walk?

**A:**

> About 30 minutes.

**B:**

> Do you feel more energetic after walking?

**A:**

> Yes, definitely.

**B:**

> That's great. Do you prefer exercising in the morning or evening?

🔥 দেখো—একটি answer থেকে next question।

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Conversation Example: Learning English

**A:**

> I'm learning English.

**B:**

> Oh, nice! How long have you been learning English?

**A:**

> For about a year.

**B:**

> That's good. Which part is most difficult for you?

**A:**

> Speaking.

**B:**

> I understand. What do you usually do to practice speaking?

**A:**

> I practice by myself.

**B:**

> That's a good start. Do you ever practice with other people?

**A:**

> Not very often.

**B:**

> Why not?

**A:**

> I don't have many people to practice with.

**B:**

> I see. Would you like to join an English speaking group?

🔥 এখানে conversation **answer → question → answer → question** pattern-এ চলছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — The “Echo” Technique

অন্যের answer-এর একটি গুরুত্বপূর্ণ word repeat করে question করো।

### Person:

> I started learning Python last year.

You:

> **Python? How did you get interested in it?**

---

### Person:

> I went to Cox's Bazar.

You:

> **Cox's Bazar? What did you enjoy most there?**

---

### Person:

> I got a new job.

You:

> **A new job? Congratulations! What kind of job is it?**

এটা conversation-কে natural করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — The “Keyword” Technique

Answer থেকে **একটি keyword ধরো।**

> I started working at a software company last month.

Keywords:

**software company**

Question:

> What kind of software does the company develop?

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — The “Why → How → Example” Chain

Opinion:

> I like working from home.

Follow-up:

> **Why do you like it?**

Answer:

> Because it's flexible.

Next:

> **How does it make your work easier?**

Answer:

> I don't have to commute.

Next:

> **How much time do you save?**

এখন conversation অনেক deep।

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Don't Ask Questions You Already Know

যদি person বলেছে:

> I went to Dhaka yesterday.

তুমি:

> Where did you go?

ঠিক আছে।

কিন্তু সে যদি বলে:

> I went to Dhanmondi yesterday.

তুমি:

> Where did you go?

❌ আবার একই information চাওয়া।

বরং:

> What did you do in Dhanmondi?

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Follow-up Question Depth

একটি topic-কে তিন level-এ গভীর করো।

### Level 1 — Fact

> Where did you go?

### Level 2 — Detail

> What did you do there?

### Level 3 — Opinion/Feeling

> What did you like most about the place?

### Level 4 — Reflection

> Would you like to go there again?

এটাই **deep conversation**।

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — 4-Level Conversation Formula

# **FACT → DETAIL → FEELING → REFLECTION**

Example:

> Where did you go?

↓

> What did you do?

↓

> How did you feel?

↓

> Would you do it again?

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Story Follow-up

যদি কেউ story বলে:

> Yesterday I missed my bus.

তুমি:

> Oh, really? What happened?

তারপর:

> What did you do after that?

তারপর:

> Were you late?

তারপর:

> How did you feel?

তারপর:

> Would you do anything differently next time?

🔥 Story naturally long হবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Emotion Questions

Conversation deeper করতে:

> How did you feel?

> Were you nervous?

> Were you excited?

> Were you surprised?

> What was the most difficult part?

> What made you happy?

> What disappointed you?

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Future Follow-up

> What are you planning to do next?

> Would you like to do it again?

> What would you do differently next time?

> What are you hoping will happen?

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Hypothetical Follow-up

Advanced:

> What would you do if you had more time?

> What would you do if you could travel anywhere?

> If you had the opportunity, would you do it again?

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — 20 Must-Learn Follow-up Questions

1. **Really?**
2. **Why?**
3. **Why do you think so?**
4. **What happened?**
5. **What happened next?**
6. **How did that happen?**
7. **How did you feel?**
8. **What was it like?**
9. **Can you tell me more about that?**
10. **Can you give me an example?**
11. **What do you mean by that?**
12. **How long did it take?**
13. **How often do you do that?**
14. **Who did you go with?**
15. **Where did you go?**
16. **What did you enjoy most?**
17. **What made you choose that?**
18. **Would you do it again?**
19. **What would you do differently?**
20. **What are you planning to do next?**

---

# 🎤 Part 47 — Practice Conversation 1

## Topic: Movies

Start:

> **What kind of movies do you like?**

If answer:

> I like action movies.

Ask:

> What do you like about action movies?

Then:

> What's your favorite action movie?

Then:

> What did you like about it?

Then:

> Who is your favorite actor?

Then:

> Would you recommend that movie?

---

# 🎤 Part 48 — Practice Conversation 2

## Topic: Travel

Start:

> **Do you like traveling?**

If:

> Yes, I do.

Don't ask another random question.

Ask:

> Where have you traveled recently?

Then:

> Who did you go with?

Then:

> What was the trip like?

Then:

> What did you enjoy most?

Then:

> Would you like to go there again?

---

# 🎤 Part 49 — Practice Conversation 3

## Topic: Career

Start:

> **What kind of work would you like to do in the future?**

Then:

> Why are you interested in that field?

> How did you become interested in it?

> What skills do you need?

> What are you doing to develop those skills?

> What is the most difficult part?

> Where do you see yourself in five years?

---

# 🎤 Part 50 — 5-Minute Challenge

আজ একটি topic নাও:

# **“Your Last Weekend”**

Partner-এর সাথে conversation:

### Start:

> What did you do last weekend?

তার answer থেকে question তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> Where...?

> Who...?

> What...?

> Why...?

> How...?

> What happened next?

> How did you feel?

> What did you enjoy most?

> Would you do it again?

> What would you do differently?

### Target:

**5 minutes without changing the topic.**

---

# 🏆 Lesson 64 — Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **LISTEN → REACT → PICK A KEYWORD → ASK → LISTEN → FOLLOW UP**

### Example:

**Listen:**

> I went to Sylhet.

### React:

> Oh, really? That sounds nice.

### Keyword:

**Sylhet**

### Ask:

> Who did you go with?

### Listen:

> I went with my friends.

### Follow-up:

> How long did you stay?

---

# ⭐ The Most Important Rule

## **Don't prepare the next question before listening.**

অন্য ব্যক্তি কী বলছে **সেটা শুনে** question তৈরি করবে।

কারণ natural conversation হলো:

> **Listen → Understand → Respond**

না যে:

> ❌ Memorize → Ask → Memorize → Ask

---

# 🎯 Today's Speaking Challenge

একজন partner-এর সাথে অথবা নিজে practice করো:

### Topic:

> **“My Last Weekend”**

প্রথম question:

> **What did you do last weekend?**

তারপর answer অনুযায়ী অন্তত **10টি follow-up question** তৈরি করো।

### Target chain:

> What?
> ↓
> Where?
> ↓
> Who?
> ↓
> Why?
> ↓
> How?
> ↓
> What happened next?
> ↓
> How did you feel?
> ↓
> What did you enjoy most?
> ↓
> Would you do it again?
> ↓
> What would you do differently?

এভাবে তুমি **একটি ছোট answer থেকে 5–10 মিনিটের conversation** তৈরি করতে পারবে।

---

## 🔗 Next Lesson — Lesson 65

### **Storytelling: How to Tell a Story in English**

Lesson 64-এ আমরা শিখলাম অন্যের story-তে **follow-up question** করা।

Lesson 65-এ শিখব নিজের story **শুরু → ঘটনা → সমস্যা → গুরুত্বপূর্ণ মুহূর্ত → ফলাফল → feeling → lesson** এই structure-এ **3–5 মিনিট ধরে naturally বলতে**।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 65

# 📖 Storytelling — How to Tell a Story in English

**Main Goal:** আজ থেকে শুধু প্রশ্নের উত্তর নয়—নিজের **একটি ঘটনা/experience 3–5 মিনিট ধরে শুরু থেকে শেষ পর্যন্ত** বলতে শিখবে।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

* **Lesson 61:** নিজের opinion বলা
* **Lesson 62:** reason + explanation + example
* **Lesson 63:** agree/disagree করে conversation চালানো
* **Lesson 64:** অন্যের answer থেকে follow-up question করা
* **Lesson 65:** **নিজের story/experience বিস্তারিত বলা**

---

# 🎯 আজকের মূল Skill

ধরো কেউ জিজ্ঞেস করল:

> **What happened yesterday?**

শুধু:

> I went to the market.

বললে conversation শেষ।

কিন্তু storytelling করলে:

> Yesterday, I went to the market because I needed to buy a few things. I left home around five in the afternoon. At first, everything was normal, but when I arrived at the market, I realized that I had forgotten my wallet. So I had to go back home...

এখন story তৈরি হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Storytelling কী?

**Storytelling = একটি ঘটনা ধারাবাহিকভাবে বলা।**

অর্থাৎ:

> **What happened first → What happened next → What happened after that → How it ended**

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — The Golden Story Structure

আজকের সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ formula:

# **WHEN → WHERE → BACKGROUND → EVENT → PROBLEM → ACTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

সহজ করে:

1. **When?** — কখন?
2. **Where?** — কোথায়?
3. **Background** — কী হচ্ছিল?
4. **Event** — কী ঘটল?
5. **Problem** — সমস্যা কী হলো?
6. **Action** — তুমি কী করলে?
7. **Result** — শেষ পর্যন্ত কী হলো?
8. **Feeling** — কেমন লেগেছিল?
9. **Lesson** — কী শিখলে?

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Start the Story

Story শুরু করার জন্য:

> Yesterday,...

> Last week,...

> A few months ago,...

> When I was in school,...

> One day,...

> Last year,...

> Recently,...

### Example:

> **Last week, something interesting happened to me.**

Excellent opening.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Set the Scene

শুরুতেই listener-কে background দাও।

### Example:

> It happened last Friday when I was returning home from university.

এখানে:

* **When:** last Friday
* **Situation:** returning home
* **Place:** implied university/home

আরও:

> It was a rainy evening, and I was in a hurry to get home.

এখন listener scene imagine করতে পারছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “At First”

Story শুরু করার জন্য অত্যন্ত useful:

> At first,...

### Example:

> At first, everything was going normally.

> At first, I didn't think anything unusual would happen.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Move the Story Forward

একটি ঘটনা থেকে পরের ঘটনায় যেতে:

> Then,...

> After that,...

> A few minutes later,...

> Shortly after that,...

> Later,...

> Eventually,...

> Finally,...

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Story Connectors

| Expression          | ব্যবহার         |
| ------------------- | --------------- |
| At first            | শুরুতে          |
| Then                | তারপর           |
| After that          | এরপর            |
| A few minutes later | কয়েক মিনিট পরে |
| Meanwhile           | একই সময়ে       |
| Suddenly            | হঠাৎ            |
| Eventually          | শেষ পর্যন্ত     |
| Finally             | অবশেষে          |
| In the end          | শেষে            |

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Suddenly”

Unexpected event:

> Suddenly, I heard a loud noise.

> Suddenly, the bus stopped.

> Suddenly, I realized that I had lost my phone.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “I Realized”

Storytelling-এ খুব useful:

> I realized that...

### Example:

> I suddenly realized that I had left my phone at home.

> Then I realized that I was going in the wrong direction.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “I Noticed”

কোনো কিছু observe করলে:

> I noticed that...

### Example:

> I noticed that everyone was looking in the same direction.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Introduce a Problem

Story interesting করার জন্য problem যোগ করো।

### Useful expressions:

> Unfortunately,...

> The problem was that...

> The only problem was...

> Things became difficult when...

> That's when...

### Example:

> Unfortunately, the bus broke down halfway through the journey.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “That's When...”

এটি storytelling-এর খুব powerful phrase।

> That's when I realized I had lost my wallet.

> That's when things became complicated.

> That's when I decided to call my friend.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — What Did You Do?

Action বলো:

> So I decided to...

> I tried to...

> I had to...

> I immediately...

> I asked...

> I called...

### Example:

> I immediately called my friend and asked him for help.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Result

তারপর কী হলো?

> Luckily,...

> Fortunately,...

> Eventually,...

> In the end,...

### Example:

> Luckily, my friend was nearby and came to help me.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Feelings

Storytelling-এ emotion খুব important।

### Positive:

> I was excited.

> I was happy.

> I felt relieved.

> I was proud.

> I was surprised.

### Negative:

> I was nervous.

> I was worried.

> I was confused.

> I felt embarrassed.

> I was disappointed.

> I was frustrated.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Explain the Feeling

শুধু:

> I was nervous.

বলবে না।

বলবে:

> I was really nervous because I didn't know what to do.

অথবা:

> I felt relieved because everything worked out in the end.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Lesson Learned

Story-এর শেষে:

> I learned that...

> The experience taught me that...

> Since then,...

> After that experience,...

### Example:

> I learned that I should always check my belongings before leaving home.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Full Story Example 1

## 🎯 Topic: Missing a Bus

> **Last month, I had a small but stressful experience while going to university.**
>
> It happened one morning when I was already running a little late. I quickly got ready and left home without having breakfast. I thought I still had enough time to catch my usual bus.
>
> **At first**, everything seemed fine. I reached the bus stop and waited for a few minutes. Then I saw my bus coming from a distance.
>
> Unfortunately, just as I reached the road, the bus started moving again. I tried to catch it, but I was too late.
>
> **That's when** I realized that I was probably going to be late for class. I felt really nervous because I had an important class that morning.
>
> So I decided to take another bus. I waited for about ten minutes, and eventually another bus arrived. Luckily, there wasn't much traffic, so I reached the university only a few minutes late.
>
> **In the end**, everything was fine. I was a little stressed, but I learned an important lesson from the experience. Since then, I try to leave home a little earlier whenever I have something important to do.

⏱️ Approximately **2 minutes**।

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Notice the Structure

উপরের story:

### When:

> Last month

### Background:

> I was going to university.

### Problem:

> I missed my bus.

### Feeling:

> I felt nervous.

### Action:

> I took another bus.

### Result:

> I reached university.

### Lesson:

> Leave home earlier.

এটাই storytelling structure।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Full Story Example 2

## 🎯 Topic: A Difficult Decision

> A few months ago, I had to make a difficult decision about how to spend my free time.
>
> At that time, I was busy with my studies, but I also wanted to improve my programming skills. I had very little free time, so I had to decide whether I should spend more time relaxing or learning new skills.
>
> At first, I wasn't sure what to do. I thought I needed more rest, but at the same time, I knew that improving my skills would be useful for my future career.
>
> After thinking about it for a few days, I decided to create a simple daily routine. I started spending a few hours studying and kept some time for relaxation.
>
> It was difficult in the beginning because I wasn't used to following a strict routine. However, after a few weeks, it became easier.
>
> Eventually, I realized that I didn't have to choose between studying and relaxing. I just needed to manage my time better.
>
> The experience taught me that good time management can make difficult decisions much easier.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Past Tense for Storytelling

Storytelling সাধারণত **Past Tense**-এ হয়।

### Past Simple

> I went.

> I saw.

> I called.

> I decided.

### Past Continuous

> I was walking.

> I was waiting.

> It was raining.

### Past Perfect

আগে ঘটে যাওয়া ঘটনা:

> I had forgotten my wallet.

> I had already left home.

### Example:

> I was walking to the bus stop when I realized that I had forgotten my wallet.

🔥 এই sentence-এ তিনটি time layer আছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Past Simple vs Past Continuous

### Past Simple:

ঘটনা:

> The bus stopped.

### Past Continuous:

Background:

> I was waiting for the bus.

একসাথে:

> **I was waiting for the bus when it suddenly stopped.**

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “When” Story Structure

খুব useful:

> I was ___ when ___ happened.

Examples:

> I was walking home when it started raining.

> I was studying when my friend called me.

> I was watching TV when the electricity went out.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “While”

দুইটা action simultaneously:

> While I was walking home, it started raining.

> While I was studying, my friend called me.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Storytelling Time Expressions

মুখস্থ করো:

> that morning

> that afternoon

> that evening

> the next day

> a few minutes later

> a few hours later

> the following week

> after a while

> eventually

> in the end

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Add Details

Story short:

> I went to a restaurant.

Story better:

> Last Friday evening, I went to a small restaurant near my university with two of my friends.

Details:

* when
* where
* who
* what

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — 5W + 1H Storytelling

একটি story expand করার জন্য:

# **Who**

কে?

# **What**

কি ঘটেছিল?

# **When**

কখন?

# **Where**

কোথায়?

# **Why**

কেন?

# **How**

কীভাবে?

---

# 🔥 Example

> Last Friday, **I** went to **a restaurant near my university** **with two friends** because **we wanted to celebrate finishing an exam**.

এক sentence-এই অনেক information।

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Make Stories Interesting

শুধু facts বলবে না।

### Fact:

> I went to Dhaka.

### Better:

> I went to Dhaka last Friday, but I didn't expect the trip to become so stressful.

Listener ভাববে:

> What happened?

এটাই storytelling।

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Create Curiosity

Useful:

> I didn't know what was going to happen.

> I had no idea what to do.

> At that moment, I thought...

> I never expected that...

### Example:

> I never expected that a normal trip would turn into such an interesting experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Add Dialogue

Story আরও natural করতে ছোট dialogue ব্যবহার করতে পারো।

### Example:

> I called my friend and said, “Where are you?”

> He said, “I'm almost there.”

> I replied, “Okay, I'll wait for you.”

Dialogue story-কে জীবন্ত করে।

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Don't Overuse Dialogue

❌ পুরো story dialogue বানিয়ে ফেলবে না।

Storytelling-এর মূল অংশ:

> **You explain → small dialogue → continue story**

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — “Luckily / Unfortunately”

দুইটি খুব useful word।

### Luckily:

> Luckily, I found my wallet.

### Unfortunately:

> Unfortunately, I missed the train.

### Both:

> Unfortunately, I missed the bus. Luckily, another one arrived ten minutes later.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Storytelling With Emotion

Weak:

> I lost my phone. I found it.

Better:

> When I realized that my phone was missing, I became extremely worried. I checked my bag several times, but I couldn't find it. Luckily, I remembered that I had left it on the table at the restaurant.

এখন listener emotion বুঝতে পারছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Reflection

Story শেষে শুধু:

> That's all.

বলবে না।

বরং:

> Looking back,...

> Thinking about it now,...

> The experience taught me...

### Example:

> Looking back, I think it was a useful experience because it taught me to stay calm in stressful situations.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Story Ending Expressions

> In the end,...

> Eventually,...

> Finally,...

> Looking back,...

> Since then,...

> The experience taught me that...

> I learned that...

> Overall, it was a valuable experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Story Formula for 3 Minutes

### 1. Opening

> Last week, something interesting happened to me.

### 2. Background

> I was going...

### 3. Event

> Then...

### 4. Problem

> Unfortunately...

### 5. Action

> So I decided to...

### 6. Result

> Luckily...

### 7. Feeling

> I felt...

### 8. Lesson

> I learned that...

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Story Formula for 5 Minutes

5-minute story হলে আরও detail যোগ করবে:

# **Opening**

↓

# **Background**

↓

# **Setting**

↓

# **First Event**

↓

# **Second Event**

↓

# **Problem**

↓

# **Reaction**

↓

# **Action**

↓

# **Unexpected Event**

↓

# **Result**

↓

# **Feeling**

↓

# **Reflection**

↓

# **Lesson**

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — Story Topic 1

## “A Memorable Day”

Answer:

> When was it?

> Where were you?

> Who were you with?

> What happened?

> Why was it memorable?

> How did you feel?

> What do you remember most?

> What did you learn?

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — Story Topic 2

## “A Time You Helped Someone”

Structure:

> When did it happen?

> Who needed help?

> What was the problem?

> What did you do?

> What happened afterward?

> How did the person react?

> How did you feel?

> What did you learn?

---

# 🧠 Part 40 — Story Topic 3

## “A Mistake You Made”

Important:

Don't just say:

> I made a mistake.

Explain:

> What happened?

> Why did it happen?

> What did you do?

> What was the result?

> How did you feel?

> What did you learn?

> What would you do differently now?

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — Story Topic 4

## “A Difficult Decision”

Use:

> At that time...

> I had two options...

> I wasn't sure...

> After thinking about it...

> I decided to...

> In the end...

> Looking back...

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — Story Topic 5

## “A Funny Experience”

Start:

> One of the funniest things that happened to me was...

Then:

> At first...

> Suddenly...

> I didn't know what to do...

> Everyone started laughing...

> In the end...

---

# 🔥 Part 43 — Story Topic 6

## “Your First Day at University”

Talk about:

* when
* where
* who
* feeling
* first class
* people
* problem
* memorable moment
* lesson

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — Story Topic 7

## “A Trip You Remember”

Formula:

> We decided to go...

> We left...

> When we arrived...

> At first...

> Then...

> Unfortunately...

> Luckily...

> In the end...

> I really enjoyed...

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — Story Topic 8

## “The First Time You Used a Computer”

Talk about:

> When?

> Where?

> Who taught you?

> What did you do?

> What was difficult?

> How did you feel?

> How has your computer use changed since then?

---

# 🧠 Part 46 — Story Topic 9

## “A Time You Were Nervous”

Start:

> I remember one time when I was extremely nervous.

Then:

> What was happening?

> Why were you nervous?

> What did you do?

> What happened?

> How did you feel afterward?

---

# 🔥 Part 47 — Story Topic 10

## “An Achievement You're Proud Of”

Structure:

> What did you achieve?

> When?

> Why was it difficult?

> What did you do?

> What problems did you face?

> How did you overcome them?

> How did you feel?

> What did you learn?

---

# 🧠 Part 48 — Don't Translate Word-by-Word

বাংলা থেকে:

> “তারপর আমি বাড়িতে চলে গেলাম।”

Word-by-word:

> Then I went to home. ❌

Natural:

> Then I went home. ✅

আর:

> “আমি খুব চিন্তিত হয়ে পড়েছিলাম।”

> I became very worried. ✅

---

# 🔥 Part 49 — Common Storytelling Mistakes

### ❌ Mistake 1: Every sentence starts with “Then”

> Then I went...

> Then I saw...

> Then I called...

Better:

> At first...

> A few minutes later...

> Suddenly...

> After that...

> Eventually...

---

### ❌ Mistake 2: Only short sentences

> I went there. I saw him. I talked to him. I came home.

Better:

> When I arrived there, I saw my friend waiting outside. We talked for a while, and then we decided to go somewhere nearby.

---

### ❌ Mistake 3: No feelings

Story শুধু facts হয়ে যায়।

Add:

> I was surprised.

> I felt nervous.

> I was really happy.

> I felt relieved.

---

# 🏆 Part 50 — The Storytelling Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **S → E → P → A → R → F → L**

### S = Situation

> When/Where/Who

### E = Event

> What happened?

### P = Problem

> What went wrong?

### A = Action

> What did you do?

### R = Result

> What happened finally?

### F = Feeling

> How did you feel?

### L = Lesson

> What did you learn?

---

## Topic:

> **“A Time I Made a Mistake”**

তোমার answer:

> When it happened
> ↓

> ↓

> ↓

> ↓
> Result
> ↓

> ↓
> Lesson

**Target: 2 minutes.**

> **“A Memorable Day in My Life”**

Must include:

* When
* Where
* Who
* What happened
* Problem/interesting event
* Feeling
* Result
* Lesson

**Target: 3 minutes.**

## Topic:

# **“A Trip I Will Never Forget”**

এই structure follow করবে:

> **Opening**
>
> Last year, I had an experience that I still remember clearly.
>
> **Background**
>
> I went to...
>
> **People**
>
> I went with...
>
> **Journey**
>
> We started...
>
> **Event**
>
> When we arrived...
>
> **Problem**
>
> Unfortunately...
>
> **Action**
>
> So we decided to...
>
> **Unexpected event**
>
> Suddenly...
>
> **Result**
>

>

>
> I felt...
>

>
> Looking back...
>

>

# ⭐ Lesson 65-এর 20 Must-Learn Story Expressions

1. **Last week, something interesting happened to me.**
2. **It happened when...**
3. **At first,...**
4. **Everything was going normally.**
5. **Then,...**
6. **A few minutes later,...**
7. **Suddenly,...**
8. **I realized that...**
9. **I noticed that...**
10. **Unfortunately,...**
11. **The problem was that...**
12. **That's when...**
13. **So I decided to...**
14. **I had to...**
15. **Luckily,...**
16. **Eventually,...**
17. **In the end,...**
18. **I felt...**
19. **Looking back,...**
20. **The experience taught me that...**

# 🎯 Today's Final Task

নিজের জীবনের **একটি সত্য ঘটনা** নিয়ে **3 মিনিটের English story** বলবে।

### Topic:

> **“A Difficult Experience I Had”**

### এই 9টি question-এর answer story-এর মধ্যে থাকবে:

1. **When did it happen?**
2. **Where were you?**
3. **Who were you with?**
4. **What happened?**
5. **What was the problem?**
6. **What did you do?**
7. **What happened in the end?**
8. **How did you feel?**
9. **What did you learn?**

### মনে রাখবে:

> **Don't memorize the story.**

শুধু **structure** মনে রাখবে:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHAT → PROBLEM → ACTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

এটাই তোমাকে future-এ **যেকোনো personal experience 3–5 মিনিট English-এ বলতে** সাহায্য করবে।
