## 🔗 Next: Lesson 66 — Describing People

পরের lesson-এ শিখব কীভাবে একজন মানুষকে শুধু **“He is good”** না বলে তার **appearance → personality → behavior → habits → relationship → example → opinion** দিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট describe করতে হয়।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 66

# 👤 Describing People — মানুষকে ইংরেজিতে বিস্তারিতভাবে বর্ণনা করা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে একজন মানুষ সম্পর্কে **2–5 মিনিট** কথা বলতে হয়—শুধু *“He is good”* না বলে তার **appearance, personality, habits, behavior, relationship এবং example** দিয়ে।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

* **Lesson 61:** Opinion → নিজের মতামত
* **Lesson 62:** Reason → কারণ + example
* **Lesson 63:** Agree/Disagree → মতামত নিয়ে conversation
* **Lesson 64:** Follow-up Questions → conversation চালানো
* **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → ঘটনা বলা
* **Lesson 66:** **Describing People → একজন মানুষকে বিস্তারিতভাবে describe করা**

---

# 🎯 আজকের Main Skill

ধরো প্রশ্ন:

> **Describe your best friend.**

শুধু:

> My best friend is good. He is nice.

এটা খুব ছোট।

Better:

> My best friend is a very friendly and reliable person. He is quite calm, and he doesn't get angry easily. We have known each other for several years. He is also very helpful. Whenever I have a problem, I can talk to him and he usually tries to help me. One thing I really like about him is that he keeps his promises.

এখন তুমি একজন মানুষকে **detail-এ describe** করছো।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — People Describe করার 6টি Area

একজন মানুষকে describe করার সময় এই order ব্যবহার করো:

# **APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → HABITS → BEHAVIOR → RELATIONSHIP → EXAMPLE → OPINION**

সহজে:

1. 👕 **Appearance** — দেখতে কেমন?
2. 🧠 **Personality** — স্বভাব কেমন?
3. ⏰ **Habits** — কী করতে পছন্দ করে?
4. 🤝 **Behavior** — অন্যদের সাথে কেমন আচরণ করে?
5. ❤️ **Relationship** — তোমার সাথে সম্পর্ক কী?
6. 📖 **Example** — একটি ঘটনা
7. 💭 **Your Opinion** — তুমি কী মনে করো?

---

# 👕 Part 2 — Appearance

Physical appearance describe করার vocabulary:

### Height

> tall
> short
> average height

### Body

> slim
> thin
> well-built
> strong
> fit

### Hair

> short hair
> long hair
> straight hair
> curly hair
> black hair
> brown hair

### Face

> round face
> oval face
> sharp features

### Other

> wears glasses
> has a beard
> has a friendly smile

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Appearance Example

> My friend is quite tall and slim. He has short black hair and usually wears glasses. He has a friendly smile, which makes him easy to talk to.

এখানে শুধু appearance নিয়ে 3 sentences।

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Don't Overfocus on Appearance

Speaking test বা normal conversation-এ personality বেশি important।

শুধু:

> He is tall.
> He has black hair.
> He wears glasses.

বললে description boring হয়ে যাবে।

এখন personality যোগ করো।

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Personality Vocabulary

### Positive personality

> friendly — বন্ধুসুলভ

> kind — দয়ালু

> helpful — সাহায্যকারী

> honest — সৎ

> polite — ভদ্র

> patient — ধৈর্যশীল

> confident — আত্মবিশ্বাসী

> hardworking — পরিশ্রমী

> responsible — দায়িত্বশীল

> reliable — নির্ভরযোগ্য

> calm — শান্ত

> generous — উদার

> funny — মজার

> creative — সৃজনশীল

> ambitious — উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী

> easygoing — সহজ-সরল/স্বচ্ছন্দ

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Negative Personality

> shy — লাজুক

> impatient — অধৈর্য

> careless — অসতর্ক

> stubborn — একগুঁয়ে

> quiet — চুপচাপ

> serious — গম্ভীর

> nervous — নার্ভাস

> selfish — স্বার্থপর

> moody — মেজাজ পরিবর্তনশীল

> lazy — অলস

**Note:** মানুষ describe করার সময় negative word carefully ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “He Is” দিয়ে সব Sentence শুরু করবে না

### ❌ Repetitive:

> He is tall.
> He is friendly.
> He is helpful.
> He is honest.
> He is hardworking.

Grammar ঠিক আছে, কিন্তু repetitive।

### ✅ Better:

> He is quite tall and friendly. **He tends to help** people whenever they need support. **One thing I really admire about him is his honesty.** He also works very hard and takes his responsibilities seriously.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Useful Sentence Patterns

### 1.

> **He tends to + verb**

> He tends to help others.

### 2.

> **He is someone who + verb**

> He is someone who always keeps his promises.

### 3.

> **One thing I like about him is...**

> One thing I like about him is his patience.

### 4.

> **What I admire most about her is...**

> What I admire most about her is her determination.

### 5.

> **He is the kind of person who...**

> He is the kind of person who never gives up easily.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Personality + Example

শুধু adjective বলবে না।

### Weak:

> He is helpful.

### Strong:

> He is very helpful. For example, whenever someone in our group has a problem, he is usually the first person to offer help.

🔥 **Adjective → Example**

এটাই Lesson 62-এর connection।

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “Reliable”

**Reliable = যার ওপর ভরসা করা যায়**

> My friend is very reliable.

Better:

> My friend is very reliable. If he says he will do something, he always tries to do it.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Hardworking”

Weak:

> He is hardworking.

Better:

> He is very hardworking. Even when a task is difficult, he doesn't give up easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “Calm”

> She is a very calm person. Even when something goes wrong, she usually thinks carefully before reacting.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Confident”

> He is quite confident. He doesn't hesitate to express his ideas in front of other people.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Shy”

> She is a little shy when she meets new people, but she becomes very friendly once she gets to know them.

🔥 এটা natural description।

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Habits

Person-এর daily habits বলো:

> He usually wakes up early.

> She enjoys reading.

> He spends a lot of time learning new things.

> She likes going for walks.

> He often watches movies in his free time.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Behavior

Person অন্যদের সাথে কীভাবে behave করে?

> He treats everyone with respect.

> She listens carefully when people talk.

> He doesn't get angry easily.

> She always tries to understand other people's problems.

> He is respectful toward older people.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Relationship

তোমার relationship explain করো:

> We have been friends for five years.

> We met at university.

> We became friends because we had similar interests.

> We often study together.

> We sometimes discuss our future plans.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — How You Met

Useful storytelling connection:

> I first met him when I was in university.

> We met through a mutual friend.

> We started talking because we were in the same class.

> At first, we didn't know each other very well.

> Over time, we became close friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “At First → Over Time”

Excellent natural structure:

> **At first**, I thought he was very quiet. **But over time**, I realized that he was actually very friendly and funny.

এটি খুব natural English।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Example Story

একটি ছোট ঘটনা বলো:

> I remember one time when I was having difficulty with a project. I was quite stressed because I couldn't solve a particular problem. He noticed that I was struggling and offered to help me. He didn't just give me the answer; he explained the problem step by step.

এখন personality **প্রমাণিত** হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Opinion

শেষে নিজের opinion:

> That's why I really respect him.

> That's one of the reasons why I consider him a good friend.

> I really admire his attitude.

> I feel lucky to have someone like him in my life.

> Overall, I think he is a great person.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Full Example: Best Friend

## 🎤 “Describe Your Best Friend”

> My best friend is someone I've known for several years. We met at university, and we became friends because we had similar interests.
>
> In terms of appearance, he is quite tall and slim. He has short black hair and usually wears glasses. He has a friendly smile and generally looks calm.
>
> Personality-wise, he is very friendly, honest, and reliable. He is also quite hardworking. He is the kind of person who takes his responsibilities seriously. Even when something is difficult, he doesn't give up easily.
>
> One thing I really like about him is that he is always willing to help others. For example, once I was having trouble with a project, and he spent a lot of time helping me understand the difficult parts. He didn't just give me the solution; he explained everything patiently.
>
> We also have similar interests, so we often talk about technology, movies, and our future careers. Sometimes we study together or discuss new things we have learned.
>
> Overall, I really respect him because he is honest, supportive, and dependable. I feel lucky to have a friend like him.

⏱️ **প্রায় 2–3 minutes**

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Notice the Structure

### Introduction

> My best friend is someone I've known for several years.

### Relationship

> We met at university.

### Appearance

> He is tall and slim...

### Personality

> friendly, honest, reliable...

### Behavior

> He helps others...

### Example

> Once I had trouble with a project...

### Shared interests

> technology, movies, careers...

### Opinion

> I really respect him...

🔥 এই structure follow করলে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা সহজ হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Describe a Family Member

Question:

> **Describe someone in your family.**

Structure:

> Who is the person?

> What does he/she look like?

> What is his/her personality like?

> What does he/she usually do?

> How does he/she treat others?

> What do you like about him/her?

> Give one example.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Describe a Teacher

Useful vocabulary:

> knowledgeable

> patient

> supportive

> strict

> friendly

> experienced

> motivating

### Example:

> My teacher is very knowledgeable and patient. He explains difficult topics in a simple way. He also encourages students to ask questions.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Describe a Good Teacher With Example

> One thing I really appreciate about my teacher is his patience. For example, if a student doesn't understand something, he doesn't get frustrated. Instead, he explains the topic again using a different example.

এটা much better than:

> My teacher is good.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Describe a Leader

Useful words:

> responsible

> confident

> decisive

> organized

> fair

> supportive

> inspiring

Example:

> A good leader should be confident, responsible, and fair. More importantly, a good leader should listen to other people.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Describe Someone You Admire

Use:

> The person I admire most is...

> I admire this person because...

> One quality I really admire is...

> For example,...

> This person has influenced me by...

> That's why I look up to this person.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Look Up To”

**Look up to someone = কাউকে শ্রদ্ধা/আদর্শ হিসেবে দেখা।**

> I really look up to my father.

> I look up to my teacher because of his dedication.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “Get Along With”

**Get along with = ভালো সম্পর্ক থাকা**

> I get along very well with my brother.

> We don't always agree, but we get along well.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Have Something in Common”

**কিছু common interest থাকা**

> We have a lot in common.

> We both enjoy technology and movies.

> That's why we became close friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Compare Two People

Advanced speaking:

> My brother and I are quite different in personality.

> He is more outgoing, while I am more reserved.

> He enjoys socializing, whereas I prefer spending time alone.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Comparison Vocabulary

> similar to

> different from

> more ... than

> less ... than

> both

> while

> whereas

> unlike

### Example:

> Unlike me, my friend is very outgoing.

> We are both hardworking, but we have different personalities.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Describe Someone Without Saying Their Name

এটি excellent speaking practice।

Example:

> I'm thinking of someone who is very important to me. This person is tall, has short black hair, and is extremely friendly. He is also very hardworking and always helps other people. We met at university...

Partner guess করবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Avoid Overusing “Very”

### ❌

> very good

> very nice

> very happy

> very smart

### Better:

> excellent

> kind

> friendly

> delighted

> intelligent

> extremely helpful

> highly motivated

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Useful Strong Adjectives

Instead of **very good**:

> excellent

> outstanding

> impressive

Instead of **very happy**:

> delighted

> thrilled

Instead of **very tired**:

> exhausted

Instead of **very angry**:

> furious

Instead of **very smart**:

> intelligent

> brilliant

Instead of **very funny**:

> hilarious

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Don't Judge Too Quickly

Instead of:

> He is a bad person.

Better:

> I don't think he is very easy to get along with.

Or:

> He can sometimes be difficult to deal with.

This sounds more natural and polite.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — “He Can Be...”

Personality sometimes changes depending on situation:

> He can be quite serious at work.

> She can be a little shy around strangers.

> He can be very funny when he's with close friends.

এটি advanced natural English।

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — “Tends to”

Habitual behavior:

> He tends to speak quietly.

> She tends to worry too much.

> He tends to think carefully before making decisions.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “Usually / Sometimes / Often”

Person description-এ frequency ব্যবহার করো:

> He usually...

> She often...

> He sometimes...

> She rarely...

> He hardly ever...

Example:

> He usually stays calm, even when things go wrong.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — 5-Minute Description Formula

একজন মানুষকে 5 মিনিট describe করতে:

### 1. Introduction

> The person I'd like to talk about is...

### 2. Relationship

> I know this person because...

### 3. Appearance

> In terms of appearance...

### 4. Personality

> Personality-wise...

### 5. Habits

> He/She usually...

### 6. Behavior

> One thing I notice is...

### 7. Example

> I remember one time...

### 8. Influence

> This person has influenced me by...

### 9. Opinion

> What I admire most is...

### 10. Conclusion

> Overall, I'd say...

---

# 🎤 Part 42 — Practice Topic 1

## **Describe Your Best Friend**

Use these questions:

1. Who is your best friend?
2. Where did you meet?
3. What does he/she look like?
4. What is his/her personality like?
5. What does he/she enjoy doing?
6. What makes him/her special?
7. What is one thing you admire?
8. Tell me about one experience together.
9. How has this person helped you?
10. Why are you still close?

🎯 **Target: 3 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Part 43 — Practice Topic 2

## **Describe a Person You Admire**

Use:

> Who?

> Why do you admire them?

> What are their best qualities?

> What have they achieved?

> How have they influenced you?

> Give an example.

> What have you learned from them?

🎯 **Target: 3–4 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Part 44 — Practice Topic 3

## **Describe Your Favorite Teacher**

Structure:

> Introduction

↓

> How you met

↓

> Appearance

↓

> Personality

↓

> Teaching style

↓

> Example

↓

> What you learned

↓

> Why you remember them

---

# 🎤 Part 45 — Practice Topic 4

## **Describe Someone You Don't Get Along With**

Don't insult the person.

Use:

> We have very different personalities.

> We don't always see things the same way.

> Sometimes we have communication problems.

> However, I still respect them.

This teaches you **balanced speaking**.

---

# 🧠 Part 46 — Follow-up Questions

Lesson 64-এর connection।

যদি কেউ বলে:

> My best friend is very hardworking.

Ask:

> What makes you say that?

> Can you give me an example?

> What kind of work does he do?

> How long have you known him?

> What do you usually do together?

---

# 🔥 Part 47 — Conversation Example

**A:**

> Tell me about your best friend.

**B:**

> My best friend is a very friendly and reliable person.

**A:**

> How long have you known him?

**B:**

> I've known him for about five years.

**A:**

> How did you meet?

**B:**

> We met at university.

**A:**

> What do you like most about him?

**B:**

> I really like his honesty.

**A:**

> Can you give me an example?

**B:**

> Sure. Once I made a mistake in a project...

এখানে **Lesson 64 Follow-up + Lesson 65 Storytelling + Lesson 66 Description** একসাথে ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🏆 Part 48 — Golden Formula

আজকের formula:

# **WHO → RELATIONSHIP → APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → HABITS → BEHAVIOR → EXAMPLE → FEELING → OPINION**

Example:

> **Who:** My best friend
> ↓
> **Relationship:** We met at university
> ↓
> **Appearance:** Tall, slim, glasses
> ↓
> **Personality:** Friendly, honest, reliable
> ↓
> **Habits:** Reads, exercises
> ↓
> **Behavior:** Helps others
> ↓
> **Example:** Helped me with a project
> ↓
> **Feeling:** I feel lucky
> ↓
> **Opinion:** I really respect him

---

# ⭐ 15 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **The person I'd like to talk about is...**
2. **I've known him/her for...**
3. **We first met when...**
4. **In terms of appearance,...**
5. **Personality-wise,...**
6. **He/She is the kind of person who...**
7. **One thing I really like about him/her is...**
8. **What I admire most is...**
9. **He/She tends to...**
10. **He/She usually...**
11. **One example that comes to mind is...**
12. **I remember one time when...**
13. **We have a lot in common.**
14. **I really look up to him/her.**
15. **Overall, I think he/she is...**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 66 Final Challenge

## Topic: **Describe a Person Who Has Influenced You**

**3–5 minutes** কথা বলবে।

এই order follow করবে:

> **1. Who is this person?**
>
> **2. How do you know them?**
>
> **3. What do they look like?**
>
> **4. What are they like?**
>
> **5. What qualities do they have?**
>
> **6. What do they usually do?**
>
> **7. Tell one story/example.**
>
> **8. How did they help or influence you?**
>
> **9. What did you learn from them?**
>
> **10. Why do you admire them?**

### শেষ করার জন্য:

> **Overall, this person has had a positive influence on my life, and I hope I can develop some of the same qualities in myself.**

---

## 🔗 Lesson 67 — Describing Places

পরের lesson-এ একই speaking system ধরে শিখবে কীভাবে **নিজের city, village, university, favorite place, tourist place** ইত্যাদি **2–5 মিনিট** describe করতে হয়:

**Location → Appearance → Atmosphere → Activities → People → Personal Experience → Opinion → Recommendation**.


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 67

# 🏙️ Describing Places — কোনো জায়গা সম্পর্কে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে কোনো জায়গা সম্পর্কে শুধু

> ❌ “My city is beautiful.”

না বলে **location → appearance → atmosphere → activities → people → experience → opinion → recommendation** দিয়ে বিস্তারিত কথা বলতে হয়।

---

## 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

* **Lesson 64:** Follow-up Questions → জায়গা নিয়ে প্রশ্ন করা
* **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → জায়গায় ঘটে যাওয়া ঘটনা বলা
* **Lesson 66:** Describing People → একজন মানুষকে বিস্তারিত describe করা
* **Lesson 67:** **Describing Places → একটি জায়গাকে বিস্তারিত describe করা**

---

# 🎯 Part 1 — Place Description-এর Golden Structure

আজকের সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ formula:

# **LOCATION → APPEARANCE → ATMOSPHERE → ACTIVITIES → PEOPLE → EXPERIENCE → OPINION → RECOMMENDATION**

সহজ করে:

1. 📍 **Location** — কোথায়?
2. 👀 **Appearance** — দেখতে কেমন?
3. 🌤️ **Atmosphere** — পরিবেশ কেমন?
4. 🚶 **Activities** — সেখানে কী করা যায়?
5. 👥 **People** — মানুষ কেমন?
6. 📖 **Experience** — তোমার experience কী?
7. 💭 **Opinion** — তোমার মতামত
8. ⭐ **Recommendation** — অন্যদের recommend করবে কি না?

---

# 📍 Part 2 — Location

জায়গা কোথায় সেটা দিয়ে শুরু করো।

### Useful expressions:

> It is located in...

> It is situated in...

> It is near...

> It is not far from...

> It is in the northern/southern part of...

### Examples:

> Dhaka is located in central Bangladesh.

> My university is located in Dhaka.

> The place is situated near the main road.

> It is about 20 minutes from my home.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — “It Is Located...”

সবচেয়ে useful pattern:

> **It is located in + place.**

Examples:

> My university is located in Dhaka.

> Cox's Bazar is located in southeastern Bangladesh.

> The park is located near my home.

---

# 👀 Part 4 — Appearance

এখন জায়গাটা দেখতে কেমন বলবে।

Useful words:

> beautiful

> modern

> crowded

> clean

> spacious

> peaceful

> noisy

> busy

> attractive

> green

> colorful

> well-designed

> traditional

> historic

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “It Looks...”

> It looks very modern.

> It looks peaceful in the morning.

> The area looks clean and organized.

> The city looks completely different at night.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Describe Buildings

> tall buildings

> modern buildings

> old buildings

> residential areas

> shopping centers

> offices

> restaurants

> schools

> universities

### Example:

> The area has many modern buildings, restaurants, and shopping centers.

---

# 🌳 Part 7 — Nature

Nature describe করতে:

> trees

> gardens

> parks

> rivers

> lakes

> beaches

> hills

> greenery

> fresh air

### Example:

> The place is surrounded by trees and greenery.

> There is a beautiful lake near the area.

---

# 🌤️ Part 8 — Atmosphere

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশ/চারপাশের feeling**

Very important vocabulary:

> peaceful

> relaxing

> lively

> energetic

> friendly

> busy

> crowded

> quiet

> calm

> exciting

### Example:

> The atmosphere is very peaceful, especially in the early morning.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “The Atmosphere Is...”

> The atmosphere is calm and relaxing.

> The atmosphere is lively during the evening.

> The area has a friendly atmosphere.

> The city has a very busy atmosphere.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — Day vs Night

Description আরও interesting করতে compare করো।

> During the day,...

> In the evening,...

> At night,...

### Example:

> During the day, the area is quite busy, but in the evening, it becomes more lively.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Activities

সেখানে কী করা যায়?

> You can...

> People usually...

> Visitors can...

> One of the main activities is...

### Examples:

> You can walk along the beach.

> People usually go there to relax.

> Visitors can enjoy the local food.

> You can take photographs and explore the area.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — “There Is / There Are”

Place description-এর basic grammar।

### Singular:

> There is a park near my house.

> There is a large shopping mall in the area.

### Plural:

> There are many restaurants nearby.

> There are several beautiful buildings.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — “You Can See...”

> You can see many people walking around.

> You can see traditional shops along the road.

> You can see beautiful scenery from the top.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — People

জায়গার মানুষ describe করো:

> friendly

> helpful

> polite

> busy

> welcoming

> hardworking

### Example:

> The local people are generally friendly and helpful.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Food

Place description-এ food যোগ করলে answer richer হয়।

> The area is famous for...

> You can find many...

> There are several restaurants that serve...

### Example:

> The area is famous for its local food.

> You can find many small restaurants and street-food stalls there.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Personal Experience

এখানে Lesson 65-এর storytelling ব্যবহার করবে।

Useful:

> I first visited this place when...

> I have been there several times.

> The last time I went there...

> I remember one time...

### Example:

> I first visited Cox's Bazar with my family when I was younger.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Tell a Small Story

Place description-এর মধ্যে ছোট story:

> The last time I visited the place, I went there with two of my friends. We arrived in the afternoon and spent several hours walking around. Later, we had dinner at a local restaurant.

এতে answer natural এবং interesting হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Feelings

জায়গা তোমাকে কেমন feel করায়?

> I feel relaxed there.

> I feel comfortable there.

> I really enjoy spending time there.

> I always feel happy when I visit the place.

> It gives me a sense of peace.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Opinion

> In my opinion,...

> Personally, I think...

> What I like most about the place is...

> One thing I really appreciate is...

### Example:

> What I like most about the place is its peaceful atmosphere.

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Recommendation

শেষে বলো:

> I would definitely recommend this place.

> I would highly recommend it.

> It's worth visiting.

> I think everyone should visit it at least once.

### Example:

> I would definitely recommend it to anyone who enjoys nature and peaceful environments.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Full Example: My City

## 🎤 Describe Your City

> The place I'd like to talk about is Dhaka, the capital city of Bangladesh. It is located in the central part of the country and is one of the busiest cities in Bangladesh.
>
> Dhaka is a large and crowded city with many modern buildings, offices, shopping centers, restaurants, universities, and residential areas. Some parts of the city are very developed, while other areas are much more traditional.
>
> The atmosphere is generally busy and energetic. During the day, the roads can be extremely crowded, especially during office hours. However, there are also many quiet places, such as parks, cafes, and residential areas.
>
> There are many things to do in Dhaka. People can visit historical places, go shopping, eat at different restaurants, or spend time with friends. The city also has many educational and job opportunities.
>
> I have spent a lot of time in Dhaka, so I am quite familiar with the city. One thing I really like is that there are always new places to explore. At the same time, traffic and overcrowding can sometimes be frustrating.
>
> Overall, I think Dhaka is a busy but interesting city. It has both advantages and disadvantages. Despite the problems, I think it is an important and exciting place to live and work.

⏱️ **2–3 minutes**

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Notice the Structure

### Location

> central part of Bangladesh

### Appearance

> modern buildings, offices, shopping centers

### Atmosphere

> busy and energetic

### Activities

> shopping, restaurants, historical places

### Experience

> I have spent a lot of time there.

### Problem

> traffic and overcrowding

### Opinion

> busy but interesting

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Describe Your Village

Use:

> My village is located in...

> It is surrounded by...

> The area is very...

> There are many...

> Most people...

> People usually...

> One thing I like about my village is...

> Compared with the city,...

### Example:

> Compared with the city, my village is much quieter and more peaceful.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — City vs Village

Useful comparison:

> My city is more crowded than my village.

> The village is much quieter.

> The city has more job opportunities.

> The village has cleaner air.

> Life in the city is faster, while life in the village is more relaxed.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Describe Your University

Structure:

> Where is it located?

> What does it look like?

> What facilities does it have?

> What is the atmosphere like?

> What do students usually do?

> What do you like?

> What could be improved?

### Example:

> My university is located in Dhaka. It has several academic buildings, classrooms, laboratories, and common areas. The atmosphere is usually busy during class hours but more relaxed after classes.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Describe Your Favorite Place

Start:

> My favorite place is...

> It is located...

> I like this place because...

> The first thing you notice is...

> What I enjoy most is...

> Whenever I go there...

> It makes me feel...

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Describe a Park

Useful vocabulary:

> walking paths

> benches

> trees

> flowers

> playground

> open space

> fresh air

### Example:

> The park has many trees, walking paths, and open spaces. People usually go there to exercise, walk, relax, or spend time with their families.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Describe a Restaurant

Use:

> It is located...

> The restaurant is quite...

> The interior is...

> The atmosphere is...

> The food is...

> The staff are...

> My favorite dish is...

> I usually go there with...

### Example:

> The restaurant has a comfortable and modern interior. The atmosphere is usually lively, but it isn't too noisy.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Describe a Tourist Place

Structure:

> Location

↓

> Natural beauty

↓

> Main attractions

↓

> Activities

↓

> Food

↓

> People

↓

> Personal experience

↓

> Recommendation

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Example: Cox's Bazar

> Cox's Bazar is a popular tourist destination in Bangladesh. It is located in the southeastern part of the country and is famous for its long natural sea beach.
>
> The main attraction is the beach, but there are also many hotels, restaurants, shops, and tourist areas nearby.
>
> Visitors can walk along the beach, watch the sunset, take photographs, eat local food, and spend time with their families or friends.
>
> I really like the atmosphere because it feels relaxing and refreshing. The sound of the waves and the open view of the sea make the place special.
>
> I would definitely recommend Cox's Bazar to anyone who enjoys nature and wants to spend some relaxing time away from a busy city.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Describe a Place You Don't Like

Balanced English ব্যবহার করবে।

### ❌

> This place is terrible.

### Better:

> I'm not a big fan of this place.

> The main problem is that it is usually too crowded.

> I find it quite noisy.

> It's not really my favorite place because...

### Example:

> I'm not a big fan of the area because it is usually very crowded and noisy. However, it does have some good restaurants and shopping centers.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — “Compared With”

Places compare করার জন্য:

> Compared with Dhaka, my hometown is much quieter.

> Compared with other restaurants, this one is more affordable.

> Compared with the old building, the new one is much more modern.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — “Although”

Balanced description:

> Although the city is crowded, it offers many opportunities.

> Although the restaurant is small, the food is excellent.

> Although the beach is beautiful, it can become very crowded during holidays.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — “While”

> The city is busy, while the village is peaceful.

> The restaurant is small, while the food court is much larger.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — “One of the Best Things About...”

Excellent expression:

> One of the best things about this place is the atmosphere.

> One of the best things about Dhaka is the variety of opportunities.

> One of the best things about my university is the learning environment.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — “What I Like Most...”

> What I like most about this place is the peaceful environment.

> What I like most about my city is the variety of activities.

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — “The Main Problem Is...”

> The main problem is traffic.

> The main problem is overcrowding.

> The main problem is that it can be expensive.

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — “If You Visit...”

Recommendation:

> If you visit this place, I recommend going in the morning.

> If you visit Cox's Bazar, you should watch the sunset.

> If you visit Dhaka, you should try some local food.

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — Describe a Place Using All Senses

Advanced speaking:

### 👀 Sight

> You can see...

### 👂 Sound

> You can hear...

### 👃 Smell

> You can smell...

### 👅 Taste

> You can taste...

### ✋ Feeling

> You can feel...

### Example:

> At the beach, you can see the wide ocean, hear the sound of the waves, and feel the cool breeze.

এতে description অনেক vivid হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 40 — Weather + Place

Weather যোগ করলে description better:

> On a sunny day, the place looks bright and lively.

> On rainy days, the area becomes quieter.

> The cool weather makes the place more enjoyable.

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — Time + Place

একই জায়গা different time-এ different হতে পারে:

> In the morning,...

> During the afternoon,...

> In the evening,...

> At night,...

### Example:

> The area is relatively quiet in the morning, but it becomes very busy in the evening.

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — Storytelling Connection

Lesson 65-এর মতো:

> The last time I visited the place...

> At first...

> Then...

> Suddenly...

> Eventually...

> In the end...

### Example:

> The last time I visited the park, it suddenly started raining. We initially tried to wait under a tree, but eventually we decided to leave.

---

# 🔥 Part 43 — Follow-up Question Connection

Lesson 64:

**A:** I really like Cox's Bazar.

**B:** Really? **What do you like most about it?**

**A:** The beach.

**B:** **What do you usually do there?**

**A:** I walk along the beach.

**B:** **Who do you usually go with?**

**A:** My friends.

**B:** **How often do you visit?**

🔥 One place → 5 minutes conversation.

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — Opinion Connection

Lesson 61:

> In my opinion, this is one of the best places to relax.

Reason:

> The main reason is that...

Example:

> For example,...

Conclusion:

> Overall,...

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — 2-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

# **Describe Your City**

Use:

> Location

↓

> Appearance

↓

> Atmosphere

↓

> Activities

↓

> People

↓

> Your experience

↓

> Advantages

↓

> Problems

↓

> Opinion

🎯 **Target: 2 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Part 46 — 3-Minute Challenge

## Topic:

# **Describe Your Favorite Place**

Answer:

1. Where is it?
2. How does it look?
3. What is the atmosphere like?
4. What can people do there?
5. Who usually visits?
6. When did you first go there?
7. What happened during your last visit?
8. How do you feel there?
9. What do you like most?
10. Would you recommend it?

---

# 🎤 Part 47 — 5-Minute Challenge

## Topic:

# **Describe a Tourist Place in Bangladesh**

Use this structure:

> **Introduction**
>
> The place I'd like to talk about is...

↓

> **Location**

↓

> **Appearance/Nature**

↓

> **Atmosphere**

↓

> **Things to do**

↓

> **Food**

↓

> **People**

↓

> **Personal experience**

↓

> **Advantages**

↓

> **Problems**

↓

> **Recommendation**

---

# 🏆 Part 48 — The Golden Formula

মুখস্থ করো:

# **L → A → A → A → P → E → O → R**

### L — Location

> Where is it?

### A — Appearance

> What does it look like?

### A — Atmosphere

> How does it feel?

### A — Activities

> What can you do?

### P — People

> What are the people like?

### E — Experience

> What happened when you visited?

### O — Opinion

> What do you think?

### R — Recommendation

> Would you recommend it?

---

# ⭐ 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **The place I'd like to talk about is...**
2. **It is located in...**
3. **It is situated near...**
4. **The first thing you notice is...**
5. **It is known for...**
6. **The area is surrounded by...**
7. **The atmosphere is...**
8. **During the day,...**
9. **In the evening,...**
10. **There are many...**
11. **You can see...**
12. **Visitors can...**
13. **People usually go there to...**
14. **I first visited the place when...**
15. **The last time I went there...**
16. **What I like most about the place is...**
17. **One of the best things about it is...**
18. **The main problem is...**
19. **I would definitely recommend this place.**
20. **Overall, I think it is...**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 67 Final Challenge

## Topic: **Describe Your City**

তুমি **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলবে।

এই structure exactly follow করো:

> **The place I'd like to talk about is...**
>
> **It is located...**
>
> **In terms of appearance...**
>
> **The atmosphere is...**
>
> **People usually...**
>
> **There are...**
>
> **The last time I...**
>
> **What I like most is...**
>
> **However, the main problem is...**
>
> **Overall, I think...**
>
> **I would recommend it because...**

### 🔥 আজকের target:

**একটি জায়গা সম্পর্কে শুধু information না দিয়ে — description + experience + opinion + recommendation একসাথে বলবে।**

এতে Lesson **64 → Follow-up**, **65 → Storytelling**, **66 → Description**, এবং **67 → Place Description**—চারটি skill একসাথে practice হবে।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 68

# 🗓️ Describing Daily Life & Routines — দৈনন্দিন জীবন সম্পর্কে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে নিজের **daily routine, study/work routine, free time, habits, changes এবং future improvement** নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট uninterrupted English বলতে হয়।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

* **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → কোনো ঘটনা বলা
* **Lesson 66:** Describing People → মানুষ describe করা
* **Lesson 67:** Describing Places → জায়গা describe করা
* **Lesson 68:** **Daily Life & Routine → নিজের দৈনন্দিন জীবন describe করা**

---

# 🎯 Part 1 — আজকের Golden Structure

Daily routine বলার সময় এই structure follow করবে:

# **MORNING → DAYTIME → STUDY/WORK → BREAK → EVENING → NIGHT → HABITS → PROBLEMS → IMPROVEMENT → OPINION**

সহজভাবে:

1. 🌅 Morning — সকালে কী করো?
2. ☀️ Daytime — দিনের শুরুতে কী করো?
3. 💻 Study/Work — পড়াশোনা/কাজ
4. ☕ Break — বিরতি
5. 🌆 Evening — সন্ধ্যায় কী করো?
6. 🌙 Night — রাতে কী করো?
7. 🔁 Habits — নিয়মিত অভ্যাস
8. ⚠️ Problems — কোন সমস্যা আছে?
9. 📈 Improvement — কীভাবে improve করতে চাও?
10. 💭 Opinion — routine সম্পর্কে তোমার মতামত

---

# 🧠 Part 2 — Routine বলার জন্য কোন Tense?

Daily routine-এর জন্য প্রধানত:

# **Present Simple**

কারণ তুমি নিয়মিত কাজের কথা বলছো।

### Examples:

> I wake up at 7 a.m.

> I have breakfast.

> I study in the morning.

> I usually work in the afternoon.

> I go to bed around 11 p.m.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — “Usually” ব্যবহার

Routine-এ **usually** খুব useful।

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m.

> I usually have breakfast at home.

> I usually study for two hours in the morning.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Frequency Words

| Word        | Meaning       |
| ----------- | ------------- |
| always      | সবসময়         |
| usually     | সাধারণত       |
| often       | প্রায়ই        |
| sometimes   | মাঝে মাঝে     |
| rarely      | খুব কম        |
| hardly ever | প্রায় কখনো না |
| never       | কখনোই না      |

### Example:

> I usually study in the morning.

> I sometimes exercise in the evening.

> I rarely stay up late.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — Morning Routine

Start:

> I usually wake up at...

তারপর:

> First,...

> After that,...

> Then,...

> Finally,...

### Example:

> I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. First, I wash my face and brush my teeth. After that, I have breakfast. Then I check my schedule and start studying.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Morning Vocabulary

> wake up

> get out of bed

> brush my teeth

> wash my face

> take a shower

> get dressed

> have breakfast

> check my phone

> check my schedule

> start studying

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “Get Ready”

**Get ready = প্রস্তুত হওয়া**

> I get ready for university.

> I usually take about 30 minutes to get ready.

> After breakfast, I get ready for work.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Study Routine

Useful expressions:

> I attend classes.

> I study for a few hours.

> I review my notes.

> I practice English.

> I work on my projects.

> I complete my assignments.

> I prepare for exams.

### Example:

> After breakfast, I usually spend a few hours studying. I review my previous lessons and then work on my current projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Work Routine

যদি work-এর কথা বলো:

> I start work at...

> I check my emails.

> I attend meetings.

> I work on different tasks.

> I take a short break.

> I finish work at...

### Example:

> I usually start work at 9 a.m. First, I check my emails and plan my tasks for the day.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “After That”

Routine connect করার জন্য:

> After that,...

> Then,...

> Next,...

> Later,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> I have breakfast. After that, I start studying. Later, I take a short break.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Break Time

> I take a short break.

> I have some tea.

> I check my phone.

> I talk to my friends.

> I relax for a while.

### Example:

> After studying for a couple of hours, I usually take a short break and have some tea.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Evening Routine

> In the evening,...

> I usually go for a walk.

> I exercise.

> I meet my friends.

> I practice English.

> I work on personal projects.

### Example:

> In the evening, I usually spend some time practicing English and working on personal projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — Night Routine

> At night,...

> I have dinner.

> I review my day.

> I prepare for the next day.

> I watch something.

> I read a book.

> I go to bed.

### Example:

> Before going to bed, I usually review what I did during the day and make a simple plan for the next day.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Time Expressions

Routine naturally বলতে এগুলো ব্যবহার করো:

> in the morning

> in the afternoon

> in the evening

> at night

> after breakfast

> before lunch

> after lunch

> before going to bed

> during the day

> before leaving home

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Full Basic Routine

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. I brush my teeth, wash my face, and have breakfast. After that, I start studying. I usually study for a few hours and take a short break in between.
>
> In the afternoon, I have lunch and continue working on my projects. In the evening, I practice English or go for a walk. At night, I have dinner, review my day, and prepare for the next day. Finally, I go to bed around 11 p.m.

এটা basic version।

এখন এটাকে **3–5 minutes** করব।

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Add “Why”

Routine আরও interesting করার জন্য কারণ যোগ করো।

### Basic:

> I exercise in the morning.

### Better:

> I exercise in the morning because it helps me feel more energetic during the day.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Add “How Often”

> I usually...

> I often...

> I sometimes...

> I rarely...

### Example:

> I usually practice English every day, but I sometimes skip it when I have a lot of work.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Add “How Long”

> I study for two hours.

> I exercise for thirty minutes.

> I spend about an hour practicing English.

### Example:

> I usually spend about two hours studying in the morning.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Add “Since When”

Long-term habit:

> I've been practicing English for several months.

> I've been exercising regularly for about a year.

> I've been following this routine since university.

🔥 এখানে **Present Perfect Continuous** আসছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Routine + Present Perfect

### Current routine:

> I study English every day.

### Started in past and continues:

> I have been studying English for several months.

Difference:

> **I study English every day.**
> = habit

> **I have been studying English for several months.**
> = শুরু হয়েছিল আগে, এখনও চলছে

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Good Habits

Talk about:

> waking up early

> exercising

> reading

> studying regularly

> drinking enough water

> planning your day

> sleeping on time

### Example:

> One good habit I have is planning my day in advance. It helps me stay organized.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Bad Habits

Natural English:

> One habit I'm trying to change is...

> I sometimes...

> I tend to...

### Examples:

> One habit I'm trying to change is using my phone too much.

> I sometimes stay up too late.

> I tend to check my phone whenever I get bored.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “I'm Trying to...”

Very useful:

> I'm trying to wake up earlier.

> I'm trying to exercise regularly.

> I'm trying to spend less time on social media.

> I'm trying to improve my English speaking skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “I Used To”

Past habit:

> I used to wake up late.

> I used to spend a lot of time playing games.

> I used to study only before exams.

Now changed:

> But now, I try to follow a regular routine.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Past vs Present Routine

This is excellent speaking practice.

> **In the past**, I used to sleep late and wake up late. **But these days**, I try to wake up earlier. I also spend more time studying and exercising.

এতে **Lesson 65-এর past experience + Lesson 68-এর present routine** connect হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Weekend Routine

Lesson 18-এর connection:

> My weekday routine is quite busy, but my weekend routine is more relaxed.

Then:

> I usually wake up later.

> I spend time with my family.

> I watch movies.

> I go outside.

> I work on personal projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Weekday vs Weekend

Use:

> whereas

> while

> compared with

### Example:

> On weekdays, I usually wake up early, whereas on weekends, I allow myself to sleep a little longer.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Productive Day

Question:

> What does a productive day look like for you?

Answer:

> For me, a productive day starts with a clear plan. I decide what I need to accomplish and then focus on the most important tasks first.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Unproductive Day

> Sometimes I have an unproductive day.

> I spend too much time on my phone.

> I lose focus.

> I postpone important tasks.

### Example:

> Sometimes I spend too much time on social media, and as a result, I don't complete all my important tasks.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — “As a Result”

Cause → result:

> I slept late. As a result, I felt tired the next morning.

> I spent too much time online. As a result, I had less time to study.

Very useful for longer speaking.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Problems in Your Routine

Talk about:

> lack of time

> distractions

> phone usage

> social media

> lack of sleep

> traffic

> stress

### Example:

> The biggest problem with my routine is that I sometimes get distracted by my phone.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Solutions

Use:

> To solve this problem,...

> I'm trying to...

> I've started...

> I plan to...

### Example:

> To solve this problem, I've started keeping my phone away while I'm studying.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — “I've Started...”

Excellent natural expression:

> I've started exercising regularly.

> I've started planning my day the night before.

> I've started limiting my social media use.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Morning Person or Night Person?

Question:

> Are you a morning person or a night person?

### Morning:

> I'm more of a morning person because I can concentrate better in the morning.

### Night:

> I'm more of a night person because I feel more relaxed and focused at night.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Explain Your Choice

Don't stop at:

> I'm a morning person.

Add:

> The main reason is that...

> For example,...

> That's why...

### Example:

> I'm more of a morning person. The main reason is that I can concentrate better after a good night's sleep. For example, I usually do my most difficult study tasks in the morning.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Full 3-Minute Example

## 🎤 “Describe Your Daily Routine”

> My daily routine is fairly simple, but I try to keep it organized.
>
> I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. First, I get out of bed, brush my teeth, wash my face, and have breakfast. After that, I usually spend some time studying or working on my projects.
>
> In the morning, I can concentrate better, so I try to do my most important tasks during this time. I usually study for a couple of hours and take a short break in between.
>
> Around lunchtime, I have lunch and take a little rest. In the afternoon, I continue working on my projects or learning something new. Sometimes I practice English speaking because I want to become more confident in conversations.
>
> In the evening, I usually relax for a while. Sometimes I go for a walk or exercise. I also spend some time with my family or friends.
>
> At night, I have dinner and review what I did during the day. I usually make a simple plan for the next day. Before going to bed, I sometimes watch a movie, read something, or practice English.
>
> One habit I'm trying to improve is going to bed earlier. I sometimes stay up too late, especially when I'm working on something interesting. As a result, I feel tired the next morning.
>
> Overall, I think having a regular routine is important because it helps me manage my time and stay focused. I'm still trying to improve my routine, but I think it's getting better gradually.

⏱️ **প্রায় 3 minutes**

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Why This Answer Is Good

এখানে আছে:

**Morning**

> I usually wake up...

↓

**Study**

> I spend time studying...

↓

**Reason**

> I can concentrate better...

↓

**Afternoon**

> I continue working...

↓

**Evening**

> I relax...

↓

**Night**

> I review my day...

↓

**Problem**

> I sometimes stay up too late...

↓

**Result**

> I feel tired...

↓

**Improvement**

> I'm trying to improve...

↓

**Opinion**

> I think having a regular routine is important.

🔥 তাই answer সহজ হলেও **long + natural**।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Daily Routine থেকে Conversation

**A:** What time do you usually wake up?

**B:** I usually wake up around 7 a.m.

**A:** Why do you wake up so early?

**B:** Because I like studying in the morning.

**A:** What do you usually study?

**B:** I usually study English and programming.

**A:** How long do you study?

**B:** Usually about two hours.

**A:** What do you do after studying?

**B:** I have lunch and continue working on my projects.

🔥 একটি question থেকে conversation অনেক দূর পর্যন্ত নেওয়া যায়।

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Follow-up Questions You Should Practice

নিজেকে এই questions করো:

1. What time do you wake up?
2. What do you do first?
3. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
4. When do you study?
5. How long do you study?
6. When do you take breaks?
7. What do you do in the evening?
8. What do you do before bed?
9. What is your best habit?
10. What is your worst habit?
11. What habit are you trying to change?
12. Are you a morning or night person?
13. How is your weekend routine different?
14. What makes your day productive?
15. What would you like to change about your routine?

---

# 🏆 Part 40 — 5-Minute Speaking Formula

যদি 5 মিনিট কথা বলতে চাও:

### ① Introduction

> My daily routine is...

### ② Morning

> I usually wake up...

### ③ Study/Work

> After that...

### ④ Afternoon

> In the afternoon...

### ⑤ Evening

> In the evening...

### ⑥ Night

> At night...

### ⑦ Habits

> One good habit...

### ⑧ Bad Habit

> One habit I'm trying to change...

### ⑨ Past vs Present

> I used to..., but now...

### ⑩ Future

> In the future, I want to...

### ⑪ Opinion

> Overall, I think...

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — Future Routine

নিজের future routine describe করো:

> In the future, I'd like to...

> I want to...

> I plan to...

> I'm hoping to...

### Example:

> In the future, I'd like to wake up earlier and spend more time exercising.

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — “I'd Like To”

Natural future desire:

> I'd like to improve my sleeping habits.

> I'd like to exercise more regularly.

> I'd like to spend less time on my phone.

---

# 🔥 Part 43 — Routine + Goals

Routine-এর সাথে goal connect করো:

> My main goal is to become more disciplined, so I'm trying to follow a regular schedule.

> I want to improve my English, so I practice speaking every day.

এটা **Goal → Reason → Action**।

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — Today's Important Connectors

আজ এগুলো বেশি ব্যবহার করবে:

> First,...

> After that,...

> Then,...

> Later,...

> In the afternoon,...

> In the evening,...

> Before going to bed,...

> Usually,...

> Sometimes,...

> However,...

> As a result,...

> Because...

> That's why...

> Compared with...

> In the future,...

> Overall,...

---

# ⭐ Part 45 — 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **I usually wake up at...**
2. **The first thing I do is...**
3. **After that, I...**
4. **Then I usually...**
5. **I spend most of my morning...**
6. **I usually spend about two hours...**
7. **I take a short break...**
8. **In the afternoon,...**
9. **In the evening,...**
10. **At night,...**
11. **Before going to bed,...**
12. **One good habit I have is...**
13. **One habit I'm trying to change is...**
14. **I tend to...**
15. **I used to..., but now...**
16. **As a result,...**
17. **To solve this problem,...**
18. **I've started...**
19. **In the future, I'd like to...**
20. **Overall, I think...**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 68 Final Challenge

## Topic: **Describe Your Daily Life**

**3–5 মিনিট** uninterrupted English বলবে।

এই structure follow করো:

> **My daily routine is...**
>
> **I usually wake up...**
>
> **First, I...**
>
> **After that, I...**
>
> **In the afternoon,...**
>
> **In the evening,...**
>
> **At night,...**
>
> **One good habit I have is...**
>
> **One habit I'm trying to change is...**
>
> **I used to..., but now...**
>
> **In the future, I'd like to...**
>
> **Overall, I think...**

### 🔥 আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য

**শুধু routine মুখস্থ করা নয়।**

একই routine নিয়ে:

> **What → When → How often → How long → Why → Problem → Result → Improvement**

এই 8টা angle-এ কথা বলতে শিখবে।

তাহলে একটি simple topic **“My Daily Routine”** থেকেও তুমি সহজে **3–5 মিনিট conversation** চালিয়ে যেতে পারবে।

---

## 🔜 Lesson 69 — Describing Things & Objects

পরের lesson-এ শিখবে কীভাবে কোনো **phone, computer, book, bag, gift, device বা favorite object** নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়:

**What it is → Appearance → Features → Function → How you use it → Why you like it → Experience → Advantages/Disadvantages → Opinion**.


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 69

# 🎒 Describing Things & Objects — কোনো জিনিস সম্পর্কে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে কোনো **phone, computer, book, bag, watch, gift, device, বা favorite object** সম্পর্কে শুধু *“It is good”* না বলে **কী, দেখতে কেমন, কী কাজে লাগে, কীভাবে ব্যবহার করো, কেন পছন্দ করো, experience এবং opinion** দিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়।

### 🔗 Previous lessons-এর connection

* **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → কোনো object নিয়ে একটি ঘটনা বলা
* **Lesson 66:** Describing People → মানুষ describe করা
* **Lesson 67:** Describing Places → জায়গা describe করা
* **Lesson 68:** Daily Life → routine describe করা
* **Lesson 69:** **Describing Things & Objects → কোনো জিনিস describe করা**

---

# 🎯 Part 1 — আজকের Golden Structure

মনে রাখবে:

# **WHAT → APPEARANCE → FEATURES → FUNCTION → USE → EXPERIENCE → WHY → ADVANTAGES → DISADVANTAGES → OPINION**

সহজভাবে:

1. 📦 **What?** — জিনিসটি কী?
2. 👀 **Appearance** — দেখতে কেমন?
3. ⚙️ **Features** — কী কী বৈশিষ্ট্য আছে?
4. 🔧 **Function** — কী কাজ করে?
5. 💻 **Use** — তুমি কীভাবে ব্যবহার করো?
6. 📖 **Experience** — তোমার experience কী?
7. ❤️ **Why?** — কেন পছন্দ?
8. 👍 **Advantages** — সুবিধা
9. 👎 **Disadvantages** — অসুবিধা
10. 💭 **Opinion** — শেষ মতামত

---

# 🧠 Part 2 — Object Introduce করার উপায়

প্রথমেই বলো:

> **The object I'd like to talk about is...**

অথবা:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

অথবা:

> **One thing I use every day is...**

### Examples:

> The object I'd like to talk about is my laptop.

> One thing I use every day is my smartphone.

> I'd like to talk about a book that I really like.

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — “What Is It?”

প্রথমে identify করো।

> It is a laptop.

> It is a smartphone.

> It is a book.

> It is a watch.

তারপর details:

> It is a relatively modern laptop that I use for studying and programming.

---

# 👀 Part 4 — Appearance

Object দেখতে কেমন?

### Size:

> small

> large

> medium-sized

> compact

> lightweight

> heavy

### Shape:

> rectangular

> round

> flat

> slim

### Material:

> plastic

> metal

> glass

> leather

> wooden

### Color:

> black

> white

> silver

> blue

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Appearance Sentence Patterns

> It is quite small and lightweight.

> It has a slim design.

> It is mostly black.

> It has a simple and modern design.

> The screen is relatively large.

> The device feels solid and well-built.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Don't List Everything

### ❌ Weak:

> It is black. It is small. It has a screen. It has a keyboard.

### ✅ Better:

> It has a simple and modern design. It is relatively lightweight, so it is easy to carry around. The screen is large enough for my daily work, and the keyboard is comfortable to use.

এখন description natural।

---

# ⚙️ Part 7 — Features

Object-এর important features বলো।

### Phone:

> camera

> battery

> display

> storage

> fingerprint sensor

> face recognition

### Computer:

> processor

> RAM

> storage

> keyboard

> display

> operating system

### Book:

> chapters

> illustrations

> examples

> exercises

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — “It Comes With...”

Useful phrase:

> It comes with a charger.

> It comes with several useful features.

> The book comes with practical exercises.

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — Function

Object কী কাজ করে?

> It is mainly used for...

> I use it to...

> It allows me to...

> It helps me...

### Examples:

> I use my laptop to study and work.

> I use my phone to communicate with people.

> This app allows me to manage my tasks.

> This book helps me improve my English.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “I Use It To...”

খুব important structure:

> I use it to study.

> I use it to watch movies.

> I use it to practice programming.

> I use it to communicate with my friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “It Helps Me...”

আরও natural:

> It helps me stay organized.

> It helps me learn new things.

> It helps me communicate with others.

> It helps me save time.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — “It Allows Me To...”

Advanced:

> It allows me to work from anywhere.

> It allows me to store a lot of files.

> It allows me to communicate with my team.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — How Often Do You Use It?

> I use it every day.

> I use it several times a day.

> I use it almost every day.

> I mainly use it in the evening.

> I only use it when I need it.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — Where Do You Use It?

> I usually use it at home.

> I use it at university.

> I often carry it with me.

> I use it mostly when I'm traveling.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — When Did You Get It?

Past experience connection:

> I bought it last year.

> I got it as a gift.

> My parents gave it to me.

> I bought it when I started university.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Why Did You Buy It?

> I bought it because...

> I needed it for...

> I wanted to...

### Example:

> I bought my laptop because I needed a reliable computer for programming and studying.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — How Long Have You Had It?

Present Perfect:

> I've had it for two years.

> I've been using it for about a year.

### Difference:

> **I bought it two years ago.**

= কখন কিনেছিলে

> **I've had it for two years.**

= কতদিন ধরে আছে

> **I've been using it for two years.**

= কতদিন ধরে ব্যবহার করছো

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Personal Experience

Object description-এ experience যোগ করো।

> I remember one time when...

### Example:

> I remember one time when my laptop suddenly stopped working while I was working on an important project.

এখন Lesson 65-এর storytelling ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Tell the Story

> I was working on an important project when my laptop suddenly stopped working. At first, I thought it was a small problem, but then I realized that the battery had completely died. Luckily, I had saved my work, so I didn't lose anything.

এটা object description-কে interesting করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Advantages

Use:

> One of the main advantages is...

> One thing I really like is...

> The biggest benefit is...

### Examples:

> One of the main advantages is its portability.

> One thing I really like is the battery life.

> The biggest benefit is that it saves me time.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Disadvantages

Balanced speaking-এর জন্য:

> One disadvantage is...

> The main drawback is...

> The only problem is...

### Examples:

> One disadvantage is that it is quite expensive.

> The main drawback is that the battery doesn't last very long.

> The only problem is that it can be difficult to carry.

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — “Although”

Balanced opinion:

> Although it is expensive, I think it is worth the money.

> Although the phone is small, the screen is quite good.

> Although the book is difficult, it is very useful.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — “Worth It”

**Worth it = মূল্য/খরচ অনুযায়ী উপকারী**

> I think it is worth the money.

> It was expensive, but it was worth it.

> For me, the price is worth it because I use it every day.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Compare With Another Object

Lesson 67-এর comparison skill:

> Compared with my old phone, this one is much faster.

> It is lighter than my previous laptop.

> This book is easier to understand than the previous one.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — “Better Than”

> This phone is better than my old one.

Better:

> Compared with my old phone, this one is faster and more reliable.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “If I Could Change One Thing...”

Advanced speaking:

> If I could change one thing about it, I would improve the battery life.

> If I could change one thing, I would make it lighter.

Excellent way to extend your answer.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Future

> In the future, I might replace it with...

> I plan to upgrade it when...

> If I buy a new one, I'd like to...

### Example:

> If I buy a new laptop in the future, I'd like to get one with a better battery and a more powerful processor.

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Full Example: Laptop

## 🎤 Describe Your Laptop

> The object I'd like to talk about is my laptop. It is one of the most useful things I own because I use it almost every day for studying, programming, and other personal projects.
>
> In terms of appearance, it has a simple and modern design. It is relatively slim and lightweight, so I can carry it around quite easily. It has a fairly large screen and a comfortable keyboard.
>
> In terms of features, it has enough RAM and storage for my daily work. I mainly use it for programming, browsing the internet, watching educational videos, and working on projects.
>
> I've had this laptop for a couple of years, and I use it almost every day. I bought it because I needed a reliable computer for my studies and programming work.
>
> One thing I really like about it is that it helps me work efficiently. For example, I can use different development tools and work on several tasks without constantly switching devices.
>
> I remember one time when I was working on an important project and the laptop suddenly stopped working. At first, I was really worried because I hadn't finished the project. Luckily, I managed to fix the problem and continue working.
>
> One disadvantage is that the battery doesn't last as long as I would like. However, considering how often I use it, I think it is definitely worth the money.
>
> Overall, my laptop is an important part of my daily life. I use it for both learning and work, so I would say it is one of the most useful things I own.

⏱️ **প্রায় 3 minutes**

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Why This Answer Is Strong

এখানে আছে:

**What**

> my laptop

↓

**Appearance**

> slim, lightweight, modern

↓

**Features**

> RAM, storage, screen, keyboard

↓

**Function**

> programming, studying

↓

**Usage**

> almost every day

↓

**Experience**

> laptop stopped working

↓

**Advantage**

> helps me work efficiently

↓

**Disadvantage**

> battery

↓

**Opinion**

> one of the most useful things I own

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Describe Your Phone

Questions:

1. What phone do you have?
2. When did you get it?
3. What does it look like?
4. What features does it have?
5. What do you mainly use it for?
6. How often do you use it?
7. What do you like about it?
8. What don't you like?
9. Have you ever had a problem with it?
10. Would you buy the same type again?

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Phone Vocabulary

> touchscreen

> camera

> battery

> charger

> speaker

> microphone

> storage

> display

> apps

> internet connection

> fingerprint sensor

> face recognition

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Describe a Book

Structure:

> What is the book?

> Who wrote it?

> What is it about?

> When did you read it?

> Why did you read it?

> What did you learn?

> Would you recommend it?

### Example:

> The book I'd like to talk about is a book I read to improve my English. It contains practical examples and exercises, so I found it very useful.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Describe a Gift

Useful phrases:

> I received it as a gift.

> Someone gave it to me on my birthday.

> It has sentimental value.

> It reminds me of...

### Example:

> Although the gift itself isn't very expensive, it has a lot of sentimental value because it reminds me of an important moment in my life.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Sentimental Value”

**Sentimental value = emotional importance**

Example:

> This watch has sentimental value because it was given to me by someone important to me.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Describe Something Useful

Topic:

> **The Most Useful Thing I Own**

Talk about:

> What is it?

> Why is it useful?

> How often do you use it?

> How does it make your life easier?

> What would you do without it?

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Describe Something You Can't Live Without

Don't literally mean impossible to survive.

Natural:

> One thing I use every day and would really miss is my phone.

Then explain:

> communication

> work

> study

> entertainment

> information

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Describe Something Old

Useful:

> I've had it for many years.

> It's quite old, but it still works well.

> It reminds me of my childhood.

> It has sentimental value.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Describe Something You Want to Buy

Future language:

> I'd like to buy...

> I've been thinking about buying...

> I'm planning to get...

> The main reason is...

### Example:

> I've been thinking about buying a new laptop because my current one is getting old.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Explain a Choice

Question:

> Why did you choose this one?

Answer:

> I chose it because...

> The main reason was...

> Another reason is...

> Compared with the alternatives,...

### Example:

> I chose this laptop because it offered a good balance between performance and price.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Explain a Problem With an Object

Useful structure:

> What happened?

↓

> When?

↓

> What caused it?

↓

> What did you do?

↓

> Result?

### Example:

> My phone suddenly stopped charging last month. At first, I thought the battery was damaged, but later I discovered that the charging cable was the problem.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Object + Storytelling

Lesson 65 connection:

> Last month, something unexpected happened with my phone.

Then:

> At first...

> Suddenly...

> I realized...

> So I decided...

> Luckily...

> In the end...

এতে object topic-এও 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা সম্ভব।

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Object + Opinion

Lesson 61:

> In my opinion, this is a very useful device.

Reason:

> The main reason is...

Example:

> For example,...

Second reason:

> Another reason is...

Conclusion:

> Overall,...

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Object Conversation

**A:** What is the most useful thing you own?

**B:** Probably my laptop.

**A:** Why is it so useful?

**B:** Because I use it for studying and programming.

**A:** How often do you use it?

**B:** Almost every day.

**A:** What do you like most about it?

**B:** I like its performance and portability.

**A:** Is there anything you don't like?

**B:** Yes. The battery life could be better.

**A:** Would you buy another one in the future?

**B:** Probably, but I'd choose one with a better battery.

🔥 এক object → অনেক conversation।

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — Useful Adjectives for Objects

### Size

> tiny
> small
> medium-sized
> large
> huge

### Weight

> lightweight
> heavy

### Quality

> durable
> reliable
> fragile
> high-quality

### Design

> modern
> simple
> elegant
> attractive
> compact

### Performance

> fast
> slow
> powerful
> efficient

### Price

> affordable
> expensive
> reasonable
> overpriced

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — Important Adjective Pairs

মনে রাখো:

> **lightweight ↔ heavy**

> **modern ↔ old-fashioned**

> **durable ↔ fragile**

> **affordable ↔ expensive**

> **fast ↔ slow**

> **simple ↔ complicated**

> **useful ↔ useless**

> **reliable ↔ unreliable**

---

# 🧠 Part 46 — “It's Designed For...”

Advanced useful expression:

> It's designed for students.

> It's designed for professional use.

> The app is designed to make learning easier.

---

# 🔥 Part 47 — “It's Suitable For...”

**Suitable for = উপযোগী**

> It's suitable for beginners.

> This laptop is suitable for programming.

> This book is suitable for intermediate English learners.

---

# 🧠 Part 48 — “It Can Be Used For...”

> It can be used for studying.

> It can be used for communication.

> It can be used for professional work.

---

Follow this exact structure:

>

>

>

>

# 🏆 Part 52 — Today's Golden Formula

# **W → A → F → U → E → A → D → O**

### W — What

> What is it?

### A — Appearance

> What does it look like?

### F — Features

> What does it have?

### U — Use

> How do you use it?

### E — Experience

> What happened?

### A — Advantages

> What is good about it?

### D — Disadvantages

> What is bad about it?

### O — Opinion

> What do you think?

---

# ⭐ 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **The object I'd like to talk about is...**
2. **It is a...**
3. **It has a simple and modern design.**
4. **It is relatively lightweight.**
5. **It is mainly used for...**
6. **I use it to...**
7. **It helps me...**
8. **It allows me to...**
9. **I've had it for...**
10. **I've been using it for...**
11. **I bought it because...**
12. **One of the main advantages is...**
13. **One thing I really like is...**
14. **The main drawback is...**
15. **The only problem is...**
16. **Compared with my old one,...**
17. **I remember one time when...**
18. **If I could change one thing,...**
19. **I think it is worth the money.**
20. **Overall, I think it is...**
