# 🎯 Lesson 69 Final Challenge

## **Describe Your Most Useful Object**

**3–5 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলবে।**

এই structure ব্যবহার করবে:

> **The object I'd like to talk about is...**
>
> **It is...**
>
> **In terms of appearance,...**
>
> **It has...**
>
> **I mainly use it to...**
>
> **I've had it for...**
>
> **One thing I really like about it is...**
>
> **I remember one time when...**
>
> **One of the main advantages is...**
>
> **However, the main drawback is...**
>
> **If I could change one thing,...**
>
> **In the future,...**
>
> **Overall, I think...**

### 🔥 আজকের আসল লক্ষ্য

একটি object নিয়ে শুধু **description** নয়—

**Description + Function + Personal Experience + Advantages/Disadvantages + Opinion**

একসাথে বলতে শেখা।

এভাবে **“My Phone”, “My Laptop”, “My Favorite Book”, “A Gift”, “Something I Bought”, “Something I Want to Buy”**—সব topic-এ একই structure ব্যবহার করতে পারবে।

---

## 🔜 Lesson 70 — Comparing Things

পরের lesson-এ শিখবে কীভাবে দুইটি **person, place, object, lifestyle বা idea** compare করে **similarities → differences → advantages → disadvantages → preference → reason → conclusion** দিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 70

# ⚖️ Comparing Things — দুইটি জিনিস তুলনা করে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে দুইটি **person, place, object, lifestyle, idea বা situation** compare করতে হয়।

শুধু:

> ❌ “A is good. B is good.”

বললে conversation এগোয় না।

আজকের লক্ষ্য:

> **A → B → Similarity → Difference → Advantage → Disadvantage → Preference → Reason → Conclusion**

---

# 🔗 Previous Lessons-এর Connection

* **Lesson 67:** Places describe করা
* **Lesson 68:** Daily life compare করা
* **Lesson 69:** Objects describe করা
* **Lesson 70:** **দুইটি জিনিস compare করে নিজের preference explain করা**

অর্থাৎ, আগের lessons-এর description skill এখন **comparison skill**-এ ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 🎯 Part 1 — Golden Structure

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **A → B → SAME → DIFFERENT → ADVANTAGE → DISADVANTAGE → PREFERENCE → REASON → CONCLUSION**

উদাহরণ:

### Topic:

**City life vs Village life**

> **A:** City life is fast-paced.
>
> **B:** Village life is more relaxed.
>
> **Same:** Both have their own advantages.
>
> **Difference:** Cities are more crowded, while villages are quieter.
>
> **Advantage:** Cities offer more job opportunities.
>
> **Disadvantage:** They also have more traffic.
>
> **Preference:** Personally, I prefer city life.
>
> **Reason:** Because of better career opportunities.
>
> **Conclusion:** However, I would like to spend holidays in the countryside.

🔥 এটাই আজকের basic structure।

---

# 🧠 Part 2 — Compare মানে কী?

**Compare = তুলনা করা**

দুইটি জিনিসের:

* Similarity → মিল
* Difference → অমিল
* Advantage → সুবিধা
* Disadvantage → অসুবিধা
* Preference → কোনটা বেশি পছন্দ
* Reason → কেন

বলতে হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 3 — Basic Comparison

সবচেয়ে সহজ:

> A is bigger than B.

> A is cheaper than B.

> A is faster than B.

> A is more expensive than B.

### Examples:

> A car is faster than a bicycle.

> A laptop is more expensive than a phone.

> City life is busier than village life.

---

# 🧠 Part 4 — Adjective + ER

ছোট adjective হলে সাধারণত:

> fast → faster

> slow → slower

> small → smaller

> large → larger

> cheap → cheaper

> easy → easier

> busy → busier

### Examples:

> A bike is cheaper than a car.

> English is easier than I expected.

> City life is busier than village life.

---

# 🔥 Part 5 — More + Adjective

Long adjective:

> beautiful → more beautiful

> expensive → more expensive

> comfortable → more comfortable

> convenient → more convenient

> interesting → more interesting

### Examples:

> A laptop is more expensive than a phone.

> Online learning is more convenient than traditional learning for some people.

---

# 🧠 Part 6 — Irregular Comparisons

এগুলো আলাদা করে মনে রাখবে:

| Basic     | Comparative     |
| --------- | --------------- |
| good      | better          |
| bad       | worse           |
| far       | farther/further |
| little    | less            |
| many/much | more            |

### Examples:

> This phone is better than my old one.

> Traffic is worse in the city.

> This method is more effective.

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “Than”

Comparison-এর সবচেয়ে common word:

> **than = থেকে**

Examples:

> My phone is faster than my old phone.

> Dhaka is more crowded than my hometown.

> Walking is cheaper than driving.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — “While”

দুইটি difference এক sentence-এ:

> City life is busy, while village life is peaceful.

> My old phone was slow, while my new phone is much faster.

> My weekdays are busy, while my weekends are more relaxed.

🔥 খুব useful connector।

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “Whereas”

**Whereas = পক্ষান্তরে/অন্যদিকে**

> City life is expensive, whereas village life is more affordable.

> My old laptop was heavy, whereas my new one is lightweight.

এটা একটু বেশি formal।

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “Compared With”

একটি strong phrase:

> Compared with my old phone, my new phone is much faster.

> Compared with city life, village life is more peaceful.

> Compared with traditional classes, online classes are more flexible.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “In Contrast”

Strong difference:

> City life is very busy. In contrast, village life is much quieter.

> The old phone was slow. In contrast, the new one is very fast.

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Similarity

শুধু difference বলবে না।

Similarity বলতেও হবে।

### Useful expressions:

> Both are...

> Both have...

> Both can...

> They are similar because...

> One similarity is...

### Examples:

> Both phones have good cameras.

> Both cities have many job opportunities.

> Both methods can help students learn.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — “Both...and...”

> Both phones are affordable and reliable.

> Both city and village life have advantages.

> Both methods can save time.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “Neither...nor...”

দুইটিতেই নেই:

> Neither option is perfect.

> Neither phone has a headphone jack.

> Neither city is completely free from traffic.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — “One Similarity Is...”

Long answer-এর জন্য:

> One similarity is that both options are affordable.

> One similarity is that both places attract many tourists.

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — Difference

Useful patterns:

> The main difference is...

> One major difference is...

> The biggest difference is...

### Example:

> The main difference is the lifestyle.

> One major difference is the cost.

> The biggest difference is convenience.

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Advantages

Comparison-এর সময়:

> One advantage of A is...

> The main benefit of A is...

> A has the advantage of...

### Example:

> One advantage of city life is better job opportunities.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Disadvantages

> One disadvantage of A is...

> The main drawback is...

> One problem with A is...

### Example:

> One disadvantage of city life is traffic congestion.

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Balanced Comparison

এটা খুব important।

### ❌ Weak:

> City life is better than village life.

### ✅ Better:

> City life has more job opportunities, but it is also more expensive and stressful. Village life is quieter and more peaceful, although it may offer fewer career opportunities.

🔥 Balanced answer।

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Preference

শেষে নিজের choice বলবে।

> Personally, I prefer...

> I would rather...

> If I had to choose,...

> For me,...

### Examples:

> Personally, I prefer city life.

> I would rather live in a quiet area.

> If I had to choose, I'd choose the second option.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — “I Would Rather”

Pattern:

> **I would rather + verb**

Examples:

> I would rather live in a city.

> I would rather study at home.

> I would rather buy a laptop.

❌ Don't say:

> I would rather to live...

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Give Your Reason

Preference-এর পরে reason অবশ্যই দেবে।

> I prefer A because...

> The main reason is...

> One reason is...

> Another reason is...

### Example:

> I prefer city life because there are more career opportunities. Another reason is that transportation is more convenient.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Give an Example

Reason → Example:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

### Example:

> City life offers more career opportunities. For example, there are usually more companies and professional organizations in large cities.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — Concession

একটি ভালো comparison-এ নিজের preference-এর বিপরীত দিকও স্বীকার করবে।

Useful:

> However,...

> Although...

> Even though...

> On the other hand,...

### Example:

> I prefer city life because of the career opportunities. However, I sometimes miss the peace and quiet of village life.

এটা অনেক natural শোনায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — “Although”

Structure:

> **Although + opposite point, + your point.**

Example:

> Although city life is stressful, I prefer it because of the job opportunities.

> Although online learning is convenient, traditional classes offer more direct interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — “On the Other Hand”

A-এর কথা বলার পর B:

> City life offers many opportunities. On the other hand, it can be stressful.

> Online learning is flexible. On the other hand, students may feel less connected to their classmates.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Example: City vs Village

## 🎤 3-Minute Answer

> If I compare city life with village life, I think both have their own advantages and disadvantages.
>
> City life is generally faster and busier. There are more offices, universities, shopping centers, restaurants, and job opportunities. Transportation is also usually more convenient.
>
> Village life, on the other hand, is much quieter and more peaceful. There is less traffic, less noise, and usually more open space. People may also have a stronger connection with nature.
>
> One major difference is the lifestyle. City people often have busy schedules, whereas village life tends to be more relaxed. Another difference is career opportunity. Cities usually provide more options for education and employment.
>
> However, city life also has some disadvantages. It can be expensive, crowded, and stressful. Village life can be more affordable and peaceful, although there may be fewer professional opportunities.
>
> Personally, I prefer city life because of education and career opportunities. However, I would definitely like to spend some time in the countryside because I enjoy peaceful environments.
>
> Overall, I don't think one is completely better than the other. It depends on a person's lifestyle, goals, and priorities.

🔥 Notice:

**Similarity → Difference → Advantage → Disadvantage → Preference → Reason → Conclusion**

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Comparison Vocabulary

### Size

> bigger / smaller

### Price

> cheaper / more expensive

### Speed

> faster / slower

### Quality

> better / worse

### Comfort

> more comfortable / less comfortable

### Convenience

> more convenient / less convenient

### Difficulty

> easier / more difficult

### Popularity

> more popular / less popular

### Safety

> safer / less safe

### Flexibility

> more flexible / less flexible

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Useful Opposite Pairs

> cheap ↔ expensive

> fast ↔ slow

> easy ↔ difficult

> quiet ↔ noisy

> crowded ↔ empty

> modern ↔ traditional

> comfortable ↔ uncomfortable

> convenient ↔ inconvenient

> flexible ↔ rigid

> safe ↔ dangerous

> healthy ↔ unhealthy

> interesting ↔ boring

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — Compare Two Objects

## Topic:

**Phone vs Laptop**

Start:

> Both are useful electronic devices, but they serve different purposes.

Then:

> A phone is smaller and more portable.

> A laptop has a larger screen and is better for serious work.

> Phones are more convenient for communication.

> Laptops are better for programming and productivity.

### Conclusion:

> Personally, I prefer using a laptop for work, but I rely on my phone for communication.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Phone vs Laptop Full Answer

> Both smartphones and laptops are useful electronic devices, but they are designed for somewhat different purposes.
>
> A smartphone is smaller, lighter, and much easier to carry. It is very convenient for making calls, sending messages, browsing the internet, and using social media. A laptop, on the other hand, has a larger screen and a physical keyboard, so it is much better for programming, writing, and professional work.
>
> One similarity is that both devices can connect to the internet and run many different applications. However, the main difference is portability and productivity.
>
> Personally, I prefer using a laptop when I need to concentrate on work, but I prefer my phone for quick tasks and communication.
>
> Overall, I think both are useful, and the better choice depends on what you need to do.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Online vs Offline Learning

Useful points:

### Online

> flexible

> convenient

> learn from home

> recorded classes

### Offline

> face-to-face interaction

> direct communication

> classroom environment

> immediate feedback

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Example Answer

> Online and offline learning both have advantages. Online learning is more flexible because students can study from almost anywhere and often choose their own schedule. It can also be convenient for people who have jobs or other responsibilities.
>
> Offline learning, however, provides direct interaction with teachers and classmates. Students can ask questions immediately and participate in face-to-face discussions.
>
> Personally, I prefer a combination of both. Online learning is useful for flexibility, while offline learning is better for interaction and discipline.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Watching Movies at Home vs Cinema

### Home

> cheaper

> comfortable

> convenient

> pause anytime

### Cinema

> large screen

> better sound

> social experience

> special atmosphere

### Speaking:

> I prefer watching movies at home because it is more comfortable and affordable. However, for action or visually impressive movies, I prefer the cinema because the large screen and sound system create a better experience.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Public Transport vs Private Car

### Public transport:

> cheaper

> environmentally friendly

> sometimes crowded

### Private car:

> comfortable

> convenient

> expensive

> traffic problems

### Example:

> Public transportation is generally cheaper than using a private car. However, a private car can be more comfortable and convenient, especially when traveling with family.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Compare Two Places

Use Lesson 67:

> Location

> Appearance

> Atmosphere

> Activities

> People

> Cost

> Preference

Example:

> Dhaka is more crowded than my hometown, but it offers more job opportunities.

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Compare Two People

Lesson 66 connection:

> Both are friendly.

> A is more outgoing than B.

> B is quieter.

> A is more talkative, whereas B is more reserved.

Don't make comparison unnecessarily negative.

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — Compare Two Lifestyles

### Busy lifestyle:

> productive

> stressful

> less free time

### Relaxed lifestyle:

> peaceful

> less stressful

> potentially less productive

### Example:

> A busy lifestyle can help people achieve their goals, but it can also cause stress. A relaxed lifestyle provides more free time, although it may require more self-discipline to stay productive.

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — Compare Past and Present

Very useful!

> In the past...

> Nowadays...

> Compared with the past,...

### Example:

> Compared with the past, people today use technology much more in their daily lives. Communication is faster, but people may spend less time interacting face-to-face.

---

# 🧠 Part 40 — Compare Yourself: Past vs Now

> I used to...

> These days...

> Compared with my younger self,...

### Example:

> Compared with a few years ago, I'm more organized now. I used to study only when I had exams, whereas these days I try to follow a regular schedule.

🔥 Lesson 68-এর direct connection।

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — Compare Two Choices

Question:

> Which is better, A or B?

Don't immediately answer.

Use:

> It depends on...

> If your priority is..., then...

### Example:

> It depends on your priorities. If your priority is convenience, online learning may be better. If your priority is face-to-face interaction, traditional learning may be better.

🔥 This is advanced speaking.

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — “It Depends On”

Very important expression:

> It depends on the situation.

> It depends on your budget.

> It depends on your goals.

> It depends on your personal preference.

---

# 🔥 Part 43 — Don't Say “Better” Too Much

Instead of:

> A is better.

Use:

> A is more convenient.

> A is more affordable.

> A is more practical.

> A is more suitable for...

> A is a better option for...

এতে vocabulary strong হবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — “Suitable For”

> A laptop is more suitable for programming.

> A phone is more suitable for quick communication.

> Online learning is suitable for people with busy schedules.

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — “A Better Option For”

> A bicycle is a better option for short distances.

> Public transport is a better option for people on a limited budget.

---

# 🧠 Part 46 — Advanced Comparison

একটি strong answer:

> **While A is better in terms of X, B has an advantage when it comes to Y.**

Example:

> While a laptop is better in terms of productivity, a smartphone has an advantage when it comes to portability.

🔥 এই structure অবশ্যই practice করবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 47 — Another Advanced Structure

> **A has the advantage of X, whereas B is better when it comes to Y.**

Example:

> City life has the advantage of better career opportunities, whereas village life is better when it comes to peace and quiet.

---

# 🧠 Part 48 — Final Conclusion

শেষে:

> Overall,...

> In conclusion,...

> All things considered,...

> Taking everything into account,...

### Example:

> Overall, I think both options are useful, but my choice would depend on my priorities.

---

# 🔥 Part 49 — 5-Minute Answer Formula

যেকোনো comparison topic-এ:

### ① Introduction

> If I compare A and B,...

### ② Similarity

> Both...

### ③ Difference 1

> The main difference is...

### ④ Difference 2

> Another difference is...

### ⑤ Advantage A

> One advantage of A is...

### ⑥ Disadvantage A

> However,...

### ⑦ Advantage B

> On the other hand,...

### ⑧ Preference

> Personally, I prefer...

### ⑨ Reason

> The main reason is...

### ⑩ Example

> For example,...

### ⑪ Opposite side

> However, I also understand why...

### ⑫ Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# 🎤 Part 50 — Conversation Practice

**A:** Which do you prefer, online or offline learning?

**B:** Personally, I prefer a combination of both.

**A:** Why?

**B:** Online learning is more flexible, while offline learning provides better face-to-face interaction.

**A:** Which one is cheaper?

**B:** Generally, online learning can be cheaper because you don't need to travel.

**A:** What is the main disadvantage of online learning?

**B:** The main disadvantage is that it can be difficult to stay focused.

**A:** So which one would you choose?

**B:** If I had to choose only one, I'd probably choose offline learning for important subjects.

🔥 লক্ষ্য করো: **এক answer → follow-up → explanation → comparison → preference**।

---

# ⭐ Part 51 — 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **If I compare A with B,...**
2. **Both have their own advantages.**
3. **Both are useful in different ways.**
4. **One similarity is that...**
5. **The main difference is...**
6. **One major difference is...**
7. **A is more... than B.**
8. **A is less... than B.**
9. **Compared with A, B is...**
10. **A is..., while B is...**
11. **A is..., whereas B is...**
12. **In contrast,...**
13. **One advantage of A is...**
14. **The main drawback is...**
15. **On the other hand,...**
16. **Personally, I prefer...**
17. **I would rather...**
18. **It depends on...**
19. **If I had to choose,...**
20. **Overall, I think...**

---

# 🏆 Part 52 — Today's Golden Formula

মনে রাখো:

# **COMPARE → EXPLAIN → BALANCE → CHOOSE**

### COMPARE

> A is faster than B.

### EXPLAIN

> The main reason is...

### BALANCE

> However, B is cheaper.

### CHOOSE

> Personally, I prefer A because...

এটাই **natural comparison speaking**।

---

## Topic 1 — **City Life vs Village Life**

**3–5 minutes** বলবে।

এই structure:

> If I compare city life with village life,...
>
> Both...
>
> The main difference is...
>
> One advantage of city life is...
>
> However,...
>
> On the other hand,...
>
> Personally, I prefer...
>
> The main reason is...
>
> For example,...
>
> Overall,...

## 🎤 Topic 2 — **Phone vs Laptop**

বলবে:

1. What are they?
2. What are they used for?
3. Which is more portable?
4. Which is better for work?
5. Which is cheaper?
6. What do they have in common?
7. Which one do you use more?
8. Which one do you prefer?
9. Why?
10. Final opinion.

---

## 🎤 Topic 3 — **Online vs Offline Learning**

এই topic-এ অবশ্যই ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Both**

> **while**

> **whereas**

> **however**

> **on the other hand**

> **personally**

> **because**

> **for example**

> **overall**

---

# 🔥 Today's Main Goal

আজ শুধু **“A is better than B”** বলা শিখবে না।

তোমার speaking answer হবে:

> **A + B → Similarity → Difference → Advantage → Disadvantage → Preference → Reason → Example → Balanced Conclusion**

এভাবে একটি ছোট comparison topic-ও **3–5 মিনিটের natural conversation**-এ পরিণত করতে পারবে।

### 🔜 Lesson 71 — **Agreeing & Disagreeing**

পরের lesson-এ শিখবে কীভাবে অন্য কারও কথায় **agree, partially agree, politely disagree, reason দেওয়া, example দেওয়া এবং conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া** যায়—যাতে real-life conversation আর discussion অনেক বেশি natural হয়।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 71

# 🤝 Agreeing & Disagreeing — একমত ও ভিন্নমত প্রকাশ করা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে conversation-এ কারও কথায় **agree, partially agree, disagree, reason, example এবং polite response** দিতে হয়।

শুধু:

> ❌ Yes.
> ❌ No.
> ❌ I agree.
> ❌ I disagree.

বললে conversation খুব দ্রুত শেষ হয়ে যায়।

আজকের লক্ষ্য:

# **OPINION → AGREE/DISAGREE → REASON → EXAMPLE → OTHER SIDE → CONCLUSION**

---

# 🔗 Previous Lessons-এর Connection

* **Lesson 61:** Giving Opinions → নিজের opinion দেওয়া
* **Lesson 64:** Follow-up Questions → conversation চালিয়ে নেওয়া
* **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → example/story দেওয়া
* **Lesson 70:** Comparing → দুই দিক compare করা
* **Lesson 71:** **Agreeing & Disagreeing → অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে respond করা**

এখন থেকে শুধু **নিজে কথা বলা নয়**, অন্য একজন কী বলছে তার ওপর **response** দিতে শিখবে।

---

# 🎯 Part 1 — Agreeing মানে কী?

**Agree = একমত হওয়া**

কেউ বলল:

> **“Technology makes our lives easier.”**

তুমি বলতে পারো:

> I agree.

কিন্তু natural conversation:

> **I completely agree. Technology has made many things much easier.**

আরও:

> **That's true.**

> **Exactly.**

> **I think you're right.**

> **I feel the same way.**

---

# 🟢 Part 2 — Strong Agreement

যখন তুমি পুরোপুরি একমত:

### সহজ:

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

> I absolutely agree.

### Natural:

> That's exactly how I feel.

> That's a good point.

> You're absolutely right.

> I couldn't agree more.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “I Couldn't Agree More”

এটা খুব useful expression।

**I couldn't agree more = আমি এর চেয়ে বেশি একমত হতে পারি না।**

Example:

**A:** Exercise is important for our health.

**B:** I couldn't agree more. It helps us stay physically and mentally active.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Agreement + Reason

শুধু:

> I agree.

না।

এরপর:

> **because...**

### Example:

> I agree because technology saves us a lot of time.

আরও natural:

> I completely agree. The main reason is that technology allows us to do things much faster.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Agreement + Example

Structure:

> **I agree + reason + example**

Example:

> I agree that technology makes life easier. The main reason is that it saves time. For example, we can now communicate with people around the world within seconds.

🔥 এটা Lesson 61-এর **Opinion → Reason → Example** structure-এর সাথে connected।

---

# 🟢 Part 6 — Simple Agreement Expressions

| Expression             | Meaning              |
| ---------------------- | -------------------- |
| I agree.               | আমি একমত             |
| Exactly.               | ঠিক তাই              |
| That's true.           | সেটা সত্যি           |
| You're right.          | তুমি ঠিক বলেছ        |
| Definitely.            | অবশ্যই               |
| Absolutely.            | সম্পূর্ণভাবে         |
| I think so too.        | আমিও তাই মনে করি     |
| I feel the same way.   | আমিও একইভাবে মনে করি |
| That's a good point.   | এটা ভালো point       |
| I couldn't agree more. | পুরোপুরি একমত        |

---

# 🔥 Part 7 — “That's a Good Point”

কেউ ভালো argument দিলে:

> That's a good point.

তারপর নিজের কথা:

> I hadn't thought about it that way before.

Example:

**A:** Online learning is useful because you can learn from anywhere.

**B:** That's a good point. I also think it gives students more flexibility.

🔥 Very natural conversational English.

---

# 🧠 Part 8 — Partial Agreement

সবসময় পুরোপুরি agree করতে হবে না।

**Partial agreement = আংশিক একমত**

Useful:

> I agree to some extent.

> I partly agree.

> I agree up to a point.

> I agree with you to some extent, but...

> That's true, but...

> You have a point, but...

---

# 🔥 Part 9 — “I Agree to Some Extent”

Meaning:

> কিছুটা একমত, কিন্তু পুরোপুরি না।

Example:

> I agree to some extent, but I think there are some disadvantages as well.

---

# 🧠 Part 10 — “That's True, But...”

সবচেয়ে useful structure:

# **That's true, but + opposite point**

Example:

> That's true, but online learning can sometimes be difficult for students who need direct interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — “You Have a Point, But...”

Polite disagreement:

> You have a point, but I see it differently.

Example:

> You have a point, but I think social media can also be useful for education.

🔥 Direct “You are wrong” না বলে disagreement করা যায়।

---

# 🧠 Part 12 — Disagreeing

**Disagree = ভিন্নমত পোষণ করা**

Simple:

> I don't agree.

কিন্তু conversational English-এ আরও polite:

> I'm not sure I agree.

> I see it differently.

> I don't quite agree with that.

> I'm afraid I disagree.

> I have a different opinion.

---

# 🔥 Part 13 — Polite Disagreement

সবচেয়ে useful:

> **I'm not sure I agree with that.**

তারপর reason:

> **I think...**

Example:

> I'm not sure I agree with that. I think social media can be useful if people use it responsibly.

---

# 🧠 Part 14 — “I See It Differently”

খুব natural:

> I see it differently.

তারপর:

> In my opinion,...

Example:

> I see it differently. In my opinion, technology has more benefits than disadvantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 15 — Avoid “You're Wrong”

### ❌ Too direct:

> You're wrong.

> That's completely wrong.

> No, that's not true.

এগুলো কখনো কখনো rude শোনাতে পারে।

### ✅ Better:

> I'm not sure I agree.

> I see your point, but...

> I have a slightly different view.

> I understand what you mean, but...

---

# 🧠 Part 16 — “I Understand What You Mean”

এটা disagreement-এর আগে খুব useful।

Structure:

> **I understand what you mean, but...**

Example:

> I understand what you mean, but I think there are some benefits to social media as well.

এতে tone polite হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 17 — Respectful Disagreement Formula

মনে রাখো:

# **ACKNOWLEDGE → DIFFERENCE → REASON**

### ① Acknowledge

> I understand your point.

### ② Difference

> However, I see it differently.

### ③ Reason

> The main reason is...

### Full:

> I understand your point. However, I see it differently. The main reason is that technology has made communication much easier.

---

# 🧠 Part 18 — Strong Disagreement

যখন তুমি খুব strongly disagree:

> I strongly disagree.

> I completely disagree.

কিন্তু everyday conversation-এ polite version বেশি useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 19 — Agree + Add Information

শুধু agreement না দিয়ে নতুন information যোগ করো।

### A:

> Exercise is good for our health.

### B:

> Absolutely. It also helps reduce stress and improve our mood.

🔥 Structure:

> **Agree → Add**

---

# 🧠 Part 20 — Agree + Example

### A:

> Reading is a good habit.

### B:

> I completely agree. It improves our knowledge and vocabulary. For example, reading English books can help us learn new expressions.

---

# 🔥 Part 21 — Disagree + Reason

### A:

> Social media is always harmful.

### B:

> I don't completely agree. Social media can be harmful if people use it excessively, but it can also be useful for communication and education.

Notice:

> ❌ “No.”

Instead:

> **I don't completely agree.**

---

# 🧠 Part 22 — Partial Agreement Formula

# **AGREE → BUT → DIFFERENT POINT**

Example:

> I agree that smartphones can be distracting, but they can also be very useful for learning and communication.

---

# 🔥 Part 23 — Agree With One Part, Disagree With Another

This is advanced but very useful.

> I agree with the first part, but I'm not sure about the second part.

Example:

> I agree that social media can waste time, but I'm not sure that it is always harmful.

---

# 🧠 Part 24 — “It Depends”

Many opinions don't have one correct answer.

Use:

> It depends.

Then explain:

> It depends on how people use it.

Example:

> Is technology good or bad?

> It depends on how people use it. Used responsibly, it can be extremely useful.

---

# 🔥 Part 25 — Opinion + Agreement

### Topic:

**“People spend too much time on their phones.”**

### Response:

> I agree to some extent. Many people do spend too much time on their phones. However, smartphones are also useful for work, study, and communication.

🔥 This is excellent balanced speaking.

---

# 🧠 Part 26 — Agreeing About Technology

**A:** Technology has made our lives easier.

**B:**

> I completely agree. It has made communication, education, and many daily tasks much easier.

---

# 🔥 Part 27 — Disagreeing About Technology

**A:** Technology has made people lazy.

**B:**

> I agree to some extent, but I don't think technology itself makes people lazy. I think it depends on how we use it.

🔥 Notice:

**Partial agreement → correction → reason**

---

# 🧠 Part 28 — Social Media

**A:** Social media is a waste of time.

**B:**

> I wouldn't completely agree with that. Social media can waste a lot of time if we use it without limits, but it can also be useful for communication, networking, and learning.

---

# 🔥 Part 29 — Online Education

**A:** Online classes are better than traditional classes.

**B:**

> I partly agree. Online classes are more flexible and convenient, but traditional classes offer better face-to-face interaction. So I think both have their advantages.

---

# 🧠 Part 30 — City Life

**A:** City life is better than village life.

**B:**

> I agree to some extent. Cities offer better career opportunities and services, but village life is quieter and less stressful. So I think it depends on a person's priorities.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Money

**A:** Money can buy happiness.

**B:**

> I don't completely agree. Money can provide comfort and security, but I don't think it can guarantee happiness. Relationships and personal satisfaction are also important.

🔥 Excellent discussion pattern।

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Education

**A:** University education is necessary for everyone.

**B:**

> I'm not sure I agree. University education is very useful for some careers, but there are also careers where practical skills and experience may be more important.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — English Learning

**A:** Grammar is the most important part of learning English.

**B:**

> I partly agree, but I think communication practice is equally important. Grammar helps us form correct sentences, but speaking practice helps us become confident.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Exercise

**A:** Exercise is difficult and boring.

**B:**

> I see your point, but I don't completely agree. Exercise can be enjoyable if you choose an activity you like, such as walking, cycling, or playing sports.

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Movies

**A:** Watching movies is a waste of time.

**B:**

> I don't think that's always true. Watching movies can be entertainment, but some movies can also teach us about different cultures, ideas, and languages.

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Polite Discussion

Conversation:

**A:** I think working from home is better.

**B:** That's a good point. I agree that working from home can save commuting time.

**A:** Exactly.

**B:** However, I think working in an office has some advantages too.

**A:** Like what?

**B:** For example, it's easier to communicate directly with colleagues.

🔥 Notice how B:

**Agreed → Added → Disagreed politely → Gave example**

এটাই real conversation।

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Conversation Don't Stop

যদি অন্যজন বলে:

> I agree.

তুমি বলতে পারো:

> Why do you think so?

> What makes you say that?

> Can you give me an example?

> What about the disadvantages?

> Do you think this is true for everyone?

এতে conversation চালু থাকবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — Ask After Agreement

### A:

> I think social media is useful.

### B:

> I agree. What do you mainly use it for?

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — Ask After Disagreement

### A:

> Online learning is always better.

### B:

> I'm not sure I agree. What makes you think it's better?

এতে debate শুরু হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 40 — “What Makes You Say That?”

Meaning:

> কী কারণে তুমি এমন বলছ?

Very natural.

Example:

> **A:** I think city life is better.

> **B:** What makes you say that?

> **A:** There are more job opportunities.

---

# 🔥 Part 41 — “Can You Give Me an Example?”

Reason আরও clear করার জন্য:

> Can you give me an example?

> Could you give me an example?

Example:

**A:** Technology is useful for education.

**B:** That's true. Can you give me an example?

**A:** Students can take online courses from anywhere.

---

# 🧠 Part 42 — “What About...?”

Opposite side introduce করতে:

> What about the disadvantages?

> What about people who don't have internet access?

> What about privacy?

Excellent discussion phrase।

---

# 🔥 Part 43 — “On the Other Hand”

Disagreement-এ খুব useful:

> I understand your point. On the other hand, there are some disadvantages we should consider.

---

# 🧠 Part 44 — “From My Perspective”

Advanced opinion:

> From my perspective,...

> From my point of view,...

Example:

> From my perspective, technology is neither completely good nor completely bad. It depends on how we use it.

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — “As Far As I'm Concerned”

More advanced:

> As far as I'm concerned, education should focus more on practical skills.

এটা formal discussion-এ ভালো।

---

# 🧠 Part 46 — Agreement Levels

### 🟢 100% Agreement

> Absolutely.

> I completely agree.

> I couldn't agree more.

### 🟡 50–70% Agreement

> I partly agree.

> I agree to some extent.

> That's true, but...

### 🔴 Disagreement

> I'm not sure I agree.

> I see it differently.

> I don't quite agree.

> I have a different view.

---

# 🔥 Part 47 — Disagreement Levels

### Soft:

> I'm not sure I agree.

### Medium:

> I don't quite agree with that.

### Strong:

> I strongly disagree.

Real-life conversation-এ সাধারণত **soft/medium** ব্যবহার বেশি natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 48 — Avoid Repeating “I Think”

বারবার:

> I think...

> I think...

> I think...

না।

Alternative:

> In my opinion,...

> Personally,...

> From my perspective,...

> As far as I'm concerned,...

> I believe...

> I would say...

---

# 🔥 Part 49 — Mini Debate

## Topic:

### “Technology makes people less social.”

**A:**

> I think technology makes people less social.

**B:**

> I partly agree. People sometimes spend too much time on their phones instead of talking face-to-face.

**A:**

> Exactly.

**B:**

> However, technology also helps people stay connected when they live far apart.

**A:**

> That's true.

**B:**

> For example, families can make video calls even when they live in different countries.

🔥 এটাই target conversation।

---

# 🧠 Part 50 — Another Mini Debate

## Topic:

### “University education is necessary.”

**A:**

> I think everyone should go to university.

**B:**

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

**A:**

> Why?

**B:**

> Because not every career requires a university degree. Practical skills can also be very important.

**A:**

> That's true, but university gives people a strong foundation.

**B:**

> I agree with that to some extent. I think the best choice depends on the person's career goals.

---

# 🔥 Part 51 — The Golden Response Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **A → R → E → C**

### A = Agree/Disagree

> I partly agree.

### R = Reason

> The main reason is...

### E = Example

> For example,...

### C = Counterpoint/Conclusion

> However,...

### Full:

> I partly agree. The main reason is that social media can waste a lot of time. For example, people may spend hours scrolling without realizing it. However, social media can also be useful for learning and communication.

🔥 এই formula দিয়ে 1–2 মিনিট সহজেই বলা যায়।

---

# 🏆 Part 52 — 5-Minute Discussion Formula

যেকোনো opinion discussion:

### ① Respond

> I completely agree.

### ② Explain

> The main reason is...

### ③ Example

> For example,...

### ④ Add another point

> Another reason is...

### ⑤ Opposite side

> However,...

### ⑥ Respect other opinion

> I understand why some people think...

### ⑦ Personal opinion

> Personally,...

### ⑧ Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# ⭐ Part 53 — 25 Must-Learn Expressions

### Agree

1. **I completely agree.**
2. **I totally agree.**
3. **Absolutely.**
4. **Exactly.**
5. **That's true.**
6. **You're right.**
7. **That's a good point.**
8. **I feel the same way.**
9. **I think so too.**
10. **I couldn't agree more.**

### Partial Agreement

11. **I partly agree.**
12. **I agree to some extent.**
13. **I agree up to a point.**
14. **That's true, but...**
15. **You have a point, but...**

### Disagree

16. **I'm not sure I agree.**
17. **I don't quite agree.**
18. **I see it differently.**
19. **I have a different view.**
20. **I don't completely agree.**

### Discussion

21. **What makes you say that?**
22. **Can you give me an example?**
23. **What about the disadvantages?**
24. **On the other hand,...**
25. **It depends on...**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 71 Final Practice

## Topic 1:

# **“Technology makes our lives better.”**

তোমার response:

> **Agreement/Disagreement**
>
> ↓
>
> **Reason**
>
> ↓
>
> **Example**
>
> ↓
>
> **Other side**
>
> ↓
>
> **Personal opinion**
>
> ↓
>
> **Conclusion**

---

## Topic 2:

# **“Social media is harmful to society.”**

Use:

> I partly agree...

> The main reason is...

> For example,...

> However,...

> Personally,...

> Overall,...

---

## Topic 3:

# **“Online learning is better than traditional learning.”**

Must use:

> **Both**

> **while**

> **however**

> **on the other hand**

> **I partly agree**

> **because**

> **for example**

> **it depends on**

> **overall**

---

# 🏆 Final Speaking Challenge

কেউ তোমাকে বলল:

> **“I think people spend too much time on their phones.”**

তুমি **30–60 seconds** response দেবে।

একটি strong answer হতে পারে:

> **I agree to some extent.** Many people do spend too much time on their phones, especially on social media. **For example**, some people check their phones constantly even when they are talking to friends or family. **However**, I don't think smartphones are completely bad. They are also useful for communication, education, and work. **Personally, I think the problem is not the phone itself but how we use it. Overall,** people should try to use technology in a more balanced way.

🔥 **এটাই Lesson 71-এর target level।**

---

## 🔜 Lesson 72 — **Asking & Answering Follow-up Questions**

পরের lesson-এ আমরা আরও এক ধাপ এগোবো: **একটা answer দেওয়ার পর কীভাবে নিজে থেকে follow-up question করা, উত্তর expand করা, clarification চাওয়া, এবং 5+ মিনিট conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া যায়।**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 73

# 🛠️ Problems & Solutions — সমস্যা নিয়ে কথা বলা ও সমাধান দেওয়া

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কোনো একটি **problem** নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে এবং কীভাবে তার **cause, effect, solution, best solution** explain করতে হয়।

Lesson 72-এ আমরা শিখেছিলাম conversation কীভাবে expand করতে হয়। আজ সেই skill ব্যবহার করে **একটি problem নিয়ে বিস্তারিত কথা বলা** শিখব।

---

# 🎯 Today's Speaking Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important structure:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → EFFECT → EXAMPLE → SOLUTION → BEST SOLUTION → RESULT → CONCLUSION**

এই structure মুখস্থ থাকলে যেকোনো problem নিয়ে কথা বলা সহজ হবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Problem কী?

**Problem = সমস্যা**

Examples:

* Traffic congestion
* Pollution
* Lack of time
* Unemployment
* Stress
* Poor English speaking
* Excessive social media use
* Internet problems
* Lack of exercise
* Financial problems

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Problem Introduce করা

কোনো problem নিয়ে শুরু করার জন্য:

> **One common problem is...**

> **One major problem is...**

> **A serious problem is...**

> **One of the biggest problems is...**

### Examples:

> One common problem is traffic congestion.

> One major problem is pollution.

> One of the biggest problems for students is lack of time.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “The Problem Is That...”

খুব useful structure:

> **The problem is that + sentence**

Examples:

> The problem is that many people don't have enough time to exercise.

> The problem is that public transportation can become overcrowded.

> The problem is that many students are afraid of making mistakes when speaking English.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Explain the Cause

Problem বলার পর অবশ্যই বলবে:

> **Why does this happen?**

Useful phrases:

> The main reason is...

> One reason is...

> This happens because...

> There are several reasons for this.

> One of the main causes is...

---

### Example:

> One major problem is traffic congestion. **One of the main causes is the large number of vehicles on the roads.**

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Multiple Causes

একটি problem-এর একাধিক কারণ থাকতে পারে।

Use:

> First,...

> Another reason is...

> In addition,...

> Furthermore,...

### Example:

> There are several reasons for traffic congestion. First, there are too many vehicles. Another reason is poor road management. In addition, people often park their vehicles in inappropriate places.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Cause → Effect

**Cause = কারণ**

**Effect = ফলাফল**

Useful:

> As a result,...

> Because of this,...

> This leads to...

> This can cause...

> Consequently,...

---

### Example:

> There are too many vehicles on the roads. **As a result, people spend a lot of time in traffic.**

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “This Leads To”

খুব useful:

> **This leads to + noun**

Examples:

> Traffic congestion leads to wasted time.

> Lack of exercise can lead to health problems.

> Too much screen time can lead to poor sleep.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “As a Result”

Structure:

> **Problem → As a result → Effect**

Example:

> Many students don't manage their time properly. **As a result, they often feel stressed before exams.**

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Give an Example

Problem explain করার পর example দেবে:

> For example,...

> For instance,...

> A good example is...

### Example:

> Lack of time is a common problem for students. For example, students may have classes, assignments, exams, and personal responsibilities at the same time.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Give a Personal Example

Speaking-এর জন্য personal example অনেক useful:

> In my experience,...

> In my case,...

> I have experienced this myself.

### Example:

> In my experience, poor time management can make studying much more stressful.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Introduce a Solution

এখন বলবে:

> **How can we solve the problem?**

Useful phrases:

> One possible solution is...

> A good solution would be...

> We can solve this by...

> One way to deal with this problem is...

> The best way to solve this is...

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Solution + Verb

### Pattern:

> **We can solve this by + verb-ing**

Examples:

> We can solve this by improving public transportation.

> We can solve this by managing our time better.

> We can solve this by reducing unnecessary screen time.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “People Should...”

Advice দেওয়ার জন্য:

> People should...

> Students should...

> The government should...

> Companies should...

> Parents should...

### Examples:

> Students should create a daily study schedule.

> People should exercise regularly.

> The government should improve public transportation.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Multiple Solutions

একটি solution-এর মধ্যে আটকে থাকবে না।

Use:

> First,...

> Second,...

> Another solution is...

> Finally,...

### Example:

> There are several ways to solve this problem. First, people can use public transportation more often. Second, the government can improve the road system. Finally, better traffic management can reduce congestion.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Best Solution

সব solution বলার পর:

> **In my opinion, the best solution is...**

Example:

> In my opinion, the best solution is to improve public transportation because it can reduce the number of private vehicles on the road.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Explain Why the Solution Is Best

শুধু:

> The best solution is public transportation.

না।

বলবে:

> I think improving public transportation is the best solution **because it can help reduce traffic, save time, and make transportation more affordable.**

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Solution → Result

Future result:

> This would help...

> This could reduce...

> As a result,...

> This would make...

### Example:

> Better public transportation would help reduce the number of cars on the road. As a result, traffic congestion could decrease.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “If We...”

Condition দিয়ে solution explain করা:

> If we improve public transportation, traffic congestion may decrease.

> If students manage their time properly, they can reduce stress.

> If people exercise regularly, they can improve their health.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Unless”

Advanced but useful:

> Unless we take action, the problem may become worse.

Example:

> Unless people reduce unnecessary plastic use, pollution may continue to increase.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Problem → Cause → Effect

একটি complete example:

### Topic:

**Lack of exercise**

> Lack of exercise is a common problem today. One major reason is that many people have busy lifestyles and spend a lot of time sitting. As a result, they may feel tired and become less physically active.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Add Personal Experience

> I sometimes face this problem myself.

Example:

> I sometimes face this problem myself because I spend a lot of time working on a computer.

এতে answer আরও natural হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Problem + Solution

> To deal with this problem, I try to take short breaks and walk for a few minutes. I also try to exercise regularly.

Notice:

**Problem → Personal solution**

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Useful Problem Vocabulary

| Word       | Meaning      |
| ---------- | ------------ |
| problem    | সমস্যা       |
| issue      | সমস্যা/বিষয়  |
| cause      | কারণ         |
| effect     | প্রভাব       |
| result     | ফলাফল        |
| solution   | সমাধান       |
| challenge  | চ্যালেঞ্জ    |
| difficulty | অসুবিধা      |
| impact     | প্রভাব       |
| improve    | উন্নত করা    |
| reduce     | কমানো        |
| prevent    | প্রতিরোধ করা |
| avoid      | এড়িয়ে চলা    |
| solve      | সমাধান করা   |
| deal with  | মোকাবিলা করা |

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Problem” vs “Issue”

দুটোই ব্যবহার করা যায়।

### Problem:

> Traffic is a serious problem.

### Issue:

> Traffic is a serious issue in large cities.

**Issue** একটু বেশি formal/natural discussion-এ useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Challenge”

Challenge মানে সবসময় negative problem নয়।

> Learning English can be challenging.

> One of the biggest challenges is speaking confidently.

### Example:

> One of my biggest challenges is speaking English without translating from Bengali.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Talking About Your Own Problem

### Topic:

**English Speaking Problem**

Start:

> One of my biggest challenges is speaking English fluently.

Cause:

> The main reason is that I don't always practice speaking regularly.

Effect:

> As a result, I sometimes hesitate when I speak.

Example:

> For example, I may know the words in my mind but struggle to say them quickly.

Solution:

> One possible solution is to practice speaking every day.

Best solution:

> In my opinion, regular speaking practice is the best solution.

Conclusion:

> If I practice consistently, I believe my confidence will improve.

🔥 Excellent 1-minute answer।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Problem: Lack of Time

> Lack of time is a common problem for students and professionals. One major reason is that people have many responsibilities. As a result, they may not have enough time for study, exercise, or relaxation.
>
> One possible solution is better time management. People can create a daily schedule and prioritize important tasks.
>
> In my opinion, planning is one of the best ways to deal with this problem because it helps people use their time more effectively.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Problem: Social Media

### Topic:

**People spend too much time on social media.**

Structure:

> Problem

↓

> Cause

↓

> Effect

↓

> Example

↓

> Solution

### Answer:

> Excessive social media use is a common problem today. One reason is that social media platforms are designed to keep users engaged for long periods. As a result, people may waste time and have difficulty concentrating on important tasks.
>
> For example, someone may open a social media app for five minutes but end up spending an hour scrolling.
>
> One possible solution is to set daily screen-time limits. People can also turn off unnecessary notifications.
>
> In my opinion, controlling how we use social media is better than completely avoiding it.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Problem: Traffic

### 2-minute answer:

> Traffic congestion is one of the biggest problems in large cities. There are several reasons for this. One major reason is the increasing number of private vehicles. Another reason is poor traffic management and limited public transportation.
>
> Traffic congestion causes many problems. People waste hours on the road, arrive late at work or school, and experience stress. It also increases fuel consumption and air pollution.
>
> There are several possible solutions. The government can improve public transportation, build better roads, and enforce traffic rules more effectively. People can also use buses or trains instead of private cars when possible.
>
> In my opinion, improving public transportation is one of the best solutions because it can reduce the number of vehicles on the road.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Problem: Pollution

Useful structure:

> Pollution is a serious environmental problem.

> One major cause is...

> Another cause is...

> As a result,...

> For example,...

> One possible solution is...

> The government should...

> Individuals can...

> In my opinion,...

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Problem: Unemployment

Possible causes:

> lack of skills

> lack of job opportunities

> economic problems

Possible effects:

> financial difficulties

> stress

> poverty

Solutions:

> skills development

> vocational training

> entrepreneurship

> job creation

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Problem: Stress

> Stress is a common problem for students and working people.

Causes:

> heavy workload

> lack of sleep

> financial pressure

> personal problems

Effects:

> poor concentration

> tiredness

> low motivation

Solutions:

> proper planning

> exercise

> enough sleep

> taking breaks

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Problem: Poor Sleep

> Poor sleep can affect people's daily lives.

Cause:

> using phones late at night

Effect:

> tiredness and poor concentration

Solution:

> avoid screens before sleeping

> maintain a regular sleep schedule

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Problem-Solution Connectors

আজকের important connectors:

### Problem

> One major problem is...

> One common issue is...

### Cause

> The main reason is...

> This happens because...

### Effect

> As a result,...

> Because of this,...

> This leads to...

### Example

> For example,...

> For instance,...

### Solution

> One possible solution is...

> We can solve this by...

### Opinion

> In my opinion,...

> I believe...

### Conclusion

> Overall,...

> In the long run,...

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “In the Long Run”

Meaning:

**দীর্ঘমেয়াদে**

Example:

> In the long run, better education can help reduce unemployment.

> In the long run, regular exercise can improve your health.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — “In the Short Term”

Meaning:

**স্বল্পমেয়াদে**

> In the short term, this solution may be expensive.

> In the long run, however, it could save money.

🔥 Problem-solution discussion-এ খুব useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Compare Solutions

সব solution equally good নয়।

বলবে:

> Solution A is cheaper, but Solution B may be more effective.

> The first solution is easier to implement, while the second may have a greater long-term impact.

এখানে **Lesson 70-এর comparison skill** ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Best Solution Formula

# **Solution → Benefit → Result**

Example:

> Improving public transportation could reduce the number of private cars. As a result, traffic congestion and pollution could decrease.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Balanced Answer

Problem নিয়ে শুধু government-কে blame করবে না।

বলবে:

> The government has a role to play, but individuals also have responsibilities.

Example:

> The government should improve public transportation, but individuals should also use public transport when possible.

🔥 এতে answer mature শোনায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Problem-Solution Conversation

**A:** What do you think is the biggest problem students face?

**B:** I think lack of time is one of the biggest problems.

**A:** Why do you think so?

**B:** Because students have classes, assignments, exams, and personal responsibilities.

**A:** How does it affect them?

**B:** It can cause stress and reduce their study time.

**A:** What can they do about it?

**B:** They can create a schedule and prioritize important tasks.

**A:** What is the best solution?

**B:** In my opinion, effective time management is probably the best solution.

🔥 Lesson 72 + Lesson 73 একসাথে ব্যবহার হচ্ছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — If You Don't Know the Solution

তখন বলবে:

> I'm not sure, but I think...

> One possible solution might be...

> I don't think there is a simple solution.

Example:

> I don't think there is a simple solution to unemployment. However, improving education and job training could help.

এতে answer থেমে যাবে না।

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — If There Are Many Solutions

> There isn't just one solution.

> I think we need a combination of solutions.

Example:

> There isn't just one solution to traffic congestion. We need better public transportation, better roads, and stronger enforcement of traffic rules.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — “Deal With”

খুব important phrase:

> **deal with a problem = সমস্যা মোকাবিলা করা**

Examples:

> We need to deal with this problem quickly.

> People need to learn how to deal with stress.

> The government needs to deal with traffic congestion.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — “Tackle”

**Tackle a problem = সমস্যা মোকাবিলা/সমাধানের চেষ্টা করা**

> We need to tackle pollution.

> The government should tackle unemployment.

এটি formal speaking-এ useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — “Prevent”

Problem হওয়ার আগেই:

> prevent the problem

Examples:

> We should prevent pollution instead of only dealing with its effects.

> Regular exercise can help prevent some health problems.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — “Reduce” vs “Solve”

খুব important:

### Reduce:

সমস্যা কমানো

> reduce traffic

> reduce pollution

> reduce stress

### Solve:

সমস্যা পুরোপুরি সমাধান করা

> solve a problem

সব problem completely **solve** করা সম্ভব নয়।

তাই বলো:

> reduce the problem

> minimize the impact

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — “Minimize the Impact”

Advanced phrase:

> We cannot completely eliminate the problem, but we can minimize its impact.

Example:

> We may not be able to eliminate traffic completely, but we can minimize its impact through better transportation systems.

🔥 Excellent speaking sentence।

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — 3-Minute Speaking Structure

যেকোনো problem:

### ① Introduction

> One major problem is...

### ② Cause

> There are several reasons...

### ③ Effect

> As a result...

### ④ Example

> For example...

### ⑤ Solution 1

> One possible solution...

### ⑥ Solution 2

> Another solution...

### ⑦ Best solution

> In my opinion...

### ⑧ Result

> This would...

### ⑨ Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# 🏆 Part 49 — 5-Minute Speaking Structure

আরও বড় answer:

> **Introduction**
>
> ↓
>
> **What the problem is**
>
> ↓
>
> **Why it happens**
>
> ↓
>
> **Who is affected**
>
> ↓
>
> **What happens as a result**
>
> ↓
>
> **Personal/example**
>
> ↓
>
> **Solution 1**
>
> ↓
>
> **Solution 2**
>
> ↓
>
> **Compare solutions**
>
> ↓
>
> **Best solution**
>
> ↓
>
> **Expected result**
>
> ↓
>
> **Conclusion**

---

# ⭐ Part 50 — 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **One major problem is...**
2. **One common issue is...**
3. **The main reason is...**
4. **This happens because...**
5. **There are several reasons for this.**
6. **One of the main causes is...**
7. **As a result,...**
8. **Because of this,...**
9. **This can lead to...**
10. **For example,...**
11. **In my experience,...**
12. **One possible solution is...**
13. **We can solve this by...**
14. **Another solution would be...**
15. **People should...**
16. **The government should...**
17. **In my opinion, the best solution is...**
18. **This would help...**
19. **In the long run,...**
20. **Overall, I believe...**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 73 Final Practice

## 🗣️ Topic 1 — **English Speaking Problem**

এই questions-এর উত্তর একসাথে দেবে:

1. What is your biggest problem when speaking English?
2. Why do you have this problem?
3. How does it affect you?
4. Can you give an example?
5. What are you doing to solve it?
6. What else could you do?
7. Which solution is best?
8. What result do you expect?

### Target:

**3 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — **Traffic Problem**

বলবে:

> What is the problem?

→

> What causes it?

→

> What are its effects?

→

> Give an example.

→

> What are two possible solutions?

→

> Which solution is best?

→

> What will happen if we solve it?

→

> Final opinion.

### Target:

**3–5 minutes**

---

# 🔥 Topic 3 — **Students Don't Have Enough Time**

Must use:

> **One major problem is...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **As a result...**

> **For example...**

> **One possible solution is...**

> **Another solution is...**

> **In my opinion...**

> **In the long run...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Today's Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে বেশি মনে রাখবে:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → EFFECT → EXAMPLE → SOLUTION → BEST SOLUTION → RESULT → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> **One major problem is lack of time.**
>
> **The main reason is** that people have too many responsibilities.
>
> **As a result,** they often feel stressed.
>
> **For example,** students may struggle to balance study and personal activities.
>
> **One possible solution is** better time management.
>
> **In my opinion,** creating a daily schedule is one of the best solutions.
>
> **This would help** people use their time more effectively.
>
> **Overall,** good planning can make the problem much easier to manage.

---

## 🏆 Lesson 73-এর Target

আজ থেকে কোনো problem নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় শুধু:

> ❌ “There is a problem.”

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What is the problem? → Why does it happen? → What happens because of it? → Can you give an example? → How can we solve it? → Which solution is best? → What will happen after solving it?**

এই flow-তে কথা বলবে।

**এটাই 2–5 মিনিটের problem-solving speaking skill।**


---

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 74

# 🗣️ Describing People & Personalities

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কোনো একজন মানুষকে শুধু দেখতে কেমন তা নয়, তার **personality, behavior, habits, strengths, weaknesses, relationship এবং personal opinion** সহ 2–5 মিনিট describe করতে।

আগের lessons-এর connection:

- **Lesson 65:** Storytelling → কোনো মানুষকে নিয়ে ঘটনা বলা
    
- **Lesson 70:** Comparison → দুইজনকে compare করা
    
- **Lesson 71:** Agree/Disagree → কারও behavior নিয়ে opinion দেওয়া
    
- **Lesson 72:** Follow-up Questions → মানুষ সম্পর্কে প্রশ্ন করা
    
- **Lesson 73:** Problem & Solution → কারও কোনো সমস্যা/দুর্বলতা explain করা
    
- **Lesson 74:** **একজন মানুষকে naturally describe করা**
    

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের main structure:

# **WHO → APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → HABITS → STRENGTHS → WEAKNESS → EXAMPLE → RELATIONSHIP → OPINION → CONCLUSION**

এই formula দিয়ে **friend, family member, teacher, colleague, classmate, role model**—যে কাউকে describe করতে পারবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Person Describe করার সময় কী বলবে?

কাউকে describe করার সময় সাধারণত:

### ① Who?

> Who is this person?

### ② Appearance

> What does he/she look like?

### ③ Personality

> What is he/she like?

### ④ Behavior

> How does he/she behave?

### ⑤ Strengths

> What is he/she good at?

### ⑥ Weakness

> What is difficult about him/her?

### ⑦ Example

> Can you give an example?

### ⑧ Relationship

> How do you know this person?

### ⑨ Opinion

> Why do you like/respect this person?

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “What Does He Look Like?”

**Look like = দেখতে কেমন**

Example:

> What does your friend look like?

Answer:

> He is tall and has short black hair. He usually wears casual clothes.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “What Is He Like?”

এটা খুব important।

**What is he like?**

মানে:

> তার personality কেমন?

Example:

> He is friendly, honest, and hardworking.

### Difference:

> **What does he look like?** → appearance

> **What is he like?** → personality

🔥 এই difference অবশ্যই বুঝবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Appearance Vocabulary

### Height

> tall

> short

> average height

### Build

> slim

> thin

> strong

> fit

### Hair

> short hair

> long hair

> straight hair

> curly hair

> black hair

### Face

> round face

> oval face

### Other

> glasses

> beard

> moustache

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Simple Appearance Sentences

> He is quite tall.

> She is of average height.

> He has short black hair.

> She has long hair.

> He wears glasses.

> She usually dresses casually.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Don't Focus Too Much on Appearance

Speaking answer-এ শুধু:

> He is tall.

> He has black hair.

শেষ করবে না।

Personality-তে চলে যাবে:

> **More importantly, he is very friendly and helpful.**

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Personality Vocabulary

### Positive

> friendly

> kind

> honest

> polite

> helpful

> patient

> confident

> hardworking

> responsible

> reliable

> generous

> creative

> calm

> cheerful

> intelligent

> ambitious

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Negative Personality

Carefully ব্যবহার করবে:

> impatient

> careless

> shy

> quiet

> stubborn

> disorganized

> sometimes lazy

> easily distracted

> short-tempered

> nervous

একজনকে insult না করে **soft language** ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “He Is” vs “He Seems”

### Direct:

> He is very confident.

### Softer:

> He seems very confident.

### Difference:

**is** → তুমি নিশ্চিত

**seems** → তোমার observation

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “He Tends To...”

Habitual behavior:

> He tends to speak calmly.

> She tends to arrive early.

> He tends to think carefully before making decisions.

**Tend to = সাধারণত এমন করে**

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “He Is the Kind of Person Who...”

Excellent speaking structure:

> He is the kind of person who always helps others.

> She is the kind of person who never gives up easily.

> He is the kind of person who thinks before he speaks.

🔥 এই structure অনেক natural।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — Describe Behavior

Personality + behavior:

> He is very helpful. Whenever someone has a problem, he tries to help.

> She is very responsible. She always finishes her work on time.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Give Evidence

শুধু adjective বলবে না।

### Weak:

> He is hardworking.

### Strong:

> He is hardworking. He usually spends several hours practicing his skills every day.

🔥 **Adjective + Evidence**

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “For Example”

Example দিয়ে personality prove করো:

> She is very responsible. For example, she always completes her assignments before the deadline.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Strengths

**Strength = শক্তি/ভালো দিক**

Useful:

> He is good at solving problems.

> She is good at communicating with people.

> He learns quickly.

> She works well under pressure.

> He is a good listener.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “One of His Strengths Is...”

Advanced:

> One of his biggest strengths is his patience.

> One of her strengths is communication.

> One of his strongest qualities is his ability to stay calm under pressure.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Weakness

Softly describe:

> One of his weaknesses is that he sometimes overthinks things.

> She can be a little shy around new people.

> He sometimes has difficulty managing his time.

Notice:

❌ He is terrible at everything.

Instead:

✅ He sometimes struggles with time management.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Struggle With”

Very useful:

> struggle with time management

> struggle with confidence

> struggle with communication

### Example:

> He sometimes struggles with time management, but he is working on it.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Balanced Description

একজন মানুষকে শুধু positive বলবে না।

### Better:

> He is hardworking and responsible, although he sometimes overthinks decisions.

এতে description natural হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Although”

> Although he is usually confident, he can be a little nervous in large groups.

> Although she is quiet, she is very friendly once you get to know her.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “But”

Simple:

> He is quiet, but he is very friendly.

> She is shy, but she communicates well with people she knows.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “However”

> He is very talented. However, he sometimes lacks confidence.

More formal।

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Relationship

কীভাবে তাকে চেনো:

> I met him at university.

> We have been friends for several years.

> She is one of my classmates.

> He is a close friend of mine.

> She is a member of my family.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “How Long Have You Known Him?”

Follow-up:

> How long have you known him?

Answer:

> I've known him for about five years.

### Since:

> I've known him since university.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “How Did You Meet?”

Past experience:

> We first met at university.

> We met through a mutual friend.

> I first met her when we were classmates.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Describe a Friend

## Example:

> I'd like to talk about one of my close friends. His name is Rahim, and we met at university several years ago.
> 
> He is of average height and has short black hair. He usually dresses casually.
> 
> More importantly, he is friendly, honest, and hardworking. He is also a very good listener. Whenever I have a problem, I can talk to him.
> 
> One of his biggest strengths is that he stays calm under pressure. For example, when we had an important university project, he helped the team stay organized.
> 
> Of course, he isn't perfect. He sometimes overthinks things and takes too much time to make decisions.
> 
> Overall, I really value his friendship because he is reliable and supportive.

🔥 এটি প্রায় **2 মিনিটের answer**।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Describe a Family Member

Structure:

> Who?

> Relationship?

> Appearance?

> Personality?

> Habits?

> Strength?

> Example?

> Why important?

Example:

> I'd like to describe my father. He is a very important person in my life. He is hardworking, responsible, and patient. He always encourages me to work hard and improve myself.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Describe a Teacher

Useful vocabulary:

> knowledgeable

> patient

> supportive

> organized

> encouraging

### Example:

> My teacher is very knowledgeable and patient. He explains difficult topics in a simple way. He also encourages students to ask questions.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Describe a Role Model

Role model = যার কাছ থেকে inspiration পাও।

Start:

> One person I really admire is...

Then:

> He/She is...

> What I admire most is...

> One quality I really respect is...

### Example:

> One person I really admire is my teacher. What I admire most is his patience and dedication.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “What I Admire Most”

Excellent phrase:

> What I admire most about him is his honesty.

> What I admire most about her is her determination.

> What I admire most is the way she treats other people.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “The Reason I Respect Him Is...”

> The reason I respect him is that he always keeps his promises.

> The reason I admire her is that she never gives up when things become difficult.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Person + Story

Lesson 65 connection।

Instead of only describing:

> He is helpful.

Tell a short story:

> I remember one time when I was having difficulty with a project. He stayed with me for several hours and helped me solve the problem.

Then:

> That experience showed me how supportive he is.

🔥 **Story → Personality evidence**

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Person + Comparison

Lesson 70 connection:

> Compared with most people I know, he is much more patient.

> Unlike me, he is very outgoing.

> While I prefer working alone, he enjoys working with a team.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Unlike Me”

Very useful:

> Unlike me, he is very outgoing.

> Unlike his brother, he is quite quiet.

> Unlike many people, she doesn't get stressed easily.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “While”

> While I am usually quiet, my friend is very talkative.

> While she is very organized, I tend to be more flexible.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Describe Personality Through Actions

Instead of:

> He is kind.

Say:

> He often helps people without expecting anything in return.

Instead of:

> She is responsible.

Say:

> She always completes her work on time.

🔥 Actions make your English more natural.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Useful Personality Patterns

### Pattern 1

> He is very + adjective.

### Pattern 2

> He tends to + verb.

### Pattern 3

> He is the kind of person who + verb.

### Pattern 4

> One of his strengths is...

### Pattern 5

> One thing I admire about him is...

### Pattern 6

> Although..., he...

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Strong Vocabulary

Instead of **good**:

> kind

> helpful

> reliable

> responsible

> supportive

Instead of **smart**:

> intelligent

> knowledgeable

> quick learner

Instead of **hardworking**:

> dedicated

> determined

> disciplined

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — “Reliable”

**Reliable = যার ওপর ভরসা করা যায়**

> He is very reliable.

> I can always depend on him.

Example:

> If he says he'll do something, he usually does it.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “Supportive”

**Supportive = সহযোগিতা/উৎসাহ দেয়**

> She is very supportive.

> She always encourages me when I face difficulties.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — “Determined”

**Determined = দৃঢ়প্রতিজ্ঞ**

> He is very determined.

> Once he sets a goal, he works hard to achieve it.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — “Open-Minded”

**Open-minded = নতুন idea গ্রহণ করতে আগ্রহী**

> She is very open-minded.

> She is always willing to listen to different opinions.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — “Easygoing”

**Easygoing = সহজ-স্বভাব / বেশি tension নেয় না**

> He is very easygoing.

> He doesn't get upset easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — “Outgoing”

**Outgoing = মিশুক**

> She is very outgoing and enjoys meeting new people.

Opposite:

> introverted / quiet

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — “Reserved”

**Reserved = কম কথা বলে / নিজের feelings সহজে প্রকাশ করে না**

> He is quite reserved around new people.

But:

> Once you get to know him, he becomes more talkative.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Avoid Repetition

❌

> He is good. He is good at work. He is good with people. He is good...

Use:

> hardworking

> friendly

> reliable

> supportive

> patient

> responsible

> talented

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Follow-up Questions

Lesson 72-এর মতো:

### Topic: A Friend

> How long have you known him?

> How did you meet?

> What does he look like?

> What is he like?

> What do you like about him?

> What is his strongest quality?

> Does he have any weaknesses?

> Can you give me an example?

> What do you usually do together?

> Why is he important to you?

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — 5-Minute Conversation

**A:** Tell me about one of your close friends.

**B:** I'd like to talk about one of my university friends.

**A:** How did you meet?

**B:** We met during our university studies.

**A:** What is he like?

**B:** He's friendly, hardworking, and very supportive.

**A:** What do you like most about him?

**B:** I like the fact that he's reliable. I can always depend on him.

**A:** Can you give me an example?

**B:** Sure. Once I had a problem with a project, and he spent several hours helping me.

**A:** Does he have any weaknesses?

**B:** Yes. He sometimes overthinks things.

**A:** Would you say you're similar?

**B:** In some ways, yes, but we're different in personality. He's more outgoing than I am.

🔥 এটাই today's target conversation।

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — Describe Someone You Don't Like

এখানে polite language ব্যবহার করবে।

❌:

> He is a terrible person.

Better:

> We have very different personalities.

> I sometimes find him difficult to communicate with.

> We don't always agree with each other.

> I don't think we are very compatible.

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — “Compatible”

**Compatible = একসাথে ভালোভাবে মানিয়ে যায়**

> We have similar personalities, so we get along well.

> We have very different personalities, so we don't always get along.

---

# 🧠 Part 51 — “Get Along”

Very important:

**get along with someone = কারও সাথে ভালো সম্পর্ক থাকা**

> I get along well with my classmates.

> I get along really well with my brother.

Negative:

> We don't always get along.

---

# 🔥 Part 52 — “Get to Know”

> I got to know him better after working together.

> She's quiet at first, but once you get to know her, she's very friendly.

---

# 🧠 Part 53 — Important Phrases

1. **I'd like to talk about...**
    
2. **He/She is...**
    
3. **He/She has...**
    
4. **He/She tends to...**
    
5. **He/She is the kind of person who...**
    
6. **One of his/her strengths is...**
    
7. **One weakness is...**
    
8. **What I admire most is...**
    
9. **The reason I respect him/her is...**
    
10. **For example,...**
    
11. **Unlike me,...**
    
12. **Compared with me,...**
    
13. **We get along well.**
    
14. **I've known him/her for...**
    
15. **We first met...**
    
16. **He/She can be a little...**
    
17. **Although..., ...**
    
18. **Overall,...**
    

---

# 🏆 Part 54 — Full 3-Minute Answer

## Topic:

# **Describe a Person You Admire**

> I'd like to talk about someone I really admire. He is one of the people who has had a positive influence on me.
> 
> He is quite calm and friendly, and he usually communicates with people respectfully. More importantly, he is hardworking, responsible, and determined.
> 
> One of his biggest strengths is his ability to stay calm when he faces difficulties. He doesn't give up easily. Instead, he tries to understand the problem and find a solution.
> 
> For example, I remember a time when he was facing a difficult situation. Instead of complaining, he made a plan and worked on the problem step by step. Eventually, he succeeded.
> 
> Of course, he isn't perfect. He sometimes works too much and doesn't give himself enough time to relax. However, I really respect his dedication.
> 
> What I admire most about him is his attitude toward difficult situations. He has taught me that problems can be solved if we stay patient and keep trying.
> 
> Overall, I consider him a great role model, and I hope I can develop some of the same qualities.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 74 Final Practice

## Topic 1 — **Describe Your Best Friend**

Answer these:

1. Who is your best friend?
    
2. How did you meet?
    
3. How long have you known him/her?
    
4. What does he/she look like?
    
5. What is he/she like?
    
6. What is his/her best quality?
    
7. What is one weakness?
    
8. Can you give an example?
    
9. What do you usually do together?
    
10. Why do you value the friendship?
    

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — **Describe a Person You Admire**

Use:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

> **More importantly...**

> **One of his/her strengths is...**

> **For example...**

> **Although...**

> **What I admire most is...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🔥 Topic 3 — **Compare Yourself With a Friend**

Must use:

> **Unlike me,...**

> **Compared with me,...**

> **While I..., he/she...**

> **We are similar because...**

> **The main difference is...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 Today's Golden Formula

আজ সবচেয়ে বেশি মনে রাখবে:

# **WHO → LOOKS → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → STRENGTH → WEAKNESS → EXAMPLE → RELATIONSHIP → OPINION → CONCLUSION**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about my close friend.
> 
> He is tall and has short black hair.
> 
> More importantly, he is friendly and hardworking.
> 
> He tends to stay calm in difficult situations.
> 
> One of his biggest strengths is his reliability.
> 
> He sometimes overthinks decisions.
> 
> For example, he once helped me with a difficult project.
> 
> I've known him for several years.
> 
> What I admire most is his supportive nature.
> 
> Overall, I'm lucky to have him as a friend.

---

## 🏆 Lesson 74-এর Target

আজ থেকে কাউকে describe করার সময় শুধু:

> ❌ **“He is good.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What is he like? → How does he behave? → What is he good at? → Give an example → What do you admire?**

এইভাবে কথা বলবে।

**Goal: একজন মানুষকে নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট naturally কথা বলা।**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 75

# 🗣️ Describing Places & Locations — কোনো জায়গা সম্পর্কে বিস্তারিত বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কোনো **place, city, village, tourist spot, restaurant, university, park, country** নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট continuously কথা বলতে।

আগের lessons-এর সাথে connection:

* **Lesson 65 — Storytelling:** কোনো জায়গায় ঘটে যাওয়া ঘটনা বলা
* **Lesson 70 — Comparison:** দুইটি জায়গা compare করা
* **Lesson 72 — Follow-up Questions:** জায়গা নিয়ে follow-up প্রশ্ন করা
* **Lesson 73 — Problem & Solution:** কোনো জায়গার সমস্যা ও সমাধান বলা
* **Lesson 74 — Describing People:** একজন মানুষকে describe করার structure
* **Lesson 75 — Describing Places:** এবার একইভাবে **একটি জায়গা describe করা**

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important structure:

# **WHERE → WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE → LOCATION → ATMOSPHERE → WHAT PEOPLE DO → SPECIAL FEATURES → GOOD THINGS → PROBLEMS → PERSONAL EXPERIENCE → OPINION → CONCLUSION**

এই formula দিয়ে যেকোনো জায়গা নিয়ে কথা বলতে পারবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Place Describe করার Basic Questions

কোনো জায়গা নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় নিজেকে প্রশ্ন করবে:

1. **Where is it?**
2. **What does it look like?**
3. **What is it like?**
4. **What can people do there?**
5. **What is special about it?**
6. **What do you like about it?**
7. **Are there any problems?**
8. **Have you been there?**
9. **What did you do there?**
10. **Would you recommend it?**

এই 10টা প্রশ্নের উত্তর দিতে পারলে সহজেই 3–5 মিনিট কথা বলা যাবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “Where Is It?”

Location বলার জন্য:

> It is located in...

> It is situated in...

> It is near...

> It is close to...

> It is about ... kilometers from...

### Examples:

> The place is located in Dhaka.

> It is near the city center.

> It is close to the university.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “Located”

**Located = অবস্থিত**

> My university is located in Dhaka.

> The restaurant is located near my home.

> Cox's Bazar is located in southeastern Bangladesh.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “Near / Close To”

দুটোই প্রায় একই।

> The park is near my house.

> The park is close to my house.

আরও:

> The hotel is close to the beach.

> The school is near the main road.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “What Does It Look Like?”

এখানে physical appearance describe করবে।

Useful words:

> big

> small

> modern

> traditional

> beautiful

> clean

> crowded

> spacious

> colorful

> quiet

> green

### Example:

> The park is quite large and green.

> The building is modern and spacious.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “What Is It Like?”

এখানে শুধু appearance নয়, পুরো experience describe করবে।

> What is the city like?

Answer:

> It's busy, crowded, and energetic.

> What is the village like?

> It's quiet, peaceful, and surrounded by nature.

---
