# 🧠 Part 7 — Appearance vs Atmosphere

খুব important difference:

### Appearance:

> The park is large and green.

### Atmosphere:

> The park has a peaceful atmosphere.

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশ / অনুভূতি**

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Atmosphere Vocabulary

### Positive:

> peaceful

> relaxing

> lively

> energetic

> friendly

> pleasant

> comfortable

### Negative:

> noisy

> crowded

> stressful

> polluted

> uncomfortable

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “The Atmosphere Is...”

Examples:

> The atmosphere is very peaceful.

> The atmosphere is lively in the evening.

> The atmosphere can be quite crowded on weekends.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Describe What People Do

Useful:

> People usually...

> Visitors can...

> You can...

Examples:

> People usually walk around the park.

> Visitors can enjoy the beautiful scenery.

> You can take photos and relax there.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “There Is / There Are”

খুব important structure।

### Singular:

> There is a lake.

> There is a large park.

### Plural:

> There are many restaurants.

> There are several shops nearby.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “It Has”

Use:

> It has...

Examples:

> The city has many restaurants.

> The park has a large lake.

> The university has a beautiful campus.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Special Features

**Special feature = বিশেষ বৈশিষ্ট্য**

Start:

> One of the most interesting things about this place is...

> What makes this place special is...

> The most attractive feature is...

### Example:

> What makes this place special is its natural beauty.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “What Makes It Special?”

Excellent speaking question।

Answer:

> What makes it special is its peaceful environment.

> What makes it special is the friendly people.

> What makes it special is its historical importance.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Give Reasons

Don't just say:

> It's beautiful.

Expand:

> It's beautiful **because it has a lot of greenery and open space.**

Structure:

> **It is + adjective + because + reason**

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — Personal Experience

এটা 2–5 মিনিটের speaking-এর জন্য খুব important।

Use:

> I have been there...

> I visited the place...

> I went there with...

> I remember...

### Example:

> I visited the place last year with some of my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Past Experience

Lesson 31-এর connection।

> I went there last year.

> I spent two days there.

> We stayed at a hotel.

> We visited several places.

> We took a lot of photos.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Describe What Happened

Short story:

> When I arrived, the first thing I noticed was the beautiful scenery.

> After that, we walked around the area.

> Later, we had lunch at a local restaurant.

> In the evening, we watched the sunset.

🔥 Place + Story = much stronger answer.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “The First Thing I Noticed...”

Excellent phrase:

> The first thing I noticed was the peaceful atmosphere.

> The first thing I noticed was how crowded the area was.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “What I Liked Most”

> What I liked most was the natural beauty.

> What I liked most was the peaceful environment.

> What I liked most was the friendly atmosphere.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “What I Didn't Like”

Balanced answer:

> One thing I didn't like was the traffic.

> The only thing I didn't really like was the crowded environment.

> Another downside was the lack of parking.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Advantages

Useful:

> One advantage is...

> One of the best things about it is...

> Another advantage is...

### Example:

> One advantage of living in the city is that everything is easily accessible.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Disadvantages

> One disadvantage is...

> One downside is...

> The main drawback is...

### Example:

> The main drawback is that the area can become very crowded.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Downside” and “Drawback”

দুটোর meaning প্রায় একই:

**Downside = অসুবিধার দিক**

**Drawback = অসুবিধা**

Examples:

> The main downside is the traffic.

> One drawback is the high cost of living.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Describe a City

Structure:

> Location

↓

> Size

↓

> Population

↓

> Atmosphere

↓

> Transportation

↓

> Places to visit

↓

> Advantages

↓

> Problems

↓

> Personal opinion

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Example: Dhaka

> I'd like to talk about Dhaka, the capital city of Bangladesh. It is a large and busy city with a huge population.
>
> Dhaka is known for its busy roads, shopping areas, restaurants, universities, and business centers. The city is usually lively and energetic, but it can also be very crowded and noisy.
>
> One of the main advantages of living in Dhaka is that many services and opportunities are available here. There are many educational institutions, hospitals, restaurants, and job opportunities.
>
> However, traffic congestion and pollution are major problems. People can spend a lot of time traveling from one place to another.
>
> Overall, I think Dhaka is a challenging but important city because it offers many opportunities.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Describe Your Village

Useful vocabulary:

> peaceful

> quiet

> green

> natural

> farmland

> ponds

> trees

> fresh air

> local people

Example:

> My village is a peaceful place surrounded by trees and farmland. It is much quieter than the city.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — City vs Village

Lesson 70 connection:

> Cities are usually busier than villages.

> Villages are generally quieter than cities.

> Cities offer more job opportunities, while villages often provide a more peaceful environment.

> Public transportation is usually more available in cities.

> Life in the village can be less stressful.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Useful Comparison Structures

### Compared with:

> Compared with Dhaka, my hometown is much quieter.

### Unlike:

> Unlike cities, villages usually have more open spaces.

### While:

> While cities offer more opportunities, villages offer a quieter lifestyle.

### On the other hand:

> Cities are convenient. On the other hand, they can be stressful.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Describe a Tourist Place

Use:

> It is famous for...

> It is known for...

> Tourists visit it because...

> The main attraction is...

### Example:

> Cox's Bazar is famous for its long natural sea beach.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Famous For” vs “Known For”

দুটোই similar।

> Cox's Bazar is famous for its beach.

> The area is known for its natural beauty.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “Popular With”

Meaning:

**কাদের মধ্যে popular**

> The park is popular with families.

> The restaurant is popular with young people.

> Cox's Bazar is popular with both local and international tourists.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Recommendation

কোনো place recommend করতে:

> I would definitely recommend it.

> I would highly recommend this place.

> It's worth visiting.

> If you get the chance, you should visit it.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Worth Visiting”

**Worth visiting = যাওয়ার মতো**

> This place is definitely worth visiting.

> I think the museum is worth visiting.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “If You Get the Chance...”

Natural speaking:

> If you get the chance, you should visit Cox's Bazar.

> If you get the chance, I'd recommend visiting the place in winter.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Place + Problem

Lesson 73 connection:

> The city has several problems.

> One major problem is traffic congestion.

> Another issue is pollution.

> These problems affect people's daily lives.

Then solution:

> Better public transportation could help solve the problem.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Place + Opinion

Don't finish suddenly।

Use:

> Personally, I think...

> In my opinion,...

> From my point of view,...

### Example:

> Personally, I think this is one of the most beautiful places in Bangladesh.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — “From My Point of View”

> From my point of view, the city is a great place for young professionals.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Place + Future

Lesson 78-এর preparation।

> I think this area will become more developed in the future.

> More tourists will probably visit this place.

> The government may improve the transportation system.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Full 2-Minute Place Description

## Topic: **A Place You Like**

> I'd like to talk about a place that I really like. It's a quiet park near my area.
>
> The park is quite large and has many trees, walking paths, and an open space. The atmosphere is peaceful, especially in the morning and evening.
>
> People usually go there to walk, exercise, relax, or spend time with their families. One of the things I like most about the park is the greenery.
>
> I usually visit it when I need some fresh air or want to take a break from work. I remember going there one evening with a friend. We walked around the park and talked for almost an hour.
>
> One downside is that it can become crowded in the evening. However, I still enjoy visiting it because it is a good place to relax.
>
> Overall, I think it's a simple but enjoyable place, and I would recommend it to anyone who wants a quiet break from a busy day.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Full 3-Minute Tourist Place Answer

## Topic: **Describe a Place You Have Visited**

> I'd like to talk about a place I visited a few years ago. It was a beautiful tourist destination in Bangladesh.
>
> The place was surrounded by natural scenery, and the atmosphere was very relaxing. There were many trees, open spaces, and beautiful views. The area was also quite popular with tourists.
>
> I went there with some of my friends. When we arrived, the first thing I noticed was the scenery. We spent most of the day exploring the area, taking pictures, and trying local food.
>
> What I liked most was the peaceful environment. It was very different from my normal daily routine.
>
> However, there were some disadvantages. The place was crowded during peak hours, and transportation was not always convenient.
>
> In my opinion, the place is still worth visiting because of its natural beauty and relaxing atmosphere. If I get the chance, I'd definitely like to go there again.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Useful Place Vocabulary

| Word/Phrase    | Meaning                    |
| -------------- | -------------------------- |
| location       | অবস্থান                    |
| area           | এলাকা                      |
| neighborhood   | আশেপাশের এলাকা             |
| atmosphere     | পরিবেশ                     |
| scenery        | প্রাকৃতিক দৃশ্য            |
| landscape      | ভূদৃশ্য                    |
| attraction     | আকর্ষণীয় স্থান             |
| landmark       | গুরুত্বপূর্ণ স্থান/নিদর্শন |
| crowded        | ভিড়পূর্ণ                   |
| peaceful       | শান্তিপূর্ণ                |
| lively         | প্রাণবন্ত                  |
| convenient     | সুবিধাজনক                  |
| accessible     | সহজে পৌঁছানো যায়           |
| nearby         | কাছাকাছি                   |
| surrounded by  | দ্বারা পরিবেষ্টিত          |
| famous for     | জন্য বিখ্যাত               |
| known for      | জন্য পরিচিত                |
| worth visiting | দেখার/যাওয়ার মতো           |
| downside       | অসুবিধার দিক               |
| drawback       | অসুবিধা                    |

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **It is located in...**
2. **It is near...**
3. **It is close to...**
4. **It is surrounded by...**
5. **The area is known for...**
6. **It is famous for...**
7. **The atmosphere is...**
8. **The first thing I noticed was...**
9. **People usually go there to...**
10. **One of the best things about it is...**
11. **What makes it special is...**
12. **What I liked most was...**
13. **One advantage is...**
14. **One downside is...**
15. **I visited the place...**
16. **I went there with...**
17. **I remember...**
18. **It is definitely worth visiting.**
19. **I would highly recommend it.**
20. **Overall, I think...**

---

# 🎯 Part 44 — Follow-up Questions

তুমি যদি কোনো place নিয়ে কথা বলো, অন্যজন জিজ্ঞেস করতে পারে:

> Where is it located?

> How far is it from your home?

> What does it look like?

> What is the atmosphere like?

> What can people do there?

> What is special about it?

> When did you visit?

> Who did you go with?

> What did you do there?

> What did you like most?

> Were there any problems?

> Would you visit again?

> Would you recommend it?

---

# 🔥 Part 45 — 5-Minute Speaking Formula

একটি place নিয়ে 5 মিনিট কথা বলতে:

### ① Introduction

> I'd like to talk about...

### ② Location

> It is located...

### ③ Appearance

> It looks...

### ④ Atmosphere

> The atmosphere is...

### ⑤ Activities

> People can...

### ⑥ Special feature

> What makes it special is...

### ⑦ Personal experience

> I visited it...

### ⑧ Good side

> What I liked most...

### ⑨ Bad side

> One downside...

### ⑩ Comparison

> Compared with...

### ⑪ Recommendation

> I would recommend...

### ⑫ Conclusion

> Overall,...

---

# 🏆 Lesson 75 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Describe Your City

Answer:

1. Where is your city?
2. What does it look like?
3. What is the atmosphere like?
4. What are people like?
5. What can people do there?
6. What is the best thing about the city?
7. What are the biggest problems?
8. How can those problems be solved?
9. Would you like to continue living there?
10. What do you think the city will be like in the future?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Describe a Place You Visited

Must use:

> **It is located...**

> **The first thing I noticed was...**

> **What I liked most was...**

> **One downside was...**

> **It is worth visiting because...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Compare City and Village

Must use:

> **Compared with...**

> **Unlike...**

> **While...**

> **On the other hand...**

> **In my opinion...**

---

# 🧠 Today's Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে বেশি মনে রাখবে:

# **WHERE → LOOKS → ATMOSPHERE → ACTIVITIES → SPECIAL → EXPERIENCE → GOOD → BAD → OPINION → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **I'd like to talk about a place I really like.**
>
> **It is located** near my area.
>
> **It looks** green and spacious.
>
> **The atmosphere is** peaceful.
>
> **People usually go there to** walk and relax.
>
> **What makes it special is** its natural beauty.
>
> **I visited it** with my friends.
>
> **What I liked most was** the quiet environment.
>
> **One downside is** that it becomes crowded in the evening.
>
> **In my opinion,** it is still worth visiting.
>
> **Overall,** it's a great place to relax.

## 🎯 Lesson 75-এর Target

আজ থেকে কোনো জায়গা নিয়ে শুধু:

> ❌ **“It is a beautiful place.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **Where is it? → What does it look like? → What is the atmosphere like? → What can people do there? → What is special? → What happened when you visited? → What are the advantages/disadvantages? → Would you recommend it?**

এই flow-তে **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলবে।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 76

# 🗣️ Telling Stories Naturally — গল্প বলা ও ঘটনা বিস্তারিতভাবে বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কোনো ঘটনা বা experience-কে শুধু 3–4টি sentence-এ শেষ না করে **2–5 মিনিট ধরে interesting ও naturally** বলতে।

আগের lessons-এর সাথে connection:

* **Lesson 65 — Storytelling:** past event নিয়ে কথা বলা
* **Lesson 70 — Comparison:** story-তে comparison ব্যবহার
* **Lesson 72 — Follow-up Questions:** story-র details expand করা
* **Lesson 73 — Problem & Solution:** story-তে problem → solution ব্যবহার
* **Lesson 74 — Describing People:** story-তে মানুষ describe করা
* **Lesson 75 — Describing Places:** story-তে location/place describe করা
* **Lesson 76:** সবগুলো skill একসাথে ব্যবহার করে **complete story বলা**

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের main structure:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → SITUATION → PROBLEM → WHAT HAPPENED → REACTION → SOLUTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

এটা মনে রাখলে যেকোনো personal story বলতে পারবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Story শুরু কীভাবে করবে?

Story শুরু করার জন্য:

> **I'd like to talk about a time when...**

> **I remember a time when...**

> **One experience I clearly remember is...**

> **Something interesting happened when...**

> **A few years ago,...**

### Examples:

> I'd like to talk about a time when I traveled with my friends.

> I remember a time when I had to make a difficult decision.

> One experience I clearly remember is my first day at university.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “A Few Years Ago”

Past story শুরু করার সবচেয়ে সহজ উপায়:

> A few years ago, I...

> Last year, I...

> When I was in school,...

> When I was at university,...

### Example:

> A few years ago, I went on a trip with some of my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — WHEN

Story-এর সময় বলবে:

> Last year...

> Last month...

> A few weeks ago...

> When I was a child...

> One evening...

> One morning...

> During my university life...

### Example:

> Last summer, I went to Cox's Bazar with my friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — WHERE

তারপর location:

> We went to...

> It happened at...

> We were staying in...

> The event took place in...

### Example:

> We stayed at a small hotel near the beach.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — WHO

কারা ছিল:

> I went with my friends.

> I was with my family.

> My brother was there.

> One of my classmates came with me.

### Example:

> I went there with three of my university friends.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Situation Setup

এখন background:

> At first,...

> Everything was going well.

> The day started normally.

> We were having a great time.

### Example:

> At first, everything was going perfectly. The weather was nice, and we were enjoying ourselves.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — তারপর Problem Introduce

Story interesting করার জন্য problem দরকার।

Use:

> Suddenly,...

> But then,...

> Unfortunately,...

> However,...

> Things changed when...

### Examples:

> Suddenly, it started raining.

> But then, we realized that we had lost our way.

> Unfortunately, my phone battery died.

🔥 এখান থেকে story interesting হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Suddenly”

**Suddenly = হঠাৎ**

> Suddenly, the lights went out.

> Suddenly, it started raining heavily.

> Suddenly, I realized that I had lost my wallet.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “Unfortunately”

খারাপ কিছু ঘটলে:

> Unfortunately, we missed the bus.

> Unfortunately, my phone stopped working.

> Unfortunately, the restaurant was closed.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “However”

Unexpected contrast:

> We were tired. However, we continued walking.

> It was raining. However, we decided to continue our trip.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Sequence Words

Story-তে events order করতে এগুলো অত্যন্ত important:

### Beginning

> First,...

> At first,...

### Middle

> Then,...

> After that,...

> A little later,...

> Meanwhile,...

### Ending

> Finally,...

> Eventually,...

> In the end,...

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — First → Then → After That → Finally

সবচেয়ে সহজ storytelling structure:

> First, we went to the beach.

> Then, we had lunch.

> After that, we visited a local market.

> Finally, we returned to the hotel.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Eventually”

**Eventually = শেষ পর্যন্ত**

> Eventually, we found the hotel.

> Eventually, we solved the problem.

> Eventually, everything worked out.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “In the End”

শেষ ফলাফল:

> In the end, everything was fine.

> In the end, we decided to go home.

> In the end, we had a great time.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “It Turned Out That...”

এটা খুব useful advanced phrase।

**It turned out that = পরে জানা গেল যে**

Example:

> We thought the restaurant was closed, but it turned out that it was open.

> I was worried about the exam, but it turned out that it wasn't very difficult.

🔥 Storytelling-এ খুব natural।

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “I Realized That...”

নিজের realization:

> I realized that I had forgotten my wallet.

> I realized that we were going in the wrong direction.

> At that moment, I realized how important preparation was.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “At That Moment”

Specific moment:

> At that moment, I felt really nervous.

> At that moment, I didn't know what to do.

> At that moment, I realized we had a problem.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Feelings

Storytelling-এ feelings অবশ্যই বলবে।

Useful:

> happy

> excited

> nervous

> scared

> worried

> confused

> surprised

> disappointed

> embarrassed

> relieved

> proud

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “I Felt...”

> I felt really nervous.

> I felt excited.

> I felt disappointed.

> I felt relieved.

### Example:

> When I realized that I had lost my phone, I felt really worried.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “I Was...”

> I was nervous.

> I was confused.

> I was surprised.

> I was really excited.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “I Couldn't Believe...”

Surprise:

> I couldn't believe what had happened.

> I couldn't believe that we had actually won.

> I couldn't believe how quickly everything changed.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Describe Other People's Reactions

Lesson 74 connection।

> My friend was worried.

> My brother looked surprised.

> Everyone started laughing.

> My parents were relieved.

Example:

> My friend looked really worried, but he tried to stay calm.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Problem + Reaction

Excellent pattern:

> **Problem → Reaction**

Example:

> We realized that we had lost our way. We became worried because it was getting dark.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Problem + Solution

Lesson 73 connection:

> We had a problem, so we decided to...

> To solve the problem, we...

> We tried to...

> Finally, we...

Example:

> We realized that we were lost, so we checked the map on my friend's phone.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “We Decided To...”

Decision:

> We decided to ask someone for help.

> We decided to return to the hotel.

> We decided to wait for the rain to stop.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “We Tried To...”

Attempt:

> We tried to find the station.

> We tried to call a taxi.

> We tried to fix the problem ourselves.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “We Managed To...”

**Managed to = শেষ পর্যন্ত পেরেছিলাম**

> We managed to find the hotel.

> I managed to finish the project on time.

> We managed to catch the last bus.

🔥 Very useful speaking phrase.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “We Failed To...”

যখন চেষ্টা করেও হয়নি:

> We failed to find a taxi.

> I failed to complete the task on time.

তবে conversational English-এ অনেক সময়:

> We couldn't...

বেশি natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Luckily”

Good luck:

> Luckily, we found someone who could help us.

> Luckily, I had a backup copy.

> Luckily, nobody was hurt.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “Unfortunately”

Bad luck:

> Unfortunately, we missed the train.

> Unfortunately, my phone battery died.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Fortunately”

**Fortunately = সৌভাগ্যক্রমে**

> Fortunately, we arrived before the shop closed.

> Fortunately, my friend had some cash.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Add Small Details

Short:

> We went to a restaurant.

Better:

> We went to a small restaurant near the beach. It was crowded, but the food was really good.

🔥 Details story-কে বড় করে।

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Describe the Place in a Story

Lesson 75:

> The restaurant was small but comfortable. It had a nice view of the beach.

এতে story আরও visual হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Describe People in a Story

Lesson 74:

> One of my friends is usually very calm, so he immediately started looking for a solution.

এখানে person description + story একসাথে।

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Story + Problem

Example:

> We were having a great time, but suddenly my friend realized that he had lost his wallet.

এখন listener জানতে চাইবে:

**What happened next?**

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — “What Happened Next?”

Storytelling-এর সবচেয়ে useful question।

নিজে নিজের story-তে transition হিসেবে:

> So, what happened next?

তারপর:

> We started looking for it.

অথবা listener জিজ্ঞেস করলে:

> What happened next?

> Well, we decided to go back to the restaurant.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — “After A While”

সময় পার হলে:

> After a while, we found the wallet.

> After a while, the rain stopped.

> After a while, everyone became relaxed.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — “A Few Minutes Later”

> A few minutes later, a police officer arrived.

> A few minutes later, we found the correct road.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — “Meanwhile”

একই সময়ে অন্য কিছু:

> Meanwhile, my friend was talking to the hotel manager.

> Meanwhile, I was checking the map.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Story Ending

Story suddenly end করবে না।

Use:

> Finally,...

> Eventually,...

> In the end,...

> After that,...

Then:

> Overall, it was...

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Feelings at the End

Ending-এর আগে feeling:

> I felt really relieved.

> I was very happy.

> I felt proud of myself.

> I was exhausted but satisfied.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Lesson Learned

Story-এর সবচেয়ে important part:

> **I learned that...**

> **The experience taught me that...**

> **It taught me the importance of...**

### Examples:

> I learned that preparation is very important.

> The experience taught me that staying calm is important.

> It taught me the importance of teamwork.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — “If I Could Do It Again...”

Advanced reflection:

> If I could do it again, I would prepare better.

> If I could do it again, I would leave earlier.

> If I could do it again, I wouldn't make the same mistake.

🔥 Storytelling-এর ending আরও strong হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — “Looking Back”

Reflection:

> Looking back, I think it was a valuable experience.

> Looking back, I realize that I learned a lot from that experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Complete Story Structure

মনে রাখো:

### ① When?

> Last year...

### ② Where?

> I went to...

### ③ Who?

> I went with...

### ④ Situation

> At first...

### ⑤ Problem

> Suddenly...

### ⑥ Reaction

> I felt...

### ⑦ Action

> We decided to...

### ⑧ Result

> Eventually...

### ⑨ Feeling

> I felt...

### ⑩ Lesson

> I learned that...

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Full Story Example

## 🎤 Topic: **A Time You Got Lost**

> I'd like to talk about a time when I got lost. It happened a few years ago when I was traveling with some of my friends.
>
> We were visiting a place that none of us knew very well. At first, everything was going perfectly. We were enjoying the scenery and taking pictures.
>
> After a while, we decided to explore a different area. Unfortunately, we took the wrong road. After walking for about thirty minutes, I realized that we were completely lost.
>
> At that moment, we became a little worried because it was getting dark. We tried to use Google Maps, but the internet connection was not very good.
>
> Luckily, we found a local person who knew the area. We explained our situation, and he showed us the correct direction.
>
> Eventually, we managed to find our way back. I felt really relieved, and we all laughed about the situation later.
>
> Looking back, it was actually a memorable experience. I learned that it's always a good idea to check the route before exploring an unfamiliar place. If I could do it again, I would definitely prepare better.

🔥 এই answer-এ **Place + People + Problem + Solution + Feeling + Lesson** সব আছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Another Story

## Topic: **A Difficult Decision**

> I remember a time when I had to make a difficult decision about my studies.
>
> At that time, I had several options, and I wasn't sure which one was best for me. I talked to some people I trusted and thought carefully about my goals.
>
> At first, I felt confused and nervous because I was afraid of making the wrong choice.
>
> Eventually, I decided to choose the option that was more closely connected to my long-term goals.
>
> It wasn't an easy decision, but in the end, I felt satisfied with my choice.
>
> The experience taught me that difficult decisions require patience and careful thinking.

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Another Story

## Topic: **A Successful Project**

> I'd like to talk about a project that I worked on with some classmates.
>
> At first, we were excited, but we soon realized that the project was more difficult than we expected. We had problems dividing the work and managing our time.
>
> Instead of giving up, we discussed the problems and created a clear plan. Everyone took responsibility for a specific task.
>
> After that, our progress became much better. Eventually, we completed the project and received positive feedback.
>
> I felt really proud because we had overcome several problems together.
>
> The experience taught me the importance of teamwork, communication, and planning.

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — Useful Story Connectors

| Connector      | Use              |
| -------------- | ---------------- |
| First          | শুরু             |
| At first       | শুরুতে           |
| Then           | তারপর            |
| After that     | এরপর             |
| A little later | কিছুক্ষণ পরে     |
| Meanwhile      | একই সময়ে         |
| Suddenly       | হঠাৎ             |
| Unfortunately  | দুর্ভাগ্যবশত     |
| Luckily        | ভাগ্যক্রমে       |
| Fortunately    | সৌভাগ্যক্রমে     |
| Eventually     | শেষ পর্যন্ত      |
| Finally        | অবশেষে           |
| In the end     | শেষ পর্যন্ত      |
| Looking back   | পিছনে ফিরে দেখলে |

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — 20 Must-Learn Sentences

1. **I'd like to talk about a time when...**
2. **I remember a time when...**
3. **A few years ago,...**
4. **At first,...**
5. **Everything was going well.**
6. **But then,...**
7. **Suddenly,...**
8. **Unfortunately,...**
9. **At that moment,...**
10. **I realized that...**
11. **We decided to...**
12. **We tried to...**
13. **Luckily,...**
14. **Fortunately,...**
15. **After a while,...**
16. **Eventually,...**
17. **In the end,...**
18. **I felt...**
19. **I learned that...**
20. **Looking back,...**

---

# 🎯 Part 51 — Follow-up Questions

তুমি story বলার পর listener জিজ্ঞেস করতে পারে:

> When did it happen?

> Where did it happen?

> Who were you with?

> What happened first?

> What happened next?

> How did you feel?

> What did you do?

> How did you solve the problem?

> What happened in the end?

> What did you learn?

> Would you do anything differently?

---

# 🧠 Part 52 — If You Forget a Word

Story থামিয়ে দেবে না।

Use:

> **What I mean is...**

> **In other words,...**

> **It's something that...**

> **I don't remember the exact word, but...**

Example:

> I don't remember the exact word, but it was a place where we could stay overnight.

🔥 Fluency-এর জন্য খুব important।

---

# 🔥 Part 53 — If You Forget a Detail

Don't panic:

> I don't remember exactly, but...

> As far as I remember,...

> I think it was...

Example:

> I don't remember exactly, but I think it happened in 2023.

---

# 🧠 Part 54 — Storytelling Without Memorization

পুরো story মুখস্থ করবে না।

শুধু **keywords** মনে রাখবে:

> Trip → Friends → Beach → Lost → No Internet → Local Person → Found Way → Relieved → Lesson

তারপর নিজের ভাষায় বলবে।

🔥 এতে speaking natural হবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 76 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — **A Memorable Day**

Answer:

1. When was it?
2. Where were you?
3. Who were you with?
4. What happened first?
5. What happened next?
6. Did any problem happen?
7. How did you react?
8. How did you solve it?
9. How did you feel?
10. What did you learn?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — **A Time You Made a Mistake**

Must use:

> **At first...**

> **Unfortunately...**

> **I realized that...**

> **I decided to...**

> **Eventually...**

> **Looking back...**

> **I learned that...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — **A Happy Memory**

Must use:

> **I remember a time when...**

> **At first...**

> **Then...**

> **After that...**

> **In the end...**

> **I felt...**

> **The experience taught me...**

---

# 🧠 Today's Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে বেশি মনে রাখবে:

# **WHEN → WHERE → WHO → SITUATION → PROBLEM → REACTION → ACTION → RESULT → FEELING → LESSON**

### Example:

> **A few years ago**, I went on a trip **with my friends**.
>
> **At first**, everything was going well.
>
> **Suddenly**, we realized that we were lost.
>
> **At that moment**, we became worried.
>
> **We decided to** ask a local person for help.
>
> **Luckily**, he showed us the correct direction.
>
> **Eventually**, we found our way back.
>
> **I felt** really relieved.
>
> **Looking back, I learned that** preparation is very important.

---

## 🏆 Lesson 76-এর Target

আজ থেকে story বলতে গেলে শুধু:

> ❌ “I went there. It was good. I came back.”

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **When? → Where? → Who? → What happened? → What went wrong? → How did you react? → What did you do? → What happened in the end? → How did you feel? → What did you learn?**

এই flow-তে **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলবে।

**Lesson 76-এর মূল লক্ষ্য: ঘটনা বলা নয়—ঘটনাকে story বানিয়ে বলা।**


---

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 77

# 🗣️ Explaining Reasons & Causes

### কারণ, কেন হয়েছে, কেন করেছ — এগুলো English-এ naturally বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে শুধু **“I did it”** না বলে **“Why did I do it?”** এবং **“Why did it happen?”** বিস্তারিতভাবে explain করতে।

এটা খুব important, কারণ conversation দীর্ঘ করতে হলে **reason + explanation** দিতে জানতে হয়।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের formula:

# **STATEMENT → REASON → EXPLANATION → EXAMPLE → RESULT → OPINION**

Example:

> I want to improve my English **because I need it for my career.**
> I communicate with people online and English is often necessary.
> **For example**, I need to read technical documentation in English.
> **As a result**, improving my English will help me professionally.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — “Because”

সবচেয়ে basic কারণ দেওয়ার word:

> **because = কারণ**

### Example:

> I study English because I want to improve my communication skills.

> I stayed home because I was tired.

> I chose this course because it was useful for me.

### Formula:

> **Action + because + reason**

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “Why?”

কারণ জানতে:

> Why did you go there?

> Why do you study English?

> Why did you choose this university?

> Why do you like this place?

### Answer:

> Because...

Example:

**Why do you study English?**

> Because I want to communicate more confidently.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Don't Stop After “Because”

Weak:

> I study English because I need it.

Better:

> I study English because I need it for my career. I often need to read English documentation and communicate with other people.

🔥 **Reason → Explanation**

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “Because of”

এটা খুব important।

### Because + sentence

> I stayed home because **I was sick.**

### Because of + noun

> I stayed home because of **the rain.**

### Remember:

> **because + subject + verb**

> **because of + noun**

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Examples

### Because:

> We cancelled the trip because it was raining.

### Because of:

> We cancelled the trip because of the rain.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “Due to”

**Due to = কারণে**

এটা একটু formal।

> The flight was delayed due to bad weather.

> The meeting was cancelled due to technical problems.

Speaking-এ **because of** বেশি সহজ ও natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Since”

**Since = যেহেতু**

> Since I was tired, I decided to stay home.

> Since the weather was bad, we cancelled the trip.

এখানে **since** কারণ introduce করছে।

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “As”

আরেকটি কারণ দেওয়ার word:

> As I was busy, I couldn't attend the meeting.

> As it was getting late, we decided to leave.

**As** একটু formal।

Daily conversation-এ:

> because

সবচেয়ে safe।

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “That's Why”

**That's why = এজন্যই**

খুব natural spoken English।

> I was tired. That's why I went home early.

> I needed money. That's why I started working part-time.

### Formula:

> **Reason. That's why + result.**

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Because vs That's Why

### Because:

> I went home early because I was tired.

### That's why:

> I was tired. That's why I went home early.

দুটো একই idea।

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “The Main Reason Is...”

Long speaking-এর জন্য excellent।

> The main reason is that...

Example:

> The main reason I study English is that I want to communicate confidently.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “One Reason Is...”

একাধিক reason দিলে:

> One reason is that...

> Another reason is that...

Example:

> One reason is that English is important for my career. Another reason is that I enjoy learning languages.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Give Multiple Reasons

আজকের important structure:

# **One reason → Another reason → For example → That's why**

Example:

> I like working from home for several reasons. One reason is that it saves time. Another reason is that I can focus better. For example, I don't have to spend hours commuting. That's why I prefer working from home.

🔥 এই structure ব্যবহার করলে answer সহজেই বড় হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “The Main Reason Why...”

> The main reason why I chose computer science is that I enjoy solving problems.

> The main reason why I moved to Dhaka was for better opportunities.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “That's Because...”

Conversation-এ:

**A:** Why do you like programming?

**B:** I enjoy it. **That's because** I like solving problems.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “This Is Because...”

একটু formal:

> I prefer online learning. This is because I can study at my own pace.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Cause and Effect

আজকের সবচেয়ে important concept:

# **CAUSE → EFFECT**

### Cause:

> It rained heavily.

### Effect:

> The roads became flooded.

Full:

> It rained heavily, so the roads became flooded.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “So”

**So = তাই**

> I was tired, so I went home.

> It was raining, so we cancelled the trip.

> I was hungry, so I ordered some food.

### Formula:

> **Cause + so + result**

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Because vs So

### Because:

> I went home because I was tired.

**Why did you go home?**

→ because I was tired.

### So:

> I was tired, so I went home.

**What happened because you were tired?**

→ I went home.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Therefore”

**Therefore = অতএব / তাই**

More formal:

> The road was blocked. Therefore, we had to take another route.

Daily speaking-এ **so** বেশি natural।

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “As a Result”

**As a result = ফলস্বরূপ**

> He didn't prepare well. As a result, he performed poorly in the exam.

> The company invested in technology. As a result, productivity increased.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “As a Result” vs “That's Why”

### That's why:

More conversational:

> I was sick. That's why I didn't go.

### As a result:

More formal:

> I was sick. As a result, I couldn't attend the class.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Because of This”

Natural:

> I didn't sleep well. Because of this, I felt tired the next day.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “This Led To...”

**Led to = এর ফলে ঘটেছে**

> Heavy traffic led to delays.

> Poor planning led to several problems.

> Regular practice led to better performance.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Resulted In”

**Resulted in = ফলস্বরূপ হয়েছে**

> The mistake resulted in a delay.

> The decision resulted in better performance.

### Difference:

> **led to**

> **resulted in**

দুটো প্রায় একই।

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “Caused”

Simple:

> The heavy rain caused flooding.

> The mistake caused a problem.

> Lack of sleep caused me to feel tired.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “Due to”

Formal cause:

> The match was cancelled due to heavy rain.

> The project was delayed due to technical problems.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Explain Personal Decisions

Question:

> Why did you choose computer science?

Answer structure:

> **Main reason → Another reason → Example → Future result**

Example:

> The main reason I chose computer science is that I enjoy technology and problem-solving. Another reason is that the field offers many career opportunities. For example, I can work in software development, AI, or web development. That's why I think it was a good choice for me.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Why Do You Learn English?

Try this:

> I am learning English because I want to communicate more confidently. Another reason is that English is important for my career. For example, many technical resources and job opportunities require English. As a result, improving my English can help me professionally.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Why Do You Like Technology?

> I like technology because it makes many tasks easier. Another reason is that technology gives people access to information very quickly. For example, we can learn almost anything online. That's why I think technology is an important part of modern life.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Why Do You Exercise?

> I exercise because I want to stay healthy and active. Another reason is that exercise helps me reduce stress. For example, even a short walk can make me feel more relaxed. That's why I try to exercise regularly.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Why Do People Use Social Media?

Possible reasons:

> communication

> entertainment

> news

> business

> education

Example:

> People use social media for several reasons. One reason is communication. Another reason is entertainment. Many people also use it for business and education.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Positive Cause & Effect

> Regular practice improves your skills.

> Exercise improves your health.

> Good planning saves time.

> Reading increases knowledge.

> Communication builds relationships.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Negative Cause & Effect

> Lack of sleep causes tiredness.

> Poor planning leads to mistakes.

> Too much screen time can affect your health.

> Heavy traffic causes delays.

> Lack of preparation can result in poor performance.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — “Can Lead To”

Important because not every cause guarantees a result।

> Too much stress can lead to health problems.

> Poor communication can lead to misunderstandings.

> Regular practice can lead to better results.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — “May Result In”

Possibility:

> Lack of sleep may result in poor concentration.

> Poor planning may result in unnecessary costs.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — “One Possible Reason Is...”

যখন নিশ্চিত না:

> One possible reason is that people don't have enough time.

> One possible reason is the lack of awareness.

🔥 এটা খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — “It Could Be Because...”

Conversation-এ speculation:

> Why is he late?

> It could be because of the traffic.

> Why did the computer stop working?

> It could be because of a software problem.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — “Maybe”

Simple possibility:

> Maybe he was busy.

> Maybe the traffic was heavy.

> Maybe she forgot about the meeting.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — “Probably”

More confident:

> He was probably stuck in traffic.

> The delay was probably caused by technical problems.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Reason + Example

এটা আজকের সবচেয়ে important speaking technique।

### Basic:

> I like online learning because it is convenient.

### Better:

> I like online learning because it is convenient. For example, I can study from home without spending time traveling.

### Excellent:

> I like online learning because it is convenient. For example, I can study from home and choose my own schedule. As a result, I can manage my time more effectively.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Reason + Example + Result

Memorize this:

> **I think X because Y. For example, Z. As a result, A.**

Example:

> I think exercise is important because it improves both physical and mental health. For example, walking regularly can reduce stress. As a result, people may feel more energetic and focused.

🔥 এই structure দিয়ে 1 minute থেকে 3 minutes করা সহজ।

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — “The Reason Is Not Just...”

Advanced:

> The reason is not just that it's convenient. It's also because it saves time.

Example:

> I prefer online shopping. The reason is not just that it's convenient. It's also because I can compare different products easily.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — “Not Only... But Also...”

Excellent speaking structure:

> Technology is not only convenient, but it is also efficient.

> Exercise not only improves physical health, but it also reduces stress.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Multiple Causes

একটি result-এর অনেক কারণ থাকতে পারে।

> There are several reasons for this.

> One reason is...

> Another reason is...

> A third reason is...

Example:

> There are several reasons why people move to cities. One reason is employment. Another reason is education. A third reason is better healthcare.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Multiple Effects

একটি cause-এর অনেক result:

> Technology has several effects.

> It saves time.

> It improves communication.

> It gives people access to information.

> However, it can also create problems.

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Balanced Answer

একদিকে:

> Technology saves time.

অন্যদিকে:

> However, too much technology can reduce face-to-face communication.

Then conclusion:

> So, I think technology is beneficial when we use it wisely.

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Full 2-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### Why is English important for your career?

> I think English is very important for my career for several reasons.
>
> The main reason is that English is widely used in the technology industry. Many technical documents, tutorials, and programming resources are written in English.
>
> Another reason is communication. If I work with international clients or colleagues, I may need to communicate with them in English.
>
> For example, when I work with a new technology, I often need to read documentation or watch tutorials in English. If my English skills are good, I can understand the information more easily.
>
> As a result, better English skills can help me learn faster and communicate more confidently.
>
> That's why I believe improving my English is an important part of my professional development.

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — Full 3-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### Why do people move from villages to cities?

> There are several reasons why people move from villages to cities.
>
> The main reason is employment. Cities usually have more companies, businesses, factories, and other job opportunities. As a result, people may have a better chance of finding work.
>
> Another reason is education. Cities often have more universities, colleges, training centers, and educational institutions. Students may move to cities because they want access to better educational opportunities.
>
> Healthcare is another important reason. Large cities usually have more hospitals and specialized medical services.
>
> However, moving to a city also has some disadvantages. Cities can be crowded, expensive, and stressful. Traffic congestion and pollution can also affect people's quality of life.
>
> So, I think people move to cities mainly because of better opportunities, but they also have to deal with several challenges.

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — Common Mistakes

### ❌ Mistake 1

> I like it because.

Incomplete।

### ✅

> I like it because it is convenient.

---

### ❌ Mistake 2

> Because of I was tired...

Wrong।

### ✅

> Because I was tired...

OR

> Because of my tiredness...

---

### ❌ Mistake 3

> I was tired because I went home.

Meaning changes।

### Correct:

> I went home because I was tired.

---

### ❌ Mistake 4

> Because of it was raining...

Wrong।

### Correct:

> Because it was raining...

OR

> Because of the rain...

---

# 🧠 Part 51 — Quick Grammar Rule

### **because + clause**

> because **I was tired**

> because **it was raining**

> because **he was busy**

---

### **because of + noun**

> because of **the rain**

> because of **traffic**

> because of **his illness**

---

### **so + result**

> I was tired, **so I went home.**

---

### **due to + noun**

> The flight was delayed **due to bad weather.**

---

# 🔥 Part 52 — Today's Speaking Connectors

আজ এগুলো actively ব্যবহার করবে:

> because

> because of

> that's why

> one reason is

> another reason is

> the main reason is

> for example

> so

> as a result

> therefore

> due to

> this led to

> resulted in

> however

> that's because

---

# 🏆 Lesson 77 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Why Are You Learning English?

Must use:

> **The main reason is...**

> **Another reason is...**

> **For example...**

> **As a result...**

> **That's why...**

🎯 **Target: 2–3 minutes**

---

## 🎤 Topic 2 — Why Do People Use Smartphones?

Try to include:

> because

> one reason

> another reason

> for example

> as a result

> however

> overall

🎯 **Target: 3 minutes**

---

## 🎤 Topic 3 — Why Do People Move to Cities?

Must use:

> **One reason is...**

> **Another reason is...**

> **For example...**

> **However...**

> **As a result...**

> **In my opinion...**

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 77 Golden Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **STATEMENT → WHY → REASON → EXAMPLE → RESULT → OPINION**

Example:

> **I am learning English.**
>
> **The main reason is** that I need it for my career.
>
> **Another reason is** that I want to communicate confidently.
>
> **For example**, I often need to read English technical resources.
>
> **As a result**, better English can help me learn and work more effectively.
>
> **That's why** I think English is important for my future.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 77-এর Target

আজ থেকে কোনো প্রশ্নের উত্তর শুধু:

> ❌ **“Yes, because it's good.”**

এভাবে শেষ করবে না।

বরং:

> **WHY? → REASON → EXAMPLE → RESULT → OPINION**

এই flow-তে কথা বলবে।

**Lesson 76-এ তুমি “What happened?” explain করেছ।
Lesson 77-এ তুমি “Why did it happen?” এবং “Why do you think so?” explain করবে।**

এভাবে conversation অনেক সহজে **2–5 মিনিট** পর্যন্ত চালিয়ে যেতে পারবে।



---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 78

# 🗣️ Talking About the Future

### ভবিষ্যৎ পরিকল্পনা, লক্ষ্য, আশা, prediction ও possibility নিয়ে কথা বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে future নিয়ে **2–5 মিনিট naturally** কথা বলতে—তোমার plan, goal, dream, prediction, possibility এবং future changes নিয়ে।

আগের lessons-এর সাথে connection:

* **Lesson 76 — Storytelling:** Past → কী ঘটেছিল
* **Lesson 77 — Reasons & Causes:** Why → কেন
* **Lesson 78 — Future:** **What will happen? What are you going to do? What do you hope to achieve?**

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

Future topic-এ এই structure ব্যবহার করবে:

# **CURRENT SITUATION → PLAN → REASON → STEPS → POSSIBILITY → PREDICTION → GOAL → EXPECTATION → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I have several goals for the future. First, I want to improve my English because it is important for my career. I'm going to practice speaking every day. I also hope to develop my technical skills. If I stay consistent, I think I will achieve these goals.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Future-এর জন্য 5টি Main Structure

আজ সবচেয়ে important:

### ① **will**

Prediction / spontaneous decision

> I will improve my English.

### ② **be going to**

Already-made plan

> I am going to practice English tonight.

### ③ **Present Continuous**

Fixed arrangement

> I am meeting my friend tomorrow.

### ④ **Present Simple**

Schedule/timetable

> The class starts at 10 tomorrow.

### ⑤ **might / may**

Possibility

> I might travel next month.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — WILL

**Will = ভবিষ্যতে হবে/করব**

### Prediction:

> I think it will rain tomorrow.

> Technology will become more advanced.

### Promise:

> I will help you.

### Decision এখনই:

> I'm thirsty. I'll get some water.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “I Think... Will”

Prediction-এর সবচেয়ে সহজ structure:

> I think + subject + will + verb.

Examples:

> I think technology will improve.

> I think the city will become more crowded.

> I think English will become even more important.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Negative WILL

> will not = won't

Examples:

> I won't give up.

> I don't think it will be easy.

> This problem won't disappear quickly.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — BE GOING TO

**Going to = আগে থেকেই plan/intention**

> I am going to learn Python.

> I am going to practice English tonight.

> We are going to visit Cox's Bazar next month.

### Formula:

> **Subject + am/is/are + going to + verb**

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — WILL vs GOING TO

এটা খুব ভালোভাবে বুঝবে।

### Plan আগে থেকেই করা:

> I am going to study tonight.

### সিদ্ধান্ত এখন:

> I'm tired. I'll rest for a while.

---

### Prediction:

> I think Bangladesh will develop further.

### Evidence-based prediction:

> Look at those clouds. It's going to rain.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Future Plan

Question:

> What are you going to do next year?

Answer:

> I'm going to improve my technical skills. I'm also going to work on some personal projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “I Plan To...”

Very useful।

> I plan to improve my English.

> I plan to learn a new technology.

> I plan to travel more.

**Plan to = পরিকল্পনা আছে**

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “I Hope To...”

**Hope = আশা করা**

> I hope to get a good job.

> I hope to become a better programmer.

> I hope to travel abroad someday.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “I Would Like To...”

Polite/soft future desire:

> I would like to work for an international company.

> I would like to visit Japan.

> I would like to improve my communication skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “I Want To...”

Simple:

> I want to become a successful developer.

> I want to improve my English.

But for natural variety:

> I plan to...

> I hope to...

> I'd like to...

ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “My Goal Is To...”

Very useful for career/study:

> My goal is to become a skilled software developer.

> My goal is to improve my communication skills.

> My main goal is to build a successful career.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “My Long-Term Goal”

Long-term:

> My long-term goal is to become an expert in my field.

> My long-term goal is to build a successful career in technology.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Short-Term vs Long-Term

### Short-term goal:

> My short-term goal is to improve my English.

### Long-term goal:

> My long-term goal is to build a successful career.

### Excellent answer:

> In the short term, I want to improve my skills. In the long term, I hope to become an expert in my field.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “In the Near Future”

Near future:

> In the near future, I want to improve my speaking skills.

> In the near future, I'm going to focus on my career.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Someday”

Long-term dream:

> Someday, I would like to travel around Europe.

> Someday, I hope to start my own company.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “Eventually”

Long-term result:

> Eventually, I want to become a senior developer.

> Eventually, I hope to build my own product.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Future Steps

শুধু goal বলবে না।

### Weak:

> I want to improve my English.

### Strong:

> I want to improve my English, so I'm going to practice speaking every day, learn new vocabulary, and listen to English conversations.

🔥 **Goal → Action Plan**

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “I'm Planning To...”

> I'm planning to take an English course.

> I'm planning to work on a new project.

> I'm planning to travel next year.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “I'm Thinking About...”

এখানে plan এখনও fixed না।

> I'm thinking about learning another programming language.

> I'm thinking about traveling abroad.

> I'm thinking about changing my daily routine.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “I Might...”

**Might = হতে পারে**

> I might travel next month.

> I might take another course.

> I might move to another city.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “I May...”

Similar:

> I may apply for a new job.

> I may travel this year.

**May** একটু formal; speaking-এ **might** খুব common।

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Probability

Future possibility describe:

### Very likely:

> I'm sure...

> I'm certain...

### Likely:

> I think...

> I expect...

### Possible:

> I might...

> It's possible that...

### Uncertain:

> I'm not sure...

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “I Expect...”

> I expect things will improve.

> I expect the job market will become more competitive.

> I expect technology to change rapidly.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “I'm Looking Forward To...”

Very useful phrase:

**Looking forward to = আগ্রহ নিয়ে অপেক্ষা করা**

> I'm looking forward to starting my new job.

> I'm looking forward to meeting my friends.

⚠️ এরপর **verb + ing**

❌ I look forward to meet.

✅ I look forward to meeting.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “I Can't Wait To...”

Informal and enthusiastic:

> I can't wait to start my new project.

> I can't wait to visit the place.

> I can't wait to see my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Future Prediction

Question:

> What will technology be like in the future?

Answer:

> I think technology will become more advanced. Artificial intelligence will probably become more common in everyday life.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “Probably”

Prediction:

> People will probably use AI more frequently.

> Cities will probably become more crowded.

> Remote work will probably become more common.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Likely To”

Advanced:

> People are likely to use more AI tools in the future.

> Cities are likely to become more crowded.

**likely to = সম্ভাবনা বেশি**

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “Unlikely To”

কম সম্ভাবনা:

> It is unlikely to happen soon.

> People are unlikely to stop using smartphones completely.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “There Is a Chance...”

Possibility:

> There is a chance that I will travel next year.

> There is a good chance that technology will change this industry.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “If...”

Future condition:

> If I practice every day, I will improve.

> If I get a good job, I will save money.

> If I have enough time, I'll travel.

### Formula:

> **If + present simple, will + verb**

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Important Mistake

❌

> If I will get time, I will study.

✅

> If I get time, I will study.

After **if**, normally use present simple for this first conditional pattern.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “When...”

Certain future:

> When I finish my work, I'll call you.

> When I get home, I'll study.

Again:

❌ When I will get home...

✅ When I get home...

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Future + Reason

Lesson 77 connection:

> I'm going to improve my English because it will help me communicate better.

> I plan to exercise regularly because I want to stay healthy.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Future + Steps

Excellent speaking structure:

> My goal is to improve my English. To achieve this, I'm going to practice speaking every day. I'm also going to read more and listen to English conversations.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — “To Achieve This...”

Excellent phrase:

> To achieve this, I need to practice consistently.

> To achieve this goal, I'm going to study every day.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — “In Order To”

Purpose:

> I'm learning English in order to communicate confidently.

> I'm saving money in order to buy a laptop.

More conversational:

> I'm learning English to communicate confidently.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — “By + ing”

Method:

> I can improve my English by practicing every day.

> I can stay healthy by exercising regularly.

> I can improve my skills by working on real projects.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Future Career

Question:

> What are your career goals?

Answer:

> My short-term goal is to find a good job and gain practical experience. I'm also planning to improve my technical and communication skills. In the long term, I hope to become a highly skilled professional in my field.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Future Study

> I plan to continue learning new technologies.

> I hope to take advanced courses.

> I might pursue higher education in the future.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Future Travel

> I'd like to visit several countries in the future.

> I hope to travel abroad someday.

> I'm planning to visit some new places next year.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Future Lifestyle

Question:

> What kind of life do you want in the future?

Answer:

> I want to have a balanced lifestyle. I hope to have a successful career, spend enough time with my family, and maintain a healthy lifestyle.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Future Technology

> I think AI will become more powerful.

> People will probably use AI in education, healthcare, and business.

> Some jobs may change because of automation.

> However, I don't think technology will replace every human job.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Balanced Prediction

Avoid absolute statements.

Instead of:

❌ Technology will definitely replace all jobs.

Say:

> Technology may change many jobs, but I think people will still be needed for creativity, communication, and decision-making.

🔥 Much better speaking answer।

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Full 2-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### What are your plans for the future?

> I have several plans for the future. In the short term, my main goal is to improve my English and develop my professional skills.
>
> I'm going to practice English regularly, especially speaking and listening. I'm also planning to work on practical projects so that I can gain more experience.
>
> In the next few years, I hope to build a successful career in my field. I would like to work in a professional environment where I can learn from experienced people.
>
> In the long term, I'd like to become highly skilled in my profession. I may also pursue higher education or learn new technologies depending on my career goals.
>
> I know it won't always be easy, but I believe that if I stay consistent and keep learning, I will gradually achieve my goals.

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Full 3-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### What will your city be like in the future?

> I think my city will change significantly in the future. As the population grows, the city will probably become more crowded.
>
> I expect technology to play a bigger role in transportation, healthcare, education, and other services. Public transportation may also become more efficient.
>
> However, there could be some challenges. Traffic congestion and pollution may become more serious if the city doesn't develop properly.
>
> I think better planning will be necessary. If the government invests in public transportation and creates more green spaces, the quality of life could improve.
>
> Overall, I believe the city has a lot of potential. If it is developed in a planned way, it could become a more modern and comfortable place to live.

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Future Conversation

**A:** What are your plans for next year?

**B:** I'm planning to focus on my career and improve my skills.

**A:** What skills do you want to improve?

**B:** Mainly my communication and technical skills.

**A:** How are you going to improve them?

**B:** I'm going to practice regularly and work on practical projects.

**A:** Do you have any long-term goals?

**B:** Yes. I hope to become highly skilled in my field.

**A:** Do you think you'll achieve that?

**B:** I think so. If I stay consistent, I believe I'll make good progress.

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — 20 Must-Learn Future Phrases

1. **I will...**
2. **I'm going to...**
3. **I plan to...**
4. **I'm planning to...**
5. **I hope to...**
6. **I'd like to...**
7. **I want to...**
8. **My goal is to...**
9. **My short-term goal is...**
10. **My long-term goal is...**
11. **I'm thinking about...**
12. **I might...**
13. **I may...**
14. **I expect...**
15. **I think... will...**
16. **I'm looking forward to...**
17. **There is a chance that...**
18. **I'm likely to...**
19. **To achieve this...**
20. **If I..., I will...**

---

# 🎯 Part 50 — Future Time Expressions

| Phrase                  | Meaning         |
| ----------------------- | --------------- |
| tomorrow                | আগামীকাল        |
| next week               | আগামী সপ্তাহ    |
| next month              | আগামী মাস       |
| next year               | আগামী বছর       |
| soon                    | শীঘ্রই          |
| later                   | পরে             |
| in the future           | ভবিষ্যতে        |
| in the near future      | নিকট ভবিষ্যতে   |
| someday                 | কোনো একদিন      |
| eventually              | শেষ পর্যন্ত     |
| in the long term        | দীর্ঘমেয়াদে     |
| in the short term       | স্বল্পমেয়াদে    |
| over the next few years | আগামী কয়েক বছরে |

---

# 🔥 Part 51 — Common Mistakes

### ❌ Mistake 1

> I will going to study.

Wrong।

### ✅

> I will study.

OR

> I'm going to study.

---

### ❌ Mistake 2

> I am plan to study.

Wrong।

### ✅

> I plan to study.

OR

> I'm planning to study.

---

### ❌ Mistake 3

> I hope I will can do it.

Wrong।

### ✅

> I hope I can do it.

OR

> I hope I will be able to do it.

---

### ❌ Mistake 4

> I am looking forward to meet you.

Wrong।

### ✅

> I'm looking forward to meeting you.

---

# 🧠 Part 52 — Future Speaking Trick

কোনো future question-এর উত্তর ছোট হলে এই 5টি প্রশ্ন নিজেকে করো:

### 1. What?

> What do I want to do?

### 2. Why?

> Why do I want to do it?

### 3. How?

> How am I going to do it?

### 4. When?

> When am I going to do it?

### 5. What if?

> What might happen?

এগুলো answer করলেই 2–5 মিনিট হয়ে যাবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 78 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — **Your Future Career**

Answer:

1. What is your career goal?
2. Why do you want it?
3. What are you going to do to achieve it?
4. What skills do you need?
5. What will you do in the next year?
6. What is your long-term goal?
7. What problems might you face?
8. How will you overcome them?
9. Where do you see yourself in five years?
10. What do you hope to achieve?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — **Your Future City**

Must use:

> **I think... will...**

> **probably**

> **may/might**

> **If..., ... will...**

> **However...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — **Your Future Plans**

Must use:

> **In the short term...**

> **In the long term...**

> **I'm going to...**

> **I plan to...**

> **I hope to...**

> **To achieve this...**

> **If I stay consistent...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 78 Golden Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **CURRENT → PLAN → WHY → HOW → POSSIBILITY → PREDICTION → GOAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **My main goal is to improve my English.**
>
> **I'm going to** practice speaking every day.
>
> **The reason is** that English is important for my career.
>
> **To achieve this**, I'm going to read, listen, and speak regularly.
>
> **I might** also join an English-speaking group.
>
> **I think** my communication skills will improve if I stay consistent.
>
> **In the long term**, I hope to communicate confidently in professional situations.
>
> **Overall**, I believe regular practice will help me achieve this goal.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 78-এর Target

আজ থেকে future question শুনলে শুধু:

> ❌ “I will get a job.”

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What? → Why? → How? → When? → What might happen? → What is the long-term goal?**

এইভাবে **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলবে।

### 🔥 Lesson 76 → **What happened?**

### 🔥 Lesson 77 → **Why did it happen?**

### 🔥 Lesson 78 → **What will happen / What are you going to do?**

এই তিনটি skill একসাথে পারলে তোমার **past + reason + future conversation** অনেক বেশি fluent হবে।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 79

# 🗣️ Agreeing, Disagreeing & Giving Your Opinion

### একমত হওয়া, দ্বিমত হওয়া এবং নিজের মতামত বিস্তারিতভাবে বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ তুমি শিখবে কীভাবে কারও কথার সাথে **agree / disagree** করে তারপর **reason + example + explanation** দিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট conversation চালিয়ে যেতে হয়।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের মূল structure:

> **Opinion → Agree/Disagree → Reason → Example → Other Side → Conclusion**

Example:

> I agree that technology makes our lives easier. The main reason is that it saves time. For example, we can communicate with people instantly. However, technology also has some disadvantages. So, I think it is beneficial if we use it wisely.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Agree মানে কী?

**Agree = একমত হওয়া**

কেউ বলল:

> Technology is very useful.

তুমি বলতে পারো:

> I agree.

কিন্তু শুধু **“I agree”** বলে থেমে যাবে না।

Better:

> I agree. Technology makes many parts of our lives easier.

Best:

> I completely agree. Technology saves time, improves communication, and gives us easy access to information.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Strong Agreement

এগুলো শিখে রাখো:

> **I completely agree.**

> **I totally agree.**

> **I strongly agree.**

> **I couldn't agree more.**

> **That's exactly what I think.**

> **I feel the same way.**

### Example:

**A:** Exercise is important for everyone.

**B:** I completely agree. Regular exercise improves both physical and mental health.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Soft Agreement

সবসময় 100% agree করতে হবে না।

> **I agree to some extent.**

> **I agree with you to some degree.**

> **I think you're right in some ways.**

> **That's a good point.**

### Example:

> I agree to some extent, but I think there are some exceptions.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “That's a Good Point”

Conversation-এর জন্য extremely useful।

কেউ ভালো argument দিলে:

> That's a good point.

তারপর নিজের কথা:

> That's a good point. However, I think there is another side to the issue.

🔥 এতে conversation naturally continue হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Disagree

**Disagree = দ্বিমত হওয়া**

Basic:

> I disagree.

কিন্তু এটা একটু direct।

Better:

> I don't completely agree.

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

> I see your point, but I think differently.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Polite Disagreement

সবচেয়ে useful:

> **I see your point, but...**

Example:

> I see your point, but I think technology can also create new opportunities.

---

> **I understand what you mean, but...**

> I understand what you mean, but I don't think it is always true.

---

> **That's true, but...**

> That's true, but there are some disadvantages as well.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Never Say Only “You're Wrong”

❌

> You're wrong.

এটা অনেক direct।

Better:

> I don't think that's always true.

আরও polite:

> I see it differently.

> I'm not sure I agree with that.

> I understand your point, but I have a different opinion.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “In My Opinion”

নিজের মতামত:

> In my opinion,...

> In my view,...

> From my point of view,...

> Personally, I think...

> As far as I'm concerned,...

সবচেয়ে সহজ:

> **In my opinion...**

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Opinion + Reason

শুধু opinion নয়।

### Weak:

> I think online learning is good.

### Better:

> I think online learning is good because it is flexible.

### Strong:

> I think online learning is useful because it is flexible and convenient. For example, students can study from home and choose a suitable time.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “I Believe”

> I believe technology has improved our lives.

> I believe education should be accessible to everyone.

> I believe regular practice is the key to improvement.

**I believe** একটু stronger opinion।

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Personally”

Natural conversational English:

> Personally, I prefer working from home.

> Personally, I think social media has both advantages and disadvantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “From My Point of View”

> From my point of view, public transportation should be improved.

> From my point of view, students should learn practical skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Give Two Reasons

Use:

> **One reason is...**

> **Another reason is...**

Example:

> I prefer online learning. One reason is that it is flexible. Another reason is that it gives me access to many resources.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Add Example

> For example,...

> For instance,...

Example:

> Online learning is convenient. For example, I can watch a lesson whenever I have free time.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Add Contrast

Opinion strong করতে:

> However,...

> On the other hand,...

> At the same time,...

Example:

> Online learning is convenient. However, it requires self-discipline.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “On the Other Hand”

একদিকে:

> Online learning is convenient.

অন্যদিকে:

> On the other hand, students may miss face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Balanced Opinion

এটা আজকের খুব important skill।

Don't say:

> Technology is completely good.

Instead:

> Technology has many benefits, but it also has some disadvantages.

Then explain both sides।

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Agree + Other Side

Excellent structure:

> I agree that technology is useful. However, I think we should also consider its negative effects.

এতে তুমি mature speaker-এর মতো শোনাবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “It Depends”

সব question-এর answer Yes/No নয়।

> **It depends.**

Example:

**A:** Is social media good?

**B:**

> It depends. It can be useful for communication and education, but excessive use can be harmful.

🔥 Very natural।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “It Depends On...”

> It depends on how people use it.

> It depends on the situation.

> It depends on the person.

> It depends on how much time you spend on it.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “To Some Extent”

মানে: কিছুটা / নির্দিষ্ট মাত্রায়।

> I agree to some extent.

> Technology is helpful to some extent.

Example:

> I agree to some extent, but I think we need to consider the disadvantages too.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “Not Necessarily”

মানে **অবশ্যই এমন নয়**।

**A:** Rich people are always happy.

**B:**

> Not necessarily. Money can make life easier, but happiness depends on many other things.

Very useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “I'm Not Convinced”

যখন অন্যের argument তোমাকে convince করেনি:

> I'm not completely convinced by that argument.

Simpler:

> I'm not sure that's true.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “I See What You Mean”

Polite response:

> I see what you mean.

তারপর:

> However, I think there is another way to look at it.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “You Have a Point”

Meaning:

**তোমার কথায় যুক্তি আছে।**

> You have a point, but I still think...

Example:

> You have a point, but I think the advantages are greater than the disadvantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Change Your Opinion

Conversation-এ নতুন information পেলে:

> **You may be right.**

> **I hadn't thought about it that way.**

> **That's an interesting point.**

> **You may have changed my mind.**

🔥 এগুলো advanced conversational phrases।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “I Hadn't Thought About It That Way”

Excellent natural phrase।

> That's interesting. I hadn't thought about it that way.

মানে:

**আমি এভাবে আগে ভাবিনি।**

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Agree After Discussion

> After hearing your point, I think I agree with you.

> You've convinced me.

> I think you have a point.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Disagree With Reason

Formula:

# **I disagree + because + reason**

Example:

> I disagree because not everyone has equal access to technology.

Better:

> I don't completely agree because access to technology is still limited for some people.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Disagree Politely

Question:

> Should students use smartphones in class?

Answer:

> I understand why some people support it, but I don't completely agree. Smartphones can be useful for research, but they can also distract students.

🔥 এখানে:

**Acknowledgement → Disagreement → Reason → Contrast**

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Although”

Contrast:

> Although technology is useful, it can also create problems.

> Although online learning is convenient, it requires discipline.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “Even Though”

> Even though social media is useful, excessive use can be harmful.

> Even though online classes are convenient, some students prefer traditional classes.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — “While”

> While technology saves time, it can also reduce face-to-face communication.

> While cities offer more opportunities, they can also be stressful.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Agree/Disagree Conversation

### A:

> I think working from home is better than working in an office.

### B:

> I see your point, and I agree to some extent. Working from home saves commuting time. However, I think offices can be better for teamwork and communication.

🔥 Perfect balanced response।

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Topic: Social Media

### Question:

> Is social media good or bad?

### Answer:

> In my opinion, social media has both advantages and disadvantages. I agree that it is useful because it helps people communicate and share information quickly. For example, people can stay connected with friends and family even when they live far away.
>
> However, excessive use can waste time and affect mental well-being. So, I think social media is useful if people use it responsibly.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Topic: Online Education

> I think online education is very useful. One reason is flexibility. Another reason is accessibility. Students can learn from almost anywhere.
>
> However, I don't think online education can completely replace traditional education. Some students need face-to-face interaction and direct guidance.
>
> So, personally, I think a combination of online and traditional education would be better.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Topic: Technology

### Question:

> Is technology making us lazy?

Answer:

> I agree to some extent. Technology makes many tasks easier, so people don't have to do certain things manually. For example, we can order food online instead of going to a restaurant.
>
> However, technology doesn't necessarily make everyone lazy. It can also help people work more efficiently.
>
> So, I think the problem is not technology itself. It depends on how people use it.

🔥 Excellent answer structure।

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Topic: Money & Happiness

### Question:

> Can money make people happy?

Answer:

> I don't completely agree that money can make people happy. Money can certainly make life more comfortable because it provides access to food, housing, healthcare, and other necessities.
>
> However, I don't think money guarantees happiness. Relationships, health, personal satisfaction, and peace of mind are also important.
>
> So, in my opinion, money can contribute to happiness, but it isn't the only factor.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Strong Speaking Structure

আজ এই structure practice করবে:

### **1. Opinion**

> In my opinion,...

### **2. Agreement**

> I agree to some extent.

### **3. Reason**

> The main reason is...

### **4. Example**

> For example,...

### **5. Other side**

> However,...

### **6. Conclusion**

> So, I think...

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — 2-Minute Answer Template

যেকোনো opinion question-এ:

> **In my opinion, [opinion].**
>
> **I think this because [reason].**
>
> **Another reason is [reason].**
>
> **For example, [example].**
>
> **However, [opposite point].**
>
> **On the other hand, [other side].**
>
> **So, overall, I believe [final opinion].**

এটা মুখস্থ না করে **structure** হিসেবে ব্যবহার করবে।

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Useful Agreement Expressions

### Strong:

> I completely agree.

> I totally agree.

> I strongly agree.

> I couldn't agree more.

### Medium:

> I agree with you.

> That's a good point.

> I think you're right.

### Partial:

> I agree to some extent.

> You have a point.

> I agree in some ways.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Useful Disagreement Expressions

### Soft:

> I'm not sure I agree.

> I don't completely agree.

> I see your point, but...

> I understand what you mean, but...

### Stronger:

> I disagree.

> I don't think that's true.

### Very polite:

> That's an interesting perspective, but I see it differently.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Don't Repeat “I Think”

Weak:

> I think technology is good. I think it saves time. I think it helps people.

Better:

> In my opinion, technology is useful. It saves time and improves communication. Personally, I believe it has made many aspects of life easier.

Use different expressions।

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Replace “Good”

Instead of always saying **good**:

> useful

> beneficial

> helpful

> effective

> convenient

> valuable

> practical

Example:

❌ Technology is good.

✅ Technology is **useful and convenient**.

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Replace “Bad”

Instead of always **bad**:

> harmful

> negative

> problematic

> dangerous

> ineffective

Example:

❌ Social media is bad.

✅ Excessive social media use can have **negative effects**.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 79 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Is Technology Making Us Lazy?

Use:

> I agree to some extent.

> The main reason is...

> For example...

> However...

> On the other hand...

> Overall...

🎯 **Target: 3 minutes**

---

## 🎤 Topic 2 — Is Online Education Better Than Traditional Education?

Answer:

1. What is your opinion?
2. Why?
3. Give two reasons.
4. Give an example.
5. What is the opposite argument?
6. Do you agree with it?
7. What is your final opinion?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

## 🎤 Topic 3 — Is Money More Important Than Time?

Must use:

> **In my opinion...**

> **I agree to some extent...**

> **However...**

> **For example...**

> **On the other hand...**

> **So, overall...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 79 Golden Formula

আজকের সবচেয়ে important formula:

# **OPINION → AGREE/DISAGREE → REASON → EXAMPLE → CONTRAST → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **In my opinion**, technology is very useful.
>
> **I agree to some extent** that it can make people lazy.
>
> **The main reason is** that technology makes many tasks easier.
>
> **For example**, people can order almost anything online.
>
> **However**, technology can also increase productivity.
>
> **On the other hand**, excessive dependence on technology can be harmful.
>
> **So, overall**, I think technology is beneficial if we use it wisely.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 79-এর Target

আজ থেকে কেউ কোনো opinion দিলে শুধু:

> ❌ **“Yes, I agree.”**

বা

> ❌ **“No, I don't agree.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **I agree/disagree → Why? → Example → Other side → Final opinion**

এই pattern-এ কথা বলবে।

### 🔥 Lesson 76

**Story → What happened?**

### 🔥 Lesson 77

**Reason → Why?**
