### 🔥 Lesson 78

**Future → What will happen?**

### 🔥 Lesson 79

**Opinion → What do you think?**

এই চারটি skill একসাথে ব্যবহার করতে পারলে **2–5 মিনিট conversation** অনেক বেশি naturally চালাতে পারবে।


---

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 80

# 🗣️ Comparing Two Things & Making Choices

### দুইটি জিনিস compare করা + নিজের choice explain করা

**Main Goal:** আজ শিখবে কীভাবে দুটি মানুষ, জায়গা, technology, lifestyle, job, study method ইত্যাদি **compare** করে নিজের পছন্দের কারণ বলতে হয়।

এটা Lesson 61–79-এর skills একসাথে ব্যবহার করবে:

* **Opinion** → তুমি কী মনে করো?
* **Reason** → কেন?
* **Example** → উদাহরণ
* **Contrast** → অন্যদিকে কী?
* **Choice** → কোনটা বেছে নেবে?
* **Conclusion** → শেষ মতামত

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের মূল structure:

# **A → B → SIMILARITY → DIFFERENCE → ADVANTAGE → DISADVANTAGE → CHOICE → REASON → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> Both online and traditional education are useful. However, online education is more flexible, while traditional education provides more face-to-face interaction. Personally, I prefer online learning because it is convenient and allows me to study at my own pace.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Compare মানে কী?

**Compare = তুলনা করা**

Example:

> Compare online learning and traditional learning.

মানে:

**Online learning আর traditional learning-এর মধ্যে তুলনা করো।**

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “Both”

**Both = দুটোই**

> Both are useful.

> Both methods have advantages.

> Both cities are beautiful.

### Example:

> Both online and traditional education can be effective.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “Both...and...”

দুটি জিনিস একসাথে বলার জন্য:

> Both **A and B** are useful.

> Both **reading and listening** are important for learning English.

> Both **cities and villages** have advantages.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “Similar”

**Similar = একই রকম**

> They are quite similar.

> Both methods are similar in some ways.

> These two systems are very similar.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “Different”

**Different = ভিন্ন**

> They are quite different.

> There are several differences between them.

> The main difference is...

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “The Main Difference Is...”

Excellent phrase:

> The main difference is that online learning is more flexible.

Example:

> Both methods are useful, but the main difference is that online learning allows students to study from anywhere.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “While”

Comparison-এর সবচেয়ে useful word।

> **While A is..., B is...**

Example:

> While online learning is flexible, traditional learning offers more face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “Whereas”

**Whereas = অন্যদিকে / যেখানে**

Slightly formal:

> Online learning is flexible, whereas traditional learning is more interactive.

> City life is fast-paced, whereas village life is usually quieter.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “Compared With”

> Compared with traditional learning, online learning is more flexible.

> Compared with city life, village life is more peaceful.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “In Comparison”

> In comparison, traditional classes provide more direct interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “On the Other Hand”

A-এর advantage বলার পর B:

> Online learning is convenient. On the other hand, traditional classes provide face-to-face communication.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “More... Than...”

Basic comparison:

> Online learning is more flexible than traditional learning.

> City life is more expensive than village life.

> This phone is more powerful than my old phone.

### Formula:

> **A + is + more + adjective + than + B**

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Less... Than...”

> City life is less peaceful than village life.

> This method is less convenient than the other one.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Faster Than”

> A car is faster than a bicycle.

> Online communication is faster than traditional mail.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “Better Than”

> I think online learning is better than traditional learning for some students.

> This method is better than the previous one.

⚠️ **Good → better**

❌ more good

✅ better

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Worse Than”

> Traffic in big cities is worse than in small towns.

> This method is worse than the previous one.

⚠️ **Bad → worse**

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “As...As”

দুটি জিনিস সমান হলে:

> Online learning is as effective as traditional learning.

> This phone is as expensive as that one.

### Negative:

> This method isn't as convenient as the other one.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Not As...As”

> City life isn't as peaceful as village life.

> My old laptop isn't as fast as my new laptop.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “The Same As”

> They are almost the same.

> This phone is similar to mine.

> The two systems are almost the same.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Different From”

> This method is different from the traditional method.

> City life is very different from village life.

⚠️ Say:

> different **from**

Not:

> different than

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Superlative

যখন 3 বা তার বেশি জিনিসের মধ্যে best/worst:

> the best

> the most useful

> the easiest

> the most convenient

Examples:

> This is the best option for me.

> English is one of the most useful languages for my career.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Choice

Comparison-এর শেষে choice দিতে হবে।

Use:

> **I prefer...**

> **I'd rather...**

> **I would choose...**

> **My preference is...**

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “I Prefer”

> I prefer online learning.

> I prefer working from home.

> I prefer city life.

### Compare:

> I prefer online learning to traditional learning.

Formula:

> **prefer A to B**

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Important Mistake

❌

> I prefer online learning than traditional learning.

✅

> I prefer online learning **to** traditional learning.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “I'd Rather”

**I'd rather = আমি বরং...**

> I'd rather work from home.

> I'd rather study in the morning.

> I'd rather travel by train.

### Compare:

> I'd rather travel by train than by bus.

Formula:

> **would rather A than B**

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Prefer vs Would Rather

### Prefer:

> I prefer tea to coffee.

### Would rather:

> I'd rather drink tea than coffee.

দুটোই correct।

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “I Would Choose”

Decision:

> I would choose the first option.

> I would choose online learning because it is more flexible.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “If I Had To Choose...”

Very useful speaking phrase:

> **If I had to choose, I'd choose...**

Example:

> If I had to choose between city life and village life, I'd choose city life because of the career opportunities.

🔥 Excellent natural expression।

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “It Depends”

Comparison সবসময় absolute নয়।

> It depends on the person's needs.

> It depends on the situation.

Example:

> Which is better, online or traditional education?

> It depends on the student. Some students learn better online, while others prefer face-to-face classes.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Advantages

Use:

> One advantage is...

> Another advantage is...

> The biggest advantage is...

Example:

> One advantage of online learning is flexibility.

> Another advantage is convenience.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Disadvantages

> One disadvantage is...

> Another disadvantage is...

> The main drawback is...

**Drawback = অসুবিধা**

Example:

> The main drawback of online learning is the lack of direct interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Advantage + Disadvantage

> Online learning is flexible, but its main drawback is that students may have less face-to-face interaction.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Compare Using 3 Categories

যেকোনো two things compare করতে:

### 1. Cost

> Which is cheaper?

### 2. Convenience

> Which is easier?

### 3. Quality

> Which is better?

Example:

> Online learning is cheaper and more flexible, while traditional learning may provide better direct interaction.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Compare Jobs

Question:

> Which is better: working for a company or working independently?

Answer:

> Both options have advantages. Working for a company usually provides more stability and teamwork. On the other hand, working independently can provide more freedom and flexibility.
>
> If I had to choose, I'd prefer working for a company at the beginning of my career because I can gain experience and learn from other professionals.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Compare City vs Village

> City life is usually faster and more expensive than village life. Cities offer more job opportunities, better transportation, and more services.
>
> On the other hand, village life is generally quieter and less expensive. It can also provide a closer connection with nature.
>
> Personally, I prefer city life because of career opportunities, although I would like to visit villages regularly.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Compare Online vs Offline Learning

> Both online and offline learning have advantages. Online learning is more flexible because students can study from anywhere.
>
> Traditional learning, however, provides more direct interaction with teachers and classmates.
>
> If I had to choose, I'd prefer a combination of both because students can enjoy the flexibility of online learning and the interaction of traditional classes.

🔥 Very strong answer।

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Compare Smartphone vs Laptop

> A smartphone is more portable than a laptop. You can easily carry it anywhere and use it for quick tasks.
>
> On the other hand, a laptop is better for professional work, programming, and multitasking.
>
> Personally, I would choose a laptop for work and a smartphone for communication and everyday tasks.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Compare Public Transport vs Private Car

> Public transportation is usually cheaper than using a private car. It can also reduce the number of vehicles on the road.
>
> However, private cars are more convenient and provide more privacy.
>
> If I had to choose, I would prefer public transportation for daily commuting because it can save money and reduce travel costs.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Compare Two People

Person comparison:

> My brother is more outgoing than me.

> My friend is more organized than I am.

> We are similar in some ways, but we have different personalities.

Useful:

> more outgoing

> more confident

> more patient

> more organized

> less talkative

> more hardworking

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Compare Places

> Dhaka is more crowded than many smaller cities.

> Cox's Bazar is more relaxing than Dhaka.

> The countryside is quieter than the city.

> This place is less expensive than the previous one.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Compare Technologies

> A laptop is more suitable for programming than a smartphone.

> Cloud storage is more convenient than storing everything locally.

> Modern smartphones are much faster than older phones.

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Make Your Choice Clear

Don't finish with:

> Both are good.

Instead:

> Both have advantages, but if I had to choose, I'd choose A because...

Example:

> Both online and offline learning are useful, but if I had to choose, I'd choose online learning because it gives me more flexibility.

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Strong Comparison Formula

Use:

> **Both A and B have advantages. However, A is more ___, while B is better for ___. Personally, I prefer A because ___.**

Example:

> Both city and village life have advantages. However, city life is more convenient for careers, while village life is better for a peaceful lifestyle. Personally, I prefer city life because of the opportunities.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Advanced Formula

For 3–5 minutes:

> **Introduction**
>
> Both A and B have advantages.
>
> **Similarity**
>
> They are similar because...
>
> **Difference 1**
>
> However, A is...
>
> **Difference 2**
>
> On the other hand, B is...
>
> **Example**
>
> For example,...
>
> **Advantages**
>
> One advantage of A is...
>
> **Disadvantages**
>
> The main drawback is...
>
> **Choice**
>
> If I had to choose,...
>
> **Reason**
>
> The main reason is...
>
> **Conclusion**
>
> Overall, I think...

---

# 🧠 Part 45 — Full 2-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### Which is better, online learning or traditional learning?

> Both online and traditional learning have their own advantages and disadvantages.
>
> Online learning is more flexible and convenient. Students can study from home and choose a suitable time. For example, someone who has a full-time job can watch recorded lessons whenever they are available.
>
> Traditional learning, on the other hand, provides more face-to-face interaction. Students can directly communicate with teachers and classmates, which can make learning more engaging.
>
> Another difference is self-discipline. Online learning requires students to manage their own time, whereas traditional classes usually provide a fixed schedule.
>
> If I had to choose, I would prefer online learning because I value flexibility. However, I think a combination of online and traditional learning would be even better.
>
> Overall, the best option depends on the student's needs and learning style.

---

# 🔥 Part 46 — Full 3-Minute Answer

## 🎤 Question:

### Is city life better than village life?

> In my opinion, both city life and village life have advantages and disadvantages.
>
> Cities usually offer more job opportunities, better educational institutions, and easier access to healthcare and other services. They are also more convenient for people who want to build their careers.
>
> Village life, on the other hand, is generally quieter and less crowded. People may have a closer connection with nature, and the cost of living can be lower.
>
> However, villages may have fewer job opportunities and specialized services. Cities have more opportunities, but they also have problems such as traffic congestion, pollution, and a higher cost of living.
>
> If I had to choose, I would prefer city life at this stage of my life because career opportunities are important to me. However, in the future, I might prefer a quieter lifestyle.
>
> So, I don't think one is absolutely better than the other. It depends on a person's priorities and stage of life.

---

# 🧠 Part 47 — Useful Comparison Vocabulary

| Word         | Meaning     |
| ------------ | ----------- |
| similar      | একই রকম     |
| different    | ভিন্ন       |
| advantage    | সুবিধা      |
| disadvantage | অসুবিধা     |
| benefit      | উপকার       |
| drawback     | অসুবিধা     |
| convenient   | সুবিধাজনক   |
| flexible     | নমনীয়       |
| affordable   | সাশ্রয়ী     |
| expensive    | ব্যয়বহুল    |
| efficient    | কার্যকর     |
| practical    | ব্যবহারিক   |
| comfortable  | আরামদায়ক    |
| reliable     | নির্ভরযোগ্য |
| suitable     | উপযুক্ত     |

---

# 🔥 Part 48 — Comparison Connectors

আজ এগুলো actively ব্যবহার করবে:

> both

> while

> whereas

> compared with

> on the other hand

> however

> similarly

> in contrast

> unlike

> instead

> although

---

# 🧠 Part 49 — “Unlike”

**Unlike A, B...**

> Unlike city life, village life is usually quieter.

> Unlike traditional classes, online learning is more flexible.

---

# 🔥 Part 50 — “In Contrast”

Formal comparison:

> City life is fast-paced. In contrast, village life is more peaceful.

---

# 🧠 Part 51 — Common Mistakes

### ❌

> More better

### ✅

> Better

---

### ❌

> More easier

### ✅

> Easier

---

### ❌

> I prefer tea than coffee.

### ✅

> I prefer tea **to** coffee.

---

### ❌

> I would rather tea than coffee.

### ✅

> I would rather **drink tea than coffee**.

---

### ❌

> Both online learning and traditional learning is useful.

### ✅

> Both online learning and traditional learning **are** useful.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 80 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Online vs Offline Learning

Answer:

1. What are the similarities?
2. What are the differences?
3. Which is cheaper?
4. Which is more convenient?
5. Which is more effective?
6. What are the advantages?
7. What are the disadvantages?
8. Which do you prefer?
9. Why?
10. What is your final opinion?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — City Life vs Village Life

Must use:

> **Both...and...**

> **While...**

> **On the other hand...**

> **Compared with...**

> **If I had to choose...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Laptop vs Smartphone

Must use:

> **more...than**

> **less...than**

> **better than**

> **whereas**

> **I would choose...**

> **because...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 80 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে বেশি মনে রাখবে:

# **BOTH → DIFFERENCE → ADVANTAGE → DISADVANTAGE → CHOICE → REASON → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **Both** online and traditional learning are useful.
>
> **However**, online learning is more flexible, **while** traditional learning provides more face-to-face interaction.
>
> **One advantage** of online learning is convenience.
>
> **One drawback** is that it requires more self-discipline.
>
> **If I had to choose**, I would choose online learning.
>
> **The main reason is** that I can study at my own pace.
>
> **Overall**, I think the best choice depends on the learner's needs.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 80-এর Target

আজ থেকে comparison question শুনলে শুধু:

> ❌ **“A is better.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **A & B → Similarity → Difference → Advantage → Disadvantage → My Choice → Why → Conclusion**

এই structure-এ **3–5 মিনিট** কথা বলবে।

### 🔥 Lesson 79 → **What do you think?**

### 🔥 Lesson 80 → **Which one is better, and why?**

এখন তোমার **Lesson 61–80** phase-এর একটি বড় skill তৈরি হলো: **opinion + reason + example + comparison + conclusion**।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 81

# 🗣️ **Problem-Solving & Giving Solutions**

### সমস্যা নিয়ে কথা বলা + কারণ বলা + সমাধান দেওয়া

**Main Goal:** আজ তুমি শিখবে কীভাবে কোনো problem নিয়ে **2–5 মিনিট** English-এ কথা বলতে হয় এবং practical solution দিতে হয়।

Lesson 80-এ তুমি শিখেছ **compare & choose**।
আজ শিখবে:

> **Problem → Cause → Effect → Solution → Result → Conclusion**

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

যেকোনো problem-এর answer এই structure-এ বলবে:

> **PROBLEM → CAUSE → EFFECT → SOLUTION → HOW → RESULT → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> Traffic congestion is a serious problem in big cities. One major cause is the large number of private vehicles. It wastes people's time and increases pollution. One possible solution is to improve public transportation. If public transportation becomes more reliable, more people may use it. As a result, traffic congestion could decrease.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Problem কীভাবে শুরু করবে?

Useful phrases:

> **There is a problem with...**

> **One major problem is...**

> **A common problem is...**

> **One of the biggest problems is...**

### Examples:

> One major problem is traffic congestion.

> A common problem among students is lack of concentration.

> One of the biggest problems in modern cities is pollution.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “The Problem Is That...”

Very natural:

> The problem is that many people don't have enough time.

> The problem is that public transportation is not reliable.

> The problem is that students spend too much time on social media.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Describe the Problem

Don't just say:

> Traffic is bad.

Explain:

> Traffic congestion is a serious problem because people spend a lot of time stuck on the roads.

আরও:

> It affects people's daily lives.

> It wastes valuable time.

> It can create stress.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Cause

**Cause = কারণ**

Useful phrases:

> **The main cause is...**

> **One major reason is...**

> **This happens because...**

> **One reason behind this problem is...**

Example:

> One major reason behind traffic congestion is the increasing number of private vehicles.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “Because of”

> Traffic has increased because of population growth.

> Many students are distracted because of excessive smartphone use.

**because of + noun**

> because of traffic

> because of pollution

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “Due To”

More formal:

> The problem is partly due to poor planning.

> Traffic congestion is partly due to the increasing number of vehicles.

**due to = because of**

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “As a Result”

**As a result = ফলস্বরূপ**

> People spend more time on the road. As a result, they have less time for their families.

> Pollution is increasing. As a result, air quality is getting worse.

🔥 Problem → Result

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “This Leads To”

Very useful:

> Traffic congestion leads to stress.

> Excessive screen time can lead to sleep problems.

> Poor planning can lead to serious problems.

**lead to = কোনো ফলাফলের দিকে নিয়ে যাওয়া**

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “This Can Cause”

> Excessive phone use can cause distraction.

> Lack of exercise can cause health problems.

> Poor time management can cause stress.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Solution

এখন problem থেকে solution-এ যাবে।

Useful phrases:

> **One possible solution is...**

> **One effective solution would be...**

> **A good way to solve this problem is...**

> **We should...**

> **The government should...**

> **People should...**

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “One Possible Solution”

সবচেয়ে useful phrase:

> One possible solution is to improve public transportation.

> One possible solution is to educate people about the problem.

> One possible solution is to manage time more effectively.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “We Should”

Simple:

> We should use public transportation more often.

> We should reduce unnecessary plastic use.

> Students should manage their time better.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Need To”

> We need to take action.

> The government needs to improve public transportation.

> Students need to develop better study habits.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “It Would Be Better If...”

Very natural:

> It would be better if more people used public transportation.

> It would be better if students spent less time on social media.

> It would be better if companies offered flexible working hours.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “If...”

Solution + result:

> If people use public transportation, traffic congestion may decrease.

> If students manage their time properly, they will become more productive.

> If we reduce plastic use, we can reduce pollution.

### Formula:

> **If + present simple → will/can/may + verb**

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “By + ing”

Solution method:

> We can reduce traffic by improving public transportation.

> We can improve English by practicing every day.

> We can save money by reducing unnecessary expenses.

🔥 Very useful for speaking।

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “In Order To”

Purpose:

> We should improve public transportation in order to reduce traffic.

> Students should manage their time in order to become more productive.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “So That”

> We should improve public transportation so that people can travel more easily.

> Students should create a schedule so that they can manage their time better.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Immediate Solution vs Long-Term Solution

এটা আজকের advanced skill।

### Immediate solution:

> In the short term, we can...

### Long-term:

> In the long term, we should...

Example:

> In the short term, we can improve traffic management. In the long term, the city needs better public transportation and urban planning.

🔥 এতে answer অনেক mature শোনায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Problem + Multiple Solutions

একটি solution-এ থেমে যাবে না।

> There are several ways to solve this problem.

Then:

> First,...

> Second,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> There are several ways to reduce traffic. First, public transportation should be improved. Second, unnecessary private car use should be reduced. Finally, better traffic management is needed.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — First / Second / Finally

Organize your speech:

> **First**, ...

> **Second**, ...

> **Another important step is** ...

> **Finally**, ...

এগুলো ব্যবহার করলে speech অনেক organized হবে।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “For Example”

Solution explain করার সময়:

> For example, schools could organize time-management workshops.

> For example, cities could introduce more buses and metro services.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Instead Of”

Alternative solution:

> People should use public transportation instead of private cars.

> Students can read books instead of spending hours on social media.

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Rather Than”

> We should improve public transportation rather than simply building more roads.

> Students should focus on learning rather than memorizing everything.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “The Best Way”

> The best way to solve this problem is to improve public transportation.

> The best way to improve English is to practice consistently.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “The Most Effective Way”

Advanced:

> The most effective way to reduce traffic is to improve public transportation.

> The most effective way to learn a language is to practice it regularly.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Result

Solution দিলে কী হবে?

Useful:

> **As a result,...**

> **This would help...**

> **This could reduce...**

> **This would make...**

Example:

> This would reduce traffic and save people's time.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “Would Help”

> Better public transportation would help reduce traffic.

> A daily schedule would help students manage their time.

> More practice would help me improve my English.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “Could”

Possible result:

> This could reduce pollution.

> This could improve people's quality of life.

> This could make education more accessible.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Problem-Solving Conversation

### A:

> What do you think is the biggest problem for students?

### B:

> I think time management is one of the biggest problems.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> Many students spend too much time on social media and don't have a clear schedule.

### A:

> How can they solve it?

### B:

> They should create a daily routine and set priorities. They can also limit their social media use.

### A:

> Would that really help?

### B:

> Yes. If they manage their time properly, they can become more productive and have more time for study and rest.

---

# 🔥 Part 31 — Full Example: Traffic Problem

## 🎤 Question:

### How can we reduce traffic congestion?

> Traffic congestion is a serious problem in many cities. One major reason is the increasing number of private vehicles. Poor traffic management and limited public transportation also contribute to the problem.
>
> There are several possible solutions. First, public transportation should be improved. Buses should be more reliable and comfortable so that people are encouraged to use them.
>
> Second, traffic management should be improved. Traffic signals and road systems need to be managed more effectively.
>
> Finally, people should be encouraged to use public transportation instead of private cars whenever possible.
>
> If these measures are implemented properly, traffic congestion could decrease significantly. As a result, people would spend less time on the road and air pollution could also decrease.
>
> Overall, I think improving public transportation is one of the most effective long-term solutions.

---

# 🧠 Part 32 — Full Example: English Learning Problem

## 🎤 Question:

### Why do many people struggle to learn English?

> I think there are several reasons why people struggle to learn English. One major reason is that they focus too much on grammar and memorization instead of actual communication.
>
> Another problem is lack of regular practice. Some people study for a few days and then stop for several weeks.
>
> One possible solution is to practice English every day, even for a short period. For example, learners can speak for ten or fifteen minutes every day, listen to English conversations, and learn useful phrases.
>
> It would also be helpful to use English in real situations instead of only studying from books.
>
> If learners practice consistently, their confidence will gradually improve.
>
> Overall, I believe consistency and regular speaking practice are more important than studying for many hours occasionally.

---

# 🔥 Part 33 — Full Example: Social Media

### Problem:

> Excessive social media use is becoming a common problem.

### Cause:

> One major reason is that social media platforms are designed to keep people engaged.

### Effect:

> As a result, people may waste time and have difficulty concentrating.

### Solution:

> One possible solution is to set daily limits on social media use.

### Result:

> This could improve concentration and productivity.

### Conclusion:

> Overall, social media isn't necessarily bad, but people need to use it responsibly.

---

# 🧠 Part 34 — Personal Problem

সব problem public issue হতে হবে না।

Question:

> What is one problem you are trying to solve?

Answer:

> One problem I'm trying to solve is poor time management. Sometimes I spend too much time on less important activities.
>
> The main reason is that I don't always follow a clear schedule.
>
> To solve this problem, I'm going to create a daily routine and prioritize important tasks.
>
> If I follow the routine consistently, I think I'll become more productive.

🔥 এটা speaking practice-এর জন্য excellent।

---

# 🔥 Part 35 — Don't Say “Problem” Repeatedly

Instead of:

> The problem is...

> The problem is...

> The problem is...

Use:

> issue

> challenge

> difficulty

> concern

Example:

> One major issue is...

> Another challenge is...

> A major concern is...

---

# 🧠 Part 36 — Problem Vocabulary

| Word        | Meaning      |
| ----------- | ------------ |
| problem     | সমস্যা       |
| issue       | সমস্যা/বিষয়  |
| challenge   | চ্যালেঞ্জ    |
| difficulty  | অসুবিধা      |
| cause       | কারণ         |
| effect      | প্রভাব       |
| solution    | সমাধান       |
| result      | ফলাফল        |
| improvement | উন্নতি       |
| action      | পদক্ষেপ      |
| approach    | পদ্ধতি       |
| measure     | ব্যবস্থা     |
| reduce      | কমানো        |
| prevent     | প্রতিরোধ করা |
| solve       | সমাধান করা   |

---

# 🔥 Part 37 — Powerful Problem-Solving Phrases

এগুলো আজ practice করবে:

> **One major problem is...**

> **The main cause is...**

> **This can lead to...**

> **As a result...**

> **One possible solution is...**

> **We need to...**

> **We should...**

> **A good way to solve this is...**

> **By doing this...**

> **If we..., we can...**

> **In the short term...**

> **In the long term...**

> **As a result...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Part 38 — Common Mistakes

### ❌

> One solution is improve transportation.

### ✅

> One solution is **to improve** transportation.

---

### ❌

> We should to improve it.

### ✅

> We should improve it.

---

### ❌

> This will helps people.

### ✅

> This will help people.

---

### ❌

> People should using public transport.

### ✅

> People should use public transport.

---

### ❌

> By improve transportation...

### ✅

> By improving transportation...

---

# 🔥 Part 39 — 2-Minute Answer Template

যেকোনো problem-এর জন্য:

> **One major problem is [problem].**
>
> **The main reason is [cause].**
>
> **As a result, [effect].**
>
> **There are several possible solutions.**
>
> **First, [solution 1].**
>
> **Second, [solution 2].**
>
> **For example, [example].**
>
> **If we do this, [result].**
>
> **In the long term, [long-term solution].**
>
> **Overall, I think [conclusion].**

---

# 🏆 Lesson 81 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Traffic Congestion

Answer:

1. What is the problem?
2. Why does it happen?
3. What are the effects?
4. What is one possible solution?
5. What is another solution?
6. What can individuals do?
7. What should the government do?
8. What will happen if nothing changes?
9. What is the long-term solution?
10. What is your conclusion?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — How Can Students Improve Their English?

Must use:

> **One major problem...**

> **The main reason...**

> **As a result...**

> **One possible solution...**

> **By + ing...**

> **If...**

> **In the long term...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — How Can People Save Money?

Try to talk about:

**Problem → Cause → Effect → Solution 1 → Solution 2 → Example → Result**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 81 Final Challenge

এখন এই question-এর উত্তর **3–5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করো:

> ### 🎤 “What is one major problem in modern society, and how can we solve it?”

তোমার answer-এ অবশ্যই থাকবে:

**1. Problem**

**2. Cause**

**3. Effect**

**4. Solution 1**

**5. Solution 2**

**6. Example**

**7. Short-term solution**

**8. Long-term solution**

**9. Expected result**

**10. Conclusion**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 81 Golden Formula

মনে রাখবে:

# **PROBLEM → CAUSE → EFFECT → SOLUTION → HOW → RESULT → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **Traffic is a major problem.**
>
> **The main cause is** the increasing number of vehicles.
>
> **As a result**, people waste a lot of time.
>
> **One possible solution is** to improve public transportation.
>
> **By improving** buses and other public transport, more people may stop using private cars.
>
> **If this happens**, traffic could decrease.
>
> **Overall**, better public transportation is one of the most effective long-term solutions.

### 🔥 Lesson 79 → **Opinion**

### 🔥 Lesson 80 → **Comparison & Choice**

### 🔥 Lesson 81 → **Problem & Solution**

এখন তোমার speaking skill আরও এক ধাপ এগিয়ে গেল: **কোনো topic নিয়ে শুধু মতামত নয়—problem identify করে logical solution দিতে পারবে।**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 82

# 🗣️ **Describing People, Personality & Behavior**

### মানুষকে describe করা + personality বলা + behavior explain করা

**Main Goal:** আজ তুমি শিখবে কীভাবে একজন মানুষকে শুধু দেখতে কেমন তা নয়, বরং **কেমন personality, কীভাবে behave করে, কী ভালো/খারাপ দিক আছে এবং কেন তুমি তাকে পছন্দ করো**—এসব নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

কোনো person সম্পর্কে বলার সময়:

> **WHO → APPEARANCE → PERSONALITY → HABITS → BEHAVIOR → STRENGTH → WEAKNESS → EXAMPLE → YOUR OPINION**

Example:

> My best friend is a very friendly and helpful person. He is quite confident and always tries to help others. He is also hardworking and responsible. For example, whenever I have a problem, he listens to me and tries to help. I really like him because he is honest and supportive.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Who Is This Person?

শুরুতেই বলবে মানুষটি কে।

> He is my best friend.

> She is my older sister.

> He is one of my classmates.

> She is someone I really admire.

### Useful:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

Example:

> I'd like to talk about one of my closest friends.

🔥 Speaking answer শুরু করার জন্য খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Basic Information

বলতে পারো:

> His name is...

> She is from...

> He is a student.

> She works as a...

> I've known him for several years.

### Important:

> I've known him **for five years.**

মানে: আমি তাকে পাঁচ বছর ধরে চিনি।

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Appearance

**Appearance = দেখতে কেমন**

Useful vocabulary:

> tall — লম্বা

> short — খাটো

> slim — রোগা/ছিপছিপে

> well-built — সুগঠিত

> young — তরুণ

> middle-aged — মধ্যবয়স্ক

> handsome — সুদর্শন

> beautiful — সুন্দর

> attractive — আকর্ষণীয়

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Hair

> He has short black hair.

> She has long hair.

> He has curly hair.

> She has straight hair.

> He usually keeps his hair short.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Eyes & Face

> He has brown eyes.

> She has a round face.

> He has a friendly smile.

> She has a cheerful expression.

### Natural:

> He has a very friendly smile.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Don't Overfocus on Appearance

Speaking exam/conversation-এ শুধু:

> He is tall. He has black hair. He has brown eyes.

এভাবে থেমে গেলে answer weak হবে।

Appearance-এর পর দ্রুত যাও:

# **Personality → Behavior → Example**

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Personality কী?

**Personality = ব্যক্তিত্ব / স্বভাব**

আজকের সবচেয়ে important vocabulary এখানেই।

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Positive Personality

### Friendly

> He is very friendly.

### Kind

> She is a kind person.

### Helpful

> He is always helpful.

### Honest

> She is very honest.

### Polite

> He is polite to everyone.

### Patient

> She is very patient.

### Responsible

> He is responsible.

### Hardworking

> She is hardworking.

### Confident

> He is confident.

### Generous

> She is generous.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — More Positive Words

> reliable — নির্ভরযোগ্য

> supportive — সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ

> calm — শান্ত

> positive — ইতিবাচক

> creative — সৃজনশীল

> organized — গোছানো

> disciplined — শৃঙ্খলাবদ্ধ

> ambitious — উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী

> independent — স্বনির্ভর

> thoughtful — বিবেচনাশীল

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Negative Personality

সব মানুষ perfect নয়।

Useful words:

> lazy — অলস

> careless — অসতর্ক

> impatient — অধৈর্য

> stubborn — একগুঁয়ে

> shy — লাজুক

> nervous — নার্ভাস

> selfish — স্বার্থপর

> disorganized — অগোছালো

> pessimistic — নেতিবাচক চিন্তাধারার

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Be Careful

কারও personality describe করার সময় খুব strong negative words ব্যবহার না করাই ভালো।

Instead of:

> He is a bad person.

Say:

> He can sometimes be impatient.

Instead of:

> She is lazy.

Say:

> She sometimes lacks motivation.

এতে English বেশি natural এবং polite শোনায়।

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “He Tends To...”

**tend to = সাধারণত এমন করে**

> He tends to be quiet.

> She tends to worry too much.

> He tends to arrive early.

খুব useful advanced phrase।

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Usually”

Habit:

> He usually helps people.

> She usually stays calm.

> He usually thinks carefully before making a decision.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Sometimes”

Balanced description:

> He is usually calm, but sometimes he gets nervous.

> She is very confident, although she can sometimes be shy.

🔥 এইভাবে contrast দিলে answer natural হয়।

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “One of His/Her Best Qualities”

খুব সুন্দর phrase:

> One of his best qualities is his honesty.

> One of her best qualities is that she is very patient.

> One of his best qualities is his ability to stay calm under pressure.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “What I Like About Him/Her”

> What I like about him is his honesty.

> What I like about her is that she always listens to me.

### Formula:

> **What I like about + person + is...**

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “What I Admire”

**admire = শ্রদ্ধা/প্রশংসা করা**

> I really admire her determination.

> I admire him because he never gives up.

> What I admire most about him is his hard work.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — Explain With Example

Personality শুধু adjective দিয়ে বলবে না।

### Weak:

> He is hardworking.

### Strong:

> He is hardworking. For example, he always finishes his work before the deadline.

---

### Weak:

> She is helpful.

### Strong:

> She is very helpful. Whenever someone has a problem, she tries to help.

🔥 **Adjective → Example**

এটাই আজকের main speaking technique।

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Whenever”

**Whenever = যখনই**

> Whenever I need help, he is there for me.

> Whenever she faces a problem, she stays calm.

> Whenever we work together, he motivates me.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Even Though”

Balanced personality:

> Even though he is quiet, he is very friendly.

> Even though she is young, she is very responsible.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Although”

> Although he is shy, he communicates very well with close friends.

> Although she is sometimes impatient, she is a very caring person.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Behavior

**Behavior = আচরণ**

Useful:

> He treats people with respect.

> She listens carefully.

> He speaks politely.

> She helps others.

> He keeps his promises.

> She respects other people's opinions.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “Treat”

**treat someone = কারও সাথে আচরণ করা**

> He treats everyone with respect.

> She treats her colleagues kindly.

> He treats people fairly.

Very useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “Get Along With”

**get along with = ভালো সম্পর্ক থাকা**

> I get along well with him.

> She gets along well with everyone.

> We have known each other for years, so we get along very well.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Have a Lot in Common”

**common = মিল**

> We have a lot in common.

> We both enjoy technology and traveling.

> That's why we get along well.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Difference Between You and the Person

এটা answer interesting করে।

> We are similar in some ways, but we are also different.

Example:

> We are both hardworking, but he is more outgoing than me.

> I'm more patient, whereas he is more adventurous.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Compare Personality

Use Lesson 80:

> He is more confident than I am.

> She is more organized than me.

> I'm quieter, whereas my friend is more outgoing.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Strength

**Strength = শক্তিশালী দিক / ভালো দিক**

> His biggest strength is his confidence.

> Her biggest strength is her ability to stay calm.

> One of his strengths is problem-solving.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Weakness

**Weakness = দুর্বলতা**

> One of his weaknesses is that he sometimes worries too much.

> She can be a little impatient sometimes.

### Polite:

> One area he could improve is time management.

🔥 এটা “weakness” বলার চেয়ে softer।

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — How the Person Influences You

এটা advanced speaking।

Question:

> How has this person influenced you?

Answer:

> He has influenced me in many ways.

> He taught me to be more patient.

> He encouraged me to work harder.

> Because of him, I became more confident.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “He Taught Me”

> He taught me how to stay calm.

> She taught me the importance of hard work.

> My father taught me to be responsible.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “He Encouraged Me To...”

> He encouraged me to learn English.

> She encouraged me to apply for the job.

> My friend encouraged me to keep practicing.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — “Because of”

> Because of her support, I became more confident.

> Because of his advice, I made a better decision.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Thanks To”

Positive result:

> Thanks to his advice, I improved my skills.

> Thanks to her support, I became more confident.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Person You Admire

Question:

> Who do you admire?

Start:

> One person I really admire is...

Then:

> I admire this person because...

Then:

> One of their best qualities is...

Then:

> For example,...

Then:

> That's why I respect them.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Full 2-Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### Describe a person you admire.

> I'd like to talk about a person I really admire. He is one of my close friends, and I've known him for several years.
>
> He is a friendly, hardworking, and responsible person. He is also quite confident, but he is not arrogant. He treats everyone with respect and always tries to help people when they need support.
>
> One of his best qualities is his determination. Whenever he faces a difficult situation, he doesn't give up easily. For example, when he was working on a difficult project, he kept trying until he solved the problem.
>
> What I admire most about him is his positive attitude. Even when things don't go according to plan, he tries to stay calm and find a solution.
>
> He has influenced me in several ways. He encouraged me to work harder and be more consistent.
>
> Overall, I really respect him because he is hardworking, supportive, and dependable.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Describe a Family Member

## Question:

### Describe someone in your family.

Structure:

> Who?

> Relationship?

> Appearance?

> Personality?

> Habits?

> Strength?

> Example?

> Why do you like/respect them?

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Example

> I'd like to talk about my older brother. He is a very important person in my life.
>
> He is friendly, calm, and responsible. He usually thinks carefully before making decisions.
>
> One thing I really like about him is that he always supports me when I face difficulties. Whenever I need advice, I can talk to him.
>
> He is also hardworking. He always tries to complete his responsibilities on time.
>
> Although we have different personalities, we get along very well.
>
> I respect him because he has taught me to be responsible and patient.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Describe Yourself

Question:

> How would you describe yourself?

Start:

> I would describe myself as...

Examples:

> I would describe myself as a hardworking person.

> I would describe myself as a calm and responsible person.

Then explain:

> I'm usually...

> I tend to...

> One of my strengths is...

> One area I'm trying to improve is...

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Full Self-Description

> I would describe myself as a hardworking and responsible person. I'm usually calm, especially when I have to deal with difficult situations.
>
> One of my strengths is that I don't give up easily. When I have a goal, I try to work toward it consistently.
>
> However, one area I'm trying to improve is time management. Sometimes I spend too much time on one task.
>
> To improve this, I'm trying to plan my day and prioritize important tasks.
>
> Overall, I think I'm still learning and developing as a person.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Important Personality Vocabulary

| Word        | Meaning          |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| friendly    | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ    |
| kind        | দয়ালু            |
| honest      | সৎ               |
| helpful     | সহায়ক           |
| polite      | ভদ্র             |
| patient     | ধৈর্যশীল         |
| confident   | আত্মবিশ্বাসী     |
| hardworking | পরিশ্রমী         |
| responsible | দায়িত্বশীল       |
| reliable    | নির্ভরযোগ্য      |
| supportive  | সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ    |
| calm        | শান্ত            |
| creative    | সৃজনশীল          |
| organized   | গোছানো           |
| ambitious   | উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষী    |
| shy         | লাজুক            |
| quiet       | শান্ত/কম কথা বলে |
| outgoing    | মিশুক            |
| stubborn    | একগুঁয়ে          |
| impatient   | অধৈর্য           |

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Useful Sentence Patterns

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

> **I've known him/her for...**

> **He/She is quite...**

> **One of his/her best qualities is...**

> **What I like about him/her is...**

> **What I admire most is...**

> **For example,...**

> **Whenever...**

> **Although..., ...**

> **He/She tends to...**

> **One of his/her strengths is...**

> **One area he/she could improve is...**

> **He/She has influenced me by...**

> **Overall, I respect him/her because...**

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Don't Just List Adjectives

### ❌ Weak:

> My friend is kind, honest, hardworking, friendly, responsible, confident.

এটা শুধু vocabulary list।

### ✅ Strong:

> My friend is very hardworking. For example, he always finishes his work before the deadline. He is also supportive. Whenever I have a problem, he listens to me and gives me useful advice.

🔥 **Adjective + Example + Behavior**

এটাই natural speaking।

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — 5-Minute Speaking Structure

যদি 5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়:

### 1. Introduction — 30 sec

> Who is the person?

### 2. Appearance — 30 sec

> What does he/she look like?

### 3. Personality — 1 min

> What kind of person is he/she?

### 4. Behavior — 1 min

> How does he/she treat others?

### 5. Example — 1 min

> Tell a real situation.

### 6. Influence — 30 sec

> How has this person influenced you?

### 7. Conclusion — 20 sec

> Why do you admire/like this person?

---

# 🏆 Lesson 82 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Describe Your Best Friend

Answer these:

1. Who is your best friend?
2. How long have you known him/her?
3. What does he/she look like?
4. What is his/her personality like?
5. What does he/she usually do?
6. What is his/her biggest strength?
7. What is one weakness?
8. Tell me about a time when he/she helped you.
9. What do you admire about him/her?
10. How has he/she influenced you?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Describe Yourself

Must use:

> **I would describe myself as...**

> **One of my strengths is...**

> **I tend to...**

> **One area I'm trying to improve is...**

> **To improve this...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Describe Someone You Admire

Use:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

> **One of his/her best qualities...**

> **For example...**

> **What I admire most...**

> **Because of...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 82 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important:

# **WHO → PERSONALITY → BEHAVIOR → EXAMPLE → STRENGTH → WEAKNESS → INFLUENCE → OPINION**

Example:

> **I'd like to talk about my best friend.**
>
> He is **friendly, hardworking, and responsible**.
>
> He **always treats people with respect**.
>
> **For example**, whenever I need help, he listens to me and gives me advice.
>
> **One of his biggest strengths** is his determination.
>
> **One area he could improve** is his patience.
>
> **He has influenced me** by encouraging me to work harder.
>
> **Overall**, I really admire him because he is supportive and reliable.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 82-এর Main Target

আজ থেকে মানুষ describe করার সময় শুধু:

> ❌ **“He is good.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What kind of person? → How does he behave? → Give an example → What is his strength? → How does he influence you?**

### 🔥 Lesson 79 → Opinion

### 🔥 Lesson 80 → Comparison

### 🔥 Lesson 81 → Problem & Solution

### 🔥 Lesson 82 → **People & Personality**

এখন তুমি **personality + example + reason + opinion** ব্যবহার করে অনেক বেশি naturalভাবে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে পারবে।


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 83

# 🗣️ **Describing Places & Experiences**

### কোনো জায়গা describe করা + সেখানে কী হয়েছিল + নিজের অনুভূতি বলা

**Main Goal:** আজ তুমি শিখবে কীভাবে একটি **place** নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলতে হয়। শুধু জায়গাটা কেমন তা নয়—**কোথায়, কখন, কার সাথে, কী দেখেছ, কী করেছ, কেমন লেগেছে এবং কেন মনে আছে**—সব বলবে।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **WHERE → WHEN → WHO → DESCRIPTION → WHAT YOU DID → WHAT YOU SAW → FEELING → WHY SPECIAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I'd like to talk about a place I visited last year. It was a beautiful beach, and I went there with some friends. The place was peaceful and the weather was pleasant. We walked along the beach, took pictures, and watched the sunset. I felt relaxed and happy. What made the trip special was spending time with my friends. Overall, it was a memorable experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — শুরু করবে কীভাবে?

Useful opening:

> **I'd like to talk about a place I visited...**

> **One place I really like is...**

> **I'd like to describe a place that is special to me.**

> **One of the most memorable places I've visited is...**

### Example:

> I'd like to talk about a place I visited a few years ago.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — Where?

প্রথমে location বলবে।

> It is located in...

> It is near...

> It is in the northern part of...

> It is not far from...

### Example:

> The beach is located in the southern part of Bangladesh.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — When?

সময় বলবে:

> I visited it last year.

> I went there during my vacation.

> I visited the place a few months ago.

> I went there when I was a student.

### Useful:

> **It was during...**

> It was during my summer vacation.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Who Did You Go With?

> I went there with my friends.

> I went there with my family.

> I traveled there alone.

> I went there with a few classmates.

### Advanced:

> I went there with a group of close friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Describe the Place

এখন জায়গাটা কেমন সেটা বলবে।

### Useful adjectives:

> beautiful

> peaceful

> crowded

> quiet

> noisy

> modern

> traditional

> clean

> attractive

> relaxing

> exciting

> scenic

> spacious

> busy

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Nature Describe করা

> The area was surrounded by trees.

> There were beautiful mountains nearby.

> The beach was surrounded by beautiful scenery.

> The place had a peaceful atmosphere.

> The air was fresh and clean.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “There Was / There Were”

Place describe করতে খুব important।

### Singular:

> There was a beautiful lake.

> There was a small restaurant nearby.

### Plural:

> There were many trees.

> There were several shops.

> There were lots of tourists.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — “It Had...”

> The place had a peaceful atmosphere.

> The city had many interesting buildings.

> The park had beautiful walking paths.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — What Did You Do?

এখানে action বলবে।

> We walked around the area.

> We took some pictures.

> We tried local food.

> We visited some interesting places.

> We talked with local people.

> We watched the sunset.

> We went sightseeing.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “We Spent Time...”

Natural phrase:

> We spent some time walking around.

> We spent the afternoon exploring the area.

> We spent the evening watching the sunset.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “I Got a Chance to...”

Meaning: সুযোগ পেয়েছিলাম।

> I got a chance to try some local food.

> I got a chance to talk to some local people.

> I got a chance to explore the area.

🔥 Very useful for speaking.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — What Did You See?

> I saw beautiful scenery.

> I saw a lot of tourists.

> I saw some traditional buildings.

> We saw a beautiful sunset.

> I saw several interesting places.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — What Did You Eat?

Travel experience হলে:

> We tried some local food.

> I tried a traditional dish.

> The food was delicious.

> It was a little spicy, but I really enjoyed it.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — How Did You Feel?

এটাই আজকের সবচেয়ে important অংশগুলোর একটি।

Don't only say:

> It was good.

Use:

> I felt happy.

> I felt relaxed.

> I felt excited.

> I felt surprised.

> I felt peaceful.

> I felt nervous.

> I felt amazed.

> I felt comfortable.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “I Was Impressed By...”

**impressed = মুগ্ধ**

> I was impressed by the natural beauty.

> I was impressed by how clean the place was.

> I was really impressed by the local people.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “What I Liked Most...”

Very useful:

> What I liked most was the peaceful atmosphere.

> What I liked most about the place was the natural beauty.

> What I liked most was spending time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “What Made It Special?”

এই phrase দিয়ে answer অনেক strong করা যায়।

> What made the trip special was the people.

> What made the place special was its peaceful environment.

> What made the experience memorable was spending time with my family.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “The Best Part Was...”

> The best part was watching the sunset.

> The best part of the trip was trying local food.

> The best part was spending time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — Add a Problem

সব experience perfect হতে হবে না।

> However, there was one problem.

> The place was quite crowded.

> The weather suddenly became bad.

> We had some transportation problems.

এটা story interesting করে।

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Contrast

Use Lesson 79:

> Although the place was crowded, I still enjoyed it.

> The journey was tiring, but the experience was worth it.

> It was expensive; however, it was worth the money.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Worth It”

**worth it = মূল্য/পরিশ্রম সার্থক**

> The journey was long, but it was worth it.

> The trip was expensive, but it was definitely worth it.

🔥 Natural spoken English।

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Talk About the Journey

শুধু destination নয়।

> The journey was quite long.

> We traveled by bus.

> It took about five hours.

> The road was sometimes crowded.

> We stopped at a few places along the way.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “It Took...”

Duration:

> It took three hours to get there.

> It took about half an hour to reach the hotel.

### Formula:

> **It took + time + to + verb**

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “After We Arrived”

Sequence তৈরি করবে:

> After we arrived, we checked into the hotel.

> After we arrived, we walked around the area.

> After we arrived, we had lunch.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Sequence Words

Story/experience-এর জন্য:

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Later,...

> Eventually,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> First, we checked into the hotel. Then, we went to the beach. After that, we had lunch. Finally, we watched the sunset.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “Eventually”

মানে: শেষ পর্যন্ত।

> Eventually, we found a good restaurant.

> Eventually, we reached our destination.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — “Finally”

শেষ action:

> Finally, we returned to the hotel.

> Finally, we went back home.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — Talk About People

Place-এর মানুষ describe করো:

> The local people were very friendly.

> The people were welcoming.

> Everyone was helpful.

> The area was full of tourists.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Talk About Atmosphere

**Atmosphere = পরিবেশ**

> The atmosphere was peaceful.

> The atmosphere was lively.

> The atmosphere was relaxed.

> The atmosphere was exciting.

Example:

> The place had a very peaceful atmosphere, especially in the evening.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Talk About Weather

> The weather was pleasant.

> It was sunny.

> It was cloudy.

> It was quite hot.

> It started raining.

> The weather was perfect for traveling.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Talk About Your Favorite Place

Question:

> What is your favorite place?

Answer structure:

> Where?

> How often?

> Why?

> What do you do there?

> How do you feel?

Example:

> My favorite place is a quiet park near my home. I usually go there in the evening. I like it because it is peaceful. I usually walk around or sit and read. It helps me relax after a busy day.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Full Example: A Memorable Place

## 🎤 Question:

### Describe a memorable place you have visited.

> I'd like to talk about a beach I visited with some of my friends. I went there last year during a short vacation.
>
> The beach was beautiful and had a very peaceful atmosphere. There were many trees nearby, and the sea looked amazing, especially in the evening. The weather was also pleasant.
>
> First, we walked around the beach and took some pictures. Then, we tried some local food. Later, we sat near the beach and watched the sunset.
>
> The place was quite crowded, which was one disadvantage. However, I still enjoyed the experience.
>
> What I liked most was the peaceful atmosphere and the time I spent with my friends. I felt relaxed and happy throughout the trip.
>
> What made the experience special was that we don't usually get many opportunities to travel together.
>
> Overall, it was one of the most memorable trips I've had, and I'd love to visit the place again.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Describe a City

Question:

> Describe a city you know.

Structure:

> Location

> Size

> Population

> Transportation

> Food

> People

> Advantages

> Problems

> Your opinion

Example:

> It's a busy and modern city. It has many restaurants, shopping centers, schools, and offices. However, traffic congestion is a major problem. Despite that, I like the city because it offers many opportunities.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Describe Your Hometown

Useful:

> I come from...

> My hometown is located in...

> It is known for...

> One thing I like about it is...

> One problem is...

> Overall, I feel...

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Describe a Place You Want to Visit

Future + place:

> I'd love to visit...

> I've always wanted to visit...

> One place I'd really like to visit is...

Then explain:

> The main reason is...

> I'd like to see...

> I'd love to try...

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Example: Future Place

> One place I'd really like to visit is Japan. I've always been interested in its culture, technology, food, and natural beauty.
>
> I'd love to visit Tokyo because it is a modern city, but I'd also like to explore some quieter areas.
>
> I would like to try traditional Japanese food and visit some historical places.
>
> The main reason I want to go there is to experience a culture that is quite different from mine.
>
> If I get the opportunity, I'd definitely like to visit Japan in the future.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Useful Place Vocabulary

| Word        | Meaning            |
| ----------- | ------------------ |
| peaceful    | শান্তিপূর্ণ        |
| crowded     | ভিড়পূর্ণ           |
| lively      | প্রাণবন্ত          |
| scenic      | মনোরম দৃশ্যপূর্ণ   |
| beautiful   | সুন্দর             |
| modern      | আধুনিক             |
| traditional | ঐতিহ্যবাহী         |
| spacious    | প্রশস্ত            |
| attractive  | আকর্ষণীয়           |
| relaxing    | আরামদায়ক           |
| atmosphere  | পরিবেশ             |
| scenery     | প্রাকৃতিক দৃশ্য    |
| destination | গন্তব্য            |
| sightseeing | দর্শনীয় স্থান দেখা |
| memorable   | স্মরণীয়            |

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Useful Experience Phrases

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

> **I'd like to talk about...**

> **I visited it...**

> **I went there with...**

> **It is located in...**

> **The place was...**

> **There were...**

> **First,...**

> **After that,...**

> **Eventually,...**

> **The best part was...**

> **What I liked most was...**

> **I was impressed by...**

> **What made it special was...**

> **I felt...**

> **Although..., ...**

> **It was worth it.**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Don't Just Describe

### ❌ Weak:

> The place was beautiful. There were trees. There was a lake. The weather was good.

### ✅ Strong:

> The place was beautiful and peaceful. There were many trees around the lake, which created a relaxing atmosphere. I especially enjoyed walking around the lake in the evening because the weather was pleasant.

🔥 **Description + Reason + Feeling**

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — 5-Minute Speaking Structure

### ① Introduction — 30 sec

> What place?

### ② Location & Time — 30 sec

> Where? When?

### ③ People — 20 sec

> Who went with you?

### ④ Description — 1 min

> What did it look like?

### ⑤ Activities — 1 min

> What did you do?

### ⑥ Experience — 1 min

> What happened?

### ⑦ Feeling — 30 sec

> How did you feel?

### ⑧ Conclusion — 20 sec

> Why is it memorable?

---

# 🏆 Lesson 83 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Describe a Place You Visited

Answer:

1. Where was it?
2. When did you go?
3. Who went with you?
4. How did you travel?
5. What did the place look like?
6. What did you do?
7. What did you see?
8. What did you eat?
9. How did you feel?
10. What made the trip special?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Describe Your Hometown

Must use:

> **It is located in...**

> **It is known for...**

> **One thing I like...**

> **One problem is...**

> **However...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Describe a Place You Want to Visit

Must use:

> **I've always wanted to visit...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **I'd love to...**

> **If I get the opportunity...**

> **In the future...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 83 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ:

# **WHERE → WHEN → WHO → DESCRIPTION → ACTION → EXPERIENCE → FEELING → WHY SPECIAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **I'd like to talk about a place I visited last year.**
>
> I went there **with my friends**.
>
> The place was **beautiful and peaceful**.
>
> **First**, we walked around and took pictures.
>
> **After that**, we tried some local food.
>
> **The best part was** watching the sunset.
>
> **I felt relaxed and happy.**
>
> **What made it special was** spending time with my friends.
>
> **Overall**, it was a very memorable experience.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 83-এর Main Target

কোনো জায়গা সম্পর্কে শুধু:

> ❌ “It is beautiful.”

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **Where → When → What it looks like → What you did → What happened → How you felt → Why you remember it**

### 🔥 Lesson 79 → Opinion

### 🔥 Lesson 80 → Comparison

### 🔥 Lesson 81 → Problem & Solution

### 🔥 Lesson 82 → People & Personality

### 🔥 Lesson 83 → **Places & Experiences**

**আজকের speaking challenge:** একটি বাস্তব জায়গা বেছে নিয়ে **না লিখে**, শুধু এই 9টি keyword দেখে 3 মিনিট English-এ বলো:

**Where → When → Who → Place → Activity → Food → Experience → Feeling → Conclusion**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 84

# 🗣️ **Talking About Daily Life, Habits & Routines**

### দৈনন্দিন জীবন, অভ্যাস এবং routine নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **“আমি প্রতিদিন কী করি”**—এটা শুধু ছোট ছোট sentence-এ না বলে, সুন্দরভাবে বিস্তারিতভাবে explain করা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **TIME → ROUTINE → ACTIVITY → FREQUENCY → REASON → EXAMPLE → CHANGE → FUTURE PLAN → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I usually wake up early in the morning. After getting up, I freshen up and have breakfast. Then I start my work or study. I usually spend several hours learning new things. In the evening, I exercise or relax for a while. Recently, I've been trying to manage my time better because I want to be more productive.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Daily Routine শুরু করা

সবচেয়ে useful phrase:

> **I usually...**

Examples:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m.

> I usually have breakfast in the morning.

> I usually study in the evening.

---

### আরও natural:

> **On a typical day,...**

মানে:

**সাধারণ একটি দিনে...**

Example:

> On a typical day, I wake up early and start my day with a glass of water.

🔥 Speaking-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “My Day Usually Starts With...”

> My day usually starts with a glass of water.

> My day usually starts with some light exercise.

> My day usually starts with checking my schedule.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — Morning Routine

Useful verbs:

> wake up — ঘুম থেকে ওঠা

> get up — বিছানা থেকে ওঠা

> brush my teeth

> wash my face

> take a shower

> get dressed

> have breakfast

> leave home

### Example:

> I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. Then I brush my teeth, wash my face, and take a shower. After that, I have breakfast.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Wake Up vs Get Up

এটা মনে রাখবে:

### Wake up

ঘুম ভাঙা:

> I wake up at 7.

### Get up

বিছানা থেকে ওঠা:

> I get up a few minutes later.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — After That

Routine connect করার জন্য:

> After that,...

> Then,...

> Next,...

> Later,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> I wake up at 7. After that, I brush my teeth. Then I have breakfast. Later, I start studying.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — Talk About Study/Work

> I usually start studying at...

> I spend several hours working on...

> I attend classes...

> I work on my projects...

> I practice my English...

### Example:

> After breakfast, I usually spend a few hours studying and working on my projects.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “Spend Time + ing”

Very important structure:

> I spend two hours **studying**.

> I spend a lot of time **reading**.

> I spend my evenings **practicing English**.

⚠️

❌ I spend time to study.

✅ I spend time studying.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Frequency

কতবার করো সেটা বলবে।

### 100%

> always

### প্রায় সবসময়

> usually

### অনেক সময়

> often

### মাঝে মাঝে

> sometimes

### খুব কম

> rarely

### কখনো না

> never

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Examples

> I always brush my teeth before bed.

> I usually study in the morning.

> I often practice English.

> I sometimes watch movies.

> I rarely eat fast food.

> I never smoke.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “Every Day”

> I practice English every day.

> I exercise every morning.

> I read books every night.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Once / Twice”

> I exercise once a day.

> I visit my family twice a month.

> I go to the gym three times a week.

### Formula:

> **once/twice/three times + a day/week/month**

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — “Usually...But Sometimes...”

এটা natural speech-এর জন্য খুব useful।

> I usually study in the morning, but sometimes I study at night.

> I usually cook at home, but sometimes I eat outside.

> I usually go to bed early, but sometimes I stay up late.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — Evening Routine

> In the evening,...

> After work,...

> After finishing my studies,...

Examples:

> In the evening, I usually go for a walk.

> After finishing my work, I usually relax for a while.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “For a While”

**for a while = কিছু সময়**

> I watch TV for a while.

> I relax for a while.

> I talk to my friends for a while.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Night Routine

> At night,...

> Before going to bed,...

> Before I go to bed,...

Examples:

> Before going to bed, I usually check my schedule for the next day.

> I usually read for a few minutes before going to bed.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Before + ing”

> Before going to bed, I read a book.

> Before starting work, I check my emails.

> Before leaving home, I have breakfast.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — “After + ing”

> After finishing my work, I take a break.

> After having breakfast, I start studying.

> After coming home, I take a shower.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Try To”

Habit or goal:

> I try to wake up early.

> I try to exercise regularly.

> I try to practice English every day.

> I try to avoid wasting time.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Make Sure”

**make sure = নিশ্চিত করা**

> I make sure to finish my important tasks first.

> I make sure to drink enough water.

> I make sure to prepare everything before work.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — Healthy Habits

Useful vocabulary:

> exercise

> drink water

> eat healthy food

> get enough sleep

> go for a walk

> meditate

> take breaks

Example:

> I try to maintain a healthy lifestyle. I exercise regularly and try to get enough sleep.

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Bad Habits

> I spend too much time on my phone.

> I sometimes stay up late.

> I procrastinate sometimes.

### Procrastinate

= কাজ পরে করার অভ্যাস

> I sometimes procrastinate when I have a difficult task.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “I'm Trying To...”

নিজের habit improvement:

> I'm trying to wake up earlier.

> I'm trying to spend less time on social media.

> I'm trying to become more organized.

> I'm trying to improve my English.

🔥 Very useful।

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “I've Been Trying To...”

Recent ongoing effort:

> I've been trying to wake up earlier.

> I've been trying to improve my speaking skills.

> I've been trying to manage my time better.

এটা natural advanced English।

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “I Used To...”

পুরনো habit:

> I used to wake up late.

> I used to spend a lot of time watching TV.

> I used to study at night.

Meaning:

**আগে করতাম, এখন করি না/কম করি।**

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Used To vs Usually

### Current:

> I usually wake up early.

### Past habit:

> I used to wake up late.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Talk About Change

Very important:

> My routine has changed recently.

> I used to..., but now I...

Example:

> I used to study at night, but now I prefer studying in the morning because I can concentrate better.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Why Do You Have This Routine?

শুধু routine বলবে না। **Reason** দেবে।

> I wake up early because I can concentrate better in the morning.

> I exercise regularly because it helps me stay active.

> I practice English every day because I want to become more confident.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “It Helps Me...”

Excellent phrase:

> It helps me stay focused.

> It helps me relax.

> It helps me manage my time.

> It helps me stay healthy.

> It helps me improve my English.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — “So That”

Purpose:

> I wake up early so that I have enough time to study.

> I make a schedule so that I can manage my time better.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Talk About Free Time

> In my free time,...

> When I have free time,...

> Whenever I get some free time,...

Examples:

> In my free time, I usually watch movies or listen to music.

> Whenever I get some free time, I practice English.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — Hobbies

> I enjoy reading.

> I enjoy watching movies.

> I like listening to music.

> I love traveling.

> I'm interested in learning new technologies.

### Important:

> enjoy + **verb-ing**

❌ I enjoy to read.

✅ I enjoy reading.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Weekend Routine

> On weekends,...

> At the weekend,...

Examples:

> On weekends, I usually relax and spend time with my family.

> On weekends, I sometimes go out with my friends.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Busy Day

Question:

> What do you do on a busy day?

Answer:

> On a busy day, I usually wake up early and start working immediately. I try to prioritize the most important tasks first. I take short breaks so that I don't get too tired.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — Relaxing Day

> On a relaxing day, I usually sleep a little longer. Then I have breakfast and spend some time reading or watching something. In the evening, I might go for a walk.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Full Daily Routine

## 🎤 Example

> On a typical day, I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. After getting up, I brush my teeth, wash my face, and take a shower. Then I have breakfast.
>
> After breakfast, I usually spend several hours studying and working on my projects. I also try to practice English every day because I want to improve my speaking skills.
>
> In the afternoon, I take a short break and have lunch. After that, I continue my work or study.
>
> In the evening, I usually go for a walk or relax for a while. Sometimes I watch a movie or talk to my friends.
>
> Before going to bed, I usually review what I did during the day and make a plan for the next day.
>
> Recently, I've been trying to manage my time better because I want to become more productive.
>
> Overall, my routine is fairly busy, but I try to maintain a balance between work, learning, and relaxation.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Describe Your Old vs New Routine

> My routine has changed quite a bit.
>
> I used to wake up late and spend a lot of time on my phone. I didn't have a fixed schedule.
>
> Now, I try to wake up earlier and plan my day. I also spend more time studying and practicing English.
>
> The main reason for this change is that I want to become more productive.
>
> I still have some bad habits, but I'm gradually improving them.

🔥 এই answer-এ Past + Present + Reason + Goal আছে।

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Useful Daily Routine Vocabulary

| Word/Phrase   | Meaning             |
| ------------- | ------------------- |
| routine       | দৈনন্দিন কাজের ধারা |
| habit         | অভ্যাস              |
| schedule      | সময়সূচি             |
| productive    | ফলপ্রসূ             |
| concentrate   | মনোযোগ দেওয়া        |
| relax         | বিশ্রাম নেওয়া       |
| exercise      | ব্যায়াম করা         |
| practice      | অনুশীলন করা         |
| prioritize    | অগ্রাধিকার দেওয়া    |
| procrastinate | কাজ ফেলে রাখা       |
| improve       | উন্নতি করা          |
| maintain      | বজায় রাখা           |
| balance       | ভারসাম্য            |
| regularly     | নিয়মিত              |
| occasionally  | মাঝে মাঝে           |

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Useful Speaking Connectors

আজ এগুলো বেশি ব্যবহার করবে:

> Usually,...

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Later,...

> In the afternoon,...

> In the evening,...

> Before going to bed,...

> Sometimes,...

> Recently,...

> However,...

> Because...

> As a result,...

> Overall,...

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Common Mistakes

### ❌

> I am wake up at 7.

### ✅

> I wake up at 7.

---

### ❌

> I usually am studying at night.

### ✅

> I usually study at night.

---

### ❌

> I enjoy to watch movies.

### ✅

> I enjoy watching movies.

---

### ❌

> I spend time to learn English.

### ✅

> I spend time learning English.

---

### ❌

> I used to wake up early now.

### ✅

> I used to wake up late, but now I wake up early.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 84 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Describe Your Daily Routine

Answer:

1. What time do you wake up?
2. What do you do first?
3. What do you do in the morning?
4. What do you do during the day?
5. How much time do you spend studying/working?
6. What do you do in the evening?
7. What do you do before bed?
8. What do you do in your free time?
9. What habit are you trying to improve?
10. Why?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Your Good and Bad Habits

Talk about:

> One good habit I have is...

> One bad habit I have is...

> The reason is...

> I'm trying to...

> If I improve this habit,...

> Overall,...

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — How Has Your Routine Changed?

Must use:

> **I used to...**

> **But now...**

> **Recently...**

> **I've been trying to...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **As a result...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 84 Golden Formula

সবচেয়ে important:

# **TIME → ROUTINE → FREQUENCY → REASON → HABIT → CHANGE → GOAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **On a typical day**, I wake up early.
>
> **Usually**, I spend the morning studying.
>
> I **try to practice English every day** because I want to improve my speaking.
>
> **I used to** waste a lot of time on my phone, **but now** I'm trying to reduce my screen time.
>
> **Recently, I've been trying to** manage my time better.
>
> **Overall**, I want to build a more productive and balanced routine.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 84 Main Target

আজ থেকে daily routine question এ শুধু:

> ❌ “I wake up. I eat. I study. I sleep.”

এভাবে বলবে না।

বরং:

> **When → What → How often → Why → Example → Old habit → New habit → Goal**

### 🔥 Lesson 83 → Places & Experiences
