# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 85

# 🗣️ **Talking About Future Plans & Goals**

### ভবিষ্যৎ পরিকল্পনা + লক্ষ্য + স্বপ্ন নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো ভবিষ্যৎ নিয়ে শুধু:

> “I will do this.”

না বলে **কী করতে চাও, কেন করতে চাও, কীভাবে করবে, কখন করবে এবং কী result আশা করো**—এসব explain করা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **GOAL → REASON → PLAN → WHEN → HOW → CHALLENGE → SOLUTION → EXPECTED RESULT → LONG-TERM GOAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> One of my main goals is to improve my English speaking skills. I want to become more confident when communicating with other people. To achieve this goal, I'm going to practice speaking every day. I plan to spend at least thirty minutes on English practice. I know it may be difficult to stay consistent, but I'll try to follow a daily routine. If I practice regularly, I believe my speaking skills will improve significantly.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Future Plan শুরু করবে কীভাবে?

সবচেয়ে useful:

> **I'm planning to...**

> **I plan to...**

> **I'm going to...**

> **I'd like to...**

> **I hope to...**

> **I want to...**

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “I'm Going To”

যে কাজ করার intention ইতিমধ্যে আছে:

> I'm going to improve my English.

> I'm going to learn a new skill.

> I'm going to apply for some jobs.

> I'm going to practice coding every day.

### Formula:

> **I am going to + verb**

❌ I'm going to improving my English.

✅ I'm going to improve my English.

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “I Plan To”

আরও organized শোনায়:

> I plan to learn Python.

> I plan to improve my communication skills.

> I plan to apply for jobs next month.

> I plan to read more books.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “I'm Planning To”

Future intention:

> I'm planning to take an English course.

> I'm planning to travel next year.

> I'm planning to build a new project.

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — “I'd Like To”

Polite / personal desire:

> I'd like to become a successful developer.

> I'd like to travel to another country.

> I'd like to improve my public speaking skills.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “I Hope To”

Hope:

> I hope to get a good job.

> I hope to become fluent in English.

> I hope to travel abroad someday.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — “My Main Goal Is...”

আজকের সবচেয়ে important phrase:

> **My main goal is to...**

Examples:

> My main goal is to become a better English speaker.

> My main goal is to build a successful career.

> My main goal is to become financially independent.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Short-Term vs Long-Term Goals

এটা অবশ্যই শিখবে।

### Short-term goal

**স্বল্পমেয়াদি লক্ষ্য**

> My short-term goal is to improve my English.

### Long-term goal

**দীর্ঘমেয়াদি লক্ষ্য**

> My long-term goal is to build a successful career.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — Short-Term Goal Example

> My short-term goal is to improve my English speaking skills. I want to become more confident in everyday conversations. To achieve this, I'm going to practice speaking for at least thirty minutes every day.

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — Long-Term Goal

> My long-term goal is to become a successful software developer.

Then explain:

> I want to build useful products and work with an international team.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — Why?

Goal বলার পর **reason** অবশ্যই দেবে।

Useful:

> **The main reason is...**

> **The main reason why I want to do this is...**

> **I want to do this because...**

Example:

> The main reason I want to improve my English is that good communication skills can help me in my career.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — How Will You Achieve It?

এটাই important।

> **To achieve this goal,...**

Examples:

> To achieve this goal, I need to practice every day.

> To achieve this goal, I'm going to follow a clear plan.

> To achieve this goal, I need to improve my technical skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “I Need To”

> I need to practice regularly.

> I need to manage my time better.

> I need to improve my communication skills.

> I need to stay consistent.

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “I Will”

Simple future:

> I will practice every day.

> I will work harder.

> I will try to stay consistent.

### Important:

**will + base verb**

❌ I will practicing.

✅ I will practice.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — “I’m Going To” vs “I Will”

### Plan:

> I'm going to start a new course next month.

### Decision/promise:

> I will work harder.

দুটোই future-এর জন্য ব্যবহার হয়।

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “I’m Hoping To”

Natural:

> I'm hoping to get a good job.

> I'm hoping to improve my skills.

> I'm hoping to travel next year.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Time Expressions

Future-এর সময় বলবে:

> tomorrow

> next week

> next month

> next year

> in the future

> in the next few months

> over the next few years

> someday

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “In the Next Few Months”

Very useful:

> In the next few months, I want to improve my English.

> Over the next few months, I'm going to focus on my skills.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Over the Next Few Years”

Long-term:

> Over the next few years, I want to build a strong career.

> Over the next few years, I hope to gain more experience.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “By + Time”

Deadline/target:

> I want to improve my English by the end of this year.

> I hope to complete the course by next month.

### Formula:

> **by + deadline**

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — “Before”

> I want to finish the course before the end of the year.

> I hope to find a job before the end of the year.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — “Within”

**within = এর মধ্যে**

> I want to finish the project within two months.

> I hope to improve my speaking within six months.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — Goals With Numbers

Specific goal বেশি powerful।

### Weak:

> I want to improve my English.

### Better:

> I want to practice English every day.

### Strong:

> I want to practice speaking English for at least thirty minutes every day for the next six months.

🔥 Specific!

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — “At Least”

> I will study for at least two hours a day.

> I will practice speaking for at least thirty minutes.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — “Every Day”

> I will practice every day.

> I will review my vocabulary every night.

> I will speak English every morning.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — Goal + Action

এই pattern:

> **Goal → Action**

Example:

> My goal is to improve my English. To do that, I'll practice speaking every day.

আরেকটি:

> My goal is to become a better developer. To achieve this, I'll build real-world projects.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Challenge

Future plan বলার সময় challenge বললে answer strong হয়।

> One challenge might be lack of time.

> Another challenge could be staying consistent.

> I may face some difficulties along the way.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “I May”

Possible future:

> I may face some difficulties.

> I may not have enough time every day.

> I may need to change my schedule.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Solution to Challenge

> To deal with this, I'll create a schedule.

> To overcome this challenge, I'll prioritize my important tasks.

> If I lose motivation, I'll remind myself why I started.

🔥 Excellent speaking sentence।

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — “Stay Consistent”

**consistent = নিয়মিত/ধারাবাহিক**

> I need to stay consistent.

> The biggest challenge is staying consistent.

> If I stay consistent, I believe I can achieve my goal.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “I Believe I Can”

Confidence:

> I believe I can achieve my goal.

> I believe I can improve with regular practice.

> I believe I can become more confident.

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — “If I...”

Future condition:

> If I practice every day, I will improve.

> If I stay consistent, I can achieve my goal.

> If I manage my time properly, I will have enough time to practice.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Expected Result

> As a result,...

> Because of this,...

> This will help me...

> This could allow me to...

Example:

> This will help me become more confident.

> This could allow me to communicate more effectively.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Eventually”

Long-term result:

> Eventually, I hope to become fluent in English.

> Eventually, I want to build my own software company.

> Eventually, I'd like to work with international clients.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Dream

Dream বলার জন্য:

> **My dream is to...**

> **I've always dreamed of...**

> **One of my biggest dreams is...**

Examples:

> My dream is to become a successful entrepreneur.

> I've always dreamed of traveling around the world.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — “Someday”

Future dream:

> Someday, I'd like to start my own business.

> Someday, I hope to travel to Europe.

> Someday, I'd like to work internationally.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Career Goal

Question:

> What are your career goals?

Answer:

> My main career goal is to become a skilled software developer. In the short term, I want to improve my technical and communication skills. In the long term, I'd like to work on challenging projects and take on more responsibility.

---

# 🔥 Part 38 — Personal Goal

> One of my personal goals is to become more disciplined.

> I want to manage my time better.

> I also want to develop healthier habits.

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Learning Goal

> One of my learning goals is to improve my English speaking skills.

> I'm planning to practice speaking every day.

> I'm also going to listen to English conversations and learn useful expressions.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — Full 2–3 Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### What are your plans for the future?

> I have several plans for the future, but one of my main goals is to build a successful career.
>
> In the short term, I want to improve my technical and communication skills. I also want to become more confident in English because good communication is important for both personal and professional life.
>
> To achieve these goals, I'm going to practice English every day and spend more time improving my technical skills. I also plan to work on real-world projects so that I can gain practical experience.
>
> One challenge I may face is managing my time. Sometimes I have many things to do, so it can be difficult to stay consistent. To overcome this, I'm going to create a daily schedule and prioritize the most important tasks.
>
> In the long term, I'd like to build a strong career and work on challenging projects. I also hope to work with people from different countries.
>
> I know these goals will take time, but I believe that if I stay consistent and keep learning, I can achieve them.
>
> Overall, my main focus is to keep improving myself and become better than I was yesterday.

---

# 🧠 Part 41 — Future Plan Vocabulary

| Word/Phrase | Meaning          |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| goal        | লক্ষ্য           |
| plan        | পরিকল্পনা        |
| dream       | স্বপ্ন           |
| ambition    | উচ্চাকাঙ্ক্ষা    |
| target      | লক্ষ্য           |
| achieve     | অর্জন করা        |
| improve     | উন্নতি করা       |
| develop     | উন্নত/বিকশিত করা |
| focus on    | মনোযোগ দেওয়া     |
| prepare for | প্রস্তুতি নেওয়া  |
| succeed     | সফল হওয়া         |
| challenge   | চ্যালেঞ্জ        |
| overcome    | অতিক্রম করা      |
| consistent  | ধারাবাহিক        |
| eventually  | শেষ পর্যন্ত      |
| opportunity | সুযোগ            |

---

# 🔥 Part 42 — Important Sentence Patterns

আজ এগুলো মুখে practice করবে:

> **My main goal is to...**

> **My short-term goal is to...**

> **My long-term goal is to...**

> **I'm planning to...**

> **I'm going to...**

> **I'd like to...**

> **I hope to...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **To achieve this goal,...**

> **I need to...**

> **One challenge might be...**

> **To overcome this,...**

> **If I..., I will...**

> **As a result,...**

> **Eventually,...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 Part 43 — Don't Make Your Goals Too General

### ❌ Weak:

> I want to be successful.

### ✅ Better:

> I want to build a successful career.

### 🔥 Strong:

> I want to build a strong career in software development by continuously improving my technical and communication skills.

---

# 🔥 Part 44 — Don't Just Say “I Will”

### ❌

> I will learn English.
> I will get a job.
> I will become successful.

এখানে explanation নেই।

### ✅

> I want to improve my English because it will help me communicate more confidently. To achieve this, I'm going to practice speaking every day. If I stay consistent, I believe my communication skills will improve.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 85 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Your Future Plans

Answer:

1. What is your main goal?
2. Why is it important to you?
3. What is your short-term goal?
4. What is your long-term goal?
5. What are you going to do?
6. When are you going to start?
7. How much time will you spend?
8. What challenges might you face?
9. How will you overcome them?
10. What result do you expect?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Career Goals

Must use:

> **My main career goal is...**

> **In the short term...**

> **In the long term...**

> **To achieve this...**

> **One challenge might be...**

> **If I stay consistent...**

> **Eventually...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Learning English

Talk about:

> Why do you want to learn English?

> What is your target?

> How will you practice?

> How often?

> What difficulties might you face?

> How will you overcome them?

> What will you be able to do after improving?

---

# 🧠 Lesson 85 Golden Formula

# **GOAL → REASON → PLAN → TIME → ACTION → CHALLENGE → SOLUTION → RESULT → DREAM → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **My main goal is to improve my English.**
>
> **The main reason is** that I want to communicate more confidently.
>
> **To achieve this**, I'm going to practice speaking every day.
>
> **In the next six months**, I'll try to practice for at least thirty minutes a day.
>
> **One challenge might be** lack of time.
>
> **To overcome this**, I'll create a daily schedule.
>
> **If I stay consistent**, my speaking skills will improve.
>
> **Eventually**, I hope to become fluent.
>
> **Overall**, I want to keep improving myself step by step.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 85 Main Target

Future নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় শুধু:

> ❌ **“I will do it.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What → Why → When → How → Challenge → Solution → Result**

### 🔥 Lesson 83 → Places & Experiences

**My main goal is → In the short term → In the long term → I'm planning to → To achieve this → One challenge might be → If I → Eventually**.

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 86

# 🗣️ **Talking About Education & Learning**

### শিক্ষা, পড়াশোনা, শেখার অভিজ্ঞতা এবং নিজের learning journey নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **নিজের education বা learning experience** নিয়ে বিস্তারিতভাবে কথা বলতে শেখা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **BACKGROUND → SUBJECT → EXPERIENCE → FAVORITE → DIFFICULTY → METHOD → IMPROVEMENT → BENEFIT → FUTURE → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I've always been interested in technology, so I chose to study computer science. I enjoy learning practical skills because they help me understand how things work. Some subjects are difficult, but I try to understand them through practice. I believe learning is a continuous process, and I want to keep developing my skills in the future.

---

# 🧠 Part 1 — Education সম্পর্কে শুরু করা

Useful opening:

> **I'd like to talk about my education.**

> **I've always been interested in...**

> **My educational background is...**

> **I studied...**

### Example:

> I've always been interested in technology, so I decided to study computer science.

---

# 🔥 Part 2 — “I Studied...”

> I studied computer science.

> I studied English at school.

> I studied mathematics in college.

### Past:

> I studied...

### Present:

> I'm studying...

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — “I’m Studying...”

যদি বর্তমানে কোনো কিছু শেখো:

> I'm studying English.

> I'm learning Python.

> I'm currently learning web development.

> I'm currently working on improving my communication skills.

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — “I Majored In...”

**major = বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ে প্রধান বিষয়**

> I majored in computer science.

> I majored in business administration.

Useful especially interview/conversation-এ।

---

# 🧠 Part 5 — Why Did You Choose It?

খুব important question:

> **Why did you choose your subject?**

Answer structure:

> I chose it because...

> The main reason was...

> I've always been interested in...

Example:

> I chose computer science because I've always been interested in technology and problem-solving.

---

# 🔥 Part 6 — “I’ve Always Been Interested In...”

> I've always been interested in technology.

> I've always been interested in learning languages.

> I've always been interested in how computers work.

### Grammar:

> **be interested in + noun / verb-ing**

❌ I'm interested in learn English.

✅ I'm interested in learning English.

---

# 🧠 Part 7 — Favorite Subject

Question:

> What is your favorite subject?

Start:

> My favorite subject is...

Then reason:

> I like it because...

Example:

> My favorite subject is programming because I enjoy solving problems and creating things.

---

# 🔥 Part 8 — Difficult Subject

> One subject I found difficult was...

> I struggled with...

**struggle with = কোনো কিছু করতে সমস্যা হওয়া**

Example:

> I struggled with mathematics when I was younger.

> I struggled with speaking English at first.

---

# 🧠 Part 9 — “At First”

Very useful for learning stories:

> At first, I found English difficult.

> At first, I didn't understand programming.

> At first, I was nervous about speaking English.

Then:

> But gradually,...

---

# 🔥 Part 10 — “Gradually”

**gradually = ধীরে ধীরে**

> Gradually, I became more confident.

> Gradually, I improved my skills.

> Gradually, I started understanding the subject better.

---

# 🧠 Part 11 — “Over Time”

> Over time, I became more comfortable speaking English.

> Over time, I developed better study habits.

---

# 🔥 Part 12 — How Do You Learn?

Question:

> How do you usually learn something new?

Useful:

> I learn by practicing.

> I watch tutorials.

> I read books.

> I take notes.

> I ask questions.

> I practice with other people.

> I learn from my mistakes.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — “Learn By + ing”

Very useful:

> I learn by practicing.

> I learn by watching videos.

> I learn by doing projects.

> I learn by making mistakes.

### Formula:

> **learn by + verb-ing**

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — “Practice Makes Perfect”

Common expression:

> Practice makes perfect.

But more natural:

> I believe regular practice is the key to improvement.

🔥 This is better for longer speaking.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Theory vs Practice

Useful comparison:

> I think theory is important, but practical experience is equally important.

> I prefer learning through practice.

> Practical experience helps me understand concepts better.

---

# 🔥 Part 16 — “Hands-On Experience”

**hands-on experience = বাস্তবে কাজ করার অভিজ্ঞতা**

> I prefer hands-on learning.

> Practical projects give me hands-on experience.

Example:

> I learn much better when I get hands-on experience.

---

# 🧠 Part 17 — Study Habits

> I usually study in the morning.

> I make a study schedule.

> I take short breaks.

> I review my notes regularly.

> I practice what I learn.

---

# 🔥 Part 18 — “Take Notes”

> I usually take notes while studying.

> Taking notes helps me remember important information.

---

# 🧠 Part 19 — “Review”

**review = আবার দেখা/পুনরালোচনা করা**

> I review my notes before an exam.

> I review what I learned at the end of the day.

---

# 🔥 Part 20 — “Keep Learning”

One of today's most important phrases:

> **I want to keep learning.**

> **I believe we should keep learning throughout our lives.**

> **I always try to keep learning new things.**

---

# 🧠 Part 21 — Lifelong Learning

**lifelong learning = সারাজীবন শেখা**

> I believe learning should be a lifelong process.

> Technology changes quickly, so lifelong learning is important.

---

# 🔥 Part 22 — Online Learning

Question:

> Do you prefer online or traditional learning?

You can say:

> I prefer online learning because it is flexible.

> I can learn at my own pace.

> I can access courses from anywhere.

---

# 🧠 Part 23 — “At My Own Pace”

**নিজের সুবিধামতো গতিতে**

> Online courses allow me to learn at my own pace.

Very useful phrase।

---

# 🔥 Part 24 — Advantages of Online Learning

> It is flexible.

> It saves time.

> It is often affordable.

> There are many resources available.

> You can learn from experts around the world.

---

# 🧠 Part 25 — Disadvantages

Balanced opinion:

> However, online learning also has some disadvantages.

> It can be difficult to stay motivated.

> There may be fewer opportunities for face-to-face interaction.

> Some people find it difficult to study independently.

---

# 🔥 Part 26 — “It Depends On”

Very natural phrase:

> I think it depends on the person.

> It depends on the subject.

> It depends on the learning style.

Example:

> I think the best method depends on the subject and the learner.

---

# 🧠 Part 27 — Learning From Mistakes

One of the most important speaking topics:

> I believe mistakes are part of learning.

> I learn a lot from my mistakes.

> When I make a mistake, I try to understand what went wrong.

---

# 🔥 Part 28 — “What Went Wrong?”

Useful when describing learning:

> I try to understand what went wrong.

> Then I try to avoid making the same mistake again.

---

# 🧠 Part 29 — Teacher Influence

Question:

> Has a teacher influenced you?

Answer:

> Yes, one of my teachers influenced me a lot.

Then:

> He/She encouraged me to...

> He/She taught me...

Example:

> One of my teachers encouraged me to think independently rather than simply memorize information.

---

# 🔥 Part 30 — Memorization vs Understanding

Important:

> I prefer understanding concepts rather than memorizing them.

> Memorization can be useful, but understanding is more important.

> When I understand something, I can apply it in real situations.

---

# 🧠 Part 31 — “Apply What I Learn”

**apply = বাস্তবে প্রয়োগ করা**

> I try to apply what I learn.

> Practical projects help me apply my knowledge.

🔥 Very useful for academic + job speaking।

---

# 🔥 Part 32 — Education Has Changed

Question:

> How has education changed?

Example:

> Education has changed significantly because of technology. Students now have access to online courses, videos, digital books, and interactive learning platforms.

Then:

> However, technology also requires students to be more disciplined.

---

# 🧠 Part 33 — Technology & Education

Useful phrases:

> online classes

> digital resources

> online courses

> educational videos

> virtual classrooms

> learning platforms

Example:

> Technology has made education more accessible.

---

# 🔥 Part 34 — “Accessible”

**accessible = সহজে পাওয়া/ব্যবহার করা যায়**

> Education has become more accessible.

> Students can access information from almost anywhere.

---

# 🧠 Part 35 — Your Learning Goal

Connect with Lesson 85:

> One of my learning goals is to improve my English speaking skills.

> I'm planning to practice every day.

> I also want to develop better study habits.

---

# 🔥 Part 36 — Full 2–3 Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### Tell me about your education and learning experience.

> I'd like to talk about my education and learning experience. I've always been interested in technology and problem-solving, so I decided to study computer science.
>
> I enjoy learning technical subjects because they allow me to understand how things work and create practical solutions.
>
> Of course, some subjects have been difficult for me. At first, I sometimes struggled with complex concepts, but gradually I became more comfortable with them. I realized that regular practice was more effective than simply memorizing information.
>
> These days, I prefer hands-on learning. I like working on projects because they allow me to apply what I learn in real situations. I also watch tutorials, read documentation, take notes, and practice regularly.
>
> I believe mistakes are an important part of learning. Whenever I make a mistake, I try to understand what went wrong and learn from it.
>
> In the future, I want to keep learning new technologies and improve my communication skills. I believe learning should be a lifelong process.
>
> Overall, my education has taught me not only technical knowledge but also how to solve problems, stay consistent, and keep improving.

---

# 🧠 Part 37 — Education Vocabulary

| Word/Phrase       | Meaning       |
| ----------------- | ------------- |
| education         | শিক্ষা        |
| subject           | বিষয়          |
| degree            | ডিগ্রি        |
| course            | কোর্স         |
| assignment        | নির্ধারিত কাজ |
| exam              | পরীক্ষা       |
| lecture           | লেকচার        |
| teacher           | শিক্ষক        |
| student           | শিক্ষার্থী    |
| skill             | দক্ষতা        |
| knowledge         | জ্ঞান         |
| practice          | অনুশীলন       |
| research          | গবেষণা        |
| improve           | উন্নতি করা    |
| understand        | বোঝা          |
| memorize          | মুখস্থ করা    |
| apply             | প্রয়োগ করা    |
| struggle          | সমস্যায় পড়া   |
| gradually         | ধীরে ধীরে     |
| experience        | অভিজ্ঞতা      |
| lifelong learning | সারাজীবন শেখা |

# 🔥 Part 38 — Important Sentence Patterns

আজ এগুলো মুখে practice করবে:

> **I've always been interested in...**

> **I decided to study... because...**

> **My favorite subject is...**

> **I struggled with...**

> **At first,...**

> **Gradually,...**

> **Over time,...**

> **I learn by...**

> **I prefer... because...**

> **I believe...**

> **I learn from my mistakes.**

> **I try to apply what I learn.**

> **One of my learning goals is...**

> **In the future, I want to...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 Part 39 — Weak vs Strong Answer

### ❌ Weak:

> I study computer science. I like programming. I learn English. I watch videos.

### ✅ Strong:

> I'm interested in computer science because I enjoy problem-solving. I prefer learning through practical projects because they allow me to apply what I learn. When I study English, I try to practice speaking regularly rather than only memorizing vocabulary.

---

# 🔥 Part 40 — 5-Minute Speaking Structure

### ① Background — 30 sec

> What did you study?

### ② Interest — 30 sec

> Why did you choose it?

### ③ Experience — 1 min

> What was easy/difficult?

### ④ Learning Method — 1 min

> How do you learn?

### ⑤ Challenges — 30 sec

> What problems do you face?

### ⑥ Improvement — 30 sec

> How have you improved?

### ⑦ Future — 30 sec

> What do you want to learn?

### ⑧ Conclusion — 20 sec

---

# 🏆 Lesson 86 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Talk About Your Education

Answer:

1. What did you study?
2. Why did you choose it?
3. What was your favorite subject?
4. What subject was difficult?
5. What did you learn from your education?
6. How do you usually study?
7. Do you prefer theory or practice?
8. What mistakes have you learned from?
9. What skill are you currently learning?
10. What do you want to learn in the future?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Online vs Traditional Education

Use:

> **In my opinion...**

> **One advantage is...**

> **Another advantage is...**

> **However...**

> **One disadvantage is...**

> **It depends on...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — How Do You Learn New Things?

Must use:

> **At first...**

> **Gradually...**

> **I learn by...**

> **I practice...**

> **I learn from my mistakes...**

> **Over time...**

> **As a result...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 86 Golden Formula

# **BACKGROUND → WHY → EXPERIENCE → DIFFICULTY → METHOD → PRACTICE → IMPROVEMENT → BENEFIT → FUTURE → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **I've always been interested in technology.**
>
> **I decided to study it because** I enjoy problem-solving.
>
> **At first**, some topics were difficult.
>
> **Gradually**, I became more comfortable.
>
> **I learn by practicing** and working on projects.
>
> **I also learn from my mistakes.**
>
> **As a result**, I've become more confident.
>
> **In the future**, I want to keep learning.
>
> **Overall**, I believe education is a lifelong process.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 86 Main Target

আজ থেকে education/learning নিয়ে শুধু:

> ❌ **“I study. I read books. I watch videos.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What → Why → Difficulty → How → Example → Improvement → Future**

### 🔥 Lesson 82 → People & Personality

### 🔥 Lesson 83 → Places & Experiences

### 🔥 Lesson 84 → Daily Life & Habits

### 🔥 Lesson 85 → Future Plans & Goals

### 🔥 Lesson 86 → **Education & Learning**

**Today's challenge:** নিজের education/learning journey নিয়ে **3–5 মিনিট English-এ বলো**, এবং চেষ্টা করো অন্তত এই 8টি phrase ব্যবহার করতে:

**I've always been interested in → I decided to → At first → Gradually → I learn by → I struggle with → I learn from my mistakes → In the future**.

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 86

## 🗣️ Education & Learning — **Revision + Speaking Mastery**

তুমি আবার **Lesson 86** চেয়েছ, তাই এবার একই topic-টা আরও **সহজ, practical এবং speaking-focused** ভাবে করব। লক্ষ্য হবে মুখস্থ করা নয়—**নিজের কথা 3–5 মিনিট ধরে বলা**।

---

## 🎯 আজকের Main Topic

### **“My Education and Learning Experience”**

কথা বলার সময় এই 8-step formula মনে রাখবে:

> **What → Why → Experience → Difficulty → Method → Improvement → Lesson → Future**

---

# 1️⃣ What did you study?

প্রথমে নিজের education সম্পর্কে বলবে।

### Useful sentences:

> I studied Computer Science.

> I'm studying Computer Science.

> I have a background in Computer Science.

> My major was Computer Science.

### Example:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university.

---

# 2️⃣ Why did you choose it?

এখানে **because** ব্যবহার করবে।

> I chose this subject because I was interested in technology.

> I chose it because I enjoy solving problems.

> The main reason was my interest in computers.

### Golden Pattern:

> **I chose ___ because ___.**

Example:

> I chose computer science because I enjoy technology and problem-solving.

---

# 3️⃣ What was your experience?

এখানে education কেমন ছিল সেটা বলবে।

> My university experience was very interesting.

> I learned many new things.

> I worked on several projects.

> I met many interesting people.

> I learned both theoretical and practical concepts.

---

# 4️⃣ What was difficult?

এটা answer-কে natural করে।

### Useful phrases:

> At first, I found it difficult to...

> I struggled with...

> One of the most difficult subjects was...

Example:

> At first, I found programming difficult.

তারপর বলবে:

> But I didn't give up.

---

# 5️⃣ “At First → Gradually”

এই combination খুব important।

### Example:

> At first, programming was difficult for me. Gradually, I became more comfortable with it.

আরও:

> At first, I was nervous about speaking English. Gradually, I became more confident.

---

# 6️⃣ How do you learn?

এখানে নিজের learning method বলবে।

> I learn by practicing.

> I learn by watching tutorials.

> I learn by reading books.

> I learn by doing projects.

> I learn from my mistakes.

### ⭐ Best sentence:

> I learn better when I practice what I learn.

---

# 7️⃣ Theory vs Practice

Question:

### Do you prefer theory or practical learning?

Natural answer:

> I think both are important, but I prefer practical learning.

তারপর reason:

> Practical learning helps me understand concepts better.

আরও:

> When I work on a real project, I can understand how a concept is actually used.

---

# 8️⃣ Learning From Mistakes

এটা speaking-এ খুব powerful।

> I believe mistakes are part of learning.

> I learn a lot from my mistakes.

> When I make a mistake, I try to understand what went wrong.

> Then I try not to repeat the same mistake.

### ⭐ Full pattern:

> **Mistake → Understand → Correct → Improve**

Example:

> When I make a mistake, I try to understand what went wrong. Then I correct it and try to avoid making the same mistake again.

---

# 9️⃣ What are you learning now?

Present learning:

> I'm currently learning English.

> I'm currently improving my communication skills.

> I'm learning new technologies.

> I'm working on improving my technical skills.

### Natural sentence:

> Currently, I'm focusing on improving both my technical and communication skills.

---

# 🔟 Why are you learning it?

> I'm learning English because I want to communicate more confidently.

> I'm learning new technologies because I want to improve my career opportunities.

> I'm improving my communication skills because they are important in professional life.

---

# 1️⃣1️⃣ How Often Do You Practice?

Frequency ব্যবহার করো:

> every day

> regularly

> several times a week

> occasionally

Example:

> I practice English every day.

> I try to practice speaking regularly.

> I spend at least thirty minutes a day practicing English.

---

# 1️⃣2️⃣ What Has Improved?

Past → Present comparison:

> I have become more confident.

> My vocabulary has improved.

> My speaking has become better.

> I can express my ideas more clearly now.

### Excellent:

> Compared to the past, I can express my thoughts more clearly now.

---

# 1️⃣3️⃣ “Compared to the Past”

এটা Lesson 86-এর powerful structure।

> Compared to the past, I'm more confident.

> Compared to a few years ago, my English has improved.

> Compared to when I started, I understand the subject much better.

---

# 1️⃣4️⃣ What Is Still Difficult?

সবকিছু perfect দেখানোর দরকার নেই।

> I still struggle with speaking fluently.

> Sometimes I have difficulty finding the right words.

> I still need to improve my pronunciation.

তারপর solution:

> That's why I'm practicing regularly.

---

# 1️⃣5️⃣ What Is Your Learning Goal?

> My goal is to become a confident English speaker.

> My goal is to improve my technical skills.

> I want to become better at communicating my ideas.

---

# 1️⃣6️⃣ Future Learning

> In the future, I want to continue learning.

> I'd like to learn more advanced topics.

> I want to gain more practical experience.

> I hope to keep improving my skills.

### ⭐ Natural:

> I believe learning is a lifelong process, so I don't want to stop learning after finishing my formal education.

---

| gradually   | ধীরে ধীরে          |
| confidently | আত্মবিশ্বাসের সাথে |

| theoretical | তাত্ত্বিক          |

---

# 🔥 10টি Sentence Pattern মুখস্থ না করে Practice করো

### 1.

> I've always been interested in ___.

### 2.

> I chose ___ because ___.

### 3.

> At first, I found ___ difficult.

### 4.

> Gradually, I became better at ___.

### 5.

> I learn by ___.

### 6.

> I learn a lot from my mistakes.

### 7.

> Compared to the past, ___.

### 8.

> I'm currently focusing on ___.

### 9.

> My goal is to ___.

### 10.

> In the future, I'd like to ___.

---

# 🎤 Full Speaking Answer

## Question:

### Tell me about your education and learning experience.

> I've always been interested in technology, so I decided to study Computer Science and Engineering.
>
> I chose this subject because I enjoy solving problems and learning how technology works. During my education, I learned many theoretical concepts, but I also got practical experience by working on different projects.
>
> At first, some subjects were difficult for me. I sometimes struggled to understand complex concepts. However, I didn't give up. I started practicing more and working on practical projects. Gradually, I became more comfortable with difficult topics.
>
> I believe I learn better through practice. I usually learn by watching tutorials, reading documentation, taking notes, and working on projects. I also believe mistakes are an important part of learning. Whenever I make a mistake, I try to understand what went wrong and learn from it.
>
> Currently, I'm focusing on improving my English communication skills as well as my technical skills. Compared to the past, I feel more confident when explaining technical topics.
>
> However, I still need to improve my speaking fluency. That's why I'm practicing English regularly.
>
> In the future, I want to continue learning new technologies and gain more practical experience. I believe learning is a lifelong process, so I want to keep improving myself.
>
> Overall, my education has taught me not only technical knowledge but also patience, problem-solving, and the importance of continuous learning.

---

# 🎯 3-Minute Challenge

এখন কোনো answer লিখবে না। শুধু এই keywords দেখে কথা বলবে:

> **Education → Choice → Reason → Difficulty → Practice → Mistake → Improvement → Current Learning → Future Goal**

প্রথমে 1 মিনিট বলো।

তারপর একই topic **2 মিনিট** বলো।

শেষে **3 মিনিট** বলো।

প্রতিবার নতুন information যোগ করবে।

---

# 🏆 Lesson 86-এর Golden Rule

### ❌ শুধু তথ্য:

> I studied computer science. I learned programming. I am learning English.

### ✅ Conversation:

> I chose computer science because I was interested in technology. At first, programming was difficult for me, but gradually I became better through regular practice. I still have some areas to improve, so currently I'm focusing on both my technical and communication skills.

এটাই **Lesson 61–90-এর মূল লক্ষ্য**:

> **Short answer → Reason → Example → Experience → Feeling/Opinion → Future**

### 🔥 Lesson 86 = **Education + Learning + Experience + Improvement**

**আজকের target: 3–5 মিনিট কথা বলা, grammar perfect করা নয়।**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 87

# 🗣️ **Talking About Work, Jobs & Career**

### চাকরি, কাজ, career এবং workplace নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **নিজের কাজ, dream job, workplace, skills, job experience এবং career goals** নিয়ে স্বাভাবিকভাবে কথা বলতে শেখা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **CURRENT STATUS → JOB → RESPONSIBILITIES → SKILLS → EXPERIENCE → PREFERENCE → CHALLENGE → SOLUTION → CAREER GOAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I'm interested in building a career in software development. I enjoy solving problems and creating useful applications. I believe technical skills, communication, and teamwork are important in this field. My long-term goal is to become a skilled developer and work on challenging projects.

---

# 1️⃣ “What Do You Do?”

এই প্রশ্নটি everyday English-এ খুব common।

### যদি কাজ করো:

> I work as a software developer.

> I work in the IT industry.

> I work for a software company.

### যদি এখনো চাকরি না থাকে:

> I'm currently looking for a job.

> I'm currently looking for opportunities in software development.

> I'm preparing myself for a career in software development.

### Student:

> I'm currently a student.

> I'm studying computer science.

---

# 🔥 2️⃣ “What Do You Do?” vs “What Are You Doing?”

### What do you do?

তোমার profession/job:

> I work as a developer.

### What are you doing?

এখন কী করছ:

> I'm working on a project.

---

# 🧠 3️⃣ Talk About Your Job

Useful structure:

> **I work as...**

> **My main responsibility is...**

> **I usually...**

> **I also...**

Example:

> I work as a software developer. My main responsibility is developing and maintaining web applications. I also work with APIs, databases, and frontend technologies.

---

# 🔥 4️⃣ “My Main Responsibility Is...”

Very useful professional phrase:

> My main responsibility is developing software.

> My main responsibility is managing customer requests.

> My main responsibility is maintaining the system.

### Plural:

> My main responsibilities include...

Example:

> My main responsibilities include developing APIs, fixing bugs, and maintaining the database.

---

# 🧠 5️⃣ “I’m Responsible For...”

Meaning:

**আমি ...-এর দায়িত্বে আছি।**

> I'm responsible for developing APIs.

> I'm responsible for maintaining the application.

> I'm responsible for testing the software.

⚠️ Important:

> responsible for + **noun / verb-ing**

❌ I'm responsible for develop APIs.

✅ I'm responsible for developing APIs.

---

# 🔥 6️⃣ Daily Work

> I usually start my work at...

> I spend most of my time...

> I attend meetings.

> I communicate with team members.

> I work on different tasks.

> I solve technical problems.

---

# 🧠 7️⃣ “I Spend Most of My Time...”

> I spend most of my time writing code.

> I spend most of my time solving problems.

> I spend most of my time working on projects.

---

# 🔥 8️⃣ Talk About Skills

### Technical skills:

> programming

> web development

> database management

> problem-solving

> debugging

### Soft skills:

> communication

> teamwork

> time management

> leadership

> adaptability

---

# 🧠 9️⃣ “I’m Good At...”

> I'm good at solving problems.

> I'm good at working independently.

> I'm good at learning new technologies.

⚠️

> good at + **verb-ing**

❌ I'm good at solve problems.

✅ I'm good at solving problems.

---

# 🔥 10️⃣ “I’m Working On...”

মানে বর্তমানে improve করছি।

> I'm working on improving my communication skills.

> I'm working on improving my English.

> I'm working on becoming a better developer.

Very natural phrase।

---

# 🧠 11️⃣ “I Have Experience In...”

> I have experience in web development.

> I have experience in building REST APIs.

> I have experience in working with databases.

### Grammar:

> experience in + noun / verb-ing

---

# 🔥 12️⃣ “I’ve Worked On...”

Past experience:

> I've worked on several web applications.

> I've worked on e-commerce projects.

> I've worked on backend systems.

---

# 🧠 13️⃣ Talk About Projects

Use this formula:

> **Project → Your Role → Technology → Problem → Result**

Example:

> I worked on an e-commerce project where I was responsible for the backend. I used Django and Django REST Framework to build APIs. One challenge was handling complex order and payment logic. I solved it by organizing the business logic carefully.

---

# 🔥 14️⃣ “I Was Responsible For...”

Past responsibility:

> I was responsible for developing the backend.

> I was responsible for fixing bugs.

> I was responsible for database design.

---

# 🧠 15️⃣ Talk About Teamwork

Question:

> Do you like working in a team?

Answer:

> Yes, I enjoy working in a team because I can learn from other people and share ideas.

Add example:

> When working on a project, team members can divide tasks and solve problems together.

---

# 🔥 16️⃣ “Work With”

> I work with a team of developers.

> I work with designers and frontend developers.

> I work with clients.

---

# 🧠 17️⃣ “Work On” vs “Work With”

### Work on = project/task

> I work on web applications.

### Work with = people/technology

> I work with other developers.

> I work with Django.

---

# 🔥 18️⃣ What Makes a Good Employee?

Useful answer:

> I think a good employee should be responsible, reliable, adaptable, and willing to learn.

Then explain:

> Good communication and teamwork are also important.

---

# 🧠 19️⃣ Important Soft Skills

### Communication

> Good communication helps teams work effectively.

### Teamwork

> Teamwork allows people to solve problems together.

### Time management

> Good time management helps me meet deadlines.

### Adaptability

> Technology changes quickly, so adaptability is important.

---

# 🔥 20️⃣ “Meet a Deadline”

**deadline = কাজ শেষ করার নির্দিষ্ট সময়**

> I always try to meet deadlines.

> Managing my time helps me meet deadlines.

---

# 🧠 21️⃣ “Under Pressure”

**under pressure = চাপের মধ্যে**

> I can work under pressure.

> Sometimes I have to work under pressure to meet a deadline.

Better:

> I try to stay calm and focus on the most important task when I'm under pressure.

---

# 🔥 22️⃣ Workplace Problems

Question:

> What challenges do you face at work?

Possible answers:

> tight deadlines

> difficult tasks

> communication problems

> changing requirements

> technical issues

Example:

> One challenge is dealing with tight deadlines.

---

# 🧠 23️⃣ How Do You Solve Problems?

Use:

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> First, I try to understand the problem. Then I break it into smaller parts. After that, I look for possible solutions. Finally, I test the solution.

🔥 Excellent interview + speaking structure।

---

# 🔥 24️⃣ “Break a Problem Into Smaller Parts”

Very useful:

> When I face a difficult problem, I break it into smaller parts.

This makes your English sound more natural and professional.

---

# 🧠 25️⃣ Dream Job

Question:

> What is your dream job?

Answer:

> My dream job would be working as a software developer.

Then explain:

> I'd like to work on challenging projects and continuously learn new technologies.

---

# 🔥 26️⃣ Ideal Workplace

Question:

> What kind of workplace do you prefer?

Use:

> I prefer a workplace where...

Example:

> I prefer a workplace where people communicate openly and support each other.

Add:

> I also value opportunities for learning and career growth.

---

# 🧠 27️⃣ “I Value...”

**value = গুরুত্ব দেওয়া**

> I value teamwork.

> I value honesty.

> I value continuous learning.

> I value a healthy work-life balance.

---

# 🔥 28️⃣ Work-Life Balance

**work-life balance = কাজ ও ব্যক্তিগত জীবনের ভারসাম্য**

Example:

> I think work-life balance is important because people need enough time to rest and spend time with their families.

---

# 🧠 29️⃣ Job Satisfaction

Question:

> What makes a job satisfying?

Answer:

> I think a job is satisfying when I can learn new things, solve meaningful problems, and see the results of my work.

---

# 🔥 30️⃣ “Meaningful Work”

> I want to do meaningful work.

> I enjoy working on projects that solve real-world problems.

---

# 🧠 31️⃣ Career Growth

Useful:

> career growth

> professional development

> learning opportunities

> promotion

> gaining experience

Example:

> I'm looking for a job that offers good opportunities for learning and career growth.

---

# 🔥 32️⃣ “Gain Experience”

> I want to gain practical experience.

> Working on real projects helps me gain experience.

---

# 🧠 33️⃣ “Develop My Skills”

> I want to develop my technical skills.

> I also want to develop my communication skills.

---

# 🔥 34️⃣ Career Goal

Connect Lesson 85:

> My short-term career goal is to get a job where I can gain practical experience.

> My long-term goal is to become a highly skilled professional.

---

# 🧠 35️⃣ Full 2–3 Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### Tell me about your career goals.

> I'm interested in building a career in software development. I enjoy technology and problem-solving, so I think this field is a good fit for me.
>
> My short-term goal is to find a position where I can gain practical experience and improve my technical skills. I'd like to work on real-world projects and learn from experienced developers.
>
> I have experience working with technologies such as Python, Django, REST APIs, databases, and React. I've worked on several projects, and these projects have helped me understand how software is developed in real situations.
>
> I also understand that technical skills are not enough. Communication, teamwork, and time management are equally important. That's why I'm working on improving my English communication skills.
>
> One challenge I may face is adapting to a professional environment. However, I'm willing to learn and take feedback from experienced people.
>
> In the long term, I'd like to become a highly skilled software developer and work on challenging projects. I also want to continue learning new technologies throughout my career.
>
> Overall, my goal is to build a strong career, keep learning, and contribute to meaningful projects.

---

# 🧠 36️⃣ Job Vocabulary

| English        | বাংলা            |
| -------------- | ---------------- |
| career         | পেশাগত জীবন      |
| profession     | পেশা             |
| responsibility | দায়িত্ব          |
| skill          | দক্ষতা           |
| experience     | অভিজ্ঞতা         |
| employee       | কর্মচারী         |
| employer       | নিয়োগকর্তা       |
| colleague      | সহকর্মী          |
| workplace      | কর্মক্ষেত্র      |
| deadline       | নির্ধারিত সময়    |
| salary         | বেতন             |
| position       | পদ               |
| opportunity    | সুযোগ            |
| promotion      | পদোন্নতি         |
| responsibility | দায়িত্ব          |
| teamwork       | দলগত কাজ         |
| leadership     | নেতৃত্ব          |
| communication  | যোগাযোগ          |
| productivity   | উৎপাদনশীলতা      |
| career growth  | ক্যারিয়ার উন্নতি |

---

# 🔥 37️⃣ Important Speaking Patterns

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

> **I work as...**

> **I'm currently looking for...**

> **My main responsibility is...**

> **I'm responsible for...**

> **I have experience in...**

> **I've worked on...**

> **I'm good at...**

> **I'm working on improving...**

> **I enjoy working with...**

> **One challenge is...**

> **To solve this problem...**

> **My short-term goal is...**

> **My long-term goal is...**

> **I'd like to...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 38️⃣ Common Mistakes

### ❌

> I am working as a developer since two years.

### Better:

> I've been working as a developer for two years.

---

### ❌

> I am responsible to develop APIs.

### ✅

> I'm responsible for developing APIs.

---

### ❌

> I'm good in programming.

### ✅

> I'm good at programming.

---

### ❌

> I have experience to work with Django.

### ✅

> I have experience working with Django.

---

### ❌

> I want a job where I can earning experience.

### ✅

> I want a job where I can gain experience.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 87 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Talk About Your Work

Answer:

1. What do you do?
2. What is your job?
3. What are your responsibilities?
4. What skills do you use?
5. What do you enjoy about your work?
6. What is difficult?
7. How do you solve problems?
8. Do you prefer teamwork?
9. What kind of workplace do you prefer?
10. What are your career goals?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Your Dream Job

Use:

> **My dream job would be...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **I'd like to...**

> **I would also like to...**

> **One important thing for me is...**

> **In the long term...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Job Interview Practice

### Question 1:

**Tell me about yourself.**

### Question 2:

**What are your strengths?**

### Question 3:

**What is one weakness you're working on?**

### Question 4:

**Why do you want this job?**

### Question 5:

**Where do you see yourself in five years?**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 87 Golden Formula

# **JOB → RESPONSIBILITY → SKILLS → EXPERIENCE → CHALLENGE → SOLUTION → PREFERENCE → GOAL → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **I'm interested in software development.**
>
> **My main responsibility is** building software and solving technical problems.
>
> **I have experience in** working with different technologies.
>
> **One challenge is** dealing with difficult problems and deadlines.
>
> **To solve problems**, I break them into smaller parts.
>
> **I prefer a workplace where** people communicate openly and support each other.
>
> **My short-term goal is** to gain practical experience.
>
> **In the long term**, I want to become a highly skilled developer.
>
> **Overall**, I want to keep learning and build a successful career.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 87 Main Target

আজ থেকে career নিয়ে শুধু:

> ❌ **“I want a good job.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What job → Why → Skills → Experience → Responsibility → Challenge → Solution → Career Goal**

### 🔥 Lesson 83 → Places & Experiences

### 🔥 Lesson 84 → Daily Life & Habits

### 🔥 Lesson 85 → Future Plans & Goals

### 🔥 Lesson 86 → Education & Learning

### 🔥 Lesson 87 → **Work, Jobs & Career**

**Today's challenge:** নিজের career নিয়ে **3–5 মিনিট English-এ বলো**। বিশেষ করে এই 8টি phrase ব্যবহার করো:

**I'm interested in → My main responsibility → I have experience in → I'm good at → One challenge is → To solve this → My short-term goal → My long-term goal**.


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 88

# 🗣️ **Talking About Hobbies & Free Time**

### Hobby, অবসর সময়, পছন্দের কাজ এবং কীভাবে free time কাটাও—এসব নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো hobby নিয়ে শুধু **“I like reading.”** না বলে **কী পছন্দ করো → কেন → কখন → কার সাথে → কতবার → কীভাবে শুরু করেছ → কী benefit পাও → ভবিষ্যতে কী করতে চাও**—এভাবে কথা বলা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

> **HOBBY → WHEN → HOW OFTEN → WHY → HOW STARTED → EXPERIENCE → BENEFIT → PREFERENCE → FUTURE → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> One of my favorite hobbies is reading. I usually read in my free time, especially in the evening. I enjoy reading because it helps me learn new things and relax. I started reading regularly a few years ago, and since then it has become an important part of my routine.

---

# 1️⃣ What Do You Do in Your Free Time?

সবচেয়ে common question:

> **What do you do in your free time?**

Possible answers:

> I usually read books.

> I watch movies.

> I listen to music.

> I play games.

> I exercise.

> I spend time with my friends.

> I work on personal projects.

### Better answer:

> In my free time, I usually read, watch movies, and work on personal projects.

---

# 🔥 2️⃣ “My Favorite Hobby Is...”

> My favorite hobby is reading.

> My favorite hobby is photography.

> One of my favorite hobbies is playing football.

> One of my favorite hobbies is learning new things.

### ⭐ More natural:

> One of my favorite hobbies is reading because it helps me relax and learn new things.

---

# 🧠 3️⃣ “I Enjoy + ing”

এটা খুব important।

> I enjoy reading.

> I enjoy watching movies.

> I enjoy playing football.

> I enjoy listening to music.

⚠️

❌ I enjoy to read.

✅ I enjoy reading.

---

# 🔥 4️⃣ “I Like + ing”

> I like reading books.

> I like watching movies.

> I like traveling.

> I like spending time with friends.

---

# 🧠 5️⃣ “I’m Into...”

**I'm into = আমি খুব interested / পছন্দ করি**

Informal spoken English:

> I'm really into photography.

> I'm into technology.

> I'm really into football.

> I'm into watching documentaries.

---

# 🔥 6️⃣ “I’m Interested In...”

> I'm interested in photography.

> I'm interested in learning languages.

> I'm interested in technology.

⚠️

> interested in + noun / verb-ing

❌ I'm interested in learn English.

✅ I'm interested in learning English.

---

# 🧠 7️⃣ How Often?

Frequency ব্যবহার করবে:

> every day

> every weekend

> once a week

> twice a week

> occasionally

> from time to time

> whenever I have time

### Examples:

> I read almost every day.

> I play football twice a week.

> I watch movies from time to time.

> I work on personal projects whenever I have time.

---

# 🔥 8️⃣ “Whenever I Have Time”

Very useful:

> I usually read whenever I have time.

> I practice English whenever I have free time.

> I watch movies whenever I get a chance.

---

# 🧠 9️⃣ When Do You Usually Do It?

> I usually do it in the evening.

> I usually practice at night.

> I normally do it on weekends.

> I often do it after work.

---

# 🔥 10️⃣ Where?

> I usually do it at home.

> I play football at a local field.

> I like reading in my room.

> I enjoy walking in the park.

---

# 🧠 11️⃣ Who Do You Do It With?

> I usually do it alone.

> I do it with my friends.

> I enjoy playing games with my friends.

> I usually watch movies with my family.

### Important:

> **alone = একা**

> **with someone = কারো সাথে**

---

# 🔥 12️⃣ Why Do You Like It?

এই question-এর answer সবচেয়ে important।

Use:

> I like it because...

> The main reason is...

> One reason I enjoy it is...

Example:

> I enjoy reading because it helps me learn new things.

আরও:

> I like playing football because it keeps me active and allows me to spend time with my friends.

---

# 🧠 13️⃣ “It Helps Me...”

Very useful pattern:

> It helps me relax.

> It helps me stay healthy.

> It helps me learn new things.

> It helps me reduce stress.

> It helps me improve my creativity.

---

# 🔥 14️⃣ Hobby + Benefit

### Reading:

> Reading helps me improve my vocabulary.

### Exercise:

> Exercise helps me stay healthy.

### Music:

> Music helps me relax.

### Photography:

> Photography helps me become more creative.

---

# 🧠 15️⃣ “It’s a Good Way To...”

Excellent spoken structure:

> It's a good way to relax.

> It's a good way to stay healthy.

> It's a good way to spend my free time.

> It's a good way to learn something new.

Example:

> Reading is a good way to relax after a busy day.

---

# 🔥 16️⃣ How Did You Start?

Question:

> How did you get into this hobby?

Start:

> I started...

> I became interested in it when...

> I first got interested in it because...

Example:

> I started reading regularly when I was in university.

---

# 🧠 17️⃣ “I Got Into...”

**get into = কোনো বিষয়ে আগ্রহী হয়ে ওঠা**

> I got into photography a few years ago.

> I got into programming when I was in college.

> I got into reading because of my friends.

Very natural spoken English।

---

# 🔥 18️⃣ “I Used To...”

Past habit:

> I used to play football regularly.

> I used to watch movies every weekend.

> I used to spend a lot of time playing games.

তারপর বর্তমান:

> But these days, I don't have as much time.

---

# 🧠 19️⃣ “These Days”

Current situation:

> These days, I mostly read books.

> These days, I don't watch many movies.

> These days, I spend more time learning new skills.

---

# 🔥 20️⃣ Free Time vs Busy Time

Natural comparison:

> When I'm busy, I don't get much free time.

> When I have more free time, I like to read or watch movies.

---

# 🧠 21️⃣ Weekend Activities

Question:

> What do you usually do on weekends?

Answer:

> On weekends, I usually relax at home, spend time with my family, and work on personal projects.

---

# 🔥 22️⃣ Indoor vs Outdoor Activities

Question:

> Do you prefer indoor or outdoor activities?

### Indoor:

> I prefer indoor activities because they're more convenient.

### Outdoor:

> I prefer outdoor activities because I enjoy being active.

### Balanced:

> I enjoy both, but it depends on my mood and the weather.

---

# 🧠 23️⃣ “It Depends On...”

> It depends on the weather.

> It depends on how much free time I have.

> It depends on my mood.

Example:

> I enjoy both indoor and outdoor activities. It mostly depends on the weather and how much free time I have.

---

# 🔥 24️⃣ Hobby With Friends

> I enjoy spending time with my friends.

> We usually play football together.

> Sometimes we go out for coffee.

> We also talk about our work and future plans.

---

# 🧠 25️⃣ Hobby With Family

> I like spending my free time with my family.

> We sometimes watch movies together.

> Sometimes we go out for dinner.

---

# 🔥 26️⃣ Hobby That Improves You

এটা higher-level speaking।

> One hobby that has helped me improve myself is reading.

> It has improved my vocabulary and general knowledge.

> It has also helped me become more patient.

---

# 🧠 27️⃣ Hobby + Personality

> Reading has made me more curious.

> Sports have made me more disciplined.

> Photography has made me more observant.

> Traveling has made me more open-minded.

---

# 🔥 28️⃣ “I Find It...”

> I find reading relaxing.

> I find photography exciting.

> I find exercise refreshing.

### Structure:

> **I find + noun + adjective**

---

# 🧠 29️⃣ Relaxation

Useful phrases:

> It helps me unwind.

**unwind = মানসিকভাবে relax করা**

> I listen to music to unwind after a busy day.

> Reading helps me unwind.

---

# 🔥 30️⃣ “Take My Mind Off...”

Very natural:

> Watching movies helps me take my mind off work.

Meaning:

**কাজ/চিন্তা থেকে কিছু সময়ের জন্য মন সরিয়ে নেওয়া।**

---

# 🧠 31️⃣ Future Hobby

Connect Lesson 85:

> In the future, I'd like to learn photography.

> I'd like to start traveling more.

> I hope to learn how to play the guitar.

> I'd like to spend more time exercising.

---

# 🔥 32️⃣ Full 2–3 Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### What do you like to do in your free time?

> In my free time, I enjoy doing several things. One of my favorite activities is reading. I usually read in the evening or whenever I have some free time.
>
> I enjoy reading because it's a good way to relax and learn new things at the same time. I especially like books and articles related to technology, personal development, and general knowledge.
>
> I started reading more regularly when I was in university. At first, I didn't read very often, but gradually it became a habit. These days, I try to read regularly, even if I only have a little time.
>
> Apart from reading, I also enjoy watching movies and listening to music. Watching movies helps me relax after a busy day, while music helps me clear my mind.
>
> I also like working on personal projects in my free time. It's a good way to practice my technical skills and learn something new.
>
> Sometimes I spend my free time with friends or family. We might go out, have a conversation, or watch a movie together.
>
> In the future, I'd like to develop some new hobbies, especially traveling and photography. I think hobbies are important because they help us relax, stay balanced, and continue learning outside of work or study.
>
> Overall, I try to use my free time in a way that is both enjoyable and useful.

---

# 🧠 33️⃣ Hobby Vocabulary

| English          | বাংলা                |
| ---------------- | -------------------- |
| hobby            | শখ                   |
| free time        | অবসর সময়             |
| leisure          | অবসর                 |
| relax            | আরাম করা             |
| unwind           | মানসিকভাবে relax করা |
| exercise         | ব্যায়াম              |
| reading          | পড়া                  |
| photography      | ফটোগ্রাফি            |
| traveling        | ভ্রমণ                |
| gardening        | বাগান করা            |
| cooking          | রান্না               |
| painting         | ছবি আঁকা             |
| gaming           | গেম খেলা             |
| music            | সংগীত                |
| movie            | সিনেমা               |
| outdoor activity | বাইরের কাজ           |
| indoor activity  | ঘরের কাজ             |
| creative         | সৃজনশীল              |
| active           | সক্রিয়               |
| refreshing       | সতেজকর               |

---

# 🔥 34️⃣ Most Useful Sentence Patterns

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

> **In my free time, I usually...**

> **One of my favorite hobbies is...**

> **I enjoy + ing...**

> **I'm really into...**

> **I'm interested in...**

> **I usually do it...**

> **I do it with...**

> **I like it because...**

> **It helps me...**

> **It's a good way to...**

> **I started...**

> **I got into...**

> **These days,...**

> **It depends on...**

> **In the future, I'd like to...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 35️⃣ Common Mistakes

### ❌

> I enjoy to read books.

### ✅

> I enjoy reading books.

---

### ❌

> My hobby is play football.

### ✅

> My hobby is playing football.

---

### ❌

> I am interested to photography.

### ✅

> I'm interested in photography.

---

### ❌

> I like watch movies.

### ✅

> I like watching movies.

---

### ❌

> I spend my free time to play games.

### Better:

> I spend my free time playing games.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 88 Final Practice

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Your Hobbies

Answer:

1. What do you do in your free time?
2. What is your favorite hobby?
3. How often do you do it?
4. When do you usually do it?
5. Where do you do it?
6. Who do you do it with?
7. Why do you enjoy it?
8. How did you start?
9. What benefits do you get from it?
10. What new hobby would you like to try?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — How Do You Spend Your Weekend?

Use:

> **On weekends,...**

> **Usually,...**

> **Sometimes,...**

> **If I have enough time,...**

> **I enjoy...**

> **It helps me...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Your Favorite Hobby

Try to speak using this order:

> **What → When → How often → Why → How started → Benefit → Future**

Don't stop after:

> “My hobby is reading.”

Continue:

> “My hobby is reading. I usually read in the evening. I started reading regularly when I was in university. I enjoy it because…”

---

# 🧠 Lesson 88 Golden Formula

# **HOBBY → WHEN → FREQUENCY → WHY → HOW STARTED → EXPERIENCE → BENEFIT → PEOPLE → PREFERENCE → FUTURE**

Example:

> **One of my favorite hobbies is reading.**
>
> **I usually** read in the evening.
>
> **I enjoy it because** it helps me relax.
>
> **I started** reading regularly a few years ago.
>
> **Gradually**, it became a habit.
>
> **It helps me** improve my vocabulary and knowledge.
>
> **In the future**, I'd like to explore more types of books.
>
> **Overall**, reading is both enjoyable and useful for me.

---

## 🎯 Lesson 88 Main Target

আজ hobby নিয়ে শুধু:

> ❌ **“I like reading.”**

বলবে না।

বরং:

> **What → When → How often → Why → Example → Benefit → Future**

### 🔥 Lesson 86 → Education & Learning

### 🔥 Lesson 87 → Work, Jobs & Career

### 🔥 Lesson 88 → **Hobbies & Free Time**

**Today's challenge:** তোমার একটি hobby নিয়ে **3–5 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলো** এবং এই 8টি phrase ব্যবহার করার চেষ্টা করো:

**In my free time → One of my favorite hobbies → I enjoy → I'm interested in → I started → Gradually → It helps me → In the future, I'd like to**.

# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 89

# 🗣️ **Talking About Food, Cooking & Eating Habits**

### খাবার, রান্না, পছন্দ-অপছন্দ এবং eating habits নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো খাবার নিয়ে শুধু:

> ❌ “I like rice.”

না বলে **কী খাবার পছন্দ → কেন → কখন খাও → কে রান্না করে → কীভাবে তৈরি হয় → healthy/unhealthy → preference → experience → future**—এভাবে কথা বলা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

> **FOOD → PREFERENCE → REASON → HABIT → COOKING → EXPERIENCE → HEALTH → COMPARISON → FUTURE → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> I enjoy eating traditional food, especially rice with vegetables and fish. I like these foods because they are tasty and familiar to me. I usually have three meals a day, although my eating habits can change when I'm busy. I also enjoy trying new dishes from time to time.

---

# 1️⃣ What Is Your Favorite Food?

Question:

> **What's your favorite food?**

Start:

> My favorite food is...

> One of my favorite foods is...

Example:

> One of my favorite foods is biryani.

Then don't stop.

Add:

> I like it because it's flavorful and filling.

---

# 🔥 2️⃣ “I Like... Because...”

Very important:

> I like biryani because it is flavorful.

> I like vegetables because they are healthy.

> I like homemade food because it tastes fresh.

### Formula:

> **I like + food + because + reason**

---

# 🧠 3️⃣ “I’m a Big Fan of...”

Natural spoken English:

> I'm a big fan of spicy food.

> I'm a big fan of homemade meals.

> I'm not a big fan of very sweet food.

Meaning:

**big fan of = খুব পছন্দ করি**

---

# 🔥 4️⃣ Food Preference

Useful expressions:

### Strong preference:

> I really like...

> I absolutely love...

> I'm a big fan of...

### Normal:

> I like...

> I enjoy...

### Neutral:

> I don't mind...

### Negative:

> I'm not a big fan of...

> I don't really like...

---

# 🧠 5️⃣ “I Prefer A to B”

খুব important structure:

> I prefer rice to bread.

> I prefer homemade food to fast food.

> I prefer tea to coffee.

### Formula:

> **I prefer A to B.**

⚠️ এখানে **than** ব্যবহার করবে না।

❌ I prefer tea than coffee.

✅ I prefer tea to coffee.

---

# 🔥 6️⃣ Why Do You Like It?

Use:

> because it's delicious.

> because it's healthy.

> because it's easy to prepare.

> because it reminds me of home.

> because I grew up eating it.

### ⭐ Excellent:

> I like it because it reminds me of my childhood.

---

# 🧠 7️⃣ “It Reminds Me Of...”

**মনে করিয়ে দেয়**

> This food reminds me of my childhood.

> The smell reminds me of home.

> This dish reminds me of family gatherings.

Very useful for storytelling।

---

# 🔥 8️⃣ What Do You Usually Eat?

Question:

> What do you usually eat in a normal day?

Structure:

### Breakfast:

> For breakfast, I usually have...

### Lunch:

> For lunch, I usually eat...

### Dinner:

> For dinner, I usually have...

Example:

> For breakfast, I usually have eggs and bread. For lunch, I usually eat rice with vegetables or fish. For dinner, I prefer something light.

---

# 🧠 9️⃣ “For Breakfast / Lunch / Dinner”

মনে রাখবে:

> **For breakfast**

> **For lunch**

> **For dinner**

Example:

> I usually have fruit for breakfast.

---

# 🔥 10️⃣ Eating Habits

Useful:

> I usually eat three meals a day.

> I try to eat on time.

> I try to eat healthy food.

> I drink plenty of water.

> I sometimes eat snacks between meals.

---

# 🧠 11️⃣ “Try To”

> I try to eat healthy food.

> I try to avoid too much sugar.

> I try to drink enough water.

### Formula:

> **try to + base verb**

---

# 🔥 12️⃣ Healthy Food

Vocabulary:

> vegetables

> fruits

> fish

> eggs

> milk

> nuts

> whole grains

> beans

Useful sentence:

> I try to include more vegetables and fruits in my diet.

---

# 🧠 13️⃣ Unhealthy Food

> fast food

> fried food

> sugary drinks

> processed food

> too much sugar

Example:

> I try not to eat too much fried food.

---

# 🔥 14️⃣ “I Try to Avoid...”

> I try to avoid junk food.

> I try to avoid sugary drinks.

> I try to avoid eating too late at night.

### Formula:

> **avoid + noun / verb-ing**

❌ I avoid to eat junk food.

✅ I avoid eating junk food.

---

# 🧠 15️⃣ Cooking

Question:

> Can you cook?

### Yes:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes.

### Not much:

> I can cook, but I'm not an expert.

### No:

> I'm not very good at cooking yet.

---

# 🔥 16️⃣ “I’m Good At Cooking...”

> I'm good at cooking simple meals.

> I'm good at making eggs.

> I'm good at preparing rice and vegetables.

### Grammar:

> good at + **verb-ing**

---

# 🧠 17️⃣ How Often Do You Cook?

> I cook almost every day.

> I cook occasionally.

> I usually cook on weekends.

> I don't cook very often.

---

# 🔥 18️⃣ “I Usually Make...”

> I usually make simple meals.

> I usually make rice, eggs, and vegetables.

> On weekends, I sometimes make something special.

---

# 🧠 19️⃣ Cooking Process

Useful sequence:

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> First, I prepare the ingredients. Then I heat the pan. After that, I cook everything together. Finally, I serve the food.

🔥 এই structure speaking fluency বাড়াতে খুব useful।

---

# 🔥 20️⃣ “Ingredients”

**ingredients = রান্নার উপকরণ**

> The main ingredients are rice, vegetables, chicken, and spices.

---

# 🧠 21️⃣ “Spicy”

> I like spicy food.

> I don't like food that is too spicy.

> I prefer mildly spicy food.

### Useful:

> **mildly spicy = হালকা ঝাল**

---

# 🔥 22️⃣ Taste Vocabulary

| English   | বাংলা      |
| --------- | ---------- |
| delicious | সুস্বাদু   |
| tasty     | সুস্বাদু   |
| sweet     | মিষ্টি     |
| salty     | লবণাক্ত    |
| spicy     | ঝাল        |
| sour      | টক         |
| bitter    | তিতা       |
| fresh     | তাজা       |
| crispy    | মচমচে      |
| soft      | নরম        |
| juicy     | রসালো      |
| flavorful | স্বাদযুক্ত |

---

# 🧠 23️⃣ “It Tastes...”

> It tastes delicious.

> It tastes spicy.

> It tastes sweet.

> It tastes a little salty.

### Smell:

> It smells amazing.

---

# 🔥 24️⃣ Homemade vs Restaurant Food

Question:

> Do you prefer homemade or restaurant food?

### Answer:

> I prefer homemade food because it's usually fresher and healthier.

Add:

> However, I enjoy eating at restaurants occasionally.

---

# 🧠 25️⃣ “Usually... But Sometimes...”

Excellent balanced structure:

> I usually eat homemade food, but sometimes I go to a restaurant with my friends.

আরও:

> I usually eat healthy food, but sometimes I enjoy fast food.

---

# 🔥 26️⃣ Eating Out

Useful:

> eat out = বাইরে restaurant-এ খাওয়া

> I don't eat out very often.

> I sometimes eat out with my friends.

> We usually eat out on weekends.

---

# 🧠 27️⃣ Restaurant Experience

Question:

> Tell me about a memorable meal.

Structure:

> **Where → Who → What → Taste → Experience → Feeling**

Example:

> A few months ago, I went to a restaurant with my friends. We ordered several dishes and shared them. The food was delicious, and we had a great time talking and laughing.

---

# 🔥 28️⃣ “We Shared the Food”

Useful:

> We ordered several dishes and shared them.

**share = ভাগ করে নেওয়া**

---

# 🧠 29️⃣ Food Culture

Question:

> Why is food important in your culture?

Possible answer:

> Food is an important part of our culture because it brings people together.

Then:

> Families often eat together during special occasions and celebrations.

---

# 🔥 30️⃣ Special Occasions

> birthdays

> weddings

> festivals

> family gatherings

> celebrations

Example:

> Special meals are often prepared during festivals and family gatherings.

---

# 🧠 31️⃣ Food and Family

> Food brings my family together.

> We often have meals together.

> Some dishes remind me of my family.

### Excellent:

> For me, food is not just about taste; it's also about memories and relationships.

🔥 Very strong speaking sentence।

---

# 🔥 32️⃣ Favorite Drink

Question:

> What's your favorite drink?

> My favorite drink is tea.

Then:

> I usually drink it in the morning or in the evening.

---

# 🧠 33️⃣ Tea vs Coffee

> I prefer tea to coffee.

> I enjoy both, but I usually choose tea.

> I don't drink coffee very often.

---

# 🔥 34️⃣ Trying New Food

Question:

> Do you like trying new food?

> Yes, I enjoy trying new dishes because it's a good way to experience different cultures.

Excellent sentence।

---

# 🧠 35️⃣ “I'm Open To...”

**open to = নতুন কিছু চেষ্টা করতে আগ্রহী**

> I'm open to trying new foods.

> I'm open to trying different cuisines.

---

# 🔥 36️⃣ Favorite Cuisine

**cuisine = কোনো দেশের/অঞ্চলের খাবারের ধরন**

> I really enjoy Bengali cuisine.

> I'm interested in trying different cuisines.

> Italian cuisine is also interesting to me.

---

# 🧠 37️⃣ Food and Health

Balanced answer:

> I think food has a big impact on our health.

> Eating a balanced diet is important.

> We should eat a variety of foods.

---

# 🔥 38️⃣ Full 2–3 Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### Tell me about your food preferences and eating habits.

> I enjoy a variety of foods, but I'm especially fond of traditional homemade food. I like homemade meals because they are usually fresh, healthy, and familiar.
>
> I usually eat three meals a day. For breakfast, I normally have something simple. For lunch, I usually eat rice with vegetables, fish, or other dishes. For dinner, I prefer something lighter.
>
> I enjoy spicy food, but I don't like food that is extremely spicy. I also try to include vegetables and fruits in my diet. I try to avoid eating too much fried food and sugary food.
>
> I can cook a few simple dishes. I don't cook every day, but I sometimes cook on weekends. When I cook, I usually prepare simple meals. First, I prepare the ingredients. Then I cook them step by step. Finally, I serve the food.
>
> I usually prefer homemade food to restaurant food, but I enjoy eating out occasionally. Sometimes I go to restaurants with my friends, especially on weekends. I enjoy trying new dishes because food is also a way to experience different cultures.
>
> I think food is more than just something we eat. It can create memories and bring people together. Family meals and special occasions are especially meaningful to me.
>
> In the future, I'd like to learn how to cook more dishes and develop healthier eating habits.
>
> Overall, I want to enjoy my food while also maintaining a balanced and healthy lifestyle.

---

# 🧠 39️⃣ Food Vocabulary

| English       | বাংলা             |
| ------------- | ----------------- |
| meal          | খাবারের সময়/খাবার |
| breakfast     | সকালের খাবার      |
| lunch         | দুপুরের খাবার     |
| dinner        | রাতের খাবার       |
| snack         | হালকা খাবার       |
| dish          | খাবারের একটি পদ   |
| ingredient    | উপকরণ             |
| recipe        | রান্নার রেসিপি    |
| cuisine       | খাবারের ধরন       |
| homemade      | বাড়িতে তৈরি       |
| restaurant    | রেস্টুরেন্ট       |
| healthy       | স্বাস্থ্যকর       |
| unhealthy     | অস্বাস্থ্যকর      |
| spicy         | ঝাল               |
| delicious     | সুস্বাদু          |
| taste         | স্বাদ             |
| flavor        | স্বাদ/ঘ্রাণ       |
| diet          | খাদ্যাভ্যাস       |
| balanced diet | সুষম খাদ্য        |
| cook          | রান্না করা        |
| prepare       | প্রস্তুত করা      |

---

# 🔥 40️⃣ Most Useful Sentence Patterns

আজ এগুলো practice করবে:

> **One of my favorite foods is...**

> **I'm a big fan of...**

> **I prefer A to B.**

> **I like it because...**

> **It reminds me of...**

> **For breakfast...**

> **For lunch...**

> **For dinner...**

> **I usually eat...**

> **I try to...**

> **I try to avoid...**

> **I'm good at...**

> **I usually cook...**

> **I don't eat out very often.**

> **I'm open to trying...**

> **In the future, I'd like to...**

> **Overall,...**

---

# 🧠 41️⃣ Common Mistakes

### ❌

> I prefer rice than bread.

### ✅

> I prefer rice to bread.

---

### ❌

> I like to eating rice.

### ✅

> I like eating rice.

অথবা:

> I like to eat rice.

---

### ❌

> I am good in cooking.

### ✅

> I'm good at cooking.

---

### ❌

> I avoid to eat fast food.

### ✅

> I avoid eating fast food.

---

### ❌

> I eat restaurant.

### ✅

> I eat at restaurants.

অথবা:

> I eat out.

---

# 🏆 Lesson 89 Final Practice

Answer:

1. What's your favorite food?
2. Why do you like it?
3. How often do you eat it?
4. Who usually prepares it?
5. Can you cook it?
6. What ingredients does it have?
7. What does it taste like?
8. Does it remind you of anything?
9. Is it healthy?
10. Would you recommend it to others?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Your Daily Eating Habits

Talk about:

> Breakfast → Lunch → Snacks → Dinner → Drinks → Healthy habits → Unhealthy habits

Try to use:

> **usually → sometimes → often → rarely → occasionally**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Homemade Food vs Restaurant Food

Use:

> **I prefer...**

> **One reason is...**

> **Another reason is...**

> **However...**

> **Sometimes...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 89 Golden Formula

# **FOOD → PREFERENCE → REASON → HABIT → COOKING → EXPERIENCE → HEALTH → COMPARISON → FUTURE → CONCLUSION**

Example:

> **I'm a big fan of homemade food.**
>
> **I prefer it to restaurant food because** it's usually fresh.
>
> **For breakfast**, I usually have something simple.
>
> **I try to** eat vegetables and fruits regularly.
>
> **I sometimes cook** on weekends.
>
> **However**, I enjoy eating out occasionally.
>
> **In the future**, I'd like to learn more recipes.
>
> **Overall**, I want to enjoy food while maintaining a healthy lifestyle.

আজ খাবার নিয়ে শুধু:

বরং:
