# 🟢 English Speaking Course — Lesson 90

# 🗣️ **Talking About Travel, Places & Trips**

### ভ্রমণ, জায়গা, trip, travel experience এবং future travel plans নিয়ে 2–5 মিনিট কথা বলা

🎯 **আজকের বড় লক্ষ্য:** Lesson 61–90-এর শেষ lesson হিসেবে এমনভাবে কথা বলা, যাতে একটি topic নিয়ে **কারণ + example + experience + opinion + future plan** যোগ করে conversation চালিয়ে যেতে পারো।

---

# 🎯 Today's Golden Formula

আজকের structure:

> **PLACE → WHEN → WHO → WHY → JOURNEY → EXPERIENCE → PROBLEM → FEELING → LESSON → FUTURE**

Example:

> I really enjoy traveling because it gives me a chance to see new places and experience different cultures. Last year, I visited a beautiful place with my friends. The journey was a little tiring, but the overall experience was wonderful. In the future, I'd like to visit more places and learn about different cultures.

---

# 1️⃣ Do You Like Traveling?

Question:

> **Do you like traveling?**

Simple:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling.

Better:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling because I like exploring new places and experiencing different cultures.

🔥 আরও:

> Traveling also helps me take a break from my daily routine.

---

# 2️⃣ “I Enjoy Traveling Because...”

> I enjoy traveling because it gives me new experiences.

> I enjoy traveling because I can meet new people.

> I enjoy traveling because I can see beautiful places.

### Formula:

> **I enjoy traveling because + reason.**

---

# 3️⃣ Favorite Place

Question:

> What's your favorite place you've visited?

Start:

> One of my favorite places is...

> One of the most beautiful places I've visited is...

Example:

> One of my favorite places is Cox's Bazar because of its beautiful beach and relaxing atmosphere.

---

# 🔥 4️⃣ “I’ve Been To...”

Very useful Present Perfect:

> I've been to Cox's Bazar.

> I've been to several places in Bangladesh.

> I've been to Dhaka many times.

Meaning:

**আমি সেখানে গিয়েছি/ভ্রমণ করেছি।**

---

# 🧠 5️⃣ “I Went To...”

Specific past trip:

> I went to Cox's Bazar last year.

> I went there with my friends.

### Difference:

> **I've been to Cox's Bazar.** → experience

> **I went to Cox's Bazar last year.** → specific past time

---

# 🔥 6️⃣ When Did You Go?

Useful:

> Last year...

> A few months ago...

> When I was a child...

> During my vacation...

> During the holidays...

Example:

> I visited the place last year during my vacation.

---

# 🧠 7️⃣ Who Did You Go With?

> I went with my friends.

> I went with my family.

> I traveled with my classmates.

> I traveled alone.

### Important:

> **with + person**

---

# 🔥 8️⃣ Why Did You Go There?

Use:

> I went there because...

> The main reason was...

> We wanted to...

Example:

> We went there because we wanted to relax and spend some time together.

---

# 🧠 9️⃣ How Did You Travel?

Transportation vocabulary:

> by bus

> by train

> by car

> by motorcycle

> by plane

> by boat

Examples:

> We traveled by bus.

> We went there by train.

> I traveled by plane.

⚠️

❌ by a bus

✅ by bus

---

# 🔥 10️⃣ “The Journey”

**journey = ভ্রমণের পথ/যাত্রা**

> The journey was comfortable.

> The journey was quite long.

> The journey took about six hours.

> The journey was tiring but enjoyable.

---

# 🧠 11️⃣ “It Took...”

Very useful:

> It took about five hours to get there.

> It took us three hours to reach the destination.

### Formula:

> **It took + time + to + verb**

---

# 🔥 12️⃣ Destination

**destination = গন্তব্য**

> Our destination was Cox's Bazar.

> We finally reached our destination in the afternoon.

---

# 🧠 13️⃣ What Did You Do There?

Use past tense:

> We visited several places.

> We walked along the beach.

> We took pictures.

> We tried local food.

> We talked to local people.

> We explored the area.

---

# 🔥 14️⃣ “We Explored...”

**explore = ঘুরে ঘুরে দেখা/অন্বেষণ করা**

> We explored the city.

> We explored the local area.

> We explored some interesting places.

---

# 🧠 15️⃣ “We Took Pictures”

Very common:

> We took a lot of pictures.

> I took some beautiful pictures.

⚠️

❌ We did pictures.

✅ We took pictures.

---

# 🔥 16️⃣ Describe a Place

Use these adjectives:

> beautiful

> peaceful

> crowded

> busy

> quiet

> clean

> scenic

> attractive

> historical

> modern

> traditional

Example:

> The place was beautiful and peaceful.

---

# 🧠 17️⃣ “The Atmosphere Was...”

Excellent phrase:

> The atmosphere was peaceful.

> The atmosphere was lively.

> The atmosphere was relaxing.

> The atmosphere was crowded.

---

# 🔥 18️⃣ “The Scenery Was...”

**scenery = প্রাকৃতিক দৃশ্য**

> The scenery was amazing.

> The scenery was beautiful.

> I really enjoyed the natural scenery.

---

# 🧠 19️⃣ Food During Travel

> We tried some local food.

> The food was delicious.

> I tried a dish I'd never eaten before.

> The local food was one of the highlights of the trip.

🔥 **highlight = সবচেয়ে ভালো/স্মরণীয় অংশ**

---

# 🔥 20️⃣ “One of the Highlights Was...”

> One of the highlights of the trip was visiting the beach.

> One of the highlights was trying local food.

Very useful for longer answers।

---

# 🧠 21️⃣ Unexpected Problem

Real stories become interesting when you add a problem.

> We got lost.

> The weather suddenly changed.

> Our bus was late.

> We couldn't find the hotel.

> It started raining.

---

# 🔥 22️⃣ “Unfortunately...”

Use this to introduce a problem:

> Unfortunately, it started raining.

> Unfortunately, our bus was delayed.

> Unfortunately, we got lost.

---

# 🧠 23️⃣ How Did You Solve It?

Use:

> First,...

> Then,...

> After that,...

> Finally,...

Example:

> First, we checked the map. Then we asked a local person for directions. Finally, we found our way back.

---

# 🔥 24️⃣ “Get Lost”

**get lost = পথ হারিয়ে ফেলা**

Past:

> We got lost.

Present:

> I sometimes get lost in unfamiliar places.

---

# 🧠 25️⃣ How Did You Feel?

Important emotional vocabulary:

> excited

> happy

> relaxed

> nervous

> surprised

> tired

> amazed

> disappointed

Example:

> I felt excited because it was my first time visiting the place.

---

# 🔥 26️⃣ “I Was Amazed By...”

> I was amazed by the natural beauty.

> I was amazed by the scenery.

> I was amazed by how friendly the local people were.

---

# 🧠 27️⃣ “It Was Worth It”

Excellent expression:

> The journey was tiring, but it was worth it.

**worth it = কষ্ট/সময়/টাকার মূল্য ছিল**

Example:

> The trip was expensive, but it was definitely worth it.

---

# 🔥 28️⃣ Travel With Friends vs Alone

Question:

> Do you prefer traveling alone or with friends?

### Friends:

> I prefer traveling with friends because it's more enjoyable.

### Alone:

> I sometimes prefer traveling alone because I can make my own decisions.

### Balanced:

> I enjoy both, but it depends on the type of trip.

---

# 🧠 29️⃣ “It Depends On...”

> It depends on the destination.

> It depends on how long the trip is.

> It depends on whether I want to relax or explore.

---

# 🔥 30️⃣ Traveling Alone

Useful:

> Traveling alone gives me more freedom.

> I can make my own schedule.

> I can decide where to go and what to do.

But:

> However, traveling with friends can be more fun.

---

# 🧠 31️⃣ Planning a Trip

Question:

> How do you plan a trip?

Structure:

> First, I choose the destination.

> Then, I decide my budget.

> After that, I book transportation and accommodation.

> Finally, I make a rough itinerary.

---

# 🔥 32️⃣ Important Travel Vocabulary

| English       | বাংলা              |
| ------------- | ------------------ |
| trip          | ভ্রমণ              |
| travel        | ভ্রমণ করা          |
| journey       | যাত্রা             |
| destination   | গন্তব্য            |
| tourist       | পর্যটক             |
| attraction    | দর্শনীয় স্থান      |
| accommodation | থাকার ব্যবস্থা     |
| hotel         | হোটেল              |
| ticket        | টিকিট              |
| luggage       | লাগেজ              |
| passport      | পাসপোর্ট           |
| itinerary     | ভ্রমণ পরিকল্পনা    |
| scenery       | প্রাকৃতিক দৃশ্য    |
| landscape     | ভূদৃশ্য            |
| local         | স্থানীয়            |
| culture       | সংস্কৃতি           |
| experience    | অভিজ্ঞতা           |
| explore       | ঘুরে দেখা          |
| adventure     | রোমাঞ্চকর অভিজ্ঞতা |
| memorable     | স্মরণীয়            |

---

# 🔥 33️⃣ “Trip” vs “Travel” vs “Journey”

### Travel

General activity:

> I love traveling.

### Trip

Specific visit:

> I went on a trip to Cox's Bazar.

### Journey

The movement from one place to another:

> The journey took six hours.

সহজভাবে মনে রাখো:

> **Travel = activity**

> **Trip = specific visit**

> **Journey = way/movement**

---

# 🧠 34️⃣ Past Experience Structure

যেকোনো travel story এভাবে বলবে:

### Beginning

> Last year, I went to...

### Reason

> I went there because...

### People

> I went with...

### Journey

> We traveled by...

### Activities

> We visited...

### Problem

> Unfortunately,...

### Solution

> We...

### Feeling

> I felt...

### Conclusion

> Overall, it was...

---

# 🔥 35️⃣ Full 3-Minute Example

## 🎤 Question:

### Tell me about a memorable trip.

> One of the most memorable trips I've had was a trip to Cox's Bazar with my friends. We went there during our vacation because we wanted to relax and spend some time together.
>
> We traveled by bus, and the journey took several hours. It was a little tiring, but we enjoyed talking and listening to music during the journey.
>
> When we arrived, we checked into our hotel and then went to the beach. The beach was beautiful, and the atmosphere was very relaxing. We walked along the beach, took lots of pictures, and watched the sunset.
>
> We also tried some local food. One of the highlights of the trip was having dinner together and talking about different things.
>
> Unfortunately, the weather suddenly changed one evening, and it started raining. We had to return to our hotel earlier than expected. At first, we were disappointed, but later we laughed about the experience.
>
> I felt very happy and relaxed during the trip. I also enjoyed spending time with my friends away from our normal routine.
>
> The trip taught me that traveling isn't only about visiting beautiful places. It's also about spending time with people, experiencing new things, and creating memories.
>
> In the future, I'd like to visit more places, both inside and outside Bangladesh. I'd especially like to explore places with beautiful natural scenery and learn about different cultures.
>
> Overall, it was a simple trip, but it became a very memorable experience for me.

---

# 🧠 36️⃣ Future Travel

Question:

> Where would you like to travel in the future?

Start:

> In the future, I'd like to visit...

Then:

> I'd like to visit this place because...

Example:

> In the future, I'd like to visit Japan because I'm interested in its culture, technology, and natural beauty.

---

# 🔥 37️⃣ Dream Destination

> My dream destination is...

> I've always wanted to visit...

> One place I'd really like to visit is...

Example:

> One place I'd really like to visit is Japan.

---

# 🧠 38️⃣ Why Do You Want to Visit?

Use:

> I'm interested in the culture.

> I'd like to try the local food.

> I'd like to see the scenery.

> I'd like to experience the local lifestyle.

> I've heard that it's beautiful.

---

# 🔥 39️⃣ Travel Benefits

Travel can:

> help us relax

> teach us about different cultures

> improve our confidence

> give us new experiences

> help us meet new people

> create memories

Excellent sentence:

> I think traveling is a great way to learn outside the classroom.

---

# 🧠 40️⃣ Lesson 90 — Final Speaking Challenge

## 🎤 Topic 1 — Your Best Trip

Answer:

1. Where did you go?
2. When did you go?
3. Who did you go with?
4. Why did you go?
5. How did you travel?
6. What did you do there?
7. What was the place like?
8. Did anything unexpected happen?
9. How did you feel?
10. What did you learn?

🎯 **Target: 3–5 minutes**

---

# 🎤 Topic 2 — Dream Destination

Talk about:

> **Place → Why → Culture → Food → Activities → People → Experience → Future**

---

# 🎤 Topic 3 — Travel Alone or With Friends?

Use:

> **Personally, I prefer...**

> **One reason is...**

> **Another reason is...**

> **However...**

> **For example...**

> **It depends on...**

> **Overall...**

---

# 🧠 Lesson 90 Golden Formula

# **PLACE → WHEN → WHO → WHY → JOURNEY → ACTIVITIES → PROBLEM → SOLUTION → FEELING → LESSON → FUTURE**

Example:

> **Last year, I went to...**
>
> **I went there with...**
>
> **The main reason was...**
>
> **We traveled by...**
>
> **When we arrived,...**
>
> **We visited...**
>
> **Unfortunately,...**
>
> **We solved the problem by...**
>
> **I felt...**
>
> **The trip taught me...**
>
> **In the future, I'd like to...**
>
> **Overall,...**

---

# 🏆 🎉 Lesson 61–90 Complete!

তুমি এখন 30টি lesson-এর মাধ্যমে **conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়ার basic structure** তৈরি করেছ।

### তোমার speaking formula এখন:

> **ANSWER → REASON → EXAMPLE → EXPERIENCE → FEELING/OPINION → FUTURE**

যেমন:

> **I enjoy traveling** → because I like exploring new places → **for example**, I visited... → **during the trip**... → **I felt...** → **in the future, I'd like to...**

এখন সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ বিষয় হলো **নতুন grammar শেখা নয়, এই structures মুখে ব্যবহার করা।**

### 🎯 Final Lesson 90 Challenge

একটি topic নিয়ে **5 মিনিট continuous English speaking**:

> **“My Life, My Experiences, and My Future Goals”**

এতে Lesson 61–90-এর বিভিন্ন topic connect করবে:

**Opinion → Experience → Education → Career → Hobbies → Food → Travel → Future Goals**

লক্ষ্য হবে **মাঝে থেমে না গিয়ে** 5 মিনিট কথা বলা। ভুল হলেও কথা চালিয়ে যাবে।


---
হ্যাঁ, **daily 1 hour দিলে তোমার English speaking অনেক ভালোভাবে improve হবে**—বিশেষ করে তুমি যেহেতু Lesson 61–90 পর্যন্ত speaking practice করেছ।

তবে শুধু ১ ঘণ্টা পড়লেই হবে না। **১ ঘণ্টাকে speaking-focused ভাবে ভাগ করা** সবচেয়ে ভালো।

## 🕐 Daily 1-Hour English Routine

### 1️⃣ 10 মিনিট — Vocabulary

প্রতিদিন **5–10টা useful word/phrase** শিখবে।

শুধু meaning মুখস্থ করবে না।

Example:

> **Improve** = উন্নতি করা
> I want to improve my English.

> **Confident** = আত্মবিশ্বাসী
> I want to become more confident.

---

### 2️⃣ 10 মিনিট — Listening 🎧

একটা ছোট English video/audio শুনবে।

প্রথমবার শুধু শুনবে।

দ্বিতীয়বার গুরুত্বপূর্ণ sentence ধরার চেষ্টা করবে।

তারপর 2–3টা sentence **জোরে বলবে**।

---

### 3️⃣ 15 মিনিট — Speaking 🗣️ ⭐

এটাই সবচেয়ে important।

একটা topic নিয়ে কথা বলবে।

যেমন:

> My daily routine

তারপর:

> What I do → Why I do it → Example → Experience → Opinion → Future

**মোবাইলে voice record করলে আরও ভালো।**

প্রথমে:

> 1 minute

তারপর:

> 2 minutes

তারপর:

> 3–5 minutes

---

### 4️⃣ 10 মিনিট — Shadowing

একজন native/advanced speaker-এর ছোট audio/video শুনে **তারপর একইভাবে বলবে**।

Example:

Audio:

> I usually wake up early in the morning.

তুমি:

> I usually wake up early in the morning.

তারপর নিজের sentence:

> I usually start studying after breakfast.

এতে **pronunciation + fluency + speaking speed** improve হবে।

---

### 5️⃣ 10 মিনিট — Grammar in Speaking

Grammar আলাদাভাবে অনেকক্ষণ পড়বে না।

আজ যে grammar দরকার, সেটা দিয়ে sentence বানাবে।

Example: **Past Simple**

> I went to university yesterday.

> I studied English last night.

> I watched a movie.

তারপর নিজের experience দিয়ে 5–10টা sentence বলবে।

---

### 6️⃣ 5 মিনিট — Review

শেষে নিজেকে 3টা প্রশ্ন:

> What did I learn today?

> What mistakes did I make?

> What will I practice tomorrow?

---

# 🔥 1-Hour Formula

মনে রাখো:

**10 min Vocabulary**
↓
**10 min Listening**
↓
**15 min Speaking** ⭐
↓
**10 min Shadowing**
↓
**10 min Grammar + Sentence Practice**
↓
**5 min Review**

= **60 minutes**

---

# 📅 তোমার জন্য আরও ভালো Strategy

তুমি যেহেতু **Lesson 61–90 complete করেছ**, এখন শুধু নতুন lesson করতে থাকলে হবে না।

আমি হলে এভাবে করতাম:

### 🟢 Day 1–7

Lesson 61–67 **revision + speaking**

### 🟢 Day 8–14

Lesson 68–74 **revision + speaking**

### 🟢 Day 15–21

Lesson 75–81 **revision + speaking**

### 🟢 Day 22–30

Lesson 82–90 **revision + speaking**

প্রতিদিন পুরোনো lesson থেকে **একটা topic নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট কথা বলবে।**

---

# 🎯 সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ পরিবর্তন

আগে:

> Read → Understand → Next Lesson

এখন:

> **Learn → Speak → Record → Find Mistakes → Speak Again**

এটাই তোমার জন্য বেশি effective হবে।

### Example

আজ Topic:

**My Career Goals**

প্রথমবার:

> I want to become a software developer...

Voice record করবে।

তারপর শুনে দেখবে কোথায় আটকে যাচ্ছ।

তারপর আবার বলবে।

দ্বিতীয়বার:

> I want to build a career in software development. My short-term goal is to gain practical experience...

এভাবে **একই topic 2–3 বার বলা** নতুন 3টা topic পড়ার চেয়ে speaking-এর জন্য অনেক বেশি useful।

---

# 🏆 3-Month Target

যদি সত্যিই **প্রতিদিন 1 hour × 90 days** করতে পারো:

### Month 1

**Sentence → Short conversation**

### Month 2

**2–3 minute continuous speaking**

### Month 3

**5+ minute conversation + opinion + experience + explanation**

তখন তোমার লক্ষ্য হবে:

> **“I don't need to translate every sentence from Bangla to English before speaking.”**

এটাই fluency-এর দিকে বড় improvement।

**তোমার ক্ষেত্রে daily 1 hour যথেষ্ট ভালো—কিন্তু এর অন্তত 15–20 মিনিট অবশ্যই active speaking হতে হবে।**


---
